Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.9

Chapter 801
Even a Divine Sea warrior would have to reattach his arm after losing it. If he didn't do so, he would become a cripple.
Such a miraculous self-healing ability shouldn't appear on a human's body. Therefore, the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace's interest in this little monster grew.
The Nine Cauldron Martial Palace's research on this little monster's body had always been carried out in secret. Until the warriors of the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace discovered a huge secret in this little monster's body, an innate talent that could help other warriors in their cultivation!
A warrior's innate talent was determined at birth. There might be some herbs that could change one's innate talent, but they couldn't be found in the Divine Kingdom.
In order to cultivate faster, and to reach a higher level, the Divine Kingdom had built many Martial Palaces, countless secret cultivation grounds, and organized warriors of all levels to train and fight with ferocious beasts. They could stimulate one's innate potential in the midst of life and death …
Everything was done in order to stimulate one's innate talent to the fullest, and make the best use of it.
When the warriors of the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace discovered this little monster's innate talent, they immediately went crazy.
A Xiantian warrior who had been stuck in a bottleneck for many years might never have the chance to break through to the Divine Reflection realm. But, after sparring with this little monster, the bottleneck was quickly broken, and not long after, he would break through to the Divine Reflection realm …
This was the effect of this little monster's innate talent.
The little monster who had just entered the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace was only at the Marrow Refining realm, he wasn't even a Xiantian lifeform.
Soon, the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace found out that this little monster could help others break through their bottleneck during sparring. But, there was a limit to it; the gap between their cultivation bases couldn't be too big.
When a Divine Core warrior sparred with this little monster, the benefits he could get were minimal. But, a Xiantian lifeform could obtain great benefits from this little monster.
The Nine Cauldron Martial Palace had built a stone tower specifically for this little monster, then they chained him to the tower with iron chains. After that, they would send some Xiantian warriors into the tower every day to fight with this little monster.
With this little monster's ferocious nature, many Xiantian warriors had died in his hands. But, after sparring with this little monster, those Xiantian warriors who survived would quickly break through to the next level.
The little monster wasn't an idiot. Although he was filled with his ferocious nature, he soon realized the purpose of these warriors.
But, for the next few days, the little monster refused to fight. No matter how the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace tortured him, he refused to fight.......
After a few months of deadlock, the Nine Cauldron Martial Palace.
After all, it would be a waste to let the little monster cultivate with the Xiantian Realm martial artists of the Nine Cauldrons Martial House. It would be better to nurture the little monster. In the future, when he reached the Divine Reflection Realm, False Tribulation Realm, or even the Life and Death Realm, he would be of great help to the Nine Cauldrons Martial House!
This was the reason why the little monster had the opportunity to blend into the human world. As his overall strength became stronger, his talent became more and more important. After breaking through to the Divine Core realm, he was able to help Void Tribulation warriors break through their bottlenecks, and after breaking through to the Void Tribulation realm, he had even helped many Life and Death warriors break through their bottlenecks!
Because of this, many Divine Sea Realm warriors were hoping that this little monster could break through to the Late Stage of the False Tribulation Realm.
If the little monster entered the Life and Death Realm, wouldn't it be able to help a bunch of Spirit Sea Realm warriors break through their bottlenecks?
This was a huge temptation, one that no Spirit Sea Realm warrior could resist.
Regardless of whether it was the Yu the Great, the Tianfeng War Emperor, or Zhou Huang, they had been stuck at the Divine Sea Realm for countless years. However, the resources in the Lower World were too scarce for them to break through to the Divine Sea Realm!
Since they couldn't ascend to the Immortal Realm, the little monster was the only hope for these Divine Sea warriors.
Taking advantage of this fact, the little monster had a high status. Even the Princes and Crown Princes didn't dare offend him.
But, ever since the little monster reached the Late Void Tribulation realm, his cultivation had suddenly stopped.
Many Divine Sea warriors were confused. The little monster's talent was incredible, so why was he stuck at the Late Void Tribulation realm?
Ten years had passed, but the little monster was still stuck at the Late Void Tribulation realm. He hadn't stepped into the Life and Death realm.
Some Divine Sea warriors couldn't help but challenge the little monster. But, the gap between the Divine Sea realm and the Void Tribulation realm was just too big, and the results were almost negligible.
But, they didn't have any better ideas. All they could do was wait for the little monster to break through.
Fortunately, Divine Sea warriors had long lives. They had plenty of time to wait. If they couldn't break through to the Void Tribulation realm in ten years, then they would wait for twenty or thirty years.
It should be easy to kill Luo Zheng with such a guy. But, after watching the battle between Luo Zheng and Huo Yuner, Bai Yuerong hesitated. Luo Zheng's talent was horrifying. Like the little monster, he also had supernatural powers that were beyond the reach of ordinary people.
Under Bai Yuerong's watch, the battle inside the seal began to quicken.
The little monster's one hand wrapped tightly around Luo Zheng, forcing Luo Zheng into a hand-to-hand fight. This was the little monster's best fighting style. Every part of his body could be used as a deadly weapon.
None of the warriors who were entangled by the little monster survived. Without exception, they all died in his hands.
Unfortunately, this time, the little monster met Luo Zheng. The little monster could turn his whole body into a weapon, but Luo Zheng himself was a weapon!
Not to mention, Luo Zheng had just refined a moment ago. He already had the body of a divine weapon!
The little monster's neck suddenly elongated, and its head was raised high. Then, it smashed its head heavily at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng looked calm. His left hand was full of power. He punched at the little monster's head. It was like punching a sandbag. The huge force directly smashed the little monster's head 200 feet away, hitting the side wall of the seal. Then, the little monster's head bounced back and smashed at Luo Zheng with an even more horrifying force!
The battle between the two was like two ferocious beasts.
Every time they attacked, even the seal set up by a mighty warrior of the Divine Sea Realm would shake violently.
Warriors of the False Tribulation Realm were so powerful that they could split mountains and rocks with a raise of their hands. In fact, each of their punches had the same effect. It was just that they were bound by the seal, so it didn't look so horrifying. If the seal was released and they were fighting outside, the West Dragon River would have been riddled with holes by now!
"I'm also stunned by this battle. It seems that there is no winner …" A warrior shook his head and said.
"It's Luo Tianxing's bad luck to meet an opponent like the little monster. It's too difficult to deal with!"
"But this Luo Zheng isn't bad either. What the hell is his body? He must have been attacked by the little monster a lot. It looks like nothing happened to him! "
At this time, a trace of impatience appeared on Luo Zheng's face. This little monster was more difficult to deal with than he had imagined. He had to get this guy off him first. Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly turned around and twisted the other hand of the little monster. Then he suddenly jumped into the air and kicked the little monster's chest hard.
Seeing Luo Zheng's action, the little monster understood Luo Zheng's action. "You want to get rid of me? In your dreams! "
But at this moment, the Vital Spirit in Luo Zheng's body suddenly burst out. The moment he kicked, the power of the dragon scales in his body also surged out. Then Luo Zheng hit the edge of the seal like a shooting star. But the little monster's hands were still wrapped around Luo Zheng's body!
Luo Zheng had expected this. The moment he hit the edge of the seal, he pulled hard again.
"Bang!"
The little monster's soft body also fell to the side of Luo Zheng.
"Dream on? I don't believe that your body has no limit! " This time, Luo Zheng swung his hands again and threw the little monster away. The little monster's body was instantly stuck on the other side of the seal …
"Bang, bang, bang, bang …"
Loud sounds were heard. The little monster's body was swung around by Luo Zheng and smashed on both sides of the seal!
Every impact caused the seal to shake. One could imagine how much force the little monster's body was bearing.
"This …" Seeing the little monster being treated like this by Luo Zheng, a Divine Sea Realm almighty showed a worried expression. Then, he asked Battle Emperor Yu the Great, "Should we stop Luo Zheng? Although the little monster has an almost immortal body, if this continues, it's hard to say! "
Many Divine Sea Realm almighty were eagerly waiting for the little monster to enter the False Tribulation Realm. They were afraid that the little monster would be smashed to death by Luo Zheng!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 802
The little monster chose to fight with Luo Zheng in close combat. It was definitely a wrong choice.
His body was indeed very special. No one in the four Divine Kingdoms could match him in terms of strength or physical strength!
Therefore, in theory, the little monster would have a great advantage in close combat with his physical strength!
Unfortunately, he met Luo Zheng.
With Luo Zheng's current strength, even a Divine Ocean Realm Almighty would not be able to withstand a punch from him.
Moreover, after reaching the Divine Artifact Body, Luo Zheng's body was as strong as a Divine Artifact! Ordinary Divine Artifacts such as sabers and swords were only thin pieces. However, Luo Zheng's body was much wider than ordinary sabers and swords. Naturally, his body was much stronger than ordinary Divine Artifacts.
How could the little monster hurt Luo Zheng in close combat?
Therefore, the little monster chose a path that was destined to fail …
Battle Emperor Yu the Great didn't say anything. The little monster wouldn't die so easily. The little monster's life force was so strong that it made people's hair stand on end. However, he was still being hit by Luo Zheng.
The problem was that if he stopped the little monster, it would be equivalent to the little monster admitting defeat. Like Bai Yuerong, Battle Emperor Yu the Great hoped that the little monster could kill Luo Zheng!
At this moment, Battle Emperor Yu the Great was a little annoyed. The little monster was too arrogant at the beginning. He crippled his own martial arts and gave up on using his True Essence. Instead, he fought with Luo Zheng with his physical strength! Just like Bai Yuerong said, Luo Zheng had killed a fourth stage False Tribulation Realm warrior. Moreover, the Star Sword Venerable Master and the Heartless Monk had also fallen.
Although Battle Emperor Yu the Great didn't believe that the Heartless Monk and the Star Sword Venerable Master were killed by Luo Zheng, it was still related to this kid. This kid's secrets were probably no less than the little monster's!
"Bang, bang, bang …"
The violent attacks continued. Battle Emperor Yu the Great's expression became more and more unpleasant. How long was this guy going to endure?
Battle Emperor Yu the Great's expression didn't look good. However, many warriors felt very comfortable.
This little monster was a notorious guy. If all the evil deeds he had committed were accounted for, even if he was the Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince, he would be crippled!
The vast majority of martial artists hated this person very much. However, the vast majority of martial artists could not afford to offend this fellow, so they could only hide far away.
This was especially true for the Nine Cauldron Martial House's martial artists. When they saw the little monster being brutally abused by Luo Zheng, their faces were filled with gratification. It was as if they were happier than celebrating the New Year. Luo Zheng had helped them vent their anger.
Huo Yuner, who had returned to the top of the stairs, saw this scene, and a smile appeared on her face. It seemed that her previous worries were superfluous. Evil people would be dealt with by evil people. This little monster deserved it!
Huo Yuner rarely fought and rarely left the Tianfeng Divine Nation. However, in order to break through faster, the Tianfeng War Emperor once brought Huo Yuner to the Great Yu Divine Nation to spar with the little monster.
At that time, the little monster had already broken through to the Divine Core realm. When he was sparring with Huo Yuner, he kept spouting dirty words, and nearly injured Huo Yuner. At that time, the Tianfeng War Emperor's face turned green, and he even wanted to kill the little monster. Luckily, Battle Emperor Yu the Great stopped him, allowing the little monster to escape death.
Although Huo Yuner was able to quickly break through to the Late Divine Core realm with the help of the little monster's innate talent, the little monster's frightening appearance had left a deep impression on her. After returning to the Heavenly Wind Divine Nation, she had spent half a year in nightmares …
Therefore, whenever Huo Yuner mentioned the little monster, a deep fear would emerge in her heart. At the same time, she hated him to the core.
On the other hand, Luo Zheng had left a deep impression on Huo Yuner. All martial artists were born to worship those who were stronger than them.
Within the four Divine Nations, there were many people who Huo Yuner worshipped, but they were all her seniors.
Amongst her peers, aside from the little monster, no one else was strong enough to enter her eyes. Even the Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince's strength was far inferior to hers.
But this time, Luo Tianxing had appeared out of nowhere.
This martial artist called "Luo Tianxing" caused a trace of curiosity to arise in Huo Yuner's heart. Therefore, after Luo Tianxing defeated Yun Longtao, she made a request to her father to spar with him.
Although the imperial family wouldn't easily accept a challenge, Huo Yuner was finally interested in sparring with someone. Since the Tianfeng War Emperor doted on her, how could he not agree?
After being defeated by Luo Zheng, her mind was filled with complicated thoughts. She even had the urge to know more about Luo Zheng.
Who was his master? How did he grow to his current level? Did he really come from the Northern Heaven Region? How could such a genius appear in such a small place? All sorts of questions kept popping up in her mind.
Of course, as a Divine Kingdom's Princess, no matter how hard it was to suppress her thoughts, her expression was very reserved. Therefore, she only opened her eyes wide and quietly looked at Luo Zheng.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to swing the little monster to the side of the barrier, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a sharp pain in his hand. Luo Zheng instantly let go, and the two long arms of the little monster quickly retracted and returned to their original state.
The little monster finally began to circulate its True Origin and floated in the air.
Luo Zheng slowly floated up and looked at the little monster indifferently. He said coldly, "I thought you really wouldn't use your True Origin!"
The little monster frankly admitted, "I underestimated you!"
After being hammered by Luo Zheng for so long, even though it had an almost immortal body, its expression was somewhat dispirited.
However, even Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh at the little monster's ability to resist attacks. It was indeed very valiant!
In the history of Luo Zheng, the little monster had the strongest physical body among warriors of the same level as him. It was second only to him!
Indeed, there were all kinds of strange things in the world. Just like what Qinglong said, his magic treasure body was probably unique in the universe. However, there were many variables in the universe, and Luo Zheng was just one of them. Perhaps the little monster was another one.
The little monster used to be arrogant and unreasonable. Not to mention Luo Zheng, even when facing Battle Emperor Yu the Great, he couldn't be serious. After all, he grew up in a forest full of ferocious beasts. What dominated him was his bestial nature!
However, after being trained by the Nine Cauldrons Martial House, the little monster slowly had his own goal. His goal was to kill all the Divine Sea Realm warriors in the Divine Kingdom Continent! He wanted to make the entire Divine Kingdom Continent his world!
This goal sounded like a fool's dream, but in the little monster's heart, it was not impossible!
Why did Battle Emperor Yu the Great want to train him? The purpose was to let him grow and then use his talent to make a breakthrough. However, after the little monster was captured by the Nine Cauldrons Martial House and secretly studied, he had already planted the seed of hatred in all the warriors. He would slowly settle the score with the little monster.
However, before he reached that step, the little monster had been enduring. Why hadn't he broken through to the False Tribulation Realm? It was because he had been suppressing his cultivation all this time!
He knew that once he broke through to the Life and Death Realm, he might really be able to influence those Divine Sea Realm warriors and help them break through to the Divine Sea Realm. This wasn't something the little monster wanted to see. Therefore, he chose to suppress his cultivation, hoping to reach a higher level when he broke through to the False Tribulation Realm!
Only in this way could he fight against Divine Sea Realm warriors after breaking through to the False Tribulation Realm!
Within the same realm, the little monster didn't see anyone as a worthy opponent. His requirement for himself was to surpass an entire realm. He was currently at late stage False Tribulation realm, but he could contend against fifth layer Life and Death realm warriors, and even sixth layer Life and Death realm!
Once he broke through to the Life and Death Realm, he could quickly reach the third or even the fourth stage of the Life and Death Realm. In a very short time, he could fight against those Divine Sea Realm warriors. Even if he wasn't a match for Battle Emperor Yu the Great and the other Monarchs, he could ensure that he could safely escape from the Divine Kingdom Continent. In the future, when his cultivation had reached a certain level, he would return and settle the score with them!
However, today, the little monster unexpectedly found another "monster". Luo Tianxing's cultivation was lower than his, only at the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm. However, this guy's strength didn't seem to be any weaker than his!
At this time, the little monster began to become very serious. He finally began to circulate the True Yuan in his body. As the True Yuan flowed slowly, specks of light began to brew in his hands!
They were specks of golden light. When the light shone, it gave people a feeling of showing off their abilities.
Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly. His face revealed a dignified expression. That golden True Yuan gave him a very strange feeling.
This feeling was very strange, so strange that it was indescribable. That golden True Yuan seemed to contain strange rules, which were incomparably complex …
"No wonder you couldn't kill him." Xun also noticed the little monster's strange phenomenon. Previously, Xun couldn't determine the little monster's origin, but after seeing the golden True Yuan, she was soon sure. "This guy is not a monster. To be exact, he's not a complete human. He's the Child of the Plane!"

Chapter 803
"Child of the Dimension?" Upon hearing Xun's words, Luo Zheng was stunned. What was that?
"The Great Thousand World you're in can be regarded as a separate dimension, and the dimension itself has its own will. Just like how the stars have their own will, the Great Thousand World also has its own will," Xun explained to Luo Zheng.
The will of the Great Thousand World was not a single will. In fact, it was a projection of the will of all the living beings in the Great Thousand World. It was an aggregate.
In this Greater Thousand World, humans made up the majority of the population. Therefore, the Children of the Dimension that were born were highly similar to humans. Of course, there were some differences. For example, the little monster had a forked tongue, sharp teeth like a wolf, and pitch-black pupils.
Not all Great Thousand Worlds would give birth to Children of the Dimension. It was only when a Great Thousand World was irreparably damaged that a Child of the Dimension would be condensed to repair the Great Thousand World.
"If I'm not wrong, this little monster should be condensed by the Great Thousand World to repair the Ascension Passage. He's the spokesperson of the Great Thousand World!" Xun judged.
The Ascension Passage of this Great Thousand World was destroyed by the True Dragon Clan, so many Divine Sea Realm cultivators were unable to ascend. That was why the Great Thousand World condensed a Child of the Dimension. Xun's analysis was not without reason.
"But the character of this Child of the Dimension is a little too strange. It seems that the process of his birth is a little complicated," Xun said, shaking her head.
When the Great Thousand World needed to repair itself, a Child of the Dimension would descend. The Child of the Dimension also had to go through the mystery of the womb. After being born, he would grow up like a normal warrior. When he reached adulthood, he would discover his mission and use the power of the dimension to repair the dimension.
However, the little monster's background was indeed too complicated. Because of his appearance, he was abandoned in the Seven Waters Forest since he was young.
Because he was a Child of the Dimension, the Ferocious Beasts did not reject him. They regarded him as one of their own and raised him.
After that, the Nine Cauldron Martial House's martial artists entered the Seven Children's Forest to train. In other words, they slaughtered Vicious Beasts, which naturally angered the little monster. In the end, the little monster stood on the opposite side of the humans, and that was how it grew into a child of the plane with such a personality.
Next, Xun smiled and said, "But your chance has come. The Child of the Plane is the embodiment of the Plane's Will. He is the condensed body of the Plane. If he uses his True Yuan, he will emit a natural talent that will allow you to comprehend more. This effect is even better than the Rising Dragon Platform and the Tower of Sin's Light of Creation!"
The Rising Dragon Platform and the Tower of Sin's Light of Creation were targeted at all races, but the natural talent of the Child of the Dimension was the amalgamation of all the rules of this dimension. Luo Zheng was born in this dimension, so the effect was naturally much better.
"I see!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
"Try to fight with him as much as possible. The longer you fight, the greater the benefits you will gain …" Xun continued to advise.
Luo Zheng and Xun were quietly communicating. The little monster saw this and noticed the changes in Luo Zheng's expression. Then, it grinned and said, "Hehehe, since you worked so hard to force me to use my True Yuan, I naturally have to repay you!"
After that, the Quintessential Essence in the little monster's hand suddenly exploded. The Quintessential Essence that was transmitted from the Quintessential Essence was extremely obscure. Then, the Quintessential Essence transformed into a long spear that suddenly cut through the air and shot towards Luo Zheng with a sharp whistle!
This golden long spear was extraordinarily powerful. How could Luo Zheng take it head-on? He suddenly twisted his body and dodged the long spear.
However, the long spear's power did not stop there. It continued to pierce through Luo Zheng's back. Behind him was the barrier formed by the Divine Sea Realm expert. Previously, even the phoenix's Nirvana Flame could not destroy this barrier. However, this golden spear pierced through it!
The Divine Sea Realm expert who formed the barrier changed his expression slightly. With a wave of his hand, the big hole in the barrier was instantly repaired.
The Divine Sea Realm experts were aware of this scene. This was also one of the many mysteries of the little monster. His True Yuan contained a strange law, which seemed to be able to restrain all the forces in the world.
The True Yuan long spear released by the little monster was not powerful enough to pierce through the barrier. Instead, it directly dissolved the True Yuan in the barrier.
They did not know that the little monster was the Child of the Plane. Even the little monster himself did not know that he had integrated all the natural talents in the Great Thousand World. Of course, this included all the natural talents of human beings.
Therefore, in this Great Thousand World, the little monster had an absolute home advantage.
With the cultivation of the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm, the little monster could defeat most of the Life and Death Realm experts. Only the Divine Sea Realm experts could suppress him!
This was because the Divine Sea Realm experts had their own Realm, which was the law created by themselves. If the law of the Realm was strong enough, it could weaken the influence of the will of the Great Thousand World.
However, at this stage, the Divine Sea Realm experts could still control the little monster. However, if this guy was allowed to grow and break through the Life and Death Realm, it would be hard to say.
Seeing the effect of his attack, the little monster was very satisfied. The will of the Great Thousand World had not awakened. Therefore, he was now just a monster with a twisted personality. Killing warriors was one of his hobbies. An opponent like Luo Zheng was becoming more and more difficult to find!
"Hahahaha …"
The little monster burst into wild laughter. The golden True Yuan gushed out like a tide, turning into golden long spears that pierced toward Luo Zheng.
While Luo Zheng dodged these golden long spears, he also felt the golden light escaping. In an instant, Luo Zheng also absorbed a lot of it!
"Just as I thought." Luo Zheng's heart moved slightly. His dantian began to spin rapidly as if it was on fire.
The natural talent emitted from these golden long spears was much more effective than the Light of Creation!
Luo Zheng had stayed in the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm for some time. Originally, Luo Zheng could have cultivated to the peak of the War Revered Realm in the Sin Tower. However, because he was worried about Luo Yan, he chose to return to the Central Domain. Then, in the Cloud Hall, Luo Zheng broke through to the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm, and his cultivation stopped at this realm.
However, he had already felt that he was about to break through. With the help of these natural talents, the opportunity to step into the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm appeared.
"Hehe! It feels good, doesn't it? " The little monster stared at Luo Zheng with a ferocious smile. "Do you feel that your mind is clear? Even your cultivation has become more profound?"
Luo Zheng did not speak. He continued to shuttle through space to avoid the golden long spears. At the same time, he absorbed the natural talent emitted from the golden True Yuan.
The little monster naturally knew that the things it released had the ability to increase one's cultivation. However, because the will of the Great Thousand World had not awakened, it did not know that this thing was actually called natural talent.
Seeing this scene, Battle Emperor Yu the Great's face darkened. This was not the result he wanted. Battle Emperor Yu the Great and Bai Yuerong's goal was to let the little monster kill Luo Zheng. However, the little monster inexplicably used his True Yuan and benefited from Luo Zheng!
"This guy is absorbing the little monster's natural talent!" These Divine Sea Realm powerhouses did not know that this kind of power was called natural talent. Therefore, they gave it a name, natural talent.
The little monster's natural talent was not unlimited. After releasing too much of it, it would revert to a greenish gray color. It would also lose the effect of enhancing the opponent's talent. In order to maintain this effect, the Nine Cauldrons Martial House would secretly kill some geniuses every once in a while and let the little monster devour them. Only in this way could his True Yuan regain its golden color.
In other words, the little monster's ability was to devour the talents of other warriors. Then, it would disperse those talents and let other warriors absorb them.
In the beginning, the little monster did not intend to use the power of True Yuan. However, it could only be defeated by Luo Zheng with its physical body. Now that Luo Zheng had discovered the little monster's secret, he simply surrounded the little monster and started to absorb the natural talent of the little monster. This was outrageous!

Chapter 804
The little monster didn't seem to mind that Luo Zheng was absorbing his natural talent. From his point of view, Luo Zheng wasn't necessarily more abominable than the Divine Sea Realm warriors on the stairs!
But since he had promised Bai Yuerong this time, there was no reason for him to let Luo Zheng leave here alive.
Hundreds of golden spears pierced out in a row, instantly riddling the entire barrier with holes. The Divine Sea Realm warrior could only repair this huge barrier time and time again.
The little monster's attacks were indeed fierce. It seemed that he was suppressing Luo Zheng inside the barrier. But that was just a way for the little monster to vent his anger. It didn't cause too much trouble for Luo Zheng.
However, after the little monster vented his anger to a certain extent, a creepy smile suddenly appeared on the corner of his mouth. "It's about time. Now it's time to send you to hell!"
Then his figure suddenly flashed. Taking advantage of the moment when Luo Zheng was crossing the space, he intercepted Luo Zheng first. The distance between him and Luo Zheng was less than two feet.
Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly froze. He punched at the little monster abruptly. But at this moment, the little monster stretched out his hand and blocked it gently!
With such a gentle block, Luo Zheng's power was eliminated into nothingness!
This time, it was Luo Zheng's turn to be astonished!
How was this possible?
Luo Zheng was afraid of being entangled by this little monster. After all, this guy was a big trouble for Luo Zheng. Therefore, he directly activated the power of 800 dragon scales. Although this wasn't Luo Zheng's limit, it was already 70% to 80% of his power!
Even a Divine Sea Realm warrior couldn't dissolve his attack so easily. But this little monster did it.
It was as if Luo Zheng's power was mixed with thousands of tons of force. It was like a punch thrown by a child, which didn't cause any harm to the little monster …
But after one breath, on a mountain beside the West Dragon River, a loud noise suddenly burst out.
"Boom!"
That mountain range was at least ten thousand feet high, and it was not a small mountain beside the West Dragon River. This mountain and a mountain on the other side of the West Dragon River were called the Hidden Dragon Gorge, and it was also a relatively famous gorge in the Divine Kingdom Continent.
Now, the mountain suddenly erupted with such a vigorous force. Then, the entire mountain range began to crack and collapse from the middle. Rolling boulders fell into the West Dragon River. The river water, which was originally rolling non-stop, was now surging with monstrous waves!
A Mountain collapsed with a loud bang.
"What's going on? There is no one's's on the West Dragon River. "
"I don't know. I was watching the battle between Luo Tianxing and the little monster. Luo Tianxing is also born with divine strength. His strength is so strong that it makes people's hair stand on end. However, the little monster easily took it.
...... "...
People were talking about it. They were getting more and more confused about the situation.
Luo Zheng looked dignified. He quickly withdrew his fist. Gritting his teeth, he accumulated a shocking force in his right fist again. This time, Luo Zheng activated all the power of the dragon scales. The Vital Spirit in his body burst out together with the power of the dragon scales.
All the power poured into Luo Zheng's fist. If this punch could hit a Divine Sea Realm warrior, he would be killed instantly!
But the little monster still didn't dodge. He reached out his hand toward Luo Zheng again.
"Bang!"
When Luo Zheng's fist hit the little monster's palm, the little monster still didn't move. At the same time, another loud noise burst out from another mountain on the West Dragon River.
"Boom …"
The second mountain collapsed …
The little monster grinned at Luo Zheng and said, "Interesting, isn't it?"
"The power has been transferred." Luo Zheng frowned.
The little monster chuckled and moved closer to Luo Zheng. He was not afraid of Luo Zheng at all. Instead, his face was full of arrogance. "Actually, it's quite boring to see you guys who think you're so talented and think you're so special every day …"
After saying that, the little monster turned his back to Luo Zheng. This time, he was facing many Divine Sea Realm warriors. "You call yourselves Divine Sea Realm geniuses, but it takes thousands of years to reach the Divine Sea Realm. You guys are geniuses? You're just a pile of sh * t. Hahahaha! "

When a Realm Lord took control of a big world, he could use the big world to help him bear the damage. For example, when two Realm Lords fought each other, they usually didn't suffer much damage. However, the stars in the big worlds they ruled would explode one by one.
Therefore, the battle between Realm Lords was often earth-shattering. It could easily destroy thousands of stars!
The little monster wasn't a Realm Lord, but he was the Son of the Dimension after all. He was the incarnation of the Will of the Vast Thousand World. It was normal for him to have this ability.

Luo Zheng couldn't help but roll his eyes. The Divine Sea Realm was still quite far away for Luo Zheng. The problem was, what should he do now?
After venting his anger, the little monster found that the god-level geniuses of the four Divine Kingdoms were all silent. No one jumped out to refute him. He suddenly felt bored.
What he hoped to see the most was to infuriate these idiots and then trample on him. He wanted to give these fellows a double blow mentally and physically. However, no one responded. It was indeed a little boring.
At this time, the little monster turned his head. Those dark eyes were full of madness. "I forgot that the battle between you and me hasn't ended yet …"
As soon as the little monster finished speaking, Luo Zheng suddenly distanced himself from him.

"Whoosh!"

If this guy could really transfer 100% of his power, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to do anything to him.

His power poured onto the little monster, but it would be transferred to the Vast Thousand World. Luo Zheng couldn't destroy the entire Vast Thousand World. Maybe the person who took away Luo Yan in the Void Spirit Sect could do it.

Chapter 805

Chapter 808

The Saintess of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom was actually a Saintess of the Esoteric Sect. It was because of the Esoteric Sect that they were able to open something called the "Reverse Spirit Channel". The resources of the Esoteric Sect from the Upper Realm were endlessly sent down through the Reverse Spirit Channel.
How rich were the resources of the Upper Realm? Some of the things that could only be considered trash in the Upper Realm were like treasures in the Lower Realm.

As for the Soul Cultivation Wood, there were three sections that were sent down through the Reverse Spirit Channel!
Two of them had been used. These two sections contained the souls of two almighty Divine Ocean Realm experts. Those two almighty Divine Ocean Realm experts had entered the Five Decay of Heaven and Man and were nearing the end of their lifespan.
Even they were unable to resist the Five Decay of Heaven and Man. In the end, they had no choice but to abandon their physical bodies and parasitize their souls in the Soul Cultivation Wood.
Now, there was only one section left, but it was in the hands of the Tianfeng Divine Kingdom, in the hands of the Tianfeng War Emperor!
The Soul Cultivation Wood's function was similar to Immortal Tian Miao's crystal ball. Back then, Immortal Tian Miao had also parasitized his soul in the crystal ball, which was why his soul was able to struggle at death's door. This Tianfeng War Emperor was an ancient powerhouse who had lived for over ten thousand years. Perhaps this section of Soul Cultivation Wood was prepared for him. If one day, he entered the Five Decay of Heaven and Man, he could parasitize his soul in it …
A section of Soul Cultivation Wood was enough to allow his soul to live for another thousand years.
After withdrawing from the memory, Luo Zheng's face was filled with determination. No matter what price he had to pay, he had to get this piece of Soul Cultivation Wood!
At this time, Luo Zheng looked over again. The exhaustion on Xun's face was even more intense, and the cracks on the surface of her soul seemed to be getting bigger and bigger. It seemed that she wouldn't be able to hold on for more than half a day.
"Luo Zheng, I … I'm a little sleepy." Smoky blinked her eyes. Her red pupils, which were originally sharp and threatening, were now in a dull state.
Luo Zheng floated over and gently stroked Smoky's face. He said softly, "Take a rest if you're tired. I'll help you ask for the Soul Cultivation Wood!"
At the same time, in the Sky Feather Holy Grounds of the Sea God Continent, a commotion suddenly broke out in the Holy Tree Hall.
"My king, my king … what's wrong?!"
"The king's statue seems to be getting dimmer and dimmer. It's almost on the verge of being extinguished!"
"Could something have happened to Luo Zheng? Go and call Liu Yu over!"
The extinguishing of a king's statue was definitely a huge earthquake for the Night Demon Clan!
In fact, this earthquake didn't just happen in the Sky Feather Holy Grounds. In the millions of worlds, there were millions of sacred grounds of the Night Demon Clan. The members of the Night Demon Clan all discovered this anomaly at the same time, and every single one of them was filled with worry!
In the Upper World, the various worlds controlled by the Night Demon Clan were also paying close attention to the changes in the king's statues.
The Fiend Night Clan controlled many great realms in the Upper Realm, and these great realms were built around three great realms. These three great realms represented the three kings of the Fiend Night Clan. They were the Dark Demon Realm, the Celestial Fox Realm, and the Blood Night Realm!
At this moment, on a star at the center of the Dark Demon Realm, a woman in a light blue feather robe coldly looked at the three king's statues. Her gaze was fixed on the King of Massacre's statue.
"Hahaha, this little bitch is still so stubborn. It seems that she's about to die! I originally planned to use the reverse spirit channel to send a few people down to kill that person in the Battle of the Worlds, but now I don't have to do it myself! "
This woman was the ruler of the Night Demon Clan, the ruler of punishment!
In the past, her king's statue had clashed with Luo Zheng, and in the end, Luo Zheng had smashed the statue. She was also Xun's greatest enemy.
When she noticed that Xun's statue was growing dimmer and dimmer, she knew that Xun's soul was probably about to collapse.
In fact, when she clashed with Xun last time, she knew that Xun was living in the body of a person in the Battle of the Worlds. She had panicked a little. No matter what, as long as Xun was still alive, the Night Demon Clan couldn't establish a new King of Massacre. This was a worry for her!
How could she not be happy when she saw that Xun's king's statue had dimmed to its limit?

…
Within the barrier, Luo Zheng finally opened his eyes. The moment he opened his eyes, he suddenly felt a strong sense of familiarity. It was as if the entire world had changed before his eyes!
Everything around him felt incomparably familiar. Every person, every face, as long as he willed it, he could know their names, their past, and even their secrets with just a thought.
Other than people, he could even trace the origin and origin of every tree, every leaf, and every grain of sand.
Of course, Luo Zheng wasn't that bored. He wasn't interested in investigating these things.
When he opened his eyes, the little monster was still standing there like a block of wood. Luo Zheng slowly walked over and gently pushed the little monster's forehead. The little monster fell to the ground with a loud bang.
The little monster's soul had been cleansed by the mysterious furnace. Now, the little monster was like a walking corpse. No matter how tenacious his vitality was, he wouldn't be able to live for long.
"Wow …"

"Oh my god, if the little monster becomes an idiot, will the Nine Cauldrons Martial House let Luo Tianxing go?"
For the Immortal Realm geniuses below, the little monster's life wasn't important. Anyway, under normal circumstances, they wouldn't have the chance to practice with the little monster and absorb its innate ability.
What's more, the little monster's original appearance was loathsome. There were many people who loathed the little monster. Huo Yuner was one of them. When she saw Luo Zheng's victory, a gratified smile appeared on her face. She had never killed anything in her life, but to Huo Yuner, the little monster was like a nightmare. Even if the little monster died, she would not feel pity for it.
Many Immortal Realm geniuses felt good, but the Divine Sea Realm cultivators' faces were very ugly.
These Divine Sea Realm cultivators were still waiting for the little monster to enter the Life and Death Realm. After entering the Life and Death Realm, the little monster's innate ability would be effective for the Divine Sea Realm cultivators. It could help them break through to the Divine Sea Realm.
When a martial artist cultivated to their level, this was probably their only pursuit.
But now, the little monster's soul had been annihilated by Luo Zheng.
Even Zhou Huang's face was a bit ugly.
"Luo Zheng! How dare you annihilate the little monster's soul! You're courting death! " Battle Emperor Yu the Great was extremely angry. As the monarch of Divine Kingdom of Yu the Great, he had never been so angry with a junior.
Hearing Battle Emperor Yu the Great's furious expression, many Immortal Realm geniuses' faces changed. Zhou Huang and the other Divine Sea Realm cultivators' eyes flickered. It seemed that Battle Emperor Yu the Great was going to make a move.
Zhou Huang sighed deeply in his heart. He regretted it. Maybe he should have warned Luo Zheng that he shouldn't have annihilated the little monster's soul. What was the difference between this and killing the little monster?
However, Zhou Huang and the other Divine Sea Realm cultivators were also puzzled. How did Luo Zheng annihilate the little monster's soul?
They had studied the little monster for all these years, but they couldn't solve the mystery of the little monster. However, they knew that the little monster was basically immortal.
Not only was its body immortal, but its soul was also very special. It was constantly emitting a strange light. Even the Battle Soul of a Divine Sea Realm cultivator couldn't compete with the little monster's soul!

Chapter 809
At this moment, Zhou Huang refused to speak. However, Prince Yan couldn't hold it in any longer. He helped Luo Zheng and said, "Monarch of Dayu Country, it's unavoidable for martial artists to make mistakes in fighting dolls. Why do you say that Luo Tianxing is courting death?"
The fact that King Yan was willing to stand out and speak to Battle God Yu the Great at this moment was already praiseworthy.

Luo Zheng's words were powerful and resonating!

Upon hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Divine Grade Geniuses nodded their heads silently. On one hand, they were impressed by Luo Zheng's courage. In the face of the enraged Fighting Emperor Da Yu, he was still able to think clearly and he even dared to reprimand Fighting Emperor Da Yu. On the other hand, they were worried for Luo Zheng. War Emperor Yu the Great was so angry that he laughed. Then, he took a step forward, passed through the Bounded Domain, and came before Luo Zheng.
Their eyes met.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great's Soul Suppression was like hundreds of mountains pressing down on Luo Zheng.
Before this, Luo Zheng was not afraid of Battle Emperor Yu the Great's Soul Suppression. Now that Luo Zheng's soul had merged with the will of the Great Thousand World, Battle Emperor Yu the Great's Soul Suppression did not have any effect on Luo Zheng.

In his rage, Battle Emperor Yu the Great did not care about his surroundings. His Soul Suppression gushed out as if it was free. He was venting his anger!

Luo Zheng looked calm. He did not refute his identity. Whether he admitted it or not, Battle Emperor Yu the Great would not recognize him as Luo Tianxing. Therefore, Luo Zheng asked directly, "How do you want to settle this score?"
Battle Emperor Yu the Great exhaled deeply. The little monster was a big bargaining chip for Battle Emperor Yu the Great. It was also because of this bargaining chip that the Great Yu Divine Kingdom gained a lot of advantages in the competition among the four Divine Kingdoms.
Firstly, the little monster could spar with the princes and princes, allowing them to cultivate faster. Secondly, as the little monster grew to the Life and Death Realm, it would be able to help the Divine Sea Realm sooner or later. Everyone was eagerly waiting for the little monster to grow.
It was because of this that the Monarchs of the other three Divine Kingdoms gave in to Battle Emperor Yu the Great in many things. Even the allocation of resources in the Reverse Spirit Channel was far more than the other three Divine Kingdoms! Now that the little monster had turned into an idiot, he had suffered a great loss!

"Ah!"
Huo Yuner, who was on the stairs, suddenly covered her mouth.
Let Luo Zheng take a punch from a Divine Sea Realm warrior? Wasn't this the same as asking Luo Zheng to die? As Huo Yuner thought this in her heart, a trace of reluctance appeared in her charming eyes.
The King of Yan closed his eyes in pain and clenched his teeth tightly.
If it was a Life and Death Realm warrior, Luo Zheng might be able to resist. With Luo Zheng's strong body, he might be able to take one punch from a Life and Death Realm warrior. However, a Divine Sea Realm warrior like Battle Emperor Yu the Great …
Luo Zheng's life was over!
Hearing Battle Emperor Yu the Great's words, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. The strength of a Divine Sea Realm warrior was unfathomable, but Luo Zheng had just merged with the will of the Great Thousand World.
Previously, the little monster could easily block Luo Zheng's attack. When Luo Zheng unleashed all his strength, he could transfer the strength to the surrounding mountains and rivers!
After Luo Zheng merged with the will of the Great Thousand World, he had a sense of familiarity with everything around him. It was as if he had established a special connection with this world. If he could use the surrounding mountains and rivers to block Battle Emperor Yu the Great's punch, this could be a solution.

Agree?
Hearing Luo Zheng's straightforward answer, even Battle Emperor Yu the Great was a little surprised. How could Luo Zheng agree so easily?
Originally, Battle Emperor Yu the Great thought that Luo Zheng was going to argue with him on the basis of reason. However, Battle Emperor Yu the Great didn't care about this at all. They were all warriors. Since they were all warriors, they should know the rules of warriors. How could a False Tribulation Realm warrior try to reason with a Divine Sea Realm warrior, Battle Emperor Yu the Great? What a joke!
As the saying went, "Reason, reason. Dao comes first, reason comes second. Dao is the Dao of warriors!" In other words, strength and fists were in front!
However, Luo Zheng didn't want to reason at all. He agreed just like that. Battle Emperor Yu the Great laughed and said, "Do you really think you can block my attack?"
Luo Zheng shook his head lightly and said, "I don't know."
"Hahahaha." Battle Emperor Yu the Great laughed wildly for a while. He was guessing Luo Zheng's thoughts. This guy might really think that he could block his punch. How naive! Battle Emperor Yu the Great said lazily, "Since you are so straightforward, I can really give you a chance. I will only use 80% of my strength for this punch. What do you think?"
"80% of your strength …" A Divine Sea Realm warrior shook his head silently. How could this be a chance?
Only the Spirit Sea Realm warriors knew how horrifying a Spirit Sea Realm warrior was. After the Spirit Sea was opened up in the body, it would form a world of its own. The Genuine Essence that was gathered would be as vast as the sea. When the Genuine Essence was condensed and punched out, the power would be unimaginable.
Not to mention 80% of his strength, even 30% of his strength would kill Luo Zheng. Those Divine Sea Realm warriors all thought so.
Luo Zheng's eyes were calm. Then, he nodded slightly. He was not confident, but there was no better choice at the moment. However, the will of the Great Thousand World and his own Divine Artifact body gave Luo Zheng some confidence. That was why he agreed without hesitation.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great sneered and began to accumulate his Vital Essence. However, at this moment, a voice came from the stairs. "Wait!"

Chapter 810
Battle Emperor Yu the Great didn't have much patience. Hearing Huo Yuner's words, Battle Emperor Yu the Great's face darkened. He said coldly, "Old Huo! Take care of your daughter! "

... Battle Emperor Yu the Great. Great Yu the Great Divine Sea Realm. 80% of the Divine Sea Realm's of the Divine Sea Realm expert was no joke..

It was too powerful.
Powerful as it was, Luo Zheng was safe and sound.
