Chapter 811
The War Emperors and Battle Emperors had planned to stop Battle Emperor Yu the Great, but to their surprise, Luo Zheng's counterattack sent Battle Emperor Yu the Great flying and even made him spit out blood.
Before this, Luo Zheng had relied on his own strength to shatter Yun Longtao's space armor. Everyone knew that Luo Zheng's strength was far greater than an ordinary martial artist's, but they never imagined that he would be so tyrannical!
The scene fell into silence again. How big was the gap between a False Tribulation Realm warrior and a Divine Sea Realm warrior? It was almost unimaginable. A False Tribulation Realm warrior wanted to injure a Divine Sea Realm warrior?
These old monsters who had lived for tens of thousands of years had never heard of such a thing.
The higher one's cultivation level was, the more thorough one's understanding of laws and power would be. It would be more difficult to challenge someone of a higher level.
It was not difficult for a Xiantian Realm warrior to challenge a Divine Reflection Realm warrior. However, it was difficult for a False Tribulation Realm warrior to challenge a Life and Death Realm warrior, and it was impossible for a Life and Death Realm warrior to challenge a Divine Sea Realm warrior!
However, these Divine Sea Realm warriors knew that Battle Emperor Yu the Great must have been careless just now. It was impossible for him to use his Protective Vital Energy to deal with a Middle Stage False Tribulation Realm warrior. Moreover, Battle Emperor Yu the Great did not use the power of his realm.
If Battle Emperor Yu the Great had activated his realm and used his own laws to envelop Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng would not have been able to fight back.
However, a Divine Sea Realm warrior needed to activate his realm to deal with a False Tribulation Realm warrior … If this news spread, he would be a laughing stock.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great was sent flying by this punch. His eyes were full of killing intent. If his eyes could kill, Luo Zheng would have been killed thousands of times.
He wiped away the blood at the corner of his mouth with one hand. He had taken a punch from Luo Zheng in front of so many people, including the Highgod Realm geniuses, the Crown Prince, and the princes of the Great Yu Divine Nation. He had lost all his face.
"Luo Zheng, although you are only in the False Tribulation Realm, you are strong enough to be my opponent!" Battle Emperor Da Yu said coldly. The killing intent in his eyes gradually faded and was replaced by boundless fighting spirit.
At the Divine Sea Realm, the Heart of Martial Arts had been thoroughly baptized. Thus, the Heart of Martial Arts had reached a level where it could be controlled freely. Battle Emperor Da Yu knew how to control his emotions.
He didn't belittle Luo Zheng anymore. After all, no matter how much he looked down on this False Tribulation Realm opponent, it was a fact that Luo Zheng had injured him. Therefore, Battle Emperor Yu the Great acknowledged Luo Zheng's strength and regarded him as an important opponent!
"Fighting with a mighty warrior of the Divine Sea Realm is your precious experience. Let me show you my field!" Battle Emperor Yu the Great smiled faintly. Then, with Battle Emperor Yu the Great as the center, tiny white threads started to float in all directions. The thin threads intertwined with each other and formed small cocoons.
"Cloud Silk Domain!"
Spirit Sea Realm warriors could make their own rules to a certain extent, but they couldn't resist all the rules of nature!
After reaching the Divine Sea Realm, one's strength would develop in two directions. One direction was to make one's own rules. The strongest control of one's field would be the more terrifying the power of the field would be. The other direction was to follow the rules and control the rules of nature at a higher level.
Therefore, to control one's own rules was called "defying the natural order". To control the rules of nature at a higher level was called "following the natural order".
In the Upper World, there were warriors who went against the natural order. They gave up on cultivating all the precepts of heaven and earth and focused on setting up their own territory. They created their own new precepts and repelled the other precepts of heaven and earth at the same time. However, it was extremely difficult for a warrior to completely repel the precepts of heaven and earth.
Warriors who chose to follow the natural order would give up on their "field" and focus on controlling the seventh, eighth, and even ninth level of the Origin Source Rules. However, when the power of law was cultivated to a higher level, it would become more and more mysterious and difficult to understand.
Regardless of whether it was going against the natural order or following the natural order, it was a path to becoming a strong warrior. However, most warriors cultivated both at the same time.
Since Battle Emperor Yu the Great had brought out his field, he had already developed a killing intent towards Luo Zheng! No matter how Luo Zheng did it, as long as Luo Zheng was enveloped by his field, there was no way he could survive!
Luo Zheng's face darkened. Although he had fused with the will of the world, it didn't mean that he was invincible. A Spirit Sea Realm warrior's field was enough to cut off his connection with the boundless universe. Was he doomed?
At this moment, Zhou Huang had already appeared in front of Luo Zheng. He faintly said to Battle Emperor Yu the Great, "Liu Zan, why kill a junior?"
Because Luo Zheng had inherited the little monster's talent, the Tianfeng War Emperor and the Sky Fortune Kingdom King also stood up. However, unlike Zhou Huang, they didn't stand in front of Luo Zheng. Instead, they spoke up for Zhou Huang.
"Liu Zan, this Luo Tianxing didn't do anything wrong. Why do you want to kill him?"
"Previously, you said that after the little monster broke through, its talent could help us break through. Now, although the little monster's soul was destroyed by Luo Tianxing and turned into an idiot, Luo Tianxing has inherited the little monster's talent. Doesn't that mean that our hopes are on Luo Tianxing?"
Over the years, because of the little monster, the Yu the Great Divine Kingdom had obtained many benefits. These Divine Sea Realm warriors were eagerly waiting for the little monster to break through to the False Tribulation Realm. The little monster was dead, but Luo Zheng had inexplicably stolen the little monster's talent.
For Battle Emperor Yu the Great, this was an unacceptable result. For the other Divine Sea Realm warriors, as long as it could help them break through to the Divine Sea Realm, it was the same. Perhaps Luo Tianxing was more reliable than the little monster?
The Tianfeng War Emperor and the Sky Fortune Kingdom King's words represented the thoughts of the other Divine Sea Realm warriors. Many of them nodded slightly.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great's face was gloomy. Seeing Zhou Huang standing in front of him, he coldly said, "Those who stand in my way, die!"
Seeing that Battle Emperor Yu the Great didn't back down and was instead so stubborn, Zhou Huang was finally angry. Zhou Huang had always played the role of a nice guy in front of the Divine Sea Realm warriors. His personality wasn't unyielding and he wasn't impatient.
For the past day, Zhou Huang had repeatedly tolerated Battle Emperor Yu the Great. But now, he was standing in front of Luo Zheng and was determined to protect Luo Zheng. Yet, Battle Emperor Yu the Great still dared to speak like this!
"Die? Liu Zan, why don't we have a fight? Let's see who dies first. " Zhou Huang's eyes flashed with a sharp light. His body exuded an old but firm aura.
In terms of cultivation, Zhou Huang was almost the same as Liu Zan. They were both at the peak of the Divine Sea Realm. Because they were restricted by the lower realms, they couldn't cross that threshold in their lives. But in terms of strength, Zhou Huang had lived for more than 10,000 years. He was more than 5,000 years older than Liu Zan!
As long as Zhou Huang didn't enter Heaven's Decay Stage Five, Liu Zan wouldn't be a match for him!
"No way, are the Divine Sea Realm warriors going to fight?"
"Since I was born, I have never seen a battle between Divine Sea Realm warriors. Won't they be able to destroy the heavens and earth when they erupt?"
"Idiot, if the Black Iron Divine Kingdom and the Great Yu Divine Kingdom really fight, it will be a war between the Divine Kingdoms. Everyone will have a hard time. Maybe one day you will become cannon fodder!"
Many warriors in the False Tribulation Realm saw this scene and pondered silently. If the two Divine Sea Realm warriors really fought, should they run far away?
Because of Zhou Huang's words, Battle Emperor Yu the Great, Liu Zan, lost half of his arrogance …
Zhou Huang was indeed easy to talk to. Normally, he wouldn't use his strength to suppress others. But in terms of strength, he was probably the strongest among the four Divine Sea Realm warriors present. Even compared to those retired Divine Sea Realm warriors, Zhou Huang's strength wasn't inferior at all!
After realizing this fact, Liu Zan wouldn't really fight.
Zhou Huang glanced at Liu Zan indifferently and said, "Then, the warrior of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom wins this battle!"
Liu Zan took a deep look at Luo Zheng. Naturally, he was extremely displeased. Thus, he let out a cold snort and walked up the stairs.

Chapter 812
Seeing the Tianfeng War Emperor's kind smile, Luo Zheng was stunned. What was the meaning of this smile? Why was the War Emperor so polite to him?
However, he didn't think too much about it. Zhou Huang had protected him time and time again, so Luo Zheng was naturally grateful. He bowed to Zhou Huang and said, "Thank you, Your Majesty."
Zhou Huang shook his head lightly and said, "I should thank you on behalf of the Black Iron Divine Nation."
After all, Luo Zheng was representing the King of Yan and the Black Iron Divine Nation.
The battle between him and the little monster was over. No one else would step forward.
Luo Zheng could even resolve the full-force attack of a Divine Sea Realm cultivator. Which genius in the False Tribulation Realm would dare to try? If they stepped forward now, they would only be asking for humiliation.
Moreover, this battle was just a review. It didn't involve the interests of any party.
At this time, the King of Yan suddenly smiled and said, "Father, since Luo Zheng won, what are you going to reward him with?"
"Haha, reward? Easy, easy." Zhou Huang looked at Luo Zheng and asked, "Don't you need the Origin Stones of Life? My Black Iron Divine Nation still has some. These … "
Hearing Zhou Huang's words, Luo Zheng refused. "Your Majesty, I don't need the Origin Stones of Life."
Although he didn't know how many Origin Stones of Life there were in the Black Iron Divine Nation, he knew that the Origin Stones of Life were usually used up quickly. Not many would be stored. A dozen or so Origin Stones of Life might not be enough to save Ning Yudie's life.
The Origin Stones of Life would be left for Luo Zheng to excavate in the Sky Feather Sacred Sea.
What Luo Zheng was most worried about now was Xun! Now that she had fallen into a deep sleep, her soul was like a candle in the wind. It could be extinguished at any time!
What he needed most now was a piece of Soul Cultivation Wood!
"Oh?" Zhou Huang's face revealed a look of surprise. He already knew that Luo Zheng seemed to be in urgent need of the Origin Stone of Life, but now he had rejected it. Why? "Since you don't want the Origin Stones of Life, what do you want?"
Luo Zheng cupped his hands and said, "Your Majesty, I want a piece of Soul Cultivation Wood!"
"Soul Nurturing Wood?" Zhou Huang raised his eyebrows slightly, and the strange look on his face became even more intense. The Soul Cultivation Wood was only a container to store souls. Under normal circumstances, it did not have much use. He did not know what Luo Zheng was doing with the Soul Cultivation Wood.
Most importantly, Zhou Huang did not have the Soul Nurturing Wood.
Zhou Huang sighed softly and said, "But there is no Soul Cultivation Wood in my Black Iron Divine Kingdom."
There was indeed a piece of Soul Cultivation Wood in the Black Iron Divine Nation. However, that piece of Soul Cultivation Wood had been used up and contained the soul of a Spirit Sea Realm warrior. Where could Zhou Huang find another piece of Soul Cultivation Wood for Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng had expected Zhou Huang's answer.
Luo Zheng had never asked anyone for a reward. However, this Soul Cultivation Wood was related to the survival of Xun's soul. Luo Zheng could only say, "I know that there is no Soul Cultivation Wood in the Black Iron Divine Nation, but …"
Luo Zheng's gaze fell on the Tianfeng War Emperor and Huo Yuner. Then, Luo Zheng said to Zhou Huang, "As far as I know, there should be another piece of Soul Cultivation Wood in the Tianfeng Divine Nation."
Hearing this, the Tianfeng War Emperor was shocked!
There was indeed a piece of Soul Cultivation Wood in the Tianfeng Divine Nation. However, it was given out through the Secret Sect's heaven-defying passage. Ordinary people would not know about it. Perhaps even Zhou Huang did not know about it. The piece of Soul Cultivation Wood in the Tianfeng Divine Nation was not used. It was kept in the hands of the Tianfeng War Emperor.
Now, the question was, how did Luo Zheng know about it?
It was impossible for the Tianfeng War Emperor to understand the meaning of the Will of the World. Otherwise, the Divine Nation would not have been unable to discover the little monster's identity for so long.
Luo Zheng, who possessed the Will of the World, was in a state of "omniscience" in this world. He could understand the entire boundless universe in an instant. Therefore, there were no secrets in this boundless universe to Luo Zheng!
Zhou Huang was even more curious. Even he did not know about it, so how did Luo Zheng know about it?
Therefore, Zhou Huang looked at the Tianfeng War Emperor with a questioning gaze. "Old Huo, do you really have a piece of Soul Cultivation Wood?"
The Tianfeng War Emperor nodded. He reached out his hand and gently rubbed his spatial ring. Immediately, a brown withered branch appeared in his hand. The withered branch did not have a special appearance. At first glance, it looked like an ordinary branch.
Just by looking at the veins on the withered branch, one could feel the strangeness of this branch. This branch seemed to be able to pull one's soul into it.
"Old Huo …" Just as Zhou Huang was about to speak, the Tianfeng War Emperor understood that Zhou Huang wanted to ask for the Soul Cultivation Wood. Although the Soul Cultivation Wood could nourish one's soul, it was something that could only be done after one's physical body died. Therefore, although the Soul Cultivation Wood was rare, it could not be considered a precious item. "I can give this piece of Soul Cultivation Wood to Luo Tianxing. However, Luo Tianxing, you have to answer one of my questions."
Luo Zheng had already mentally prepared himself. No matter what the Tianfeng War Emperor said, Luo Zheng would pay any price to obtain this piece of Soul Cultivation Wood!
He did not expect that the so-called price would only be an answer to the Tianfeng War Emperor's question.
However, there were many types of questions. Luo Zheng had some secrets on him that were enough to make most warriors lose their minds. If he could not answer a question, Luo Zheng would be in even more trouble.
Then, Luo Zheng heard the Tianfeng War Emperor ask, "How did you know that I have a piece of Soul Cultivation Wood? Who told you that? "
Luo Zheng's eyelids twitched slightly. This was a question that Luo Zheng did not know how to answer.
"Tell me who told you. I won't pursue the matter. After all, this is not a big secret. I was just curious," the Tianfeng War Emperor said with a smile.
Looking at the piece of Soul Cultivation Wood, Luo Zheng felt conflicted. He could not possibly tell the Tianfeng War Emperor that he had inherited the will of this world, right?
Not only did Luo Zheng know that the Soul Cultivation Wood was in the hands of the Tianfeng War Emperor, but he also knew how the Tianfeng War Emperor grew, what cultivation techniques he cultivated, and what abilities he possessed! He could even know how many strands of hair the Tianfeng War Emperor had!
This was how terrifying the state of omniscience was!
Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment. At this moment, he was searching for the origin of the Soul Cultivation Wood in his mind. He wanted to know how many people knew about the existence of the Soul Cultivation Wood and see if he could make up a reason.
The results of his search disappointed Luo Zheng. After the Soul Cultivation Wood was passed down from the reverse spirit channel, it was personally handed to the Tianfeng War Emperor by Bai Yuerong. Only two or three people knew about the existence of the Soul Cultivation Wood. Bai Yuerong and Huo Yuner also knew about it.
Luo Zheng could not possibly lie and say that it was Bai Yuerong who told him. He was afraid that his lie would be exposed.
What about Huo Yuner?
Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat and he looked over. He did not expect Huo Yuner to also be looking at him. This girl was the daughter of the Tianfeng War Emperor. It would not be easy to fool her …
"What? You can't even answer this simple request? "the Tianfeng War Emperor asked with a smile.
Luo Zheng clenched his teeth and began to think about what he could use to exchange for the Soul Cultivation Wood. It was better than revealing his secret.
However, at this moment, Huo Yuner suddenly said, "Father, I was the one who told Luo Zheng."
"En?" Hearing Huo Yuner's words, the Tianfeng War Emperor was naturally puzzled. "You told Luo Zheng? When? "
"Just now, when Luo Zheng asked for the Soul Cultivation Wood from the Black Iron Divine Kingdom's Monarch, I told him with a true essence sound transmission." Huo Yuner smiled elegantly.
"Is that so …" the Tianfeng War Emperor stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Huo Yuner had taken the initiative to cooperate with him. How could Luo Zheng deny it? He immediately nodded and said, "It was indeed your daughter who told me."
Although this excuse was far-fetched, it was still true …
The Tianfeng War Emperor did not believe Huo Yuner's words. His daughter did not know how to lie. He could tell from her expression that Huo Yuner was lying. However, he was only curious about Luo Zheng's secret. Since his daughter had said so, he would not make things difficult for Luo Zheng.
Thus, the Tianfeng War Emperor handed the Soul Cultivation Wood to Huo Yuner. Huo Yuner personally handed the Soul Cultivation Wood over to Luo Zheng.
"Thank you." The moment Luo Zheng grabbed the Soul Cultivation Wood, his heart trembled. No matter what, Shao Xun's soul had survived.
Huo Yuner blinked and gave Luo Zheng a faint smile. She then turned around and returned to the stairs.

Chapter 813
After getting the Soul Cultivation Wood, Luo Zheng quickly returned to the square. Even though he tried his best to keep calm, he couldn't hide the excitement on his face.
Many Divine Sea Realm warriors saw this and were sure that Luo Zheng was going to use the Soul Cultivation Wood to save someone's life. As for whose life he was going to save, they didn't know.
As for Battle Emperor Yu the Great and Saintess Bai Yuerong, they didn't look too good. They didn't expect that the little monster they sent out would fail to kill Luo Zheng. Instead, it gave him great benefits. How could they tolerate this?
Qinglong said that Xun's soul could last for one or two days, but that was just a rough estimate. It could be one or two days, or it could be two or four hours. It could also be that it would collapse immediately!
Luo Zheng hurriedly asked Qinglong, "How should I use the Soul Cultivation Wood?"
But at this moment, the sharp voice came again, "The competition is over. Now, we will start the sacrifice! Open the Sky Feather Holy Sea! "
The moment the voice came out, all the Divine Sea geniuses looked toward the platform. The previous competition was just a review. The most important thing was to explore the Sky Feather Holy Sea!
Luo Zheng wasn't in the mood to pay attention to the sacrifice. He had to put Xun in the Soul Cultivation Wood first.
If Xun's soul wasn't in a deep sleep, she could enter the Soul Cultivation Wood by herself. Qinglong told Luo Zheng that he had to move her soul into the Soul Cultivation Wood …
Luo Zheng looked around and found a quiet corner in this spacious square. He sat down with his legs crossed, closed his eyes, and immersed himself in his own mind.
In his mind, Xun's soul was still lying there quietly. Many cracks appeared on the surface of her body.
Luo Zheng flew over, hugged her, and dragged her out of his mind …
At this moment, he didn't forget to look back at the mysterious furnace. The glow of Qinglong and the red dragon on the furnace had dimmed a lot.
In this battle with the little monster, Luo Zheng had also paid a considerable price. However, his gains were also relatively great. First of all, after merging with the Will of the World, Luo Zheng's soul had risen to a new level. More importantly, he could now connect himself with the entire world.
The attacks he received could be transferred to the Great Thousand World!
In addition, there was a huge amount of information for Luo Zheng to explore …
"Hiss hiss …"
According to the method taught by Qinglong, Luo Zheng placed a section of the Soul Cultivation Wood between his eyebrows!
The moment Luo Zheng 'transported' Smoky's soul out of her mind, her soul naturally drilled into the Soul Wood!
After a few breaths, the smoky soul completely disappeared. That piece of Soul Cultivation Wood emitted a faint red halo. When Luo Zheng touched this piece of Soul Cultivation Wood, it emitted a trace of warmth.
"What a magical Soul Cultivation Wood!" After saying this, Luo Zheng let out a long sigh of relief.
Although Smoky's soul was seriously injured, with the nourishment of the Soul Cultivation Wood, it shouldn't be a big problem. The Soul Cultivation Wood's name had the two words "Soul Cultivation", which meant that it could nourish the soul. After a period of time, it might be able to heal Smoky's injuries.
Even if Smoky couldn't heal completely, Luo Zheng had enough time to help her find a panacea to heal her soul injuries.
Smoky hadn't been with him for a very long time. However, since she woke up, she had helped him through several calamities.
Although she did have a favor to ask of him, she truly stood in his position and helped him consider everything. Luo Zheng vowed that he would never forget this friendship!
Just as Luo Zheng finished his business here, the sacrificial ceremony on the high platform had already begun!
"Whoosh!"
On the high platform, a huge circular pillar of light suddenly appeared. After that pillar of light appeared, it soared all the way to the sky!
"Is this the sacrificial ceremony? I wonder what the sacrificial ceremony is used for? "
Luo Zheng didn't know much about the Divine Kingdom's continent. However, when he thought of this, he suddenly patted his head. He had forgotten that he now had the ability to know everything!
A thought flashed through his mind. Luo Zheng already knew the answer in his heart.
The Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was originally occupied by the Dragon Bloodline Clan. Before the Dragon Bloodline Clan died, they carried out a blood sacrifice of all the creatures in their clan!
At that time, the Dragon Bloodline Clan was at its peak. The Holy Land of the Dragon Bloodline Clan was at the late stage of the Eighth Grade Realm. It was even comparable to a Ninth Grade Force, which was far more powerful than the seven Divine Kingdoms!
Even before they were destroyed, the Dragon Bloodline Clan's population was frighteningly large.
The blood sacrifice was actually a kind of curse!
After a creature was killed, it would give birth to a curse. This was also the source of the Evil Spirit on the warrior's body.
However, the Dragon Bloodline Clan cleverly used this curse. After they sacrificed all the creatures in their clan, they cast this curse. Any creature that stepped into the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea would be affected by this curse.
The specific manifestation of this curse was a layer of golden light on the surface of the sea!
What the four Divine Kingdoms had to do now was to temporarily disperse a portion of this golden light. Only then could the warriors enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea smoothly.
As the pillar of light soared into the sky, all the Divine Sea Realm Almighties at the bottom of the stairs slowly ascended around the pillar of light at the same time. The four Divine Sea Realm Almighties occupied the highest position. Below them were dozens of Divine Sea Realm Almighties who released their True Essence.
The shrill voice rang out again. "Comrades, let's work together to disperse the curse of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea!"
This shrill voice rang out from time to time. However, its identity was extremely mysterious. From beginning to end, it never showed its face. Under its command, the huge pillar of light that soared into the sky began to tilt slowly, tilting towards the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
Wild winds surged and the weather changed.
The various god-grade geniuses were also watching this scene with serious expressions.
The moment the pillar of light tilted towards the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, it blended with the golden light of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea!
A miraculous scene occurred. The light contained within the pillar of light seemed to be the nemesis of the golden light on the surface of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. The moment the pillar of light entered the sea, the golden light was constantly eroded by the pillar of light.
The originally calm Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was like a frantically struggling giant beast. Huge waves rose from the golden sea, sweeping in all directions layer by layer!
At the same time, the golden light was constantly melting and shrinking at a visible speed …
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. Although he already knew the process of this sacrifice through the memories in his mind, seeing this scene with his own eyes still shocked him greatly.
This method couldn't have been thought of by the four Divine Kingdoms. They must have received help from the Upper Realm's Human Race.
After an incense stick's worth of time, the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea slowly calmed down. The pillar of light also began to dim.
The surface of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was already suffused with a faint golden light. However, compared to before, this golden light's luster was much fainter.
After the pillar of light disappeared, a person stood within it. This person wore a greenish-grey Daoist robe. His eyebrows were snow-white, and his expression was dignified. It was Daoist White Eyebrows.
"Alright! The sacrifice is complete. Everyone can receive the dragon blood and enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea! "
This Daoist White Eyebrows was the owner of that sharp voice!
When Luo Zheng sized up Daoist White Eyebrows, that person seemed to have noticed Luo Zheng as well. He casually glanced at Luo Zheng, then commanded the Divine Sea Realm experts of the four Divine Kingdoms to begin distributing the dragon blood!
"I can't see through this person's strength!" Luo Zheng's heart suddenly began to beat wildly.
Even Divine Sea Realm experts would find it difficult to conceal their cultivation in front of Luo Zheng. However, Luo Zheng couldn't see through Daoist White Eyebrows' cultivation at all.
There was only one possibility! This Daoist White Eyebrows' strength was definitely above the Divine Sea Realm!
"Aren't only Divine Sea Realm experts allowed in the Lower Realm?" Luo Zheng asked Qinglong in confusion.
In theory, even Divine Sea Realm experts couldn't tolerate the Vast Thousand Worlds. When a Life and Death Realm expert was born in the Vast Thousand Worlds, the Heavenly Dao would send down punishment, forming a Life and Death Tribulation, which was also a minor Heavenly Tribulation.
After entering the Divine Sea Realm, the Heavenly Dao would send down a major Heavenly Tribulation!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 814
Although it was easy for warriors to die under the Heavenly Tribulation, more warriors managed to overcome their Heavenly Tribulations and cultivate step by step, making themselves stronger.
Since even the Heavenly Tribulation couldn't punish these warriors, the Great Wide World would find a way to kick them out!
Of course, this was the "ascension" that the Divine Sea Realm warriors spoke of.
Unfortunately, there was a problem with the ascension channel of this Great Wide World. Even if the Great Wide World's consciousness wanted to kick them out, it couldn't.
Qinglong replied calmly, "Yes, the resources in the Lower Realm can't give birth to warriors more powerful than the Divine Sea Realm …"
"I feel that he's far more powerful than the Divine Sea Realm warriors," Luo Zheng said seriously.
"It's simple. He's not from this plane. He's from the Human Clan of the Upper Realm," Qinglong explained.
"I see!" Luo Zheng was startled. After thinking for a while, a thought flashed through his mind. He searched the memories of the Great Wide World's consciousness for the White Brow Daoist's background …
"Eh, there's no information about this person!" After searching, Luo Zheng found that the White Brow Daoist didn't exist in the Great Wide World's consciousness.
"It's normal. The Great Wide World's consciousness you've merged with is indeed in a state of omniscience about this plane. However, the White Brow Daoist came down through the Reverse Spirit Channel. Because he's not from this plane, he paid a considerable price!" Qinglong explained.
Luo Zheng finally understood. However, Luo Zheng thought about it again and felt that something was wrong. "But I can search for the Soul Cultivation Wood, but I can't find any information about the White Brow Daoist."
"The Soul Cultivation Wood is an inanimate object. If you don't believe me, you can search for the Mysterious Underworld Cave, the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb, and the Immortal Mansion in the Eastern Realm. Outsiders can't search for them …"
Luo Zheng actually followed Qinglong's method to search. The facts proved that Qinglong's words were correct. He couldn't search for the Dark Nether Cave, the Tower of Sin, or the immortal mansion in his memories, but he could find the Firmament Immortal Tomb!
There was no need for Qing Long to explain. Luo Zheng had already guessed the reason. Although Daoist Tian Miao was also a martial artist from the Upper Realm, he was a native martial artist born in this Greater Thousand World. That was why he was able to find out about Immortal Tian Miao.
However, both the Immortal Mansion and the Tower of Sin were foreign objects, so Luo Zheng naturally couldn't find them.
As for the young man who took Luo Yan away, Luo Zheng also tried to find him, but he couldn't find him either …
Unable to find any information, Luo Zheng left him alone for the time being. Under the orders of Daoist White Brow, the four Divine Kingdoms began to distribute the dragon blood.
Luo Zheng saw several Life and Death powerhouses continuously distributing small bottles to many Divine Rank geniuses.
Although the curse of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean was dispelled, False Tribulation Realm martial artists still couldn't enter. The remaining power of the Blood Sacrifice was enough to make it difficult for False Tribulation Realm martial artists to move even a single step. They would eventually be trapped in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean and die. Therefore, before entering, every human martial artist had to take a drop of True Dragon's blood!
For ordinary martial artists, True Dragon's blood was a great tonic. After taking it, it could increase the strength of one's body and allow one's Blood Qi to contain a trace of the True Dragon's power. However, for martial artists of this level, this trace of power was negligible. The most important thing was to use the True Dragon's blood to resist the Blood Sacrifice of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean.
A True Dragon was several thousand miles long, comparable to a continent. In the Upper Realm, it wasn't anything rare. Otherwise, when Luo Zheng first entered the True Dragon World, he wouldn't have been given a drop of True Dragon's blood.
However, it wasn't clear how the four Divine Kingdoms obtained this True Dragon's blood. Presumably, they obtained it through a reverse spirit channel.
Luo Zheng was also given a drop of True Dragon's blood.
True Dragon's blood was very rare for martial artists of the lower realms. However, when Luo Zheng grabbed the small bottle, a strange smile appeared on his face. The True Dragon's blood in his body was far more than the others. Not to mention, Luo Zheng also had True Dragon's blood essence!
Ordinary Dragon's blood wasn't precious, but True Dragon's blood essence was much more precious. This was because Dragon's blood could quickly rise again, but True Dragon's blood essence couldn't. Every drop used was a drop less. Even in the True Dragon World, wanting to obtain a drop of True Dragon's blood essence wasn't easy.
After some martial artists obtained a drop of True Dragon's blood, they couldn't wait to swallow it.
Although this True Dragon's blood wasn't of much use to Luo Zheng, he still absorbed it like the others.
In a short while, the martial artists in the square began to disperse. They knew that they were about to set off.
Just as King Yan had said, the martial artists exploring the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea had to form their own team. Those martial artists gathered in twos and threes.
At this moment, a voice was heard. "Brother Tianxing! Can you join my team for this exploration of the secret realm? "
Luo Zheng turned around and discovered that the one who spoke was the Crown Prince of the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom called "Zhuo 'er." Now, Luo Zheng already knew that the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom was the world of the Lu Clan, and this Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince was called Lu Zhuo.
To be invited by the Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince, Luo Zheng's face was quite big.
But then again, the strength that Luo Zheng had displayed was shocking enough. Who wouldn't want Luo Zheng to join their team?
Although the four Divine Kingdoms had explored the Four Great Secret Realms for so many years and had a considerable understanding of them, there were still many unpredictable dangers in this secret realm. With an expert like Luo Zheng in the team, the chances of success would naturally be much higher.
In the face of Lu Zhuo's invitation, Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said, "Sorry, I've already accepted King Yan's invitation."
"King Yan? Zhou Zhihua? "Lu Zhuo frowned slightly.
As soon as his voice fell, Zhou Zhihua had already brought Yue Ying and the others over to Luo Zheng. King Yan patted Luo Zheng's shoulder and said to Lu Zhuo, "Yes, Luo Zheng has already joined my team. You're a step too late!"
Lu Zhuo's eyes flickered slightly and revealed a regretful expression. In fact, he already knew that it would be difficult for him to invite Luo Zheng. Although Luo Zheng wasn't a warrior from the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, he was representing the Black Iron Divine Kingdom this time. Lu Zhuo just wanted to try his luck.
"Too powerful, Luo Tianxing!" Yue Ying approached Luo Zheng and an excited smile appeared on her face.
Although they already knew that Luo Zheng's strength wasn't to be underestimated and had even sparred with Luo Zheng before, Yue Ying and the others thought that Luo Zheng had displayed at least 80% of his strength! In front of them, he hid at most 20% of his strength.
However, they had never expected that Luo Zheng would be so powerful. Not only did he defeat the most powerful geniuses of the four Divine Kingdoms, but he also wounded a Divine Sea Realm warrior. What's more, the Divine Sea Realm warrior was War Emperor Yu the Great, one of the best among the Divine Sea Realm warriors!
If they hadn't witnessed this scene with their own eyes, no one would dare to believe it.
Therefore, the way Yue Ying and the others looked at Luo Zheng had completely changed. Especially Yue Ying, there was only fanatical worship left in her eyes!
To be able to form a team with Luo Zheng was an extremely lucky thing for them. This trip to the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean would definitely be fruitful for them.
Luo Zheng nodded lightly. "Okay, when do we set off?"
"We can set off now," King Yan said with a smile. "According to the rules, if you win the challenge of the other three Divine Kingdoms, our Black Iron Divine Kingdom can be the first to enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean. However, it doesn't matter who we are."
While they were talking, the Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince Zhou Ning had already led several people to fly up high. They went upstream along the West Dragon River and entered the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean.
"In that case, let's set off too!" Luo Zheng nodded. After wandering around the Divine Kingdom, his most important goal was the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean. Now, he could finally step into it.
After speaking, Luo Zheng, King Yan, Yue Ying, and the others also flew up and headed straight for the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean.
The waves in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean rushed to the shore wave after wave. The faint golden light still contained a considerable amount of the power of the blood sacrifice. The moment Luo Zheng entered the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean, he felt an omnipresent power enveloping him.
"Is this the power of the blood sacrifice?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Under the influence of the power of the blood sacrifice, Luo Zheng's flying speed slowed down significantly. Luo Zheng's speed slowed down, but King Yan and Yue Ying's speed slowed down even more.

Chapter 815
The scattered cultivators continued to head towards the Sky Feather Sacred Sea.
To them, the Sky Feather Sacred Sea was like a huge mine. After a sacrifice, they could suppress the power of the blood sacrifice to a lower level. However, it could only last for about five days.
After five days, the power of the blood sacrifice would gush out from the depths of the Sky Feather Sacred Sea and cover this sea area again. By then, even a Divine Sea Realm Almighty would find it difficult to move.
As the crowd slowed down, Luo Zheng also slowed down behind King Yan. At the same time, he also gradually lowered his altitude.
However, at this moment, several figures shot towards them from the shore. The person in the lead was surrounded by faint lightning.
King Yan looked back and said, "That is the Crown Prince of the Yu Dynasty, Liu Xiu."
Liu Xiu did not participate in the battle just now.
The royal family usually did not participate in this kind of battle. However, since Huo Yuner had jumped out, many Crown Princes and Princes of the Divine Kingdoms wanted to step out and fight Luo Zheng.
However, after Luo Zheng fought with the little monster, Battle Emperor of the Yu Dynasty personally stepped out and was punched by Luo Zheng until he vomited blood. The Crown Princes and Princes of the Divine Kingdoms all gave up on this idea.
To be able to injure a Divine Sea Realm Almighty, Luo Zheng's strength could no longer be described as extraordinary.
However, this did not mean that they would be convinced by Luo Zheng. The four Divine Kingdoms had been established for many years and had given birth to many amazing people. Luo Zheng's strength might be one step ahead of them. However, it did not mean that the latecomers did not have a chance.
Moreover, these Crown Princes and Princes of the Divine Kingdoms had Semi-Divine Artifacts and even Divine Artifacts in their hands. They might not take Luo Zheng seriously.
"Swish, swish, swish …"
Liu Xiu, the Crown Prince of the Yu Dynasty, led six people and rushed forward at an extremely fast speed. Although Luo Zheng and King Yan set off first, they did not pursue speed. After a while, Liu Xiu led his people and overtook Luo Zheng and the rest.
When he passed by Luo Zheng, Liu Xiu glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently. He didn't stop and continued to fly forward.
"Liu Xiu's hidden talent is also extremely powerful. I haven't seen him fight for seven or eight years. I guess his strength is not below Huo Yuner's." King Yan reminded Luo Zheng.
However, the King of Yan was still quite confident in Luo Zheng. Even the little monster was no match for Luo Zheng. Moreover, Liu Xiu had withstood a punch from Battle God Yu the Great. No matter how terrifying Liu Xiu's talent was, it would be difficult for him to go against Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng only nodded slightly. He maintained a uniform speed and followed behind the King of Yan.
As the group went deeper and deeper into the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, the influence of the Blood Sacrifice Power grew stronger and stronger. Everyone's altitude became lower and lower.
This was because everyone had swallowed the Blood of the Real Dragon, which could offset a portion of the Blood Sacrifice Power. Otherwise, most of the ancient martial art practitioners would have been pulled into the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea.
After traveling for dozens of miles, other than Luo Zheng, the King of Yan, Yue Ying, Zuo Chuandong, and the rest could no longer maintain their altitude. They could only fly close to the surface of the sea.
"I can't take it anymore. Everyone, come down. If you continue like this, you will be pulled into the sea." The King of Yan stopped and took out a small paper boat from his Sumeru Ring. The paper boat looked like it was made of folded pieces of paper. However, densely packed Sacred Patterns were drawn on it.
The Sacred Pattern Art of the four Holy Kingdoms was passed down from the Human Race in the Upper Realm. Those Sacred Patterns were very familiar. However, Luo Zheng was still unable to recognize the effects of these Sacred Patterns.
The King of Yan folded the paper boat and threw it into the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. The paper boat instantly expanded with the wind. It rapidly changed from a small piece of paper to a large boat that was dozens of feet long.
This large boat was overflowing with lights and colors. It was extraordinarily exquisite. It was as if it was a large boat that was ridden by immortals.
Seeing Luo Zheng's curious gaze, the King of Yan laughed self-deprecatingly. "This paper boat is a Sacred Weapon. Although it's a High-grade Sacred Weapon, it can only be used to carry people. It's not very meaningful …"
"This paper boat is also passed down from the Upper Realm, right?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes. Only the weapon refiners from the Upper Realm are willing to spend their time on these aspects," the King of Yan said with a smile.
The resources in the Lower Realm were scarce. Weapon refiners would usually only forge divine weapons. They would rarely spend their time on auxiliary Dharma treasures. This could be seen from Huo Yuner's jade bracelet.
After saying that, Yue Ying was the first to stand on the paper boat. Compared to Zuo Chuandong and Lan Chen, although she had mastered the Law of Time, her strength was indeed slightly inferior. Under the suppression of the power of the blood sacrifice, she was already on the verge of collapse.
After that, Lan Chen and Zuo Chuandong also jumped onto the paper boat, followed by the King of Yan and Luo Zheng. After everyone stood firm, the paper boat steadily moved forward.
Perhaps it was because of the True Dragon's Blood Essence that the power of the blood sacrifice suppressed Luo Zheng much less than other warriors. Even if he did not ride on this paper boat, Luo Zheng was confident that he could cross the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. It was just that there was no need for that.
Other than Luo Zheng's group, the other teams also took out various Dharma treasures. Some were large boats, some were light boats, and there were even two houses floating on the pale golden sea.
The paper boat lived up to its name as a high quality saint artifact. It cut through the waves and moved at an extremely fast speed. Six hours later, the paper boat had been flying for a long time.
The sun gradually set, and the night slowly descended. At this time, a huge island appeared in front of everyone.
Luo Zheng glanced at it. Just as he was about to speak, he realized that he should be able to find this information in his memory. He did not speak, but directly searched in his mind.
Previously, he had not only heard Cao Junfeng describe the Four Great Mystic Realms, but he had also heard the King of Yan talk about the distribution of the Four Great Mystic Realms. However, the effect of other people's descriptions was not as good as his own understanding.
The Feather Emperor Mystic Realm, the Dream God Mystic Realm, and the Divine Emperor Mystic Realm were all located on three islands. Comparing the memories in his mind, Luo Zheng had already obtained the answer. The island in front of him should be the Feather Emperor Mystic Realm.
The closer the paper boat was to the island in front of him, the greater the pressure of the power of the blood sacrifice. Although the sacrifice had offset most of the power of the blood sacrifice to a certain extent, the remaining power of the blood sacrifice could still bring quite a bit of pressure to a False Tribulation Realm warrior.
After a while, the paper boat finally slowly approached the shore. The King of Yan was the first to jump off the paper boat.
Yue Ying's body also swayed. She stepped on the shore, but she seemed to be unable to stand steadily and was about to fall.
However, at the moment when she was about to fall, Luo Zheng arrived in an instant. He reached out his hand to support Yue Ying and helped her up.
"Thank you!" Yue Ying smiled sweetly. Luo Zheng's gaze was indifferent as he nodded slightly.
For a False Tribulation Realm warrior, it was extremely difficult to fly on this island. The True Origin in the King of Yan's hand flickered gently and suddenly shot towards the paper boat. The paper boat spun and then quickly turned into the size of a palm and flew back to the King of Yan's hand.
The King of Yan looked at the forest on the island and searched for a direction. He said, "Let's go!"
At the same time, warriors were constantly approaching the shore and entering the forest.
After entering the forest, the King of Yan's expression became very cautious. As he kept moving, he was counting softly.
"Huahua, why are you counting?" Yue Ying asked the King of Yan with a puzzled expression.
The King of Yan frowned and focused on counting. "336, 327 …"
Other than the King of Yan, Luo Zheng, Zuo Chuandong, Yue Ying, and Lan Chen were exploring the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea for the first time. When they saw the King of Yan's actions, they were indeed surprised.
"390 …"
"400!"
When the King of Yan counted to 400, he suddenly stopped in his tracks and said to Yue Ying, "We have already entered the 'Arrow Delay Zone'."
"Arrow Delay Zone?" Yue Ying's face was filled with confusion.
The King of Yan nodded and replied, "Yes, that's a type of extremely slow arrow. Everyone has to be careful as we move forward."
Hearing the King of Yan's words, everyone was still a little confused. What exactly was this' Arrow Delay Zone '?
However, the moment the King of Yan took the lead and took a step forward, everyone heard the sound of gears being pulled. Then, a row of densely packed sharp arrows appeared in front of them!
Every arrow was glowing with a blue light, and it was obvious that they contained a deadly poison!

Chapter 816
Arrows were generally a symbol of speed. Everyone had seen a lot of arrows that were as fast as lightning, but it was the first time they had seen such slow arrows.
Yue Ying's gaze froze slightly as she said with a serious face, "These arrows seem to be restricted by the law of time. That's why their speed is so slow!"
"Hehe, such slow arrows. They're even slower than my walking speed. Who can they hit?" Zuo Chuandong said with a smile.
King Yan said with a serious face, "Don't underestimate these arrows. The poison on these arrows is Dragon Arsenic! Even a Divine Sea Realm Almighty will die upon contact! It is said that many Life and Death Realm Warriors who first explored the mystic realm died from these arrows! "
Zuo Chuandong's face was full of disbelief. "King Yan, I believe you when you say that the poison on these arrows is very powerful. But I don't believe you when you say that a Divine Sea Realm Almighty died from these arrows!"
The speed of these arrows was so slow that even an old man could easily dodge them. How could he be killed by these arrows? Moreover, he was a Life and Death Realm Warrior!
King Yan smiled bitterly and said, "These arrows are defensive measures left behind by the Dragon Bloodline Clan. I'm just reminding everyone to be careful. As for why, you'll know soon enough."
Although Zuo Chuandong said that he didn't believe him, since King Yan emphasized the danger, a cautious expression appeared on his face.
The four of them followed King Yan and continued to advance in the forest.
In the beginning, there weren't many arrows in this area. They were sparse and flew slowly.
Then, arrows shot from the front. Everyone just had to carefully avoid them. In any case, the speed of these arrows was extremely slow.
But very soon, everyone understood what Prince Yan meant. This area was indeed very dangerous because there were too many sharp arrows!
In the beginning, there might be three to five arrows flying slowly around them. But as everyone went deeper, the number of arrows floating around them began to increase rapidly!
Often, there would be ten or twenty arrows shooting from different directions! Some arrows were arranged in a row, completely blocking the path ahead. They could only fly over or roll on the ground.
Even so, it wasn't difficult for them. Because of the suppression of the power of blood sacrifice, it was difficult for everyone to fly. But False Tribulation Realm Warriors were still quite nimble.
However, Zuo Chuandong couldn't help but reveal a hint of impatience on his face as he was obstructed by these slow-flying sharp arrows!
Facing the incoming arrow, Zuo Chuandong raised the dagger in his hand and slashed at it!
"Don't!"
The King of Yan tried to stop him, but it was too late.
The short saber in Zuo Chuandong's hand was also a medium-grade saint artifact. But when the saber slashed out, the arrow shook slightly. Not only was Zuo Chuandong unable to cut through the arrow, but the arrow actually accelerated!
Swish!
The speed of the arrow increased countless times in an instant, and it shot toward Zuo Chuandong's chest.
Zuo Chuandong was also stunned. He didn't expect that this slowly flying arrow would actually accelerate on its own …
At this critical moment, Luo Zheng's hand suddenly struck out and hit Zuo Chuandong's shoulder. Zuo Chuandong's whole body swayed, and his chest was deflected. The sharp arrow shot through his chest and pierced a big hole in his clothes!
"Hurry up and help him take off his clothes!" The King of Yan shouted anxiously when he saw this scene.
There was a bit of blue light on the sharp arrow that stained Zuo Chuandong's clothes. Was this blue light the dragon that the King of Yan mentioned? Poison.
Ordinary poisons would spread in the body of living creatures. However, this Dragon Arsenic was like a living creature. It actually spread through Zuo Chuandong's clothes!
Even if a Life and Death Realm Warrior was stained with this Dragon Arsenic, he would die instantly. Zuo Chuandong panicked. When he heard the King of Yan's words, he actually wanted to take off his clothes …
However, Luo Zheng was even more direct. He struck out with his palm again. The power in his palm spread out and directly shattered Zuo Chuandong's clothes. As for the clothes that were stained with Dragon Arsenic, they also dissipated under the wind from Luo Zheng's palm.
Seeing this scene, the King of Yan heaved a deep sigh of relief.
After escaping death, Zuo Chuandong's mind wasn't clear for a moment. He was still in a daze.
"Wear it! If it wasn't for Luo Zheng, you would be a dead man now! "The King of Yan took out a piece of clothing from his Sumeru Ring and threw it to Zuo Chuandong.
Yue Ying, Lan Chen, and the rest also heaved a sigh of relief. At this moment, Luo Zheng's image in their hearts was raised again. They were extremely lucky to be able to form a team with such a person.
"Don't be careless anymore. As we go deeper, there will be more and more sharp arrows in the Slow Arrow Zone! Although the speed is slow, it's still very difficult to dodge them completely! "The King of Yan warned.
Hearing this, Yue Ying, Lan Chen, and Zuo Chuandong subconsciously moved closer to Luo Zheng. They probably felt a sense of security only by following behind Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's gaze was indifferent. He didn't mind protecting them. This Slow Arrow Zone was indeed a little difficult for ordinary warriors. However, it wasn't a problem for Luo Zheng. After all, his body was immune to poison. The scariest thing about these slow-flying sharp arrows was the Dragon Arsenic. In terms of power, it wouldn't cause much harm to Luo Zheng.
"Another row of arrows is coming. Everyone, follow me!" The King of Yan ordered.
As the crowd went deeper, the sharp arrows here had reached a terrifying level. Usually, there would be hundreds of sharp arrows flying over slowly. These sharp arrows were evenly spaced and formed a wall of arrows, pressing towards the crowd.
However, there was a small gap in this' wall of arrows'!
The crowd followed behind the King of Yan. Everyone stood sideways while the 'wall of arrows' covered the crowd. As they were standing sideways, the crowd just happened to pass through the gap.
When they passed through the gap in the 'wall of arrows', there were densely packed sharp arrows in front of them, above them, and behind them. Blue rays of light flashed everywhere. It was indeed hair-raising.
After passing through the wall of arrows, the King of Yan ordered, "Continue walking!"
He didn't expect that just as he gave the order, he would suddenly hear a wail coming from not far away.
"Ah!"
"Li Chan! He was hit by an arrow! "
Hearing this scream, Luo Zheng looked in the direction of the sound and saw that in the open space hundreds of feet to the side, a cultivator had been shot by a sharp arrow!
The warrior who was hit by the arrow seemed to be a god-tier genius from the Xingye Kingdom. The moment he was hit by the arrow, a blue luster appeared on the skin all over his body. Very quickly, the skin started to fester rapidly. His flesh and blood seemed to be boiling and wriggling continuously.
In a short while, the warrior's physical body completely disappeared …
Seeing this horrifying scene, Yue Ying's beautiful face turned pale. Although the King of Yan had warned them that the Dragon Arsenic was extremely powerful, they didn't expect it to be to this extent. Looking at the sharp arrows floating slowly in all directions, the expression in their eyes became more and more fearful.
The team over there looked at their dead teammate and didn't stay for long. Since everyone chose to explore the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, they were already prepared to die. Moreover, they were in this arrow retardation zone. As long as they were careful, it wouldn't be a dangerous place.
"Let's go. Don't look anymore," Luo Zheng said to Yue Ying. Then, he said to the King of Yan, "This time, I will walk in front."
This team was led by the King of Yan. According to the usual practice, he would definitely be the one leading the team. However, since Luo Zheng had spoken, the King of Yan would naturally agree. After all, in the King of Yan's heart, Luo Zheng was still a trustworthy partner.
As they moved forward, everyone fearlessly avoided a few waves of arrows. However, just as they were about to leave the arrow retardation zone, a few figures suddenly shot out in front of them. The figure in the lead had a thunderous momentum.
"Liu Xiu!" The King of Yan's eyes flashed. Liu Xiu's group should be at the front. According to the King of Yan's estimation, they should have already passed through the arrow retardation zone. However, he didn't know why they turned back.
When Liu Xiu heard the King of Yan calling him, he said coldly, "The road ahead is blocked!"
"Why?" The King of Yan asked curiously. He had been to this island before. This was definitely the right way to go!
"See for yourself!" Liu Xiu pointed to the back and revealed a helpless expression.

Chapter 817
Behind Liu Xiu, there were sharp arrows that covered the sky and the earth, shooting towards them!
Every arrow was shining with a little blue light. Under the night sky, it was extremely beautiful, like the stars in the sky. However, everyone knew that this beauty came from the most deadly poison!
"This … How do we pass through this!" Seeing this scene, Zuo Chuandong said with a trembling voice.
The previous' arrow wall 'only had a few hundred to a thousand arrows. Those arrows were also arranged in a row, so it was not too difficult to pass through.
However, this' arrow wall 'in front of them probably had a few hundred thousand arrows. If the previous' arrow wall 'was a wall, this' arrow wall 'was probably a city wall!
King Yan's eyes flashed and he said, "Go around from the side!"
"It's useless." Liu Xiu shook his head and then pointed to a beautiful woman beside him and said, "I've tried. Concubine Lan and I, one on the left and one on the right, explored the width of this arrow wall. In the end, this arrow wall seems to cover the entire arrow retardation zone!"
As everyone spoke, they began to retreat carefully and discuss countermeasures at the same time.
As everyone retreated, they met up with more and more warriors, including the Crown Prince of the Star Field Divine Kingdom and the Crown Prince of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, Zhou Ning, Zhan Yun, and the others. They also joined the retreating team …
"Strange, when we passed through the arrow retardation zone in the past, we didn't see such a large-scale arrow wall …"
"Is it a little strange this year?"
"Let's avoid this wave of arrow wall first. After this wave of arrow wall disappears, let's move forward and take a look!"
As more and more cultivators gathered, everyone began to discuss the matter.
At this moment, Yue Ying said, "Let's go directly into the sea and go ashore from the other side. Will this method work?"
As soon as Yue Ying finished speaking, everyone looked at Yue Ying as if they were looking at an idiot. Yue Ying was a little embarrassed and her face turned red. King Yan reminded them, "I forgot to remind you that this is the only path to the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm. The two paths … are not paths that we can take."
As a result, dozens of teams and hundreds of God-level geniuses were forced to retreat. After everyone retreated out of the arrow retardation zone, the arrows that were like a city wall dissipated one by one.
"Again!" Liu Xiu gritted his teeth and continued to lead his team forward.
The martial artists that were able to enter the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea were the most powerful young elites of the four Divine Kingdoms. How could they be defeated by such a tiny setback?
Everyone followed Liu Xiu and the others and stepped into the Arrow Retardation Zone.
King Yan frowned and said, "I feel that something is amiss. Something seems to have happened in the Emperor of Feathers Secret Land!"
"No matter what happens, we have to go in," Luo Zheng said seriously.
Others could retreat, but Luo Zheng couldn't. Although Luo Zheng could check Ning Yudie's situation through the memories in his mind, the earlier he got the Life Source Stone, the earlier Luo Zheng could feel at ease.
"Of course!" King Yan said with a smile. He was not someone who would give up so easily.
As a result, Luo Zheng and the others stepped into the Arrow Retardation Zone again.
Although everyone was careful, there were still people who were careless and fell into a desperate situation. Under the encirclement of hundreds of arrows, they couldn't avoid it no matter if they were in front or behind. In the end, they were pierced by the arrows and were poisoned by the Dragon Saliva Poison. Their bodies instantly rotted and disappeared …
Looking at the God-level geniuses who died from the poison, everyone sighed in their hearts. However, they still pressed forward.
However, when everyone had advanced to a certain point, a dense barrage of arrows appeared in front of them once again. It was still a barrage of arrows that looked like a city wall, flying towards them in a black mass.
"This …" Liu Xiu was speechless.
Many of the Divine Kingdoms' princes and crown princes had conflicted expressions on their faces. They probably wouldn't be able to cross today.
If this matter spread, it would be too embarrassing. They explored the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea once a year, but so many of them couldn't even pass through the Arrow Retardation Zone. No one would believe it!
Just as everyone was hesitating, they heard someone say, "Everyone, follow behind me! I can cross! "
Hearing that voice, everyone looked over. The person who spoke was the crown prince of the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom, Huo Chen!
Behind Huo Chen were Huo Yuner and other martial artists from the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom.
Although Huo Chen was the crown prince of the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom, his reputation wasn't as resounding as the other three crown princes. This was because generally speaking, the crown prince of a Divine Kingdom was the most outstanding martial artist of the younger generation. Only such a person could become the crown prince of a Divine Kingdom.
However, Huo Chen's reputation was far from Huo Yuner's. There was even a rumor that Huo Yuner might become the first empress in the history of the four Divine Kingdoms!
However, this didn't mean that Huo Chen wasn't outstanding enough. It was just that Huo Yuner's radiance was too bright and had completely overshadowed the crown prince.
"Huo Chen? What can you do? " Liu Xiu asked with a cold smile. He had always looked down on Huo Chen. In Liu Xiu's heart, this crown prince of a Divine Kingdom was like trash.
Huo Chen's expression was extremely calm. He only said, "Use something to block these arrows!"
"Haha, Huo Chen, are you joking? Don't you know that these arrows are made of Meteorite Iron? What can block these arrows? "Liu Xiu said with a cold smile.
Everyone was part of the royal family of a Divine Kingdom. They more or less had some secret treasures. Huo Yuner had a semi-divine tool. Some of the crown princes even had semi-divine level defensive treasures!
However, there wasn't much they could do in the face of these sharp arrows!
It wasn't that no one had thought of this method in the past. In order to easily pass through the arrow zone, someone had brought a ten foot tall Geng Metal Stone. This Geng Metal Stone was a treasure used to refine tools. It was ten feet tall and ten feet wide. Even a False Tribulation Realm martial artist would have to spend a lot of effort to split this Geng Metal Stone.
Even this Geng Metal Stone couldn't block those sharp arrows. The moment those sharp arrows shot towards the Geng Metal Stone, the time law on them suddenly changed. Their speed instantly increased by a thousand times. Immediately, countless holes appeared in the Geng Metal Stone. That martial artist who thought himself clever naturally died from the Dragon Arsenic.
Seeing that no one believed him, Huo Chen's face revealed a stubborn expression. He knew that everyone looked down on him. He hated it when others looked down on him. Therefore, Huo Chen said, "I have it!"
"Yes, my brother does have it!" At this time, Huo Yuner chimed in from the side.
Unlike the royal families of other Divine Kingdoms, the royal family of Tianfeng Divine Kingdom didn't have so many open and secret struggles. Therefore, the relationship between Huo Yuner and Huo Chen was very good.
"Then take it out and let us see!" A Divine Kingdom Prince said indifferently. However, there was a hint of ridicule in his tone. Obviously, they didn't believe Huo Chen's words.
At this time, Huo Chen lightly tapped the ring hanging on his chest. With a flash of light, a huge stone suddenly appeared in front of Huo Chen.
The surface of the stone emitted a greenish-grey luster. However, the luster on the stone was extremely varied. Ordinary stones could only sense the power of the law of earth. However, some special stones, such as thunder spirit stones, contained the power of the law of thunder. However, everyone could sense the power of different laws from this stone.
To be precise, it was the power of five laws. In other words, the power of the five elements of metal, wood, water, fire, and earth!
Luo Zheng had seen this stone before. Before participating in the Martial Arts Conference, when Luo Zheng was tested, the World Business Alliance used a stone that was exactly the same as this stone. It was the Divine Origin Magnet Stone!
It was said that this Divine Origin Magnet Stone was mined from the Divine Origin Magnet Stone under the Chaotic Astral Ocean. The World Business Alliance only had a small piece. However, Huo Chen took out such a large piece.
Seeing this Divine Origin Magnet Stone, everyone was speechless …
Everyone's vision was enough to recognize the origin of this stone. The problem was that the Divine Origin Magnet Stone was extremely difficult to mine. To take a piece from the Divine Origin Magnet Mountain, it would take a Herculean effort from a Divine Sea Realm expert.

Chapter 818
But now, everyone was convinced. The Divine Magnetic Stone was useless, but it was hard. It was so hard that even Divine Sea Realm Almighty couldn't break it.
Who knew that Huo Chen would carry such a large piece of Divine Origin Magnet Stone with him. The other princes had nothing to say about him carrying such a useless thing with him.
"Hmph, do you believe me now?" Huo Chen sneered.
Huo Yuner seemed to be very proud of her brother's foresight. She smiled and said, "If you want to pass through the arrow barrier, follow me!"
Liu Xiu sighed and smiled helplessly. He could only say, "You're good. You even have this thing with you!"
Huo Chen's hands flickered with True Essence as he lifted the Divine Magnetic Stone. Although the Divine Magnetic Stone was only as tall as two people, it was extremely heavy. Even though Huo Chen's body was sturdy and he used the power of True Essence, it was still very strenuous for him. His face turned red.
When Huo Yuner saw that Huo Chen was struggling, she extended her jade-like hand. The jade bracelet on her arm trembled slightly, and a surge of energy gushed out. She helped Huo Chen hold up the huge Divine Magnetic Stone.
The brother and sister carried the Divine Magnetic Stone and opened the path in front.
When everyone saw this, they immediately followed behind. This was probably their only chance to enter the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm. How could they let it go?
King Yan smiled and said to Luo Zheng, "Let's go too!"
Luo Zheng nodded and followed the group.
Just as everyone was hiding behind the Divine Origin Magnet Stone in a long line, the dense barrage of arrows that resembled a city wall was slowly flying over.
Although everyone knew that the Divine Magnetic Stone was impenetrable and abnormally hard, they were still worried when they saw the sharp arrows approaching. If the Divine Magnetic Stone couldn't block them, then they would be in trouble …
"Ding!"
Everyone's vision was blocked by the Divine Magnetic Stone, so they couldn't see the situation of the arrows. They only heard a clear sound and understood that the first arrow had been deflected.
Following that, the voices began to ring out densely.
"Ding, ding, ding, ding, ding …"
The sound lasted for a while. Huo Chen and Huo Yuner's faces turned red.
Huo Chen said, "These, these arrows are very powerful. Come and help!"
Although these arrows were flying slowly, it wasn't because of their original speed. It was because of the Law of Time that they were flying so slowly.
Under the impact of the arrows, all of their power was exerted on the Divine Origin Magnet Stone. Even if Huo Yuner used the semi-divine tool on her wrist, it was still a bit strenuous.
Just as Huo Chen's voice faded, Luo Zheng's figure flashed by the side of the group. He arrived under the Divine Origin Magnet Stone and gently pushed with his hands.
When Luo Zheng's power surged out, Huo Chen and Huo Yuner immediately felt relaxed. Luo Zheng was actually bearing 90% of the power!
Seeing this, Huo Yuner smiled at Luo Zheng. "Thank you!"
Luo Zheng's expression was indifferent as he replied, "Everyone should be thanking you and your sister. If it weren't for the Divine Origin Magnet Stone, I'm afraid no one would be able to pass through!"
Luo Zheng's words were a bit absolute. It wasn't that no one would be able to pass through this area. It wasn't difficult for cultivators who cultivated the Law of Space to pass through the area. For example, Luo Zheng could easily travel through space to avoid the arrows.
However, in the four Divine Kingdoms, the exploration of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was done in groups. There weren't many cultivators who comprehended the Law of Space. It was difficult for them to abandon their companions and rush through alone!
This majestic group moved forward with Luo Zheng, Huo Chen, and Huo Yuner pushing the Divine Origin Magnet Stone.
Just as everyone heaved a sigh of relief and thought that they had passed through the area, someone suddenly screamed, "Left! There are arrows coming from the left too!"
Everyone looked over at the first moment, only to see that the left side was also filled with sharp arrows!
"There are arrows on the right too!" Another cultivator screamed.
Seeing this scene, everyone's expression darkened.
King Yan also cursed, "Damn it! Why is the Jade Emperor Secret Territory so difficult?!"
Now, other than the back, there was a rain of arrows coming from the front, left, and right. There was no blind spot to dodge them!
Under such circumstances, even Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to pass through the rain of arrows without getting hit …
The only person who could pass through the rain of arrows unscathed was probably Yun Longtao. He was proficient in the third level of the Law of Space and could travel through space. However, it was meaningless to pass through him alone.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly and roared in a low voice, "Follow me!"
As soon as his voice fell, Luo Zheng crossed his palms and slammed them heavily on the Divine Origin Magnet Stone!
"Bang!"
With a muffled sound, the Divine Origin Magnet Stone shot forward and instantly covered a distance of fifty to sixty feet.
With these two palms, Luo Zheng had fully activated the power of the dragon scale. However, due to the dense rain of arrows in front of him, he could only cover this distance.
Many Divine Grade geniuses reacted extremely quickly. Now, they could only compete with the slow-moving rain of arrows. They had to rush through the area before the rain of arrows closed in!
"Whoosh whoosh …"
Luo Zheng used the Divine Origin Magnet Stone to open up a path. Many Divine Grade geniuses behind him immediately followed.
When Luo Zheng caught up with the Divine Origin Magnet Stone again, he slammed his palms on it again. With a loud bang, the Divine Origin Magnet Stone continued to move forward sixty to seventy feet. Borrowing the momentum of his body, the Divine Origin Magnet Stone moved an additional twenty feet!
"We can rush out!"
"Quick! Hurry up! "
"Don't push me!"
Only the space behind the Divine Origin Magnet Stone was safe. Naturally, everyone rushed to follow behind.
"I'll help you, Luo Tianxing!" At this time, a Divine Kingdom Prince suddenly rushed forward. He swept past Huo Chen and Huo Yuner and crashed into the Divine Origin Magnet Stone.
This Divine Kingdom Prince was a prince of the Star Field Divine Kingdom. Not only was he a Divine Grade genius, but he also had innate divine strength. Among the younger generation, there was probably no one stronger than him!
Therefore, at this time, he chose to step forward bravely.
"Pu!"
What he never imagined was that after this collision, the Divine Origin Magnet Stone only moved less than a foot …
Seeing this scene, even in the face of danger, everyone still looked at Luo Zheng as if they were looking at a monster …
The Divine Kingdom Prince's innate strength was something that everyone was well aware of. If a martial artist were to compare notes with the Prince, they would avoid engaging in close combat with him. His strength was far greater than the average martial artist's!
But this prince who possessed innate divine strength actually only knocked this Origin Magnetic Divine Stone a foot away. This was simply too exaggerated, right?!
Before this, many cultivators wanted to help Luo Zheng push the stone. Everyone saw that Luo Zheng could push the Divine Origin Magnet Stone dozens of feet away with every palm strike. Their strength might not be as strong as Luo Zheng's, but they should be able to push it 30 or 40 feet away.
Now that they saw it, it was simply wishful thinking. If they went up, they wouldn't even be able to push it half a foot away.
This guy's strength was indeed too abnormal. No wonder even a Divine Sea Realm cultivator was injured by him!
Luo Zheng didn't care about everyone's gazes. He continued to push the stone forward. Under the cover of the stone, the entire team moved forward rapidly …
"Coming, coming!"
Someone screamed. At this time, a rain of arrows flew over from the left and right.
"Hurry up and go forward!"
"There are people in front. We can't go forward!"
Although Luo Zheng's speed of pushing the Divine Origin Magnet Stone had reached its limit, it was still a bit slow!

At this time, how could they pay attention to their own image and bearing? They had used all the methods they could think of to survive!
Even so, there were still people who were hit by the arrows!
"Ah …"
Miserable screams sounded. At this moment, five or six people were hit by the arrows. Their bodies began to rot rapidly …

At this point, Huo Yuner finally understood that the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was indeed full of dangers.
Just as she shouted this sentence, four sharp arrows came from the left and right. There was almost no space left for her to dodge.

Chapter 819
These arrows contained an extremely strong inertia. In addition, the body and arrowhead contained Dragon Arsenic. If an ordinary martial artist were to touch them, they would surely die.
However, this group of martial artists were all god-level geniuses of the four Divine Kingdoms. Each one of them was incomparably nimble and had their own unique skills. So, even though these arrows were coming from three directions, only seven or eight people had died to them.

Moreover, as long as one touched these arrows, their speed would instantly increase and they would explode in their original direction. When two people used the Laws of Time at the same time, the stronger Laws of Time would completely cover the weaker Laws of Time. So, after she released the Laws of Time, not only did it not affect the arrows, but it even slowed down the time for the surrounding martial artists …
Seeing this, Huo Yuner finally panicked. She hurriedly withdrew her Laws of Time. Then, the other martial artists began to curse her. It was because her Laws of Time had nearly caused the deaths of other martial artists …
Since the Laws of Time were ineffective, she didn't dare to use any other martial skills to attack these arrows. As she looked at the slowly approaching arrows, despair slowly filled her heart.
"Am I not strong enough? Father was right. With my current strength, I am still not qualified to explore the secret realm alone. I never thought that I would die in my first exploration of the secret realm. "Huo Yuner thought to herself in despair.
Her personality was positive and cheerful. The only problem was that she was too naive. She imagined everything in the world to be beautiful and never felt the cruelty of the world.

Yan Wang, Yue Ying, Zuo Chuandong, and the others did not speak. Although they were also very anxious and did not understand why Luo Zheng turned back, Luo Zheng's previous performance had already made everyone fully trust Luo Zheng.

Huo Yuner stared blankly at Luo Zheng. Although she knew that she was saved, she also knew that Luo Zheng was dead for sure!

Only Huo Chen, Huo Yuner, King Yan, and Yue Ying were staring blankly at Luo Zheng.
"The Dragon Arsenic is spreading along his body, but why … why didn't this fierce poison corrode his body?" The same question flashed in Huo Yuner and Huo Chen's minds.

Chapter 820

Chapter 821

"Hurry up and get out!"
