The Vengeful Spirit Beast slowly walked towards a warrior. The warrior was bound by thousands of weeds and was unable to break free. He could only watch as the Vengeful Spirit Beast slowly raised its palm and smashed it heavily on the warrior's body!
Although the Vengeful Spirit Spiritual Beast moved slowly, its strength was shocking. When the palm came down, it directly split the martial artist into two. Then, it moved on to the next martial artist.
Zhou Ning brought Xiao Daoyuan, Zhan Yun and the others away, but they just happened to avoid the area where the weeds grew. It was all thanks to his brother Zhou Zhihua's reminder, but he was also bound by the weeds.
"Peng!"
The warriors who were bound by the weeds would not sit still and wait for death. Some warriors who were proficient in the Law of Fire suddenly released raging flames from their bodies and began to burn the damned weeds!
"Stop burning! It's burning me! "
"If we don't burn it, how can we get out?"
"The Resentful Spirit Spiritual Beast is coming towards me. Who can save me …"
In this patch of weeds, there was chaos. Everyone wanted to break free from these damn weeds. Some powerful warriors or warriors with sharp weapons in their hands had indeed broken free from these weeds. However, most of the warriors were caught off guard and were still trapped in these weeds.
In the midst of this struggle, everyone suddenly heard shrill screams. In the sky, a black mass of vengeful spirits was flying towards them!
The warriors who were bound by the weeds showed despair on their faces.
"Don't look, let's go!" When Luo Zheng left, he patted Huo Yuner's shoulder. She was previously worried about the Resentful Spirit Spiritual Beast, but now, she was worried about the safety of the warriors …
However, now was not the time to worry about others. The black mass of Resentful Spirits numbered in the tens of thousands. If they were caught in it, their fate could be imagined.
The group quickly left the place.
However, when Luo Zheng rushed out for six to seven miles, he suddenly heard a huge tremor behind him!
"Rumble!"
Luo Zheng turned his head and saw an extremely thick thunderbolt exploding towards the sky!
The deafening roar shook the entire Spiritual Beast District, and the vengeful spirits that were hovering in the sky disappeared in an instant.
"This thunderbolt!" Luo Zheng saw the dazzling light and his heart was beating rapidly.
When King Yan saw the thick thunderbolt, he smiled helplessly and said, "It's Liu Xiu."
"He's so powerful?" Luo Zheng asked. The power of the thunderbolt was comparable to the full blow of a Divine Sea Realm Almighty!
King Yan shook his head and laughed, "He's the Crown Prince of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom."
The headquarters of the Esoteric Sect was built in the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. Even the Saintess Bai Yuerong lived in the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. The Great Yu Divine Kingdom was able to stand out among the four Divine Kingdoms because of the Esoteric Sect.
The resources that were passed down from the Reverse Spirit Channel were basically given priority to the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. As the Crown Prince of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, Liu Xiu naturally had a lot of good treasures in his hands.
King Yan continued, "If I'm not wrong, he must have used the Heavenly Thunder Halberd. The Heavenly Thunder Halberd can only be used once. Moreover, it can be ranked as a Semi-Divine Artifact!"
A one-time use treasure, and a Semi-Divine Artifact at that. Luo Zheng was shocked. No wonder it was so powerful!
But then again, if he did not use this thing, he would have become a corpse. The members of the royal family did have many good things in their hands. Presumably, Huo Chen, Huo Yun 'er, and King Yan more or less had life-saving things in their hands, right?
Almost 99% of the vengeful spirits in the spirit beast area were wiped out by the lightning bolt just now. As a result, the pressure on everyone was instantly relieved.
Everyone maintained a certain speed and advanced along the grass in the Spiritual Beast Area. At this time, Huo Yuner asked, "After passing through the Spiritual Beast Area, can we enter the secret realm?"
When King Yan heard Huo Yuner's words, he nodded and said, "In theory, yes. After passing through the Spiritual Beast Area, we will arrive at the central area of the island. We just need to find the entrance to the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm!"
In the second half of the journey, everyone didn't encounter any trouble. They passed through the Spiritual Beast Area easily. Then, everyone saw a magnificent scenery in front of their eyes!
The lines formed by various jades and rays of light outlined a world that was like a fairyland. Every pavilion emitted a riot of colors. Usually, if the colors were too rich, it would give people a feeling of vulgarity. However, the colors in front of them were perfectly matched. It didn't make people feel uncomfortable or dazzled!
"It's so beautiful!" When she saw the scenery, Huo Yuner was the first to rush in. Her eyes were shining with a riot of colors. Everyone's hearts were tense along the way. When they suddenly saw the beautiful scenery, everyone's mood was relaxed.
Luo Zheng also walked in slowly. He looked carefully at the buildings in front of him. The buildings of the Dragon Bloodline Clan were very exquisite. The materials of the buildings were mixed with various colors of luminous sand.
These luminous sand absorbed the sunlight during the day and refracted different rays of light at night. Even after thousands of years, they were still dazzling.
"The entrance is here." It wasn't King Yan's first time here. He was already used to it. After a glance, he led everyone to find the entrance to the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm.

Chapter 823
The Dragon Bloodline Clan had once built a glorious civilization in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean. Along the way, the group admired the buildings that were different from the style of the human race. Occasionally, someone would sigh.
"How great would it be if we could stay here forever?" Yue Ying couldn't help but sigh.
"Stay here …" Zuo Chuandong smiled and said, "The power of the blood sacrifice could directly crush you!"
Under the strong influence of the power of the blood sacrifice, only the members of the Dragon Bloodline Clan could survive here. There were rumors that the descendants of the Dragon Bloodline Clan were still living in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean. However, it was ultimately just a rumor.
Luo Zheng ignored the scenery and followed closely behind King Yan. This time, Luo Zheng had to bring along enough Origin Stones of Life. This was also his main purpose in entering the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean.
Under King Yan's lead, the group saw a "pyramid" appear in front of them.
The shape of this pyramid was very unique. From the bottom to the top of the pyramid, there were many narrow doors that were as wide as a person and as tall as a person! On the top of this pyramid, there were many such narrow doors. These narrow doors could only allow one person to pass through. The person who built these narrow doors probably wanted only one person to pass through.
"Which door is the entrance?" Luo Zheng asked as he looked at the numerous narrow doors. He roughly estimated that there were at least tens of thousands of narrow doors on such a huge pyramid.
Unexpectedly, King Yan spread his hands and smiled. "They are all entrances. Every single door is an entrance!"
"So many doors …" Yue Ying looked at King Yan curiously.
King Yan smiled faintly and said, "This mine of the Dragon Bloodline Clan does indeed have tens of thousands of doors …"
Just as King Yan was explaining, the group suddenly saw an azure light circle appear on the upper side of the pyramid. Then, a few people fell from the light circle!
"Poof, poof, poof …"
These martial artists were also god-grade geniuses who had passed through the Arrow Stalling Area at the same time as Luo Zheng. Only about half of the martial artists had stayed in the Spirit Beast Area. The other half had directly chosen to pass through the Spirit Beast Area and come here. Therefore, they were the first to enter the mine.
Luo Zheng glanced at them. They were carrying golden-red pickaxes. "These guys, even their pickaxes are low-grade immortal tools?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, King Yan smiled and said, "Brother Tianxing, you are ignorant and inexperienced. The mine under the Dragon Vein Clan is totally different from what you imagined. Ordinary iron pickaxes can't dig it at all. You have to use an immortal weapon pickaxe to dig it."
In Luo Zheng's mind, a normal mine was a mixture of mud and stone. Even if there were extremely hard gangue in the ore bed, in the hands of a martial artist, wasn't it the same as mud? Why would he need an immortal artifact?
After those martial artists fell, they carried the pickaxes on their shoulders and rushed into the narrow doors of the pyramid again …
Seeing the puzzled expression on Luo Zheng's face, King Yan shook his head and said, "Alright, in a while, everyone will understand what is going on! Yue Ying, come and get your pickaxes! "
"What? They also want to mine? "Yue Ying said with a face full of reluctance.
King Yan naturally ignored Yue Ying's nonsense. He took out a pickaxe from his Sumeru Ring and threw it at Yue Ying.
Just now, those martial artists were holding low-grade immortal tools. Now, the pickaxe in King Yan's hand was even more ridiculous. It was actually a high-grade immortal tool …
Thinking that the weapon refiners of the Black Iron Divine Nation could actually refine such a strange thing, Luo Zheng could only sigh. The gap was really huge.
Huo Chen and Huo Yuner also distributed the pickaxes to the people they brought.
After King Yan distributed the pickaxes to Yue Ying, Lan Chen, and Zuo Chuandong, only Luo Zheng and King Yan did not have pickaxes in their hands.
However, King Yan chuckled and took out two azure pickaxes from his Sumeru Ring.
When Luo Zheng's eyes saw the pickaxes, his eyes flashed. These two pickaxes could actually be classified as low-grade immortal tools!
"Brother Tianxing, it is easy to get a low-grade immortal tool sword. However, I still spent a lot of effort to get a pair of pickaxes of this grade!" King Yan threw one of the pickaxes to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng waved the pickaxe in his hand a few times. The strange expression on his face became even more intense. He thought about how the Central Domain would often fight over a single immortal tool. However, King Yan had forcefully made it into something that could be used for mining.
"Brother Tianxing, you don't have to be too surprised. Our four Divine Nations would not waste materials on small pickaxes. However, this mine in the Jade Emperor Secret Realm is very special. If we are willing to spend a lot of money on pickaxes, we will definitely be able to make a profit!" As King Yan spoke, he walked toward the pyramid that was full of narrow doors. As he walked, he said to the crowd, "Let me demonstrate it to everyone first. When you enter the narrow door, you will fall into the mine …"
After King Yan finished speaking, he had already entered the narrow door at the bottom of the pyramid. Soon after, he disappeared …
"Hey, you went in halfway through your nonsense!" Yue Ying pouted and said helplessly.
Everyone was also puzzled. However, in less than ten breaths' time, King Yan, like the other warriors before him, fell from a light circle at the side of the pyramid and appeared in front of everyone.
At this moment, there was a fist-sized ore in King Yan's hand. King Yan shook it and smiled. "Fire Crystals. This thing is worthless!" After saying that, he casually threw the fist-sized ore to the side.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng became even more curious. Previously, Luo Zheng did not use the memory in his mind to search for the mystery within. However, at this moment, he could not resist this impulse. However, when he searched with his mind, he realized that there was no memory of this mine in the Will of the Vast Thousand World!
Unable to search, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. The Will of the Vast Thousand World recorded everything that happened in this Vast Thousand World. There were very few omissions. However, he did not know the origin of this mine.
Since he could not search for the relevant information, Luo Zheng decided to investigate it himself. He carried the Lower Grade Sacred Tool pickaxe and walked toward the narrow door at the bottom of the pyramid!
When Luo Zheng's figure disappeared into the narrow door, he suddenly felt himself falling crazily!
"Whoosh!"
While falling, Luo Zheng turned over and saw that there seemed to be a huge plant below him …
However, Luo Zheng was not sure what this thing was. It just looked like a huge plant. From the main stem of this plant, there were many slender branches extending out. At the end of the branches, there were many "leaves" that stretched out.
Whether it was the main stem of this "plant" or the "leaves", they were all shining with magnificent colors. The main color of this plant was azure blue, as if it was wrapped in a layer of crystal on the outermost layer. However, within the outermost layer, there were countless particles that were shining with different colors.
"This … What the hell is this?"
Just as this thought flashed in Luo Zheng's mind, he directly fell onto a "leaf"!
"These leaves seem to correspond to the narrow doors on the pyramids outside." Luo Zheng raised his head and took a look. He entered from the narrow door at the bottom of the pyramid and fell onto the leaf at the bottom of this plant!
"The bottom of this leaf seems to be wrapped with ores! Could it be that these ores are grown from the inside of this plant? " Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. He finally understood what those colorful particles were. Those should be various ores.
Luo Zheng raised his head and looked at this Sacred Tool tree. His face was full of awe. Nothing was too strange in this world. This tree itself probably did not produce ores. Did this tree extract ores from the depths of the earth through its roots?
The leaf under Luo Zheng's feet was wrapped with several different colored ores. He glanced around and did not find the Life Origin Stone he wanted. However, since he had entered, he naturally had to take away some ores. Therefore, Luo Zheng began to wave the pickaxe in his hand at the leaf!
"Kacha!" With a crisp sound, sparks flew in all directions.
When Luo Zheng's pickaxe hit the azure leaf, a crack suddenly appeared.

Chapter 824
Fortunately, Luo Zheng had a Low Grade Sacred Tool pickaxe in his hand, so it was not difficult for him to dig out the leaf. As Luo Zheng waved the pickaxe repeatedly, the cracks on the leaf suddenly burst open, and several unequal sized ores fell into Luo Zheng's hands!
These ores were not valuable. Even in the Central Domain, they could only be regarded as rare ores, but they were not among the best. In the four Divine Kingdoms, warriors at the level of King Yan would casually throw away these ores.
Luo Zheng did not have the habit of throwing things away. After he put away these ores, he hesitated for a while. Since he had dug out ores, he should be able to choose to leave here. But how could he leave now?
This place was neither touching the sky nor touching the ground …
But at this moment, Luo Zheng saw a warrior about four or five rows higher than him above his head. He was rapidly digging out the ores in the leaf. After he finished digging out the ores, the warrior jumped and fell into the void below.
But after a while, a ring spread out from the void like a ripple. At the same time, the warrior disappeared into it.
Thinking of the scene he saw on the side of the pyramid, Luo Zheng immediately understood. There must be a transmission array under this big tree that could allow warriors to return to the outside of the pyramid.
Therefore, Luo Zheng was ready to follow suit and jump up. But at this moment, Luo Zheng saw that among the roots of this big tree, there were also strange ores!
This big tree used its trunk to absorb a lot of ores, and then transmitted them to countless other trunks through its trunk, and finally to the leaf.
"If I were to cut off a section of this branch …" Luo Zheng had this thought in his mind.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly jumped toward the branch. Just as Luo Zheng was about to take out his High Grade Sacred Tool long sword, an irresistible force suddenly pulled Luo Zheng down!
The speed of the force was so fast that Luo Zheng did not have much time to react. By the time he came back to his senses, he had already appeared at the side of the pyramid with a long sword and a pickaxe in his hands. King Yan was standing not far away, looking at him with a smile.
King Yan's gaze fell on Luo Zheng's long sword, and he chuckled. "It seems that you are planning to cut off the branches of this big tree. Countless people have tried before, but no one could do it!"
The four Divine Kingdoms had explored the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea for so many years. Naturally, countless people had tried the method Luo Zheng could think of.
Cutting off the roots of this strange big tree was definitely much faster than digging out the ores in the leaves. However, no matter if it was a warrior of the False Tribulation Realm, a warrior of the Life and Death Realm, or even a mighty warrior of the Divine Sea Realm, no one could cut off the roots of this big tree.
Luo Zheng just smiled. Some things would only be clear after he tried. He put away his long sword, and this time, he looked at the pyramid in front of him in a proper way.
"If I guessed correctly, the ores excavated after entering the narrow door on the lower floors of this pyramid are of poor quality. The higher the floor, the better the ores excavated, right?" Luo Zheng asked.
King Yan nodded with a smile. "You are right. This pyramid has 47 floors, which correspond to the 47 leaves of the big tree. Every time you enter the narrow door, you can drop to any leaf to excavate the ores. The higher the floor, the better the quality and rarer the ores excavated. At the same time, the more difficult it is!"
"Which floor is the Origin Stone of Life on?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
He had traveled thousands of miles to the Divine Kingdom Continent to search for the Origin Stone of Life. As for the other ores, Luo Zheng was not very interested.
King Yan pointed at the pyramid and said, "The distribution of these ores is only a rough estimate. However, in principle, the Origin Stone of Life will only appear on the 25th floor and above. The higher the floor, the better the ores. However, the requirements for pickaxes are also higher. Moreover, some of the leaves are even dangerous."
After hearing King Yan's words, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. "In that case, I will go up too!"
At this moment, a group of warriors swarmed over. The person in the lead was Liu Xiu, whom he had met several times.
Previously, Liu Xiu had used the powerful Heavenly Thunder Halberd to fight against those resentful spirits. Even so, under the siege of so many resentful spirits, Liu Xiu was in a very difficult situation.
As the warriors clamored, they began to climb the pyramid. They took out their pickaxes and directly entered through the narrow doors on the 20th floor of the pyramid.
After they entered, Luo Zheng kept jumping up and down the pyramid. In his heart, he was silently counting the number of floors he had climbed. Since King Yan said that the Origin Stone of Life would only appear on the 25th floor, he naturally chose the 25th floor.
The higher the floor, the fewer the number of narrow doors on the pyramid. The first floor had thousands of narrow doors. After reaching the 25th floor, there were less than 100 narrow doors on the pyramid.
Luo Zheng randomly chose a narrow door and entered. The moment he stepped into the narrow door, he began to fall rapidly. Then, a light flashed in front of him and he fell directly into a leaf.
Now, Luo Zheng was occupying the upper middle section of the big tree. Standing here, Luo Zheng could overlook all the leaves below.
However, his gaze swept across for a while and then he looked at the leaf below his feet. He wanted to find the Origin Stone of Life, the kind of ancient jade that looked like an egg.
At a glance, he could see that the leaf contained a lot of ores. The ores of this level seemed to contain a strange power. Some of the ores even had a faint power of law flowing on the surface. At a glance, it was extraordinary.
However, Luo Zheng's eyes still showed disappointment. "No Origin Stone of Life …" Perhaps it was because of disappointment, Luo Zheng did not even have the mood to excavate the ores in the leaf.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng jumped and then began to fall rapidly. After a while, Luo Zheng appeared on the side of the pyramid again.
Below the pyramid, more and more warriors gathered. King Yan had probably also begun to excavate the ores. This time, Luo Zheng did not see him.
Since he did not find the Origin Stone of Life on the 25th floor, Luo Zheng simply increased the height by three floors and went directly to the 28th floor of the pyramid!
When Luo Zheng reached the 28th floor, many warriors gathered below began to whisper.
"That Luo Tianxing actually chose the 28th floor as soon as he came up …"
"He has the ability! With the strength he displayed, I'm afraid he can contend with some Life and Death experts. What's wrong with the 28th floor? "
"Yes, below the 30th floor is not a big problem. However, above the 30th floor, I'm afraid it'll be difficult!"
While everyone was discussing, Luo Zheng entered the narrow door of the 28th floor. However, after a few breaths, his figure once again fell from the halo on the side of the pyramid. At the same time, a depressed expression appeared on his face.
Obviously, the Origin Stone of Life that Luo Zheng needed was not found on the 28th floor.
This made Luo Zheng somewhat depressed. The number of Origin Stones he needed was not one or two. According to Luo Zheng's estimation, if he wanted to completely replenish Ning Yudie's life force, he would need at least a thousand Origin Stones. If he continued to excavate with such a probability, it would be a problem for him to obtain one or two hundred Origin Stones of Life in the end.
After falling from the halo, Luo Zheng continued to carry the pickaxe and climbed to the 29th floor.
"What is this Luo Tianxing trying to do?"
"I guess he's looking for some kind of ore."
This time, Luo Zheng directly fell on a leaf on the 29th floor. When his feet touched the leaf, his eyes flashed with a light, and a faint smile appeared on his face.
Origin Stone of Life, he had finally found it!
This azure leaf was about one zhang wide and one zhang thick. It looked like a huge blue crystal. In the center of this azure leaf, there were many egg-like ancient jades sealed inside. Impressively, these were the Origin Stones Luo Zheng was looking for!
Luo Zheng casually counted and found that there were sixteen Origin Stones of Life wrapped inside the leaf. Although it was far from the number he needed, it was still a lot. He could accumulate them.

Chapter 825
The 16 Life Source Stones in the leaf formed a circle, wrapping around the shiny object. That object emitted a dazzling light, making it difficult for Luo Zheng to distinguish it.
Without thinking too much, Luo Zheng raised the pickaxe high in the air.
"Kacha!"
He already knew that the higher the layer of the leaf, the thicker it was, and the bluer it was. At the same time, it was also more solid!
However, the firmness of this leaf still exceeded Luo Zheng's expectations. He smashed it so heavily, but it didn't even leave a mark …
"This …" Luo Zheng's face revealed a stunned expression.
Although he was only holding a low-grade Saint artifact pickaxe in his hand, when combined with Luo Zheng's own strength, even if he didn't use astral essence to erupt, he would still be able to easily split apart a mountain. Yet, this leaf in front of him was actually this hard?
However, he had found these Life Source Stones with great difficulty, so how could Luo Zheng give up?

At this time, he used the pickaxe to remove the broken parts, revealing the Life Source Stones below.

Liu Xiu snorted coldly and shifted his gaze to Luo Zheng. As the Crown Prince, he didn't say anything, but the people beside him naturally understood his meaning. Someone sneered and said, "I wonder what Luo Tianxing of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom has found? Can he let everyone take a look? "
Luo Zheng's face was like a zombie's. He glanced at the group of people indifferently. These god-level geniuses were really bored. They had dug out some ores, but they still took them out to compare. Luo Zheng really didn't want to use the word "childish" to describe their behavior …

Luo Zheng might refuse other things, but for life source stones, Luo Zheng would never refuse.

Luo Zheng didn't want to accept benefits from others for no reason, but he had only dug up a dozen life source stones. He couldn't just take them out as gifts. Thinking of this, a smile suddenly appeared on his face. He said, "Thank you. Since that's the case, I also have something to give you!"

Chapter 826
Luo Zheng did not know the value of this item. He was just a little embarrassed to accept Huo Yuner's gift.

King Yan originally wanted to say that the Life Origin Stone could not be compared to the stone in his hand, but he did not want to say it too bluntly.
"To me, the Life Origin Stone is the most important thing. The other ores are not important. I'll give it to you!" After saying that, Luo Zheng threw the shiny, square stone over.
Seeing that Luo Zheng threw it out so casually, many warriors wanted to stretch out their hands and hold the shiny stone in their hands. Of course, Luo Zheng threw it very accurately. Naturally, he would not throw it to anyone other than Huo Yuner.
Although Liu Xiu and the others showed greed in their eyes, they did not dare to snatch it here. After all, the rules of the four Divine Kingdoms were much stricter than the rules of the Central Domain. If they snatched it, they would be in trouble.
Huo Yuner's eyes flashed with a strange color. Looking at the stone in his hand, he smiled and said, "Luo Tianxing, do you really not know what this is?"
Luo Zheng shook his head.
"Phew …" Huo Yuner exhaled and said with a smile, "It seems that you are really not from the four Divine Kingdoms!"
At this moment, Huo Yuner completely confirmed that Luo Zheng was not from the four Divine Kingdoms through this shiny stone. However, it was no longer important whether Luo Zheng was a native warrior of the four Divine Kingdoms or not.
Luo Zheng smiled and did not say anything. However, from Huo Yuner's tone, it seemed that he had indeed unearthed something strange …
"This shiny thing is called the Eye of Heavenly Splendor! Erm … That's it. It's too precious. I can't accept it! " As she spoke, Huo Yuner threw the item to Luo Zheng. Then, she smiled at King of Yan and said, "King of Yan, please explain to him! The Black Iron Divine Kingdom should know best! "
King of Yan smiled faintly and said, "The most powerful weapon refiner in Black Iron Divine Kingdom can only refine a weapon at the level of a Semi-Divine Weapon. Even with all the resources we have, we can't refine a real Divine Weapon!"
The Black Iron Divine Nation was famous for its artificers. They represented the highest level of artificers in the four Divine Nations.
Since the Black Iron Divine Nation could only refine demigod tools, then none of the four Divine Nations could refine divine tools.
Until one day, a Life and Death Realm warrior dug out such a shiny stone from the Feather Emperor Secret Realm!
In the beginning, those weapon craftsmen could not integrate the stone into their weapons. Therefore, the stone was considered useless. However, the stone could not be destroyed by swords or fire. It also had a strange characteristic. Therefore, many weapon craftsmen were interested in it.
Later, a weapon craftsman named Zhong Tianhui finally discovered its secret and successfully used the stone to refine the first Divine Weapon in the four Divine Kingdoms!
In order to commemorate this weapon craftsman named Zhong Tianhui, the square stone was named the Eye of Heavenly Splendor. As for this weapon craftsman's fate, let's not talk about it for now.
Later on, whenever a warrior found an Eye of Heavenly Splendor in the Emperor of Feathers' Secret Realm, a brand new Divine Weapon would be born in the four Divine Kingdoms! The Emperor of Feathers' Secret Realm had produced seven Eyes of Heavenly Splendor. Therefore, there were seven Divine Weapons in the four Divine Kingdoms!
In other words, the value of an Eye of Heavenly Splendor was almost equivalent to a Divine Weapon!
Therefore, the value of this shiny stone could be imagined! This was also the reason why everyone's eyes were so hot!
Even the crown prince of a Divine Kingdom did not have a Divine Weapon. Liu Xiu, the crown prince of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom did not have one. Huo Chen, the crown prince of the Sky Wind Divine Kingdom did not have one. Even the crown prince of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom, Zhou Ning, who was famous for his weapon craftsmen, did not have one!
If they could bring back an Eye of Heavenly Splendor, with their status, there was a high possibility that they could get a Divine Weapon. Even if they could not get a Divine Weapon, they would be greatly rewarded!
Unfortunately …
Unfortunately, it was Luo Zheng who had dug it out.
They did not dare to snatch from the other crown princes and important princes. However, they dared to snatch from the god-level geniuses from the Martial Houses!
Luo Zheng was not a crown prince or a prince. The problem was that his strength was obvious. Who would dare to snatch from him? It was probably because they were tired of living.
After listening to King Yan's explanation, Luo Zheng gradually understood. However, his heart was still very calm. Even if it was a Divine Weapon, to Luo Zheng, it was only a worldly possession. A warrior's strength was the most important thing.
Since Huo Yuner was determined to retreat, Luo Zheng could not force him to accept it. Afterward, he casually stuffed this so-called Eye of Heavenly Splendor into his Sumeru Ring.
"I will continue to mine!" Luo Zheng said to King Yan with a smile.
King Yan asked, "No problem. You just have to pay attention to the activity below the thirtieth layer. Don't go above the thirtieth layer."
"Why?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression.
"Above the thirtieth layer is the area where Life and Death Realm warriors are active. For False Tribulation Realm warriors, it is life-threatening," King Yan replied.
This big tree was divided into forty-seven layers. Above the thirtieth layer was the layer where Life and Death Realm warriors could enter. Above the fortieth layer, only Divine Sea Realm warriors could enter. Therefore, most False Tribulation Realm warriors were active below the thirtieth layer.
Although the higher the layer, the better the quality of the ore. However, above the thirtieth layer, the hardness of the leaves was unimaginable. Ordinary False Tribulation Realm warriors could not dig through them at all!
"Hehe, you don't even dare to enter the thirtieth layer. In any case, you have defeated a little monster. You don't have to be so afraid of death, right?" Liu Xiu sneered coldly. Then, he dashed toward the top of the pyramid. On his shoulder, there was an exaggeratedly shaped pickaxe. The front and back of the pickaxe were constantly emitting flames. It was actually a high-grade Sacred Weapon.
After Liu Xiu jumped several times, he directly climbed to the thirtieth layer of the pyramid. He found a narrow door and immediately went in.
"Don't listen to his provocation. The current layer is enough to find the Life Origin Stone." King Yan was worried that Luo Zheng would really climb above the thirtieth layer after being provoked.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He understood King Yan's concern. However, how could he be provoked by such a childish trick? There was not much time left. Finding the Life Origin Stone was more important …
Therefore, Luo Zheng once again entered the narrow door on the twenty-ninth layer of the pyramid.
Overall, Luo Zheng's luck was not bad. He did not gain anything the second time he entered, but on his third time, he found a nest of Life Origin Stones!
Then, Luo Zheng quickly smashed the leaves and put the Life Origin Stones into his spatial ring.
Luo Zheng found that the Life Origin Stones either did not appear, or when they did, there would be nests of them. When there were fewer, there would be a dozen of them. When there were more, there would be dozens of them!
Then, Luo Zheng continuously entered the narrow door and fell out of the light circle. Ten times, twenty times, thirty times …
Gradually, Luo Zheng accumulated two to three hundred Life Origin Stones in his spatial ring. However, this number was still some distance away from Luo Zheng's goal. The most troublesome thing was that he had searched all the leaves on the twenty-ninth layer. He was almost certain that there were no more Life Origin Stones on the twenty-ninth layer.
Luo Zheng also searched the twenty-eighth layer and only found a nest of Life Origin Stones. As for the twenty-seventh layer, Luo Zheng did not find any at all.
Seeing that time was passing and he had only completed a quarter of his goal, Luo Zheng turned his gaze to the thirtieth layer. Even if the thirtieth layer was difficult as King Yan said, it should not be difficult for Luo Zheng.

Chapter 827
After returning to the side of the pyramid, Luo Zheng prepared to head towards the 30th level of the pyramid.
Every time Luo Zheng came out, he would always find a large group of people chatting here. Luo Zheng was curious. Why weren't these people mining ores, but instead just looking around?
However, when Luo Zheng came out this time, he discovered that these fellows had gathered here to trade the ores in their hands.
He didn't know where this tree had sucked up ores from, but there were many different types of ores. Through trading, everyone could naturally get what they needed.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to climb up, he suddenly thought of something and walked towards the group of people.
When Luo Zheng walked over, the group of people suddenly stopped talking.
This time, Luo Zheng had been in the limelight. He had left an extremely deep impression in the minds of the young god-rank geniuses.
Luo Zheng was too lazy to pay attention to them. He took out some ores from his spatial ring and piled them on the ground. Then, he said, "Whoever digs out a life source stone can trade with me."
Most of these god-rank geniuses were active on the 20th level and above. But, their limit was the 25th or 26th level. With their strength, they simply couldn't dig through the solid leaves and naturally couldn't take out the ores.
Luo Zheng had been active on the 29th level. Besides the life source stone, he would also casually dig out any ores with beautiful colors.
Combined with a low-grade saint artifact level pickaxe and a small amount of dragon scale power, Luo Zheng didn't find it particularly difficult to dig out ores.
The higher the level, the better the ores that could be dug out. Other ores were useless to Luo Zheng, but there was no lack of rare ores. Some ores were even worth as much as the life source stone.
So when Luo Zheng threw out the scattered life source stones, some of the martial artists' eyes suddenly shot out a strange light.
Some of these ores were exactly what they needed!
Many warriors were attracted by the pile of ores on the ground. One of them pointed at the pile of ores and said, "I don't have a Life Origin Stone. You name a price for this emerald green ore. I can buy it from you with a top grade True Essence Hand."
These Divine rank geniuses were all geniuses from fifth-grade aristocratic families, so they were naturally wealthy. They might not have Life Origin Stones, but they had many top grade true essence stones.
Luo Zheng shook his head indifferently and said: "My ores can only be traded, not sold. If you want to take them away, you have to exchange them with the Life Origin Stone."
When the cultivator heard Luo Zheng's words, a look of disbelief appeared on his face. He immediately shouted, "Two hundred top-grade True Origin Stones."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng didn't even lift his eyelids. He was too lazy to explain his words to Deaf twice.
"Three hundred, three hundred top grade True Essence Stones." When the martial artist saw Luo Zheng's expression, he probably felt that he had been humiliated, so he immediately increased the price by 50%.
The expression on Luo Zheng's face remained unchanged.
"Four hundred!" The martial artist increased the price by another 100. In fact, the price of this turquoise in the outside world was about 500 top-grade True Origin Stones. This was the place where ores were produced, so the purchase price would naturally be much lower.
However, Luo Zheng still didn't move. Instead, he continued to look around and then said: "I don't have much time. If you have Life Origin Stones, you can exchange them with me."
"Kid, what do you mean? My purchase price is not low! " The martial artist said coldly. His eyes also revealed an unfriendly look. He found that he was completely ignored by Luo Zheng. A dark red color appeared on his face. Martial artists at this age were the most prideful. The most intolerable thing was to be ignored by others.
Hearing the martial artist's question, Luo Zheng revealed a sarcastic smile on his face. He stared at the martial artist and said: "Didn't you understand my words? Only trade, not sell! I'll say it again. If you want it, then exchange it with the Life Origin Stone! If you don't have the Life Origin Stone, then don't join in the fun. Do you understand now? "
At this time, the martial artist suddenly realized that this kid in front of him was a guy who even dared to provoke a mighty warrior of the Divine Sea Realm. How could he afford to offend him? Therefore, he was so annoyed by Luo Zheng that his face turned red and white. Finally, he retreated to the side in dejection.
Just as the martial artist retreated, another martial artist said to Luo Zheng: "I have 10 Life Origin Stones. I want to exchange them with your Green Mist Stone. How about that?"
The martial artist was referring to a green ore near Luo Zheng's feet. The ore's surface was covered with wisps of green. It looked transparent, but if one looked carefully, one would find that the middle was hazy.
Luo Zheng smiled, "Deal!" Then he pointed his finger and that Green Mist Stone flew straight towards the other party.
He was not afraid that the other party would go back on his word. As for whether or not 10 Life Origin Stones could be exchanged for one Green Mist Stone, Luo Zheng did not consider it.
That martial artist knew how powerful Luo Zheng was. How could he dare to fool Luo Zheng? He straightforwardly took out 10 Life Origin Stones and gave them to Luo Zheng. He revealed a pleasantly surprised expression. Obviously, he had made a profit.
Seeing this scene, some martial artists secretly cursed Luo Zheng as an idiot. This exchange was a loss. However, they would not remind Luo Zheng.
"Anyone else who has a Life Origin Stone can exchange it with me." Seeing the other party's expression, Luo Zheng knew in his heart that the Life Origin Stone was far more significant to Luo Zheng than other ores. No matter how high the price, it was worth it.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was so easy to talk to, some martial artists also became restless. Although there weren't many Life Origin Stones, there were still several martial artists who had dug them up. One of them pointed at the golden ore beneath Luo Zheng's feet and said, "I have six Life Origin Stones here. How about I exchange this with you?"
Luo Zheng was just about to nod his head in agreement when a crisp voice floated over, "Six Life Origin Stones in exchange for one 'unpolished gold'. You are too good at doing business!"
The one who spoke was Yue Ying who had just returned from the circle of light. She saw Luo Zheng casually throw a pile of ores on the ground and exchange with someone.
Among the pile of ores in front of Luo Zheng, that golden unpolished gold was the most valuable one! Such a large piece of unpolished gold could be sold for at least 4000 top grade true essence stones!
The martial artist who had offered the price heard Yue Ying's words and his face darkened.
But Yue Ying did not have the slightest fear. Instead, she coldly said to the martial artist, "What? You want to fight? Brother Tianxing can stab you to death with a single finger! If you want a piece of 'unpolished gold', you have to take out at least 80 Life Origin Stones to exchange for it! "
Hearing Yue Ying use him to threaten the other party, Luo Zheng's face revealed a helpless expression. Luo Zheng did not expect the other party to take out 80 Life Origin Stones. Even if there were only six Life Origin Stones, for Ning Yudie, he would exchange without hesitation.
When the other party heard this threat, he could only grit his teeth and say, "I don't have that many Life Origin Stones! I only have twelve of them! "
"You clearly have 12, but you left behind six. I'm afraid you are bullying Brother Tianxing for not knowing how to distinguish between these ores and want to exchange for this Golden Warmth Stone?" Yue Ying bent down and picked up a slender golden ore, waving it at the martial artist.
Having his thoughts seen through by Yue Ying, the martial artist's expression was extremely unnatural. Before he could speak, Yue Ying spoke first, "How about this. For this piece of unpolished gold, I'll give you 3500 top grade true essence stones in addition to the 12 Life Origin Stones in your hands! Nonnegotiable! "
"You …" Originally, he could have obtained six Life Origin Stones, but this woman had forcefully messed up his plans. How could he not be depressed? Moreover, he did plan to use the remaining six Life Origin Stones to exchange for the Golden Warmth Stone …
Unfortunately, he could not do anything to Yue Ying!
However, 3500 top grade true essence stones in addition to 12 Life Origin Stones … he still had some profit. Wanting this, he clenched his teeth and said, "Alright, deal!"
After Yue Ying and the martial artist completed the transaction, Luo Zheng took the 12 Life Origin Stones from her hands, but blocked the top grade true essence stones. "Thank you. This is your reward. I don't need it …"
"You're really stupid!" Yue Ying stuffed the top grade true essence stones into Luo Zheng's hands and said, "Besides bartering, you can also use the top grade true essence stones to purchase other people's Life Origin Stones! Moreover, the Life Origin Stones are dug up in a nest. At the very least, there are eight or ten. It's very rare to see someone dig up six. He said he has six in his hands, so he's obviously hiding it and trying to extort you … "
Being reprimanded by Yue Ying like this, Luo Zheng was a little dazed. This woman was much more shrewd than Luo Zheng had imagined.
However, Luo Zheng could not be blamed. He did not understand the market price of these ores, and Yue Ying was a martial artist of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. Since she was young, she had come into contact with and identified these ores. Moreover, these ores were produced in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea. Luo Zheng was unable to inquire about them through the memories of the world's will.

Chapter 828
With such a shrewd woman helping him take care of these ores, he didn't care about the value of the ores. As for the commission, he didn't care about it at all. On the contrary, he was very happy.
Thus, Luo Zheng immediately nodded and said with a smile, "Alright, then I'll leave this to you!"
Yue Ying's strength wasn't enough. Adding on the fact that female martial artists were generally weaker than male martial artists, she could only mine ores from the nineteenth to the twentieth layer. Even if she painstakingly dug for half a day, she might not be able to dig out anything good. Helping Luo Zheng take care of these ores could be considered as' making the best use of people '.
After Luo Zheng left, Yue Ying's elegant eyebrows slightly raised. Then, she sweetly smiled at the numerous martial artists, "Friends, you can choose as much as you want. I have a discounted price here! However, Life Origin Ores take priority! "
Hearing Yue Ying's crisp words, the martial artists rolled their eyes one by one. Apart from the first person who picked up a bargain, the others were a step slower. Fortunately, the woman's price wasn't too outrageous. Some people still surrounded Yue Ying and picked the ores they liked.
Luo Zheng flew up the pyramid and quickly entered the thirtieth layer.
Generally, only Life and Death experts could enter the thirtieth layer. After passing through the narrow door, Luo Zheng instantly landed on one of the leaves.
The leaves on the thirtieth layer were twice as wide as those on the twenty-ninth layer. The higher the tree's leaves went, the wider the leaves became, and the deeper the color. At the same time, the leaves became harder and harder to dig.
However, just as Luo Zheng landed on the leaf, he immediately felt an intense killing intent!
"Squeak squeak squeak …"
On this leaf, there was a big bug flickering with emerald green light.
That bug was as tall as two people. It had six legs and a pair of brown compound eyes on its head. Its abdomen was covered with scales flickering with azure light.
When Luo Zheng stood on the leaf, that bug pounced at Luo Zheng!
Seeing this huge bug, Luo Zheng's expression slightly changed. His brows also wrinkled. Didn't all the creatures in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean have been sacrificed? Why were there still creatures here?
"Ka!"
That bug's spear-like foreleg stabbed at Luo Zheng. That sharp spike actually pierced a small hole in the leaf!
"What sharp forelegs! What great strength! "
Even for the 29th layer of leaves, Luo Zheng had to activate more than a hundred dragon scales before he could break them open with a Holy-ranked pickaxe. The 30th layer of leaves should be even tougher!
But, this insect's attack had actually pierced a small hole in the ground. The sharpness of its forelegs was comparable to a Saint artifact, and its strength was probably no weaker than an ordinary Life and Death realm martial artist!
It was no wonder that King Yan had told Luo Zheng that the mines above level 30 were not suitable for ordinary Virtual Tribulation Realm warriors to mine.
After the insect spread its wings, it rushed at Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng's figure flashed slightly, instantly avoiding the front leg that was poking at him.
When he was in the Eastern Region, Luo Zheng had dealt with many blade insects. The strength of blade insects naturally couldn't be compared to the insect in front of him. However, the weakness of all insects should be in their abdomens. Just look at the abdomen covered with scales.
Therefore, after avoiding the awl-like forelegs, Luo Zheng leaned close to the insect's abdomen and slapped out with his palm!
Due to the burst of Vital Essence, this palm strike was fast and ruthless. With a "crack", a large piece of scales on the insect's abdomen was directly shattered by Luo Zheng.
"Squeak squeak …"
The giant insect let out a miserable shriek, but it suddenly spread its wings and retreated.
"Huh!"
After Luo Zheng smashed the scales on its abdomen, he saw that its transparent abdomen seemed to be filled with various colored ores!
"Does this insect live on ores? Is this thing a living creature … "Luo Zheng sighed in his heart, but his figure flashed and he didn't slow down in his pursuit. Since there were many ores in this insect's body, Luo Zheng didn't have to search for them leaf by leaf.
However, this big insect was very cunning. With a flap of its wings, it directly jumped from one leaf to another!
At this time, Luo Zheng was at his wit's end …
As long as he left this leaf, Luo Zheng would be pulled back by a tremendous gravitational force. At the same time, he would fall outside through the circle of light. Then, he would have to climb up the pyramid again and enter the narrow door.
Luo Zheng simply couldn't resist this tremendous gravitational force. Naturally, he couldn't jump from one leaf to another.
"Squeak squeak!" The big insect seemed to know that Luo Zheng couldn't chase after it, so it raised its two spear-like forelegs and constantly provoked Luo Zheng.
With Luo Zheng's current temperament, even if some martial artists provoked him, he would turn a blind eye to it, let alone a big insect. His expression was indifferent, but he began to look at the ores in this leaf. If this damned big insect dared to fly over, Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't let it go.
In this wide leaf, Luo Zheng also looked around. His eyes revealed a look of disappointment. This leaf didn't have any Origin Stones of Life, but he still found a few pieces of colorful and special shaped ores. So, he waved his pickaxe and dug them out of the leaf.
The hardness of the 30th layer was much higher than that of the 29th layer, but it was still within the range that Luo Zheng could accept. He only needed to use the power of 300 dragon scales to dig out this leaf.
After Luo Zheng dug out several pieces of ores, he shook his head and saw that the big insect seemed to be using its two spear-like forelegs to poke at these leaves. After a while, it poked a big hole in the leaf. Then, it lowered its head and swallowed the ores in one gulp.
"This big insect really lives by eating ores!" Luo Zheng stared at the big insect. Suddenly, his eyes flashed. That big insect was only on the leaf next door, not far from Luo Zheng. From here, he could clearly see the big insect's transparent abdomen. Looking in from the abdomen, Luo Zheng saw dozens of Origin Stones of Life! With a rough estimate, there were at least 50 to 60 of them!
"Origin Stones of Life!"
Luo Zheng clenched his hands. It seemed that he had to kill this big insect. Thinking of this, Luo Zheng jumped down. At the same time, Luo Zheng looked back. That big insect didn't care about his movements. It still lowered its head and swallowed the ores.
The moment he fell into the circle of light, Luo Zheng appeared at the side of the pyramid. He was about to climb up the pyramid when he heard Yue Ying shout from the side, "Brother Tianxing! I've already helped you exchange for ten Origin Stones of Life! There are also two thousand top grade True Origin Stones. "
The god-rank geniuses who came here had a lot of top grade True Origin Stones in their hands, but the number of Origin Stones of Life was not many.
Luo Zheng nodded. He lightly touched the spatial ring in his hand, and seven or eight ores flew towards Yue Ying. He, on the other hand, rushed up the pyramid.
"Ah, Purple Net Crystal, New Moon Stone, and Jade Water Heart Stone …" While those ores were still in the air, Yue Ying had already called out the names of most of the ores.
When the other martial artists heard Yue Ying's words, they also swarmed up. When they saw these ores clearly, each and every one of their faces revealed an extremely unbalanced expression.
"What kind of dog shit luck does this Luo Tianxing have! Even if it's the 30th floor, it's impossible to dig up so many good things at once! "
"Yes, we also have several martial artists who entered the 30th floor and above. They broke two pickaxes and only dug out some sparse and ordinary things. How could this guy …"
"Let's not talk about his strength, his luck is also so good. God is really unfair!"
Luck was one of the easiest things to be envied by others. Most people would admire the strength of a powerhouse, but if the other party relied on luck to achieve a certain level of success, it was inevitable that they would feel unbalanced in their hearts. It was just that they didn't know that for a very small number of people in this universe, luck was a part of strength!
The reason why Luo Zheng rushed up was naturally to target that big bug. He quickly rushed up, found the narrow door from before, and then went in through the narrow door next door.
Each narrow door corresponded to a leaf of this big tree, and the narrow door next door corresponded to the leaf next door. If Luo Zheng's speed was fast enough, that big bug should still be engrossed in devouring the ores.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 829
Luo Zheng quickly passed through the narrow door and fell straight down.
While falling, Luo Zheng's eyes were firmly locked on the leaf. He could see that the big bug was bowing its head and swallowing the ores in the leaf!
One hundred Zhang, fifty Zhang, ten Zhang …
Luo Zheng's fists began to accumulate power!
"Kill!"
Luo Zheng longed for the Life Source Stone. Even if this big bug only had one Life Source Stone in its body, Luo Zheng still wanted to kill it!
His attack was wrapped in the impact force of falling, and at the same time, he activated the power of five hundred dragon scales!
This punch was aimed at the big bug's back, but the moment Luo Zheng approached, the big bug seemed to have some kind of special premonition. Its two hind legs suddenly stomped, and with a roll, it dodged the punch!
"Bang!"
Luo Zheng's huge fist directly hit the leaf, and the huge leaf and the roots next to it shook unceasingly.
"Zhi Zhi Zhi!"
The big bug kept a distance of two Zhang from Luo Zheng, and it was still waving its sharp forelegs, provoking Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng looked at the leaf under his feet. The punch just now had caused many cracks to appear on the leaf, but he still couldn't find the Life Source Stone.
His body's hardness was comparable to a sacred tool, and mixed with this terrifying power, even if he didn't use the pickaxe, he could still break thirty layers of the leaf. Of course, using the pickaxe would save him a lot of effort.
"Provoking me?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly, and he bent his leg slightly.
Although this big bug was very agile, Luo Zheng was confident that he could catch it!
While the big bug was still waving its forelegs and squeaking, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly flashed. He directly crossed space, and stretched out his hands to grab the big bug!
It had to be said that this big bug was even more agile than ordinary Life and Death experts. Its perception of danger seemed to be completely instinctive. Even if Luo Zheng used the method of crossing space, the big bug still sensed it with its inconceivable keen sense of smell. At the same time, it spread its wings one step ahead, and was about to leave the leaf!
"I can't leave!"
The moment Luo Zheng crossed the space, the Vital Spirit in his body burst out, and his whole body instantly shot forward a distance of two feet!
After two Chi, Luo Zheng's body had left the leaf. At the same time, the invisible force began to pull Luo Zheng's body. But the moment Luo Zheng was pulled down by that huge force, his hand had already grabbed the big bug's hind legs!
"Zhi Zhi!"
That big bug kept stomping its legs! But how powerful was the power in Luo Zheng's hand? As long as he grabbed it, the big bug would not be able to get away!
The big bug was not affected by the huge gravitational force, and could fly freely among the leaves. But now, Luo Zheng was holding its legs tightly. In other words, half of the gravitational force of Luo Zheng's falling body was shared by the big bug!
Although the big bug desperately flapped its wings to prevent itself from falling with Luo Zheng, it was still pulled down by Luo Zheng under that irresistible gravitational force.
"Zhi Zhi Zhi …"
Even so, the big bug was still flying desperately out of instinct. It kept circling left and right with Luo Zheng, and kept falling down.
On the other side, King Yan was still on the 27th level of the leaf, digging for ores painstakingly. There was a Vast Cloud Stone buried under the leaf. This kind of ore was very valuable, and King Yan naturally would not let it go. The moment he swung his pickaxe, he suddenly saw a figure flash past him. The figure even circled around him. He was stunned.
In this space, there were extremely strict rules. Even a mighty warrior of the Divine Sea Realm could not fly, nor could he resist the force of falling. But he clearly saw a figure just now.
So, King Yan hurried to the edge of the leaf. With one look, King Yan's face was full of astonishment. "Brother, Brother Tianxing …"
He saw Luo Zheng dragging a big green bug. It was like a headless fly, circling around the big tree!
"What, what is going on?" King Yan felt a little dizzy.
King Yan wasn't the only warrior who had witnessed this scene. At least two-thirds of the warriors were working hard on mining on the leaves of this big tree. Most of the god-level geniuses had also witnessed this baffling scene.
"It seems to be that Luo Tianxing! Why is he dragging a, a Ore Devouring Bug! "
"How … how can he not catch a Ore Devouring Bug!"
"It seems that only a mighty warrior of the Divine Sea Realm has ever caught a Ore Devouring Bug …"
Under everyone's gaze, Luo Zheng's figure fell rapidly. Finally, he fell to the bottom of this big tree and disappeared in the halo.
The moment he disappeared, Luo Zheng dragged that big bug and appeared on the side of the pyramid again.
Yue Ying was bargaining with a warrior in front of her with a smile. According to Yue Ying's judgment, this warrior probably had at least 10 Origin Stones of Life in his hands. She knew that Luo Zheng valued Origin Stones of Life the most. So, even if she suffered a little loss, she would still plunder all of his Origin Stones of Life!
But at this moment, she suddenly heard a violent sound. Then, there was a sharp and hurried sound of bugs!
Yue Ying turned her head to take a look. Her eyes suddenly trembled. "Brother, Brother Tianxing … that, that is a Ore Devouring Bug!"
The warriors who were trading ores outside the pyramid were immediately attracted by this scene.
After passing through space, Luo Zheng's body was no longer affected by the powerful gravitational force. The big bug spread its wings and flew high, directly dragging Luo Zheng into the sky!
How could Luo Zheng let this big bug succeed?
After taking a deep breath, he forcefully twisted his waist. His legs suddenly swung as he flipped over and climbed onto the back of the big bug.
"Swish swish!"
The big bug's reaction was extremely agile. It could be said that it far surpassed ordinary Life and Death Realm warriors. Its two front legs twisted and stabbed towards Luo Zheng!
"In your dreams!"
Luo Zheng's figure gently swayed, dodging the two sharp stabs. Then, he used his hand as a blade and heavily chopped towards the back of the big bug.
"Puff!"
With a crisp sound, the carapace on the back of the big insect was cut off by Luo Zheng's palm blade. Then, the flapping wings were also cut off, and the insect's body plummeted downwards!
"Bang!"
The moment it fell, Luo Zheng's body slightly tilted. Bending his left leg, he directly pressed his knee on the big bug's head. Therefore, the moment the big bug fell to the ground, Luo Zheng crushed its head. The big bug's vitality was also very tenacious. Even without its head, it constantly waved its six legs. After struggling for a while, the joints of its six legs slowly stiffened, and its vitality was gradually cut off.
Luo Zheng patted the dust off his body and jumped down from the big bug's body. At this time, Yue Ying had already rushed in front of Luo Zheng. Because she was in a hurry, she didn't even have time to put the pile of ores into her spatial ring. Instead, she directly used her clothes to cover the pile of ores. She grabbed the hem of her clothes with both hands and asked with wide eyes, "Brother Tianxing, how, how did you catch a Ore Devouring Bug?"
"Oh, so this big bug is called a Ore Devouring Bug?" Luo Zheng nodded calmly.
At this time, King Yan also appeared from the light circle and rushed over. He had just left the light circle when he saw that Luo Zheng had already subdued the Ore Devouring Bug. His face revealed a surprised and happy expression.
Not only King Yan, but all the warriors in the "mining tree" also left the light circle one after another and quickly surrounded Luo Zheng!
When King Yan heard Luo Zheng's words, he laughed loudly and said, "Yes, Brother Tianxing, this big bug is called a Ore Devouring Bug. It lives by devouring the ores in the mining tree!"
Hearing King Yan's words, Luo Zheng looked at the god-level geniuses who were continuously rushing over. A strange expression appeared on his face. Even if it lived by devouring ores, this Ore Devouring Bug shouldn't have much value, right? It was just a little more ores in its stomach!

Chapter 830
Hearing this, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. That was the voice of Liu Xiu, the Crown Prince of the Yu Kingdom.
He didn't take the threat seriously. After all, among these Immortal Level Geniuses, no one could threaten him. Even Liu Xiu was not qualified!
To be fair, Luo Zheng's strength was not on the same level as these Immortal Level Geniuses. This was a fact that many Immortal Level Geniuses, Princes, and Crown Princes were unwilling to admit.
Since Liu Xiu dared to threaten him, this Ore Devouring Bug must be related to something big. There must be some secrets hidden in it.
However, Luo Zheng still smiled faintly. His gaze passed through the crowd and landed on Liu Xiu. "Dead? How will you die? "
Liu Xiu also stared at the Ore Devouring Bug. He didn't expect Luo Zheng to catch this Ore Devouring Bug with his bare hands, even though it was just a green Ore Devouring Bug. Liu Xiu then said, "This Ore Devouring Bug is caught by a Divine Sea Realm expert every year! Since when is it your turn to catch it?! "
Luo Zheng shifted his gaze to King Yan. King Yan nodded and told Luo Zheng, "There aren't many Ore Devouring Bugs. There are only 30 to 40 of them every year. However, even Life and Death Realm experts can't catch these Ore Devouring Bugs. Normally, the Divine Sea Realm experts of the four Divine Kingdoms would do it in person."
"Is this bug important?"
In Luo Zheng's mind, this Ore Devouring Bug was a little strange. It could live by eating ores. More importantly, its reaction was extremely fast. When Luo Zheng traveled through space, there was almost no time for him to react. However, the bug was able to dodge it.
However, this bug was not worth the Divine Sea Realm expert's effort! Perhaps the ores in the Ore Devouring Bug's stomach were valuable, but how could it catch the eye of a Divine Sea Realm expert?
"Yes." King Yan smiled and explained to Luo Zheng, "This Ore Devouring Bug has a special ability. It can cut any leaf on a tree at will!"
"So what?" Luo Zheng was still puzzled.
King Yan also knew that Luo Zheng would ask this question. He then continued to explain to Luo Zheng, "Normally, only Life and Death Realm warriors would enter the 30th floor. The Ore Devouring Bug is the reason, which is life-threatening for False Tribulation Realm warriors. if they are not strong enough, they are not Tribulation Realm warriors should not enter it!"
"Only Divine Sea Realm experts of the Divine Sea Realm can enter the 40th floor and Death, because there are Purple Ore Devouring Bugs in the 40th layer!" The King of Yan explained to Luo Zheng slowly, "The purple Ore Devouring Worm is the mature form of the green Ore Devouring Worm.
"The important thing is that the 45th to 47th levels, which are the highest levels, contain some extremely heaven-defying ores. One of the most important ores is called a 'soul core'. This type of ore is extremely important to the four Divine Kingdoms! To some extent, it, it... The Devouring Stage Devouring God............
"Even a Divine Sea Realm warrior can't dig out the top leaves?" Luo Zheng's pupils shrank.
Luo Zheng had witnessed the strength of a Divine Sea Realm expert several times. The most recent time was when War Emperor Yu the Great attacked. Luo Zheng transferred the strength of his punch, which destroyed a row of mountains!
Luo Zheng was shocked that a Divine Sea Realm expert with such strength could not dig the leaves on the top of the tree.
However, these were all public secrets of the four Divine Kingdoms. Some of the Deity rank geniuses present didn't show any changes in their expressions. However, Huo Chen and Huo Yuner, who had rushed over, revealed looks of surprise. They had never explored the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea before. Although they knew of the four great mystic realms, they didn't know what they were.
"The Devouring Stage Devouring Bug can't dig the leaves on the 45th to 47th level, but the Devouring Devouring Bug under your feet can!" King Yan smiled and said, "However, the Devouring Devouring Bug usually lives on the 30th level. It is unlikely that you can find a Soul Core in it. However, every time the Feather Emperor Secret Realm is opened, both the Devouring Devouring Bug and the Devouring Bug will be killed. Do you understand now?"
After hearing what the King of Yan had said, Luo Zheng finally understood everything.
The Devouring Devouring Bug could not dig the leaves on the top three levels. However, they urgently needed the Soul Cores. Since they could not dig the leaves, they could only place their hopes on the Devouring Devouring Bug. Therefore, they would search for the Devouring Bug every year. On the one hand, it was to get rid of the bugs. On the other hand, it was to find the Soul Cores in the Devouring Bug's body.
"However, the War Emperors and War Emperors did not forbid others from killing the Devouring Bug, right?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
King Yan shook his head and answered, "No."
In fact, ordinary Virtual Tribulation Realm martial artists could not capture the Devouring Bug. Even the god-tier geniuses who were at the top of the ladder, such as Huo Yuner, would find it difficult to capture the Devouring Bug.
Only the most powerful Life and Death Realm martial artists and Divine Sea Realm martial artists had the chance to capture the Devouring Bug.
The Life and Death Realm martial artists and Divine Sea Realm martial artists acted together. They would not be so bored as to make such a rule.
"Since there are no rules forbidding us from capturing the Devouring Bug, why can't I touch this Devouring Bug?" Luo Zheng suddenly looked at Liu Xiu. His eyes revealed a cold look.
Liu Xiu could not help but shudder under Luo Zheng's gaze. This guy was not even afraid of his father. Why would he be afraid of a Crown Prince of the Divine Kingdom?
However, seeing Luo Zheng capture a green Devouring Bug, Liu Xiu was not willing to give up. He threatened, "Hmph, even if you get a Soul Core from this Devouring Bug, I'm afraid you don't have the guts to take it for yourself!"
In response to Liu Xiu's warning, Luo Zheng only smiled faintly. With a light tap of his finger, a long sword shot out from his Sumeru Ring. Holding the long sword in his hand, he slashed at the Ore Devouring Bug's abdomen! What he cared about was the Origin Stone of Life in the Devouring Bug's stomach!
The defensive power of the Devouring Bug's scales was amazing. The slash only broke a row of the scales. The second slash only cut open the abdomen of the Devouring Bug.
"Ding ding dang dang!"
The appetite of the Devouring Bug was also amazing. After its stomach was cut open by Luo Zheng, all kinds of ores flowed out. There were about two to three hundred ores!
The hundreds of god-tier geniuses who were watching all stared blankly at the Devouring Bug's stomach. Although many of the ores were very precious, they were not concerned about anything else. They were most concerned about whether they could find a Soul Core in the Devouring Bug's stomach. That was the most important thing to the four Divine Kingdoms!
Green Devouring Bugs were rarely active at the top of mining trees. Even the possibility of finding a Soul Core in the stomach of a purple Devouring Bug was very small, let alone a green Devouring Bug.
The key was that even if some Devouring Bugs did dig out a Soul Core from the top of the leaves, they would be digested after swallowing it into their stomachs …
Unless a green or purple Devouring Bug flew up to the top of the 45th leaf and dug out a Soul Core not long before the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm opened and swallowed it before digesting it. At that time, it was caught by a Divine Sea Realm cultivator and obtained a Soul Core!
From this, it could be seen that this Soul Core was really too precious. Often, it would be difficult to obtain a Soul Core even after killing hundreds of Devouring Bugs for several years.
When the ores in the Devouring Bug's stomach flowed out slowly along with its body fluid, King Yan's eyes revealed a look of disappointment. Luo Zheng smiled, "Why? No Soul Core? "
King Yan nodded and replied, "No!"
Luo Zheng did not pay too much attention to it. On one hand, he was not clear about the use of this Soul Core. On the other hand, he was focused on finding Life Origin Stones. He would not be too distracted by other things.
Although there were no Soul Cores in the Devouring Bug's stomach, there were many Life Origin Stones. Luo Zheng was already satisfied.
His eyes flashed and he smiled at Yue Ying, "I'll have to trouble you. Please help me trade these away."
Yue Ying's eyes widened slightly, but she pouted and said, "So dirty!"
Naturally, he would not let a woman say such dirty words. Only Luo Zheng could clean the ores out of the Devouring Bug's body fluid one by one.

Chapter 831
After Luo Zheng cleaned up the ores, he handed them over to Yue Ying.
There were quite a few Origin Stones of Life, but they were still far from Luo Zheng's goal.
Ning Yudie's life force was on the verge of drying up. Perhaps these Origin Stones of Life could awaken Ning Yudie, but they couldn't replenish her life force and restore her to her original state.
Luo Zheng had always felt guilty for letting Ning Yudie fall into such a state. He also blamed himself deeply. Hence, the more Origin Stones of Life he had, the better it was for Luo Zheng.
This Ore Devouring Worm didn't have a Soul Core in its body. Most of the god-tier geniuses were disappointed.
Even if Luo Zheng dug out a Soul Core from this Ore Devouring Worm's corpse, it probably wouldn't fall into their hands. However, when everyone saw that Luo Zheng had caught an Ore Devouring Worm, they couldn't help but hope to dig it out.
After dealing with this Ore Devouring Worm's corpse, Luo Zheng asked King Yan curiously, "Why is this so-called Soul Core so important?"
This wasn't a secret. Even those god-tier geniuses who entered the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea for the first time seemed to know very well. King Yan simply told Luo Zheng, "Do you remember the Heavenly Chen Secret Realm that I mentioned to you before?"
Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
Right now, many Divine Sea Realm Almighties weren't interested in the Feather Emperor Secret Realm, the Dream God Secret Realm, and the Godly Emperor Secret Realm. The only thing they were interested in was the Heavenly Chen Secret Realm, which was above the three Secret Realms. That was the most important place for the Dragon Bloodline Clan. It was also where the Ancestral Dragon's egg was.
"Could this Soul Core be related to entering the Heavenly Chen Secret Realm?" Luo Zheng continued to ask. In fact, King Yan's words had already informed Luo Zheng.
"Yes, only by obtaining the Soul Core will you be qualified to enter the Godly Emperor Secret Realm and control the corpse of the Dragon Bloodline Clan!" King Yan smiled.
Hearing King Yan's words, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed.
At the same time, he felt a slight tremor in his mind. He could feel Qinglong's soul trembling.
Luo Zheng promised Qinglong to get the Ancestral Dragon egg, but only if there was a chance.
Even Divine Sea Realm experts would find it hard to enter the Heavenly Star Mystery Land. With Luo Zheng's current strength, he might not be able to do anything.
However, the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea would open every year. If Luo Zheng didn't have the opportunity this year, he only needed to accumulate enough Life Origin Stones and go back to Ning Yudie to replenish her life force. When the time was right, he could come back to Scandinavia.
After all, the four Divine Nations had not taken the Ancestral Dragon's egg from the Heavenly Star Secret Realm even after so many years. It was unlikely that they would be able to take it away in a short period of time.
Before Qinglong could speak, Luo Zheng had already replied in his mind, "If I can obtain the Soul Core from this mining tree, I will also enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm."
After hearing Luo Zheng's reply, Qinglong's face revealed a look of relief. Although he lived in Luo Zheng's mind, he couldn't ask too much of Luo Zheng. With his current strength, it was still too difficult for him to fight against those Divine Sea Realm experts. Not to mention how dangerous the Heavenly Star Secret Realm was, just dealing with those War Emperors and Battle Emperors was already a difficult test for Luo Zheng. Furthermore, Luo Zheng had already formed a feud with the Yu Da Divine Nation …
As a result, Qinglong and the crimson dragon didn't say anything. They closed their huge dragon eyes in relief. Previously, when they helped Luo Zheng block the little monster's Soul Impact, it was not a small loss for them. They needed to rest and recuperate for a period of time.
As for why the Soul Core was needed to control the corpses in the Divine Emperor Secret Realm, Luo Zheng didn't ask any further. Everything was based on the premise that Luo Zheng obtained the Soul Core. As for the other answers, it was useless no matter how much Luo Zheng knew. Right now, he still needed to hurry and excavate the Origin Stone of Life.
Yue Ying accumulated all of Luo Zheng's ores and started a new round of trading with numerous martial artists. This time, all of the martial artists who were mining were attracted by Luo Zheng. Among them, there must be many martial artists who had dug out the Origin Stone of Life. The shrewd Yue Ying would naturally seize this opportunity.
After bidding farewell to Yan Wang, Luo Zheng continued to enter the pyramid's 30th floor.
Luo Zheng originally thought that the higher the floor, the higher the probability of finding the Origin Stone of Life. However, after searching for half a day, Luo Zheng was somewhat disappointed.
The leaves on the 30th floor were harder to excavate than on the 29th floor. However, the number of Origin Stones of Life didn't increase. On the contrary, it even decreased slightly …
No wonder Yan Wang suggested Luo Zheng to excavate above the 25th floor and below the 30th floor. It was estimated that the number of Origin Stones of Life in these five floors would be greater.
The problem was that Luo Zheng had basically explored all of the Origin Stones from the 25th floor to the 29th floor. The majority of the Origin Stones of Life were probably excavated by Luo Zheng himself. A portion of them might have fallen into the hands of other martial artists. However, those numbers were definitely not many.
According to Luo Zheng's estimation, the number of Origin Stones of Life in the hands of other martial artists would probably only add up to a hundred. Adding the Origin Stones of Life in Luo Zheng's hands, it would probably only be around three to four hundred. This number was still too far away from Luo Zheng's goal of a thousand.
"Every year, this mining tree will absorb all kinds of ores from unknown places. Every year, it will be completely excavated by the people of the four Divine Kingdoms. Next year, it will grow new leaves. In other words …"
Luo Zheng came to a troublesome conclusion. Just based on this year's production, it was likely that there would only be five to six hundred Origin Stones of Life!
Standing on the giant leaf, Luo Zheng's heart sank. Perhaps he could gather enough Origin Stones of Life to protect Ning Yudie, but his goal was still too far away.
"Huu …"
Luo Zheng let out a long breath. Even so, Luo Zheng could only brace himself and continue to excavate the ores and collect as many Origin Stones of Life as possible.
"Kacha, kacha …"
As Luo Zheng continued to excavate, the number of other ores also increased. However, the number of Origin Stones of Life didn't increase significantly.
From the 30th floor onwards, the Origin Stones of Life became rarer and rarer. 30th floor, 30th floor, 32nd floor …
He continued to excavate layer by layer. The leaf became more and more solid. Luo Zheng could only continuously borrow the power of the dragon scales.
Six hours later, when Luo Zheng went out again, the colorful ores around Yue Ying had already piled up into a small mountain the height of a person …
The ores in the small mountain flickered with all kinds of colors. This was the result of Luo Zheng's random selection. Otherwise, there would be even more.
Of course, there were many precious ores, but there was also a considerable amount of ores that weren't very valuable. After all, Luo Zheng didn't know how to identify ores. He was just randomly excavating.
It was also this small mountain of ores that attracted the attention of half of the Divine Rank geniuses.
"This Luo Tianxing, I suspect that he's a puppet that doesn't know fatigue. This efficiency, tsk tsk, I'm afraid that even a Divine Sea Realm expert wouldn't be as good as him."
"In any case, I have nothing to say. I'll forget about this year. Next year, I'll definitely suggest to Imperial Father not to let him in. If this continues, he can dig up all the ores in the entire mining tree!"
"Ai, I want that Saint Forged Rock, but that damned woman's price is too high. She even said that 50 Origin Life Stones would be enough! I really can't take out 50 Origin Life Stones! Not even a single one … "
Many Divine Rank geniuses were also very envious, but they didn't dare to openly snatch it. This Luo Zheng didn't give face to anyone, even the Great Yu Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince Liu Xiu. If they really snatched it, it would be difficult for them to escape.
Luo Zheng's mining efficiency wasn't any worse than a Divine Sea Realm expert. After borrowing the power of the dragon scale, in terms of pure strength, he was on par with some Divine Sea Realm experts. He was even a lot stronger than some Divine Sea Realm experts.
What's more, when those Divine Sea Realm warriors were mining like this, they had to consume the energy stored in their bodies. Luo Zheng, on the other hand, was directly borrowing the endless energy from the dragon scales. Although the energy from the dragon scales would be consumed, with Luo Zheng's current strength, the consumption of the dragon scales would be much slower than the recovery speed.
This meant that Luo Zheng had an endless source of energy. In this way, who could compete with him in mining efficiency?
Mining efficiency wasn't a problem, the Origin Life Stones were.
"Yueying, how many Origin Life Stones did you exchange?" Luo Zheng walked over and threw a colorful ore into the small mountain. There were at least thousands of ores piled up in this small mountain.
