Chapter 832
"Not much, but I sold a lot of top grade true essence stones. I have over 30,000 top grade true essence stones here …" Yue Ying curled her lips as she spoke. She also knew that Luo Zheng was indifferent towards top grade true essence stones. But, as long as there was even the slightest hint of a martial artist possessing a life source stone, Yue Ying would buy it for a high price. Now, no one really had any life source stones on hand. She had been using some rare ores to tempt these divine rank geniuses, and these divine rank geniuses were just short of borrowing life source stones from others.
But, all the life source stones were with Yue Ying, so who could they borrow them from?
"Oh, I understand." Luo Zheng faintly replied. Then, he climbed up the pyramid once more.
He had already finished exploring the 34th floor. Now, his goal was the 35th floor. Perhaps the higher he went, the fewer life source stones there would be. But, he didn't have a choice.
As Luo Zheng climbed up to the 35th floor of the pyramid, he saw a special narrow door.
All the narrow doors of the pyramid were supported by two gray pillars, forming a narrow passageway that was wide enough for a person. So, only one person could enter at a time.
But, on the 35th floor, there was a narrow door that was different. The pillars of this narrow door were carved with two True Dragons. These two True Dragons had their mouths wide open, glaring at Luo Zheng. They looked very lifelike.
"Why is this narrow door different?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of curiosity.
However, the majority of martial artists were active on the 30th floor and below. As for the divine rank geniuses that were willing to enter the 35th floor, there wasn't a single one. The reason was simple – it was a pure waste of time. They simply couldn't dig up the leaves on the 35th floor. Not only that, but if they encountered the Ore Devouring Bugs, their lives would be in danger. Even if they were green Ore Devouring Bugs, it would still be difficult for them to resist.
Without thinking too much, Luo Zheng decided to personally enter and investigate.
After Luo Zheng stepped through the narrow door, he continued to fall. Soon, he fell onto a giant leaf. But, this leaf was completely different from the others!
The other leaves were transparent and as beautiful as glass. Their entire bodies emitted a deep blue brilliance. The lower the leaf, the whiter it was, and the higher the leaf, the bluer it was. But, the giant leaf beneath Luo Zheng's feet was suffused with a golden brilliance.
"This leaf seems very special." Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of curiosity as he began to carefully size it up.
As he looked, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly widened!
"This is … a pickaxe! What grade is this pickaxe!? "Luo Zheng cried out.
Embedded within this pale golden leaf was a short pickaxe. The pickaxe was pitch black, but the aura it emitted caused Luo Zheng to be inwardly startled. More importantly, there were strange divine runes swirling around the pickaxe's head. And these divine runes …
".... Furthermore, it's three ten-star divine runes! "Luo Zheng cried out once more.
Luo Zheng couldn't tell the difference between the four Divine Kingdoms' Divine Rune Inheritance and the human race's Divine Rune Inheritance. But, the Divine Rune Technique of the Dragon Bloodline Clan was inherited from the True Dragon Clan. Luo Zheng had learned it from Qinglong!
Ten-star Divine Runes, even in the Divine Realm, were the highest grade of Divine Runes. Even when Qinglong was at his peak, he had to spend a considerable amount of energy and time to draw a ten-star Divine Rune. This wasn't even considering the materials required to draw it!
Luo Zheng had relied on three five-star Divine Runes to block the invasion of Life and Death experts. The difference between each star of the Divine Runes was like heaven and earth. What about six-star Divine Runes? Seven-star? Eight-star?
Then, what grade of treasure was this pickaxe with three ten-star Divine Runes?
Luo Zheng couldn't tell at the moment. But, he understood. Since there were three ten-star Divine Runes engraved on this pickaxe, its grade was definitely not low!
"Dig it out?"
This thought uncontrollably jumped out of Luo Zheng's mind.
But, on second thought, since the entrance was so obvious, this leaf must have been visited by many experts. Moreover, most of them were Life and Death experts and Divine Sea experts. Even Divine Sea experts couldn't do anything to this pickaxe, so how could he dig it out?
Although this was what he thought, Luo Zheng still took out the Divine Tool grade pickaxe. Then, he walked towards the pickaxe on the pale golden leaf. After standing still, Luo Zheng used the pickaxe in his hand to lightly knock on the leaf twice.
The pale golden leaf gave out two muffled sounds.
After taking aim, Luo Zheng raised the pickaxe in his hand high and swung it at the leaf with all his might!
"Pa! Bang … Pa da da … "
The first sound was the sound of Luo Zheng's pickaxe hitting the surface of the leaf. The second sound was the sound of the pickaxe in Luo Zheng's hand breaking. The third sound was the sound of the broken pickaxe rolling to the edge of the leaf and falling straight down!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was stunned for a moment. Then, he revealed a trace of a bitter smile.
Although the pickaxe given to Luo Zheng by Yan Wang was a low-grade Divine Tool, after being ravaged by Luo Zheng, it was already somewhat damaged. Now, the hardness of the pale golden leaf under Luo Zheng's feet was simply unimaginable!
According to Luo Zheng's judgment, perhaps the strength of this pale golden leaf was already equivalent to a Divine Tool!
In other words, just by cutting off this pale golden leaf, one could already make a weapon of the same strength as a Divine Tool!
When this low-grade Divine Tool pickaxe was hit, it was like an egg hitting a rock. It instantly broke.
The pickaxe was broken, but there was not even the slightest trace of it left on the leaf …
Looking at the pitch-black pickaxe lying quietly under the leaf, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of unwillingness to admit defeat. The Sumeru Ring in his hand flashed, and he took out the high-grade Divine Tool in his hand.
Swords were known for their sharpness, but they were not suitable for mining. In terms of overall strength, they were far inferior to the pickaxe. Therefore, Luo Zheng drew out the sword and gestured for a while. In the end, he was reluctant to stab it down. The result would most likely be his sword breaking.
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng sighed and jumped down from the pale golden leaf.
When Luo Zheng carried the broken pickaxe and appeared beside the pyramid, Yan Wang asked curiously, "Luo Zheng, you broke the pickaxe!"
Luo Zheng gently waved the broken pickaxe in his hand and smiled helplessly. After all, this pickaxe was lent to him by Yan Wang. Now, he had broken it.
"That's impossible. Even if you're mining on level 35, a Lower Grade Sacred Artifact can't be broken so quickly." King Yan's face was filled with confusion. Although the pickaxe was a Lower Grade Sacred Artifact, its shape was suitable for mining. In particular, the head of the pickaxe was wide and thick. Under normal circumstances, it would be very difficult to break it. King Yan did not blame Luo Zheng. Although it was difficult to obtain a Lower Grade Sacred Artifact, it was still a Lower Grade Sacred Artifact after all.
Luo Zheng could only answer truthfully, "I found a strange thing on the 35th floor."
"Oh!" Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Yan Wang's face suddenly revealed an expression of realization. "You mean the pickaxe in the golden leaf? Don't tell me you want to dig it out? "
That pale golden leaf was not difficult to find. Otherwise, Yan Wang would not know. Luo Zheng nodded immediately.
"Haha, no wonder! Blame me for not reminding you! " Yan Wang said immediately, "That golden leaf can't be broken at all. The pickaxe wrapped in the golden leaf is called 'Black Star'. It is recorded in the literature of the Dragon Bloodline Clan that it is a Supreme Artifact!"
"A Supreme Artifact?" Luo Zheng was stunned. Originally, Luo Zheng thought that the divine tools were the same as the Divine Tools. They were also divided into upper, middle, and lower grades. It seemed that it was not what he had guessed. It was just that Luo Zheng had never come into contact with them before. Now, Qinglong and Red Dragon were also in a deep sleep, and Xun was also immersed in the Soul Cultivation Wood. There was no one to ask him now.

Chapter 833
Starting from the 45th layer, all the leaves would reach an unimaginable level. Even a Divine Sea Realm warrior would not be able to move them. Even someone stronger than a Divine Sea Realm warrior would not be able to break them open.
After conquering the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea, the four Divine Kingdoms obtained many inheritances of the Dragon Bloodline from the four Mystery Realms. From these documents, they understood that only the "Black Star" in the Golden Leaf could open the top layer of the leaf!
The problem was that the Black Star Pickaxe was sealed in the Golden Leaf …
In other words, they could not get the supreme artifact, nor could they open the top layer of the Mineral Tree, nor could they get the "Soul Core". This was a dead knot that could not be opened.
Until someone found the worms that grew on the Mineral Tree. These worms did not have a name. Later, when the Divine Kingdoms found out that these worms ate ores for a living, they called these worms Ore Devouring Worms.
Even a Divine Sea Realm cultivator couldn't dig through the leaves, but they couldn't stop the Ore Devouring Bugs. Therefore, the Divine Kingdom's martial artists shifted their attention away from the Blackstar Pickaxe and focused on the Ore Devouring Bugs. Their efforts paid off. In the end, they obtained the Soul Core and were able to enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm!
Knowing that they could not get the Black Star Pickaxe, everyone in the four Divine Kingdoms had given up. However, Luo Zheng still wanted to take it out.
"So, I can't take it out." Luo Zheng let out a sigh of relief. He was a bit disappointed.
Then, Luo Zheng threw this thought out of his mind. He gathered his courage and went into the narrow door of the pyramid again to look for the Origin Stone of Life.
Although the pickaxe was broken, Luo Zheng's fists were still as strong as a Divine Weapon. He just needed to use a little more strength.
However, in the next two hours, Luo Zheng did not find anything.
In order to find the Origin Stone of Life, Luo Zheng even gave up on digging for other ores. The ores above the 35th layer were all valuable. However, Luo Zheng did not have the patience to waste time and energy here. He kept going back and forth between the pyramid and the Mineral Tree, trying to find the Origin Stone of Life.
From the 35th floor to the 40th floor, the number of leaves on each floor decreased. Luo Zheng shuttled back and forth thousands of times, carefully examining each leaf, but he still did not find a single Origin Stone of Life.
During this time, Luo Zheng also encountered a few green Ore Devouring Bugs, but these Ore Devouring Bugs seemed to have become smarter. As long as Luo Zheng landed on a leaf, these Ore Devouring Bugs would immediately flap their wings and carefully avoid him. Luo Zheng had no chance to catch these bugs.
Luo Zheng's heart sank, and his brows furrowed tightly. "I'm afraid this is going to be troublesome.
In this way, it would be a problem whether he could gather 500 Origin Stones of Life. Thinking of this, Luo Zheng didn't do anything. Instead, he directly climbed to the 40th floor of the pyramid!
"Luo Zheng!" Seeing that Luo Zheng was about to enter the 40th floor, King Yan couldn't help but stop him. "Above the 40th floor, only the mighty warriors of the Divine Sea Realm can enter. If you go in, I'm afraid …"
The purple Ore Devouring Bugs on the 40th floor were ten times more powerful than the green Ore Devouring Bugs! Even the top warriors of the Life and Death Realm would find it difficult to deal with them, let alone Luo Zheng entering the 40th floor. So what? It was impossible for him to dig out the leaves on the 40th floor.
"That guy is courting death. Just let him go in! Every ore on the 40th floor is priceless. As long as he can dig out one ore, he will make a fortune! "
"He can even injure Battle God Yu the Great. Hehe, maybe he can really dig out the ore on the 40th floor!"
Some of the Deity rank geniuses could no longer hold themselves back. Luo Zheng was slowly climbing up the ranks, and in their eyes, this was a blatant show off! After all, for these god-level geniuses of the False Tribulation Realm, going above the 40th floor was almost like a forbidden area.
Why would Luo Zheng care about these words? He just said to King Yan, "I'm just going in to have a look. Don't worry, I will be careful!"
Even if he encountered purple Ore Devouring Bugs on the 40th floor, which were equivalent to Level 11 Ferocious Beasts and comparable to Divine Sea Realm warriors, as long as the purple Ore Devouring Bugs couldn't use the power of the Realm and cut off his connection with the Vast Thousand World, they couldn't kill Luo Zheng.
After all, he could use "Force Transfer" to transfer the attack on his body to another place.
Luo Zheng also knew that the Origin Stones of Life were so rare on the 30th floor, so the probability of finding one on the 40th floor was even lower. However, he had no other choice.
After stepping through the narrow door, Luo Zheng instantly fell onto the leaves on the 40th floor!
Stepping on the leaves on the 40th floor, Luo Zheng glanced around. There were no legendary purple Ore Devouring Bugs on the leaves in front of him, but there were six of them on the leaves next to it!
The purple Ore Devouring Bugs were twice the size of the green ones, and there were two more pairs of compound eyes on their heads. A gloomy and cold Qi Power was emitted from their heads.
The six purple Ore Devouring Bugs had their heads on the leaves. It seemed that they had just dug out the ores in the leaves and were swallowing the ores with the mouthparts on their faces. Their huge purple abdomens were raised high.
The sound of Luo Zheng falling on the leaves alarmed one of the purple Ore Devouring Bugs. The purple Ore Devouring Bug lowered its abdomen, and its body was like a lever. Its head was raised high, and the feelers on its head swayed slightly. It turned its head and looked at Luo Zheng with its four black compound eyes.
Although Luo Zheng was confident that he could escape from the purple Ore Devouring Bugs, he would not challenge them for no reason. With his strength, he might not be able to cut open the outer shell of the purple Ore Devouring Bugs.
He saw that the purple Ore Devouring Bugs had noticed him, and they quickly swept their eyes over the leaves under his feet. The ores in the leaves were almost the size of a human head. Some of the ores faintly transmitted various mysterious power of law. Even if Luo Zheng did not know the type and value of the ores, he knew that these ores were destined to be valuable!
"There are no Origin Stones of Life." Luo Zheng had already expected this result. Since there were no ores on the 35th floor, there would be no ores on the 40th floor! It was just that he did not give up.
"Squeak — —"
The purple Ore Devouring Bug on the leaves suddenly made a sharp sound. Then it waved its slender and sharp forelegs and pointed at Luo Zheng.
The other five purple Ore Devouring Bugs, who were still lowering their heads to swallow the ores, suddenly stopped. Then, with a "boom", they flew up and turned into five purple insect shadows, surrounding Luo Zheng!
Five purple Ore Devouring Bugs. This thing's strength could be ranked as a Level 11 Vicious Beast, which was equivalent to five Divine Sea Realm experts!
Luo Zheng did not feel the power of a Territory from these bugs, but his face changed greatly. Almost without thinking, he turned over and directly jumped out from the leaves!
"Squeak squeak squeak …"
The moment Luo Zheng jumped out, the purple Ore Devouring Bug flying at the front was only three feet away from Luo Zheng. It did not use its sharp forelegs to attack Luo Zheng, but wanted to use the slender legs under its abdomen to capture Luo Zheng.
Once Luo Zheng was captured by these purple Ore Devouring Bugs, Luo Zheng would be attacked by these purple Ore Devouring Bugs. The consequences would be unimaginable!
"Capture me! In your dreams! "
Luo Zheng twisted his waist in the air. A cold light flashed in his hand, and the long sword was already slashed out!
"Ding, ding!"
The originally invincible Upper Grade Holy Weapon did not have any effect at this moment! The slender legs of the purple Ore Devouring Bug were only as thick as an arm, but when the Upper Grade Holy Weapon hit them, it only made a crisp sound and some sparks.
"This bug's armor is so hard?" Luo Zheng's face was filled with astonishment. However, with the reverse thrust of the two swords, Luo Zheng continued to fall down!
The speed of his fall was extremely fast. After all, in addition to the pushing force, there was also a strong gravitational force pulling him down. However, the purple Ores Devouring Bugs were still chasing after him. They actually swooped down towards Luo Zheng!
"Whoosh!"

Chapter 834
Level 11 vicious beasts were indeed much more powerful than Luo Zheng had imagined! At such a high speed, he managed to get rid of the Purple Ore Devouring Bugs and fell straight to the bottom of the halo. At the moment of falling, Luo Zheng was still worried that the Purple Ore Devouring Bugs would not chase after him, right? If they did, how would the many Void Tribulation Realm martial artists outside resist?
However, the purple Ore Devouring Bugs were unable to chase after Luo Zheng. They discovered that there were still some humans mining in the sacred tree. Like a whirlwind, they rushed towards those humans!
At this moment, Huo Chen was concentrating on mining on the 27th floor. Suddenly, he heard some commotion. When he looked up, his expression changed greatly.
"This, what the hell is this!" Huo Chen stared blankly at the huge purple Ore Devouring Bugs.
"Ah! Save me, save me!" Not far from Huo Chen, a divine rank genius was caught by a purple Ore Devouring Bug. The purple Ore Devouring Bug threw the divine rank genius into the air …
As long as a martial artist left the leaf, they would be pulled in by a huge gravitational force. However, when the purple Ore Devouring Bug threw him, it forcefully counteracted the gravitational force. One could imagine how strong the purple Ore Devouring Bug was.
"Save …" The martial artist could only instinctively shout for help. He did not know how to resist.
After the martial artist was thrown into the air, the other purple Ore Devouring Bugs surrounded him. They stretched out their slender legs and began to tear the martial artist apart!
The Ore Devouring Bugs lived by eating the ores on the mining tree. They did not eat the flesh of humans. Therefore, by tearing, they were purely torturing the martial artist.
How could the body of a Void Tribulation Realm martial artist withstand the tearing of a level 11 vicious beast? The martial artist's screams for help came to an abrupt end. Then, he was torn into pieces. The pieces of his corpse fell towards the bottom of the mining tree …
When the other martial artists, including Huo Chen and the others, saw this scene, their faces turned green. At this time, even a fool would know that they had to run for their lives. All of them desperately rushed out of the leaf and swooped down.
Outside the pyramid, most of the martial artists were still unaware of what had happened in the mining tree. Most of them were still trading their ores or bargaining with Yue Ying …
The number of top grade true essence stones in Yue Ying's hands had already reached 70,000 to 80,000!
With so many top grade True Essence Stones, even for a Divine Kingdom Crown Prince, this was not a small sum of money. Even in the four Divine Kingdoms' national treasuries, there were usually only two to three hundred thousand top grade True Essence Stones in reserve. But in these short four to five hours, Yue Ying had already earned seventy to eighty thousand top grade True Essence Stones.
If it was in the past, after earning so many top-grade true essence stones, Yue Ying would probably be overjoyed. Even if she only took a commission, it would still be a huge sum of money for her!
However, what made her depressed was that she hadn't been able to help Luo Zheng obtain too many Life Source Stones. It seemed that the number of Life Source Stones she had obtained from the exchange was far below Luo Zheng's expectations. However, the mining tree could only produce so many Life Source Stones in a year.
.....................................
........
. The ripples from the halo on one side of the pyramid had yet to completely disperse, but a large crater had already appeared. Luo Zheng was lying in the middle of the crater.
"Huh? What happened to Brother Tianxing?" Yue Ying blinked her eyes and stared at Luo Zheng in the crater.
Luo Zheng had almost used his fastest speed to get rid of the purple Ore Devouring Bugs. With this speed, he was unable to stop himself, so he smashed a large crater in the ground.
Of course, with Luo Zheng's saint tool body, he would not be injured. However, under such an impact, he felt his blood tumble.
Everyone watched as Luo Zheng climbed out of the crater. Then, countless halos began to flicker above Luo Zheng's head. Then, bloody chunks of corpses fell down like rain from the densely packed halos.
Luo Zheng turned his head and saw this scene. His face revealed a trace of helplessness. Then, the densely packed halos continued to flicker. One by one, the god-grade geniuses with pale faces drilled out of the halos.
Only a small number of the god-grade geniuses came out standing. Most of the god-grade geniuses were smashed out directly. They did not have a body as strong as Luo Zheng's, so they all fell unbearably.
"What, what happened?!"
"The interior of the pyramid shouldn't be so dangerous. Why does it seem like everyone is running for their lives?"
"Is it the Ore Devouring Bugs? The purple Ore Devouring Bugs came down? "
The god-grade geniuses trading outside were discussing spiritedly. Some of them had already guessed the truth.
"Luo Tianxing!" A prince of the Star Wilderness Divine Kingdom climbed out of the crater. This prince's face was pale, and there was a trace of blood at the corner of his mouth. He roared at Luo Zheng in anger, "You actually led the purple Ore Devouring Bugs to the lower level!"
Hearing the prince's words, the god-grade geniuses outside the pyramid immediately understood what had happened.
Even the green Ore Devouring Bugs were difficult for the god-grade geniuses to deal with. Only a small number of first-tier god-grade geniuses, such as Luo Zheng and Liu Xiu, dared to provoke them. However, Luo Zheng could kill the green Ore Devouring Bugs, while Liu Xiu and the others could not.
The purple Ore Devouring Bugs were much more powerful than the green ones. Not to mention False Tribulation Realm experts, even False Tribulation Realm experts would be afraid to avoid them.
The habits of these big bugs were very regular. Generally, green Ore Devouring Bugs would not enter the 30th floor and below, while purple Ore Devouring Bugs would not easily enter the 40th floor and below. Just now, everyone had seen Luo Zheng enter the 40th floor with their own eyes. Hearing the prince's words, everyone was certain that Luo Zheng had lured the purple Ore Devouring Bugs down.
Luo Zheng himself did not want this to happen. Facing the prince's accusation, Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all. He said, "I didn't do it on purpose."
"Humph, you caused the death of someone, and you think you can just say that you didn't do it on purpose?" The prince was still unconvinced.
Luo Zheng shrugged. He could not predict the situation, but even so, he did not intend to change his decision. He would search the entire mining tree until he could not find any more Life Origin Stones.
Seeing Luo Zheng ignore him, the prince was even more furious. However, he could not do anything to Luo Zheng. He could only say, "Everyone, don't go mining! Who knows if the purple Ore Devouring Bugs have flown to the upper floors! "
These god-grade geniuses could not escape the purple Ore Devouring Bugs. If they were unlucky and encountered another purple Ore Devouring Bug, they would die.
"Alas, there's no other way. Let's go pick up the trash. Maybe we can find something good!"
"I don't want to mine, I'll go pick up the trash too!"
"Let's go, it's better than losing our lives here!"
Many warriors said indignantly. Then, they went around the pyramid in groups of three to five and walked in another direction.
King Yan walked over with a bitter smile and asked, "Did you really lure the purple Ore Devouring Bugs down?"
Luo Zheng nodded. There was nothing he could not admit.
"How many?" King Yan did not care about the lives of the other warriors. Moreover, the dead warriors were not from the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. The god-grade genius who died could only blame himself for his bad luck.
"I think there were five …" Luo Zheng replied.
"…"
Luo Zheng was able to lure five purple Ore Devouring Bugs down at once. The problem was that this guy could escape unscathed. King Yan did not know what to say.
"I advise you not to go in. The 40th floor is too dangerous. If you are besieged by five or six purple Ore Devouring Bugs, I'm afraid you won't be able to escape!" King Yan advised.
Luo Zheng shook his head slightly. There was no change in his expression. No matter how dangerous the mining tree was, Luo Zheng had to go in!
King Yan knew that Luo Zheng was stubborn. Since he could not persuade him, he did not say anything more.
"What do they mean by picking up the trash?" Luo Zheng asked King Yan when he heard the god-grade geniuses mention this.
"Picking up the trash?" King Yan smiled. "Picking up the trash means going to the armory to pick up weapons that are not cursed. This place is called the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm. It was originally the mine of the Dragon Bloodline Clan. All the blacksmiths of the Dragon Bloodline Clan used to refine weapons on this island. The most famous blacksmith of the Dragon Bloodline Clan was called the Feather Emperor."
