Chapter 835
The name of the Feather Emperor Mystic Realm came from the fact that this island had once given birth to an Emperor Weapon Refiner. The entire Feather Emperor Island was a collection of mineral mining, weapon refining, and so on. It could be considered as an arsenal of the Dragon Bloodline Clan.
When the entire Dragon Bloodline was attacked, the Dragon Bloodline people on the Feather Emperor Island were unable to destroy the numerous weapons. However, they were unwilling to let the weapons fall into the hands of the Human Race. Therefore, they cast an irreversible curse on the weapons!
The curse could not be removed, and the cursed weapons could not be used. Therefore, in the eyes of these god-level geniuses, the weapons became trash.
However, the number of these weapons was simply too large. From the lowest level of Xuan Weapons to Spirit Weapons, Immortal Weapons, and Sacred Weapons, there were probably millions of them. There were bound to be some missing weapons that were not tainted by the curse.
If one could find one or two Sacred Artifacts among them, it would be a huge profit.
However, the probability of finding an uncursed weapon among a large pile of cursed weapons was not high. In the eyes of these god-level geniuses, it became "picking up trash."
In fact, a portion of the god-level geniuses had given up mining at the beginning and went to the arsenal to "pick up trash." However, most of the warriors still thought that mining was more cost-effective. However, now that Luo Zheng had lured the purple Ore Devouring Bugs down, no one was willing to go in. Although mining was good, no one was willing to throw away their lives.
"I see." Luo Zheng nodded. He was not interested in picking up trash, so he decided to enter the mining tree.
Huo Chen and Huo Yuner did not go to pick up trash. In fact, Liu Xiu, Zhou Ning, and the other princes of the Divine Kingdom did not go in either. At their position, other than semi-divine and divine artifacts, even high-grade Sacred Artifacts were not attractive.
However, they did not choose to enter the pyramid. The warriors who stayed behind all looked at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not care how the others looked at him. However, when he lured the Ore Devouring Bugs to the lower level, it scared Huo Chen and Huo Yuner out. Therefore, Luo Zheng said, "If those Ore Devouring Bugs return to the upper level, I will come out and inform you."
Huo Yuner was not very interested in mining. She had only entered three times. Most of the time, she sat in a corner of the pyramid and watched Luo Zheng go in and out.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Huo Yuner also wanted to persuade Luo Zheng to go in again. However, after thinking for a while, she didn't say anything. Like Prince of Yan, she also knew that she couldn't persuade Luo Zheng.
However, when Liu Xiu and Zhou Ning heard Luo Zheng's words, they were a bit happy. After all, there was still some time before the mighty warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm arrived. Since they didn't want to "pick up trash", they naturally didn't want to waste this precious time.
After that, Luo Zheng did not choose the narrow door on the 40th floor this time. Instead, he chose the narrow door on the 41st floor.
This time, after entering the narrow door, Luo Zheng didn't encounter any purple Ore Devouring Bugs. This Ore Tree had 100,000 leaves. Luo Zheng stood on the 41st floor, which was probably the top.
On the 41st floor, there were only 200 leaves. On the 42nd floor, there were only 150 leaves. On the 43rd floor, there were 100 leaves. On the 44th floor, there were only 60 leaves.
On the 45th floor, there were 10 leaves …
On the 46th floor, there were 6 leaves …
On the 47th floor, there were 3 leaves …
On the 41st floor, Luo Zheng still didn't find any Origin Stones of Life. He didn't have much hope now. After glancing around, he came to the edge of the leaves and swept the traces of the purple Ore Devouring Bugs.
"There's one on the left …"
"On the 43rd floor, there's one …"
"Five of them, there!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He saw that the five purple Ore Devouring Bugs had returned to the 40th floor, which was to say, they had returned to their original positions!
After letting out a sigh of relief, he jumped down again and entered the light circle at the bottom. He sent the message that the purple Ore Devouring Bugs had returned to the 40th floor. Then, Luo Zheng went straight to the 42nd floor.
Even though he didn't have any hope of finding any Origin Stones of Life, Luo Zheng still carefully scanned around. When he found that there were no leaves on the 42nd floor, he continued to jump down and then entered the 13th floor.
On the 44th floor …
On the 45th floor …
Huo Chen had already entered the pyramid to start mining again. His talent wasn't outstanding among the Crown Princes, but he was the most hardworking one.
Huo Yuner sat in a corner of the pyramid, watching Luo Zheng enter and exit the pyramid …
"Who is he collecting Origin Stones of Life for?" Huo Yuner guessed in her heart. The corner of her mouth curved up slightly. Her eyes flashed slightly. At this moment, she saw Luo Zheng enter the 45th floor …
The 45th floor?
Huo Yuner blinked her eyes. Even though she didn't know anything about the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm, she now knew that from the 45th floor to the 47th floor, there was no need to enter the last three floors.
The leaves in the last three floors couldn't be dug out at all. For so many years, the four Divine Sea Realm experts of the four Divine Kingdoms had used all kinds of methods, but they still couldn't dig out anything from the 45th floor and above. It was because they couldn't dig it out at all!
Not to mention that there were no Origin Stones of Life in the last three floors, even if there were, they could only stare at them.
How could Luo Zheng not understand this? But he still chose to enter the 45th floor, the floor that was known as the level that couldn't be dug out!
The moment Luo Zheng entered the 45th floor, he smelled a faint fragrance.
"Strange. I can't smell this fragrance on the 44th floor. I don't know where this fragrance is coming from!" Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression. Then, he looked up and saw six huge leaves above his head.
In the gap, Luo Zheng saw three more leaves. The six leaves were the 46th floor, and the three leaves were the 47th floor, which was the top floor!
In the center of the three leaves, there was a blooming flower!
"The fragrance is coming from that flower! But the shape of the flower … "Luo Zheng's eyes flickered, and his face revealed a surprised expression.
That flower didn't seem to be a product of nature. Instead, it seemed to be a realm of minerals. Whether it was the petals, the stamen, or the stem of the flower, it seemed to be made up of all kinds of minerals, which was why it looked sharp and angular at first glance.
Because of this, the flower gave people a very symmetrical sense of beauty.
After staring at it for a while, Luo Zheng withdrew his gaze. Although he was instinctively reluctant, he could only sigh at the wonders of the Creator. He still had more important things to do …
But this time, when he lowered his head, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly bloomed with endless colors. "This, this isn't … the Origin Stone of Life!"
Under Luo Zheng's feet, there were fourteen round egg-sized stones circling together. At first glance, they were very similar to the Origin Stone of Life!
They were the same size, the same color. Moreover, Luo Zheng had excavated so many of them. Every time the Origin Stone of Life appeared, it was in a circle. Not a single Origin Stone of Life appeared alone. Moreover, it was always in an even number, ten, twelve, fourteen … The Origin Stone of Life should be a kind of cluster of minerals. Every time it appeared, it would appear in clusters.
The circle of ores in front of him was the same, but the surface of this circle of ores was in a translucent state. Through the transparent leaves and the translucent shell, Luo Zheng saw that there were grains of crystals constantly floating inside.
"This is also the power of life! What a rich power of life! "Luo Zheng's eyes looked carefully, and he felt it even more deeply! Even through the thick layer of leaves, Luo Zheng could feel the power of life within!
After a Origin Stone of Life was crushed, there would only be a trace of power of life. The power of life it contained was too little.
But these stones in front of him were not ordinary. Any one of them contained a thousand times more power of life than the Origin Stone of Life. In other words, only one of them was needed to completely restore Ning Yudie!
Seeing these small stones, Luo Zheng once again sighed at the magic of the Creator. At the same time, his mood was unusually excited.
"One, I only need one! As long as I can take out one, that's enough! "Luo Zheng's eyes stared unwaveringly at his feet.
The problem was that from the ten leaves under Luo Zheng's feet to the six leaves above Luo Zheng's head, and then to the three leaves at the top, even the four Divine Sea Realm experts of the four Divine Kingdoms had not been able to dig out these three leaves even once. Moreover, it was very likely that the four Divine Kingdoms had asked for help from the human race of the Upper Realm through the reverse spirit channel, but they still could not do anything about these nineteen leaves.

Chapter 836
Looking at the round stones under his feet, Luo Zheng's mind was in turmoil. His mind was constantly thinking about how he should take these stones out.
"Let's try it once!"
Luo Zheng took two steps back and tightened his legs. He began to circulate his true essence and vital essence at the same time. At the same time, the power of the dragon scales continuously surged into Luo Zheng's body. At this moment, he had already drawn out all the power of the dragon scales!
With the current strength of Luo Zheng's body, the power of the dragon scales wouldn't put too much of a burden on Luo Zheng's body.
In addition to this, Luo Zheng also closed his eyes and began to communicate with the stars.
"Two stars …"
The moment Luo Zheng closed his eyes and began to communicate with the stars, the light of two stars suddenly flashed in the sky above the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm!
The rich power of the stars began to seep into Luo Zheng's true essence.
Then, Luo Zheng leaped up!
On the leaf, Luo Zheng wasn't affected by the strong gravitational force. When he leaped up, his body rose to a height of one zhang. After circling in the air, he dived down toward the leaf below.
His huge fist heavily pounded on the leaf.
Luo Zheng used all the strength in his body for this punch. The power of this punch couldn't be underestimated. It was even more powerful than the punch that injured Battle Emperor Yu the Great. Moreover, he also used the Stellar Battle Body for this punch!
"Boom!"
When Luo Zheng's punch hit the leaf, a violent sound suddenly erupted. This sound was like thunder, and the entire ore tree could hear it clearly.
Below, several Ore-Devouring Bugs were frightened. They even spread their wings and flew in the air!
Luo Zheng's fist was tightly pressed against the leaf. Below his fist, traces of blood began to seep out, flowing along the leaf below …
Even though Luo Zheng had the body of a sacred tool, the leaf under his feet was much stronger than a sacred tool. When Luo Zheng smashed it, it was like an egg hitting a rock. Not only did the leaf not crack, but his fist was also injured.
"The last three layers of leaves are even stronger than the golden leaf below. No wonder those Divine Sea Realm experts couldn't take out the ores." Luo Zheng didn't look too disappointed. He just wanted to test this fist.
The pickaxe …
If he could get his hands on the Black Star Pickaxe, he should be able to dig out the leaf at the top layer. However, the people of the four Divine Kingdoms couldn't open the golden leaf that wrapped the pickaxe, and Luo Zheng couldn't open it either.
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng left the 45th layer of the leaf.
This time, when he was falling, two purple Ore-Devouring Bugs on the 41st and 43rd layers noticed Luo Zheng. They wanted to chase after Luo Zheng. However, because Luo Zheng was too far away and falling too fast, the two Ore-Devouring Bugs returned to the leaf after realizing that they couldn't chase after him.
While falling along these leaves, Luo Zheng was still thinking.
With his current strength, Luo Zheng indeed couldn't open the golden leaf. However, compared to the leaf on the 45th layer, it was still much easier. In other words, Luo Zheng's last chance was still the Black Star Pickaxe. If he could get his hands on this supreme artifact, Luo Zheng would be able to open the leaf on the top layer as well.
If he couldn't do it alone, what about several people? If several people gathered on one leaf and attacked it at the same time …
However, this idea was soon rejected.
Outside the pyramid, King of Yan told Luo Zheng that only one warrior could enter through a narrow door. If several warriors entered through the same narrow door, only the first warrior would land on the leaf accurately. The others would be "squeezed" down automatically. It was impossible for them to climb up the leaf.
Luo Zheng wasn't surprised by King of Yan's answer. If he could think of something, how could those Divine Sea Realm warriors not think of it?
For example, if several Divine Sea Realm warriors were to attack one leaf from several connected leaves … all these methods would be restricted by the rules of the mining tree. It wasn't something that warriors at their level could resist.
To get the Black Star Pickaxe, they had to break it with their own strength!
Standing at the foot of the pyramid, Luo Zheng gazed at the top of the pyramid indifferently. However, he kept repeating two words, "Strength …"
Strength … more strength …
Right at this moment, more than a dozen warriors turned back from the left side of the pyramid. They were murmuring to themselves.
"Motherf * cker, all the trash in the armory has been picked up over the years! Where can we find more? Why don't we just come back and mine? "
"I've only found two Spiritual Weapons after searching for so long. Damn, they're low-grade Spiritual Weapons!"
"I wonder if those Ore-Eating Bugs have gone back. If they have, we can continue mining in the mining tree!"
The dozen or so cultivators were god-ranked geniuses who had gone to 'pick up trash'. Although there were many weapons in the armory, it still required a certain amount of luck to pick out an uncursed weapon. Not to mention, there were many cultivators who went to pick up trash every year. The number of uncursed weapons was rapidly decreasing, and the chances of picking up a Saint artifact were getting smaller and smaller.
Weapons? Weapons? Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
As long as a weapon was not damaged, the Heaven's Derivation Essence in it would not dissipate and return to heaven and earth …
I can refine all those weapons!
Luo Zheng's heart started pounding when he thought of this.
Previously, Luo Zheng had not been in a hurry to absorb the Heaven's Derivation Essence. His body could not withstand too much power. His Immortal Weapon Body was already unstable under the power of a thousand dragon scales.
Now that Luo Zheng had broken through to the Immortal Weapon Body, Luo Zheng's strength could be increased by several levels with his Divine Weapon Body!
Luo Zheng might not be able to break open the golden leaf or take the Black Star Pickaxe with the power of a thousand dragon scales.
But … what about five thousand dragon scales? Ten thousand dragon scales!
Even if Luo Zheng couldn't do it, he would still try his best. Now that there was a plan in front of him that had a high possibility of being realized, he certainly wouldn't let go of this opportunity.
The only problem was whether the cursed weapons still contained the Heaven's Derivation Essence. Would the curse disappear because of the Heaven's Derivation Essence?
He would know after taking a look. Luo Zheng made up his mind and said to King Yan, "King Yan, I also want to go and pick up the trash …"
Yue Ying was still worried about the pile of ores. When she heard that Luo Zheng wanted to pick up the trash, she was puzzled. "Why do you want to pick up the trash …? Even if you are lucky, you can only find one or two Divine Weapons among the cursed weapons. You might as well mine."
Just the ores piled up like a mountain beside Yue Ying, if they were to sell them in the Four Great Divine Kingdoms, they would be able to purchase five or six saint artifacts.
"Yue Ying is right. With your strength, there is no need to pick up the trash." King Yan did not agree, but for some reason, he had a premonition that Luo Zheng did not go to the armory to pick up the trash.
"It's okay. I am just curious. I will definitely come back to mine later!" Luo Zheng replied with a smile. As he said this, Luo Zheng raised his head slightly and met Huo Yuner's gaze.
Although Luo Zheng went in and out of the pyramid, how sharp were his senses? He could naturally feel Huo Yuner's gaze on him.
As their eyes met, Huo Yuner suddenly revealed an elegant and gentle smile. She jumped down from the pyramid. Because of the suppression of the power of blood sacrifice, no one could fly, but Huo Yuner was able to release a fiery red shield of true essence. She glided several dozen feet and arrived in front of Luo Zheng.
"I will go with you to take a look!" Huo Yuner said to Luo Zheng with a smile.
Huo Yuner's purpose of exploring the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea and entering the Feather Emperor Mystic Realm was not for the ores and treasures, but for training.

Chapter 837
When they passed by Huo Yuner and Luo Zheng, some warriors couldn't help gossiping and whispering.
"What do you think is going on with Princess Huo of the Tianfeng Divine Nation? Why does she always follow Luo Zheng around? "
"Who would've thought that she would even lend out her Quasi God Weapon at the West Dragon River …"
"Luo Zheng really has good luck with women. If Huo Yuner likes me, it'll be worth it even if I die!"
Those god-grade geniuses weren't talking about Luo Zheng, but about Huo Yuner. They didn't let Luo Zheng and Huo Yuner hear them.
Walking along this straight road for about two or three miles, a continuous wall appeared in front of them. When Luo Zheng and Huo Yuner walked in, they found that the fence was actually a dragon-shaped sculpture.
The dragon's long body made a circle, and its head and tail were the pillars of the armory's main entrance.
After entering the dragon-shaped fence, there were continuous warehouses inside. Some god-grade geniuses were still coming out of those warehouses.
"Those weapons should be displayed in these warehouses." Huo Yuner took the lead and walked into one of the warehouses after taking a glance.
After entering, Luo Zheng's eyes swept over the big boxes in the warehouse.
These boxes were all made of pure copper. The surface of the boxes had been specially treated, so there was no green rust. They still shone with a brass luster.
Most of the copper chests had been opened, and many weapons had fallen out of the chests, scattered on the ground.
"The surface of these weapons …"
At first glance, these weapons were all new and shiny, as if no one had used them before.
But if one looked carefully, they would be able to see that there was a faint black smoke emitting from these weapons …
"What a strong curse power." Huo Yuner slowly walked over and picked up a weapon. It was a middle-grade immortal tool, an exquisite short spear. She shook it lightly, and a little black smoke escaped from the body of the spear, visible to the naked eye.
Luo Zheng didn't care about the curse power in the weapon. He was more concerned about whether he could extract the Heaven's Divination essence from it.
While Huo Yuner was sizing up the long spear, Luo Zheng had already walked into the depths of the warehouse. On the left and right sides of the warehouse, there were copper boxes neatly placed. Each copper box had been opened, and the originally neatly arranged weapons were thrown inside in a mess.
These were the leftovers of the god-level geniuses who were "picking up trash".
"I can't get rid of this curse power, but if I can extract the Heaven's Divination Essence from it, it will be enough for me." Luo Zheng picked out a large saber from a copper box. This saber should be the standard equipment of the Dragon Bloodline Clan. Although it was only a mid-grade immortal artifact, its workmanship was extraordinary.
Luo Zheng glanced around and saw that Huo Yuner didn't follow him. He gently closed his eyes and began to communicate with the mysterious furnace in his mind.
As the mysterious furnace slowly rotated, a black flame shot out from the furnace.
Luo Zheng was already familiar with using the black flame to refine weapons. The black flame was very intelligent. After shooting out from between Luo Zheng's eyebrows, it circled slightly in the air and then gently wrapped around the large saber.
The large saber embedded in the shaft instantly melted into molten iron. Then, the molten iron dissolved and finally condensed into a ball of Heaven's Divination Essence!
After all, it was a mid-grade immortal artifact. The amount of Heaven's Divination Essence that could be refined into a Profound Artifact and a Spiritual Artifact was much more than a hundred drops. This ball of Heaven's Divination Essence was enough for Luo Zheng to light up 20 dragon scales.
However, Luo Zheng wasn't in a hurry to absorb the Heaven's Divination Essence. This was because he saw a faint black smoke on top of the Heaven's Divination Essence.
"The black flame can refine all weapons and Dharma treasures, but it can't burn away the power of the curse." Luo Zheng frowned. This wasn't good news for Luo Zheng.
So many weapons hadn't been taken away by the four Divine Kingdoms because these weapons were cursed. The power of the curse might be difficult to remove!
If he absorbed the Heaven's Divination Essence, would it cause negative effects?
Looking at the ball of Heaven's Divination Essence emitting black smoke, Luo Zheng's face revealed a hesitant expression.
In fact, Luo Zheng had a faint idea. Qinglong had once said that if he could obtain a large amount of Heaven's Divination Essence, it would be enough to help Qinglong build a Heaven's Divination Body. It was equivalent to helping Qinglong reconstruct a body.
There were countless weapons left in this armory. Most of them were Spiritual Artifacts and Immortal Artifacts. There were even many Sacred Artifacts!
Just the Immortal Artifacts alone were enough to refine a large amount of Heaven's Divination Essence. A Sacred Artifact contained a large amount of Heaven's Divination Essence. If he absorbed all of these weapons, perhaps Luo Zheng could help Qinglong reconstruct a body!
This Heaven's Divination Body wouldn't be too large. It couldn't be restored to the length of Qinglong's body. However, it would be about the size of an ordinary flood dragon.
Even so, it would be enough to help Luo Zheng. It would be a great help to him for the time being.
Now, the problem was the curse here …
"Let's try it first!"
Luo Zheng's Sacred Artifact Body was impervious to poison. However, this black smoke wasn't poison. It was a curse. He wasn't sure if he could withstand it.
Then, the Heaven's Divination Essence entered Luo Zheng's glabella.
He had indeed absorbed the Heaven's Divination essence. Above the mysterious furnace, more than twenty dragon scales on the surface of the Azure Dragon's body were lit up. Luo Zheng did not seem to feel anything out of the ordinary …
"I'm fine?" Luo Zheng revealed a relaxed smile. Perhaps he was worrying too much.
The black fire continued to churn and flew directly into one of the copper boxes. Inside this huge copper box, there were more than a hundred middle-grade Immortal Artifact sabers. As the black fire continued to sway, the sabers were like soft candles that were quickly melted by the black fire.
At the same time, the Heaven's Divination Essence rapidly flowed down. Drop by drop, they absorbed each other and sparkled like flowing mercury, forming a huge ball of Heaven's Divination Essence.
A single saber could produce more than a hundred drops of Heaven's Divination Essence. The sabers in this huge copper box could produce more than ten thousand drops of Heaven's Divination Essence! It was enough to light up two thousand dragon scales.
In the past, Luo Zheng had to pay for the weapons he smelted himself …
Now that Luo Zheng was sitting in this armory, there were middle-grade Immortal Artifacts everywhere. In other warehouses, there might be a sea of high-grade Immortal Artifacts or even Saint Artifacts!
It was as if Luo Zheng was sitting on a mountain of treasures, or a little bird flying into a huge granary. Luo Zheng began to devour the Heaven's Divination Essence without restraint.
After smelting the large blade in one of the large copper boxes, the wisp of black flame gently floated and directly drilled into another large copper box. It directly drilled a small hole in the two inch thick copper box. After which, it quickly began to smelt the blade within.
The weapons in this copper box were Wolf Fang Hammers. Although they were both middle-grade Immortal Artifacts like the sabers, the Wolf Fang Hammers were heavier and made with more materials. Therefore, each Wolf Fang Hammer produced more Heaven's Divination Essence!
One box, two boxes, three boxes, four boxes, five boxes …
The middle-grade Immortal Artifacts in these copper boxes were all burned up by the black fire! In just a few hours, Luo Zheng had absorbed more than sixty to seventy thousand drops of Heaven's Divination Essence.
Qinglong's dragon scales were lit up one by one. Looking at the large green dragon scales shining with a green light, Luo Zheng smiled again. Qinglong was still in a deep sleep. After helping him block the little monster's attack, it and the red dragon seemed to be too tired and hadn't woken up yet. Luo Zheng wondered if it would be shocked when it woke up.
"I've lit up a total of fourteen thousand dragon scales …"
The power of a thousand dragon scales was almost equivalent to the full force of a Divine Sea Realm cultivator's physical body.
What if it was the power of these fourteen thousand dragon scales? The power would be unimaginable. But even with Luo Zheng's current Saint Artifact body, it would be difficult for him to withstand it.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 838
Even if a Divine Sea Realm expert used all of his strength and activated his Protective Zhen Yuan, he still wouldn't be able to withstand one punch from Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng still couldn't fight against a Divine Sea Realm expert. After all, a Divine Sea Realm expert was not only powerful in terms of strength, but also in terms of speed, martial arts, cultivation methods, and comprehension of laws. All of these were better than Luo Zheng's.
Luo Zheng's target was the Golden Leaf. He wanted to use his own strength to smash open the Black Star Pickaxe inside the Golden Leaf!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng wanted to test the limits of his body.
Therefore, he tried to borrow the power of the dragon scale. However, just as he was about to pour the power of the dragon scale into his body, Huo Yuner's voice came from not far away.
"Luo Tianxing? Where are you? "
Huo Yuner entered the warehouse with Luo Zheng. She was also looking around at these weapons. Looking at the power of curses on them, Huo Yuner was thinking if there was any way to purify the power of curses on these weapons.
Among these Origin Source Laws, some Origin Source Laws would have the effect of returning to the same destination when cultivated to a high level.
For example, the Law of Space, the Law of Thunder, and the Law of Fire all had a similar effect, which was the purification effect.
It was just that the principle behind each purification was slightly different. The Law of Lightning could produce countless tiny bolts of lightning to purify a target, but the Law of Fire was different. The Law of Fire's' purification 'was not so much' purification 'as' incinerating '.
Huo Yuner controlled a special kind of flame, the "Cleansing Flame."
The temperature of this "Cleansing Flame" was not high. In fact, it was very low. Even if this "Cleansing Flame" was placed on dry wood, it couldn't ignite the wood. However, the Cleansing Flame was very effective against negative forces such as the power of curses. Of course, it was also very effective against those evil beings.
However, Huo Yuner wasn't sure if her "Cleansing Flame" would be effective against the power of curses.
Thinking of this, Huo Yuner began to experiment on her own. A milky white flame appeared in her hand and began to burn the curse power on the spear!
Huo Yuner's Cleansing Fire and the black fire in Luo Zheng's mind could be said to be two extremes. The black fire in Luo Zheng's mind could burn almost everything, but Huo Yuner's Cleansing Fire would not cause any damage to weapons.
When the milky white flames appeared, the black smoke on the long spear began to disappear!
"Effective!" Huo Yuner also revealed a pleasantly surprised expression. However, she was only happy. For other God-ranked geniuses, if they could obtain a few saint tools from these cursed weapons, it would be very profitable. However, for Huo Yuner, the value of saint tools in her eyes wasn't much higher than trash.
Even if her Cleansing Fire had such an effect, she was too lazy to "purify" all these weapons.
At this time, Huo Yuner suddenly realized that Luo Zheng seemed to have gone inside the warehouse. She threw away the purified spear and went to look for Luo Zheng.
"Luo Tianxing …"
She softly called out a few times and saw Luo Zheng's figure suddenly flash out. Huo Yuner glanced at him and reached out to cover her small mouth. "Luo Tianxing, you, what happened to you?"
Luo Zheng saw Huo Yuner looking at him in surprise and was extremely puzzled. What happened to him? He had just swallowed a lot of Heaven's Evolution Essence. However, other people should not be able to tell that he had swallowed the Heaven's Evolution Essence, right?
However, Huo Yuner's next words gave Luo Zheng a fright. "Did you eat those weapons …"
These words made Luo Zheng's heart beat wildly. He did not want anyone to know that he had swallowed the Heaven's Evolution Essence. After all, it involved the black fire and the mysterious furnace. Up until now, the only person who knew about this was probably Xun, and Xun was still in the form of a soul attached to his body.
"What do you mean?" No matter how Huo Yuner saw through it, Luo Zheng would not admit it.
Huo Yuner pointed at Luo Zheng's face and said, "If you hadn't swallowed those weapons, how could the power of the curse be in your body? Your body has been smoking all this time … "
Luo Zheng did not know that after he had absorbed the Heaven's Evolution Essence, the power of the curse had not disappeared. Instead, it had gathered in his body.
And this was where the problem came from.
What Luo Zheng did not know was that the power of the curse had a lot of damage to living beings, and this damage came from two aspects.
The power of the curse would constantly seep into the physical body and then slowly erode the person's mind, causing the person's mind to constantly distort and even become abnormal, turning the person into a madman …
However, this was for warriors who often carried cursed weapons, so no one was willing to carry these weapons.
Judging from the characteristics of the power of the curse, it had the same effect as poison, but Luo Zheng's sacred tool body was completely immune to this effect.
However, the power of the curse had another effect, which was to make these weapons easier to break. The strength of these weapons was about half of what it was before the curse. Luo Zheng was afraid that he would have to pay attention to this negative effect, because his body was a sacred tool body. After being contaminated by the power of the curse, the strength of his physical body would be greatly reduced!
Luo Zheng did not know the effects of the power of the curse, but he would definitely not let the black smoke continue to come out of his body …
Therefore, when he heard Huo Yuner's words, Luo Zheng was shocked. He directly picked up a large saber from another copper box. The shiny blade was like a mirror. Looking at himself through the mirror of the blade, he saw that there was indeed black smoke lingering in his body.
This was a bit troublesome. Luo Zheng's heart suddenly sank. He immediately asked, "Does the power of the curse have any effect?"
"I don't know …" Huo Yuner blinked her eyes and stared at Luo Zheng with a face full of curiosity. She just couldn't figure out how Luo Zheng got the power of the curse into his body. Ordinary people couldn't do it even if they wanted to, right? Unless they wore these cursed weapons for a long time.
Could it be that he had really swallowed the weapons into his stomach? This was an immortal tool. No matter how strong his physical body was, it was impossible for him to digest an immortal tool.
Seeing Huo Yuner staring at him so curiously, Luo Zheng's face revealed a helpless expression. Huo Yuner might be easy to fool. If he didn't say anything, she wouldn't ask. However, the others were not so easy to fool. If those almighty Divine Sea Realm experts saw him and interrogated him, he would be in a lot of trouble.
However, Luo Zheng really didn't have a solution now.
Seeing Luo Zheng's tangled expression, Huo Yuner smiled slightly. Her beautiful eyes revealed a calm expression. She said, "I don't know what the consequences of the power of the curse are, but it's definitely not good. However, I can purify it!"
"You can purify it?" Luo Zheng was surprised.
"Yes," Huo Yuner said seriously. She was the kind of woman who didn't know how to joke.
"Since you can purify it, then you …" Luo Zheng's face revealed a dumbfounded smile.
According to Luo Zheng's logic, with this kind of ability, he would naturally purify all these weapons one by one and then sell them … However, Huo Yuner was a dignified princess of the Divine Kingdom. How could she be interested in these immortal tools and sacred tools?
Huo Yuner probably guessed what Luo Zheng wanted to say. She smiled knowingly and said to Luo Zheng, "Don't move!"
After saying that, a milky white flame rose from Huo Yuner's hand. Luo Zheng didn't feel any heat from this flame.
"What kind of flame is this?" Looking at this milky white flame, Luo Zheng's face revealed a stunned expression.
"This is the Purifying Flame, a flame that can purify everything," Huo Yuner said as she slowly pushed the flame towards Luo Zheng.
Under the burning of this flame, Luo Zheng didn't feel any heat. Instead, he felt a trace of coldness from it.
Luo Zheng also felt that there was such a flame in the world. However, under the invasion of this coldness, Luo Zheng's mind became abnormally pure!

Chapter 839
At this moment, Huo Yuner was very close to Luo Zheng. She was constantly releasing the Cleansing Fire, slowly pushing it into Luo Zheng's body.
From Luo Zheng's angle, he could see the serious expression on her face.
Because his thoughts were constantly being cleansed by the Cleansing Fire, Luo Zheng didn't have any desires. Therefore, Luo Zheng's state of mind fell into a very strange state. Even in this state of no distracting thoughts, Luo Zheng was still able to admire the Divine Kingdom Princess in front of him with an extremely pure state of mind.
In Luo Zheng's eyes, Huo Yuner's porcelain white skin, pitch-black deep eyes, dense eyelashes that were constantly trembling, and long hair that was appropriately draped over her shoulders were like exquisite works of art ….
However, when Huo Yuner was stared at by Luo Congheng's "pure" gaze, two red clouds immediately appeared on her fair cheeks. Her fingers trembled slightly, and the Cleansing Fire kept jumping in her hand, as if it was beating at the same frequency as her heart.
Fortunately, her Cleansing Fire wasn't harmful. Even if it pulsed randomly, it wouldn't burn Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had absorbed so much Heaven's Evolution Essence, and the curse power within the weapons had also been completely absorbed by Luo Zheng. The amount of curse power gathered together was quite large.
Even though Huo Yuner's Cleansing Fire had extraordinary purifying abilities, this martial skill, although not very powerful, required one to have perfect control over the Law of Fire. Therefore, even though she had comprehended the third level of the Law of Fire, she could only transform her True Essence into a Cleansing Fire as thick as a thumb. It would still take a lot of time to purify all the curse power.
Even under the Cleansing Fire's Cleansing Fire, the curse power that Luo Zheng had absorbed was constantly dissipating. After a full hour, Huo Yuner's forehead was already covered in fine beads of sweat, and the curse power that was escaping from Luo Zheng's body was gradually decreasing.
"Phew … Done!" Huo Yuner let out a long sigh, and a comfortable smile appeared on her face.
However, even after Huo Yuner extinguished the Pure Flame, Luo Zheng still stared at Huo Yuner in a daze. During this hour, his state of mind had really entered a wonderful state.
In Luo Zheng's opinion, Huo Yuner's' Cleansing Flame 'had many other uses besides expelling the power of curses. For example, if a cultivator was cultivating and was unable to calm down, they would often have many distracting thoughts. If one had this' Cleansing Flame ', they would be able to instantly eliminate these distracting thoughts.
Just now, without any distracting thoughts, Luo Zheng stared at Huo Yuner for half an hour. In this state, Huo Yuner's face was imprinted in Luo Zheng's mind like a blank piece of paper.
Therefore, in Luo Zheng's mind, he had a very strange feeling about Huo Yuner. This feeling was not love, but rather a feeling similar to the feeling of intimacy a chick would feel when it saw a hen for the first time after it hatched from its shell.
Huo Yuner found that Luo Zheng was still staring at her. How could she know what Luo Zheng was thinking? She said with an unnatural expression, "You still haven't told me how you got the power of curses into your body!"
"This …" Luo Zheng didn't know how to answer directly. After thinking for a while, he said with a smile, "Just think of it as me eating these weapons!"
Huo Yuner's face was full of disbelief. However, with her temperament, she didn't continue to ask. She had never been aggressive. If Luo Zheng didn't want to say something, she naturally wouldn't force him to say it.
However, even if a cultivator wore these cursed weapons, they wouldn't be affected by the power of curses in a short period of time. It would take some time for the power of curses to take effect and erode the body.
Even if Luo Zheng really swallowed the weapons, it was impossible to absorb the power of curses into his body in a short period of time. Moreover … Huo Yuner's eyes swept over the copper boxes in front of her. They were empty. There wasn't even a single weapon. What was going on?
This Luo Tianxing …
She had originally been quietly observing Luo Zheng. However, after entering the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean, she realized that not only did her observation learn more about Luo Zheng, this fellow became more and more mysterious. It was as if he had countless secrets on him.
However, unearthing a person's secrets was indeed very interesting. To Huo Yuner, exploring the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean was like a vacation. Unearthing Luo Zheng's secrets was also like a small game!
Since Luo Zheng had already swallowed so much Sky Spill Essence, he couldn't wait to pour out the power in his body. Thus, he left the armory with Huo Yuner and returned to the pyramid.
At this time, most of the cultivators who were "picking up trash" had returned from the armory. Because the danger of the Ore Devouring Bugs had been eliminated, many god-level geniuses had returned to the mining trees to mine.
Thus, the cultivators trading in front of the pyramid became active again!
Seeing Luo Tianxing return, Yue Ying hurriedly waved her hand and said with a face full of joy, "Brother Tianxing, Brother Tianxing! I received another thirty-six Life Origin Stones! "
Those god-level geniuses had already grasped Yue Ying's mentality. This year, all the Life Origin Stones had been bought by Yue Ying and Luo Zheng. Now, the Life Origin Stone had become a rare item. It was even rarer than other rare ores …
The reason was very simple. Other rare ores that were dug up might not be suitable for use, or they might not need them at all and could only be exchanged. However, the Life Origin Stones that were dug up could be exchanged directly with Yue Ying for the ores that they needed.
Moreover, the Life Origin Stones were getting fewer and fewer. The price that Yue Ying offered was also getting higher and higher. The value that she offered had already far exceeded the value of the Life Origin Stone itself!
Yue Ying obviously knew Luo Zheng's goal. Now, it was a big sale at a loss. No matter what, as long as she used the Life Origin Stone to exchange, she would try her best to satisfy the other party's request.
Although Yue Ying was very unwilling, in order to help Luo Zheng exchange for the Life Origin Stone, she could only use this method.
Luo Zheng smiled and nodded. Although Luo Zheng's goal had changed, he could still use the Life Origin Stone.
Even if he used those cursed weapons to refine the Heaven's Divination Essence and lit up tens of thousands of dragon scales, Luo Zheng still did not have the confidence to smash the Golden Leaf and take out the Black Star Pickaxe!
Therefore, the Life Origin Stone was still Luo Zheng's choice. At most, he would take a portion of the Life Origin Stone this year and make a comeback next year!
At this time, Huo Chen also appeared in the circle of light. His face was full of excitement as he ran towards Huo Yuner and shouted, "Sister, sister! Look at this ore of mine! "
In Huo Chen's hand was a bright red stone. The surface of the stone looked rugged and sharp, forming a curling fire cloud. It looked very beautiful.
"What kind of stone is this?" Huo Yuner blinked her eyes and curiously stared at the ore.
The siblings of the Huo Family did not know much about ores. Seeing Huo Chen's excited appearance, Huo Yuner also curiously stared at the ore.
Luo Zheng also stared at the ore with a puzzled expression. Aside from the ore's unique shape, he could not see anything special about it. The fire clouds were distributed in such a regular pattern that it was most likely carved by hand.
Although Luo Zheng himself lacked the ability to identify ores, with his instincts and perception, he could roughly distinguish between good and bad ores. Although there were quite a few trash ores among the ores he threw to Yue Ying, more than 70% of them were still very rare.
"This is the Black Heart Fire Ore! It is very useful for martial artists who cultivate the Law of Fire! Sister, I'll give it to you! "Huo Chen said with a face full of smiles.
Hearing Huo Chen say this, Luo Zheng's expression became even more strange. Yue Ying blinked her eyes and curiously asked, "Black Heart Fire Ore? How come I've never heard of this ore? "
There were thousands of types of ores in the Ore Tree, but Yue Ying recognized almost all of them. The only thing she had never heard of was the Black Heart Fire Ore.
She blinked her eyes and looked at the stone in Huo Chen's hand. In her heart, she thought that Huo Chen was a simple and honest person. Could he have been deceived by someone? If this stone was dug out by himself, then it would be fine. But if it was traded from someone else, then it would be a great loss.
Thinking of this, Yue Ying used a true essence sound transmission to tell Luo Zheng her thoughts.

Chapter 840
Huo Chen didn't know why Luo Zheng asked such a question. Instead, he said in a dull voice, "I didn't dig it out. Liu Xiu sold it to me. What about it?"
Hearing Liu Xiu's name, Huo Yuner frowned slightly. Among the four princes of the four Divine Kingdoms, other than her younger brother who was simple and honest, she didn't like the other three. Especially the Great Yu Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince Liu Xiu, who was extremely sinister and cunning. Although Huo Yuner didn't have much contact with him, he definitely didn't leave a good impression on her.
Therefore, Huo Yuner immediately asked, "How many True Origin Stones did you spend to buy it?"
Huo Chen replied, "Nine thousand …"
The True Origin Stones mentioned here were naturally top-grade True Origin Stones.
Nine thousand top-grade True Origin Stones was definitely not a small number for a Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince. It was equivalent to more than half of Huo Chen's net worth.
Huo Yuner took the Fire Core Ore from Huo Chen's hand and frowned even more.
Seeing his sister's expression, Huo Chen said, "Sister, they demonstrated that this ore can withstand the burning of True Origin Fire. They said that after refining it, it can greatly stabilize the purity of the flame!"
Just as Huo Chen's voice fell, a wisp of flame rose from Huo Yuner's hand. Although the flame in her hand wasn't big, its temperature was extremely intense. The power of the third level of the Law of Fire was no joke!
Under the burning of this pure flame, a crackling sound was emitted from the Crow Heart Fire Ore. Cracks began to appear on the exquisite fire clouds above the Crow Heart Fire Ore.
In the blink of an eye, the entire Fire Core Ore was burned to pieces …
Huo Chen's eyes widened as he stared blankly at the fragments. His face was filled with disbelief. Huo Chen's strength might not be as strong as Huo Yuner's, but he also cultivated the third level of the Law of Fire. Previously, he used his True Origin Fire to refine the Fire Core Ore. After burning for half an incense stick's worth of time, he still couldn't burn the Fire Core Ore. Why did his sister make a move …
At this time, Huo Chen also understood that he was probably deceived.
In the hearts of Luo Zheng and Yue Ying, it was most likely that Liu Xiu had used some kind of method to deceive Huo Chen. This Huo Chen was not scheming at all, and was a dull and honest person. It was not difficult to deceive him.
Huo Yuner's gaze was indifferent, but her face was already cold. She had an easy-going temperament and never liked to argue with others, but she would never allow others to deceive her little brother!
At this moment, the ring of light on the side of the pyramid flickered slightly. Liu Xiu was carrying a pickaxe and coming out of the mining tree. After Liu Xiu came out, the ring of light also flickered continuously. Some of the warriors following Liu Xiu also left the ring of light.
Although each leaf could only accommodate a single cultivator, Liu Xiu's group could choose to enter through a narrow door. This way, after they entered the ore tree, they would land on several adjacent leaves. Now that Liu Xiu had come out from mining, the Divine Step geniuses that followed him naturally followed.
Huo Yuner held the fragments of the Blackheart Fire Ore and looked at Liu Xiu. Although she was angry in her heart, her voice was still elegant and indifferent. However, her words revealed a majesty that could not be refused.
"Liu Xiu, return the nine thousand True Origin Stones to me. Take these things back and let bygones be bygones."
This time, after entering the Feather Emperor's Mystic Realm to mine, Liu Xiu's harvest was quite bountiful. He was also in a good mood. After calculating, other than that abnormal Luo Zheng, the other Crown Princes and Divine Step geniuses' harvest should not be able to compare to his.
As for Luo Zheng, although he was envious, the income from Yue Ying alone was probably more than a hundred thousand top grade True Origin Stones. He simply could not compare. However, this did not matter. In Liu Xiu's eyes, Luo Zheng was no different from a dead man. That brat was too sharp, and his Imperial Father would not let him go.
Hearing Huo Yuner's words, Liu Xiu's eyes flashed. He looked at the pile of fragments in Huo Yuner's hands and knew that his trick had been exposed. However, he did not show the slightest bit of nervousness. Instead, he replied seriously, "Oh? What is this? "
"The stone you sold to Huo Chen is fake. Please return the True Origin Stones to Huo Chen." Seeing that Liu Xiu was playing dumb, Huo Yuner's brows furrowed even more. She was kind-hearted and pure, and did not guard against strangers. However, she was unable to accept others lying to her younger brother.
Hearing Huo Yuner's words, Liu Xiu chuckled. "Princess Yuner, that's not right. When did I say that this stone is real?"
"You, you clearly …" Huo Chen was also anxious. Just as he was about to speak, Liu Xiu interrupted him. "What about me? Huo Chen, did I tell you that this stone is real? "
"Did I also remind you that I can't grasp the value of this stone?"
"Also, didn't I say that I don't want to sell it to you?"
"You were the one who insisted on buying it. Moreover, you swore that regardless of whether it was real or fake, you wouldn't pursue the matter, right?"
Liu Xiu was very eloquent. Even Huo Chen was unable to say a single word. In the end, he could only hold it in until his face turned red and he nodded.
Liu Xiu's words were indeed true. In reality, he had only played a small game with a few god-tier geniuses and easily lured Huo Chen.
"Princess Yuner, your younger brother has already admitted it. Isn't this matter very clear? I already said that I won't sell it to him. This Huo Chen should have bought it. Now that you're looking for trouble with me, isn't that inappropriate? "Liu Xiu questioned.
Facing Liu Xiu's questioning, Huo Yuner did not know how to answer. She was not someone who was particularly good at refuting. When Liu Xiu opened his mouth, all the logic was on his side. It seemed that her younger brother was in the wrong.
Seeing Huo Yuner's expression, Liu Xiu revealed a pleased smile. However, he chuckled. "Actually, although Huo Chen forced me to sell this stone to him and it was even burned by you, Princess Yuner, it's not that I can't return it!"
Huo Yuner's expression slightly changed. "What do you mean?"
Liu Xiu smiled and said, "If Princess Yuner were to lend me the jade bracelet you're wearing for a year or so, I would admit that I was unlucky and return these True Origin Stones! What do you think, Princess Yuner? "
The value of the semi-divine tool on Huo Yuner's arm was immeasurable! Ordinary Crown Princes did not have this kind of treatment! Even Liu Xiu, as the richest Crown Prince of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, only had two "Heavenly Thunder Halberds" that could be ranked as semi-divine tools.
However, the Heavenly Thunder Halberd was a one-time use magic weapon. Previously, in the spirit beast area, Liu Xiu had used one of the Heavenly Thunder Halberds in order to escape. He only had the other Heavenly Thunder Halberd in his hand.
"Hahahaha …" Hearing this, Luo Zheng finally could not help but laugh.
Seeing Luo Zheng laughing so unscrupulously in front of him, Liu Xiu's face sank. "What are you laughing at?"
"I'm laughing at some people who can boast shamelessly. To be able to be shameless to this extent, I really admire, admire!" Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
"Presumptuous, Luo Zheng, who are you calling shameless?!"
"His Highness is the Crown Prince of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. How can you insult him?"
"Luo Zheng, it seems that you still don't know your identity. You are just a commoner. In front of the Imperial Family, you are not even a fart!"
Before Liu Xiu could say anything, the God-tier geniuses who followed Liu Xiu began to clamor.
In the four Divine Kingdoms, there was an insurmountable gap between martial artists and mortals, and the Imperial Family also had an insurmountable gap between martial artists and martial artists. This was the power of the Divine Kingdom's Imperial Family.
This kind of narrow-minded thinking was established by the four Divine Kingdoms after thousands of years, but to Luo Zheng, it was not the slightest bit of deterrence!
To put it bluntly, the establishment of a Divine Kingdom was based on one's own strength. If the Imperial Family of a Divine Kingdom lost their strength, then the Divine Kingdom would be in imminent danger. There was no Divine Sea Realm expert here. Luo Zheng's strength could almost sweep away these God-tier geniuses, so why would he put Liu Xiu in his eyes?
Even if Liu Xiu was the Crown Prince of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom, so what? Luo Zheng had the Black Iron Divine Kingdom behind him. Not to mention, Luo Zheng was standing up for Huo Yuner and Huo Chen. Behind them stood the Heavenly Wind Divine Kingdom!
"You guys talk too much nonsense!" Luo Zheng coldly smiled, and his eyes fell on Liu Xiu. "I will only say this once. Return the True Meta Stone to Huo Chen, and this matter will be over. Don't embarrass yourself!"
As soon as these words came out, Liu Xiu's face suddenly became very ugly.
Huo Yuner's strength might be very strong, but Liu Xiu didn't have much of a fear. This Huo Family brother and sister was too naive.
But Luo Zheng had to stand up for them!
If Luo Zheng had been a bit more polite, Liu Xiu might have given in. But his words were too harsh. If he gave in, where would he put his face in the future?
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 841
Since the incident at the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea, Liu Xiu had several conflicts with Luo Zheng.
Yu the Great's Kingdom and Luo Zheng had irreconcilable conflicts. Both Bai Yuerong and Battle Emperor Yu the Great wanted to get rid of Luo Zheng.
However, Liu Xiu knew that there was a huge gap between his strength and Luo Zheng's. Therefore, he tried his best to endure these small conflicts. In Liu Xiu's mind, Luo Zheng was like a grasshopper after autumn. He would not be able to survive for more than a few days.
However, Liu Xiu was the Crown Prince of the Kingdom. He was also the most outstanding one among the four Crown Princes of the Kingdom!
There was a limit to his tolerance. It was already very rare for him, the first Crown Prince, to tolerate an insignificant commoner. However, this person was so arrogant. How could Liu Xiu endure it?
An indescribable anger rushed straight to his head!
If it were anyone else, even if it was a guy like the little monster, Liu Xiu would have already attacked. However, thinking of Luo Zheng's abnormal strength, Liu Xiu gritted his teeth and said, "What if I don't return it? What do you want? Do you dare to attack me? "

With Luo Zheng's Soul Coercion that was infinitely close to the Blue Soul Realm, the Soul Coercion released was enough to crush these Immortal Level geniuses, including Liu Xiu! What's more, Luo Zheng's Soul Coercion had merged with the will of the Boundless World!
Because of the conflict here, many warriors who had left the mining tree did not enter the pyramid again. Instead, they watched the conflict here.
In fact, most of the warriors knew that even if there was a conflict between Liu Xiu and Luo Zheng, it would be difficult for them to fight.
The reason was simple. At the West Dragon River, Luo Zheng suffered a blow from Battle Emperor Yu the Great. However, he was still safe and sound. In other words, the Divine Sea Realm warriors could not do anything to Luo Zheng without activating their Realm.
In this way, warriors below the Divine Sea Realm could not do anything to him!
On the other hand, Luo Zheng wouldn't dare to touch Liu Xiu. Previously, he had already offended the Great Yu Divine Country.
If Luo Zheng fought with Liu Xiu here and injured or even killed him, Battle Emperor Da Yu would definitely not let Luo Zheng go when the Divine Sea Realm came to the Emperor of Feathers Mystic Realm to retrieve the Soul Core.
Therefore, everyone was just here to watch the show.
But as soon as Luo Zheng released his soul pressure, all the god-rank geniuses' faces changed dramatically!
The aura released by Luo Zheng's soul was no less than that of a Spirit Sea Realm warrior. It was even stronger than that of an average Spirit Sea Realm warrior. Plus, there was the will of the universe. How could they resist?
At this moment, everyone felt like they weren't facing a person, but the heavens, the earth, the sea, the mountains …
This aura was so majestic and so difficult to resist. At this moment, even the Battle Emperors and Battle Emperors would have to bow their heads!
At first, the god-rank geniuses behind Liu Xiu were still trying to resist Luo Zheng's soul pressure. How could they be willing to be forced back by a kid in the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm?
However, as Luo Zheng's soul pressure kept increasing, those god-rank geniuses finally couldn't stand it anymore. One, two, three … Their legs were trembling. At the same time, they almost couldn't help but retreat!
Liu Xiu was the only one left. He stood firmly on the spot and stared at Luo Zheng. He gritted his teeth and said, "Luo Zheng! Don't go too far! "
Luo Zheng didn't attack. He only used his soul pressure to suppress Liu Xiu. The problem was, what was the difference between using such a powerful soul pressure and attacking?
Liu Xiu was wearing an upper-grade Holy Grade necklace called the "Heart Concentrating Necklace". This necklace could eliminate 60% of the opponent's soul attack! In other words, when Luo Zheng released his soul pressure, his necklace could eliminate 60% of the soul pressure!
Holy Grade defensive equipment was very rare. Its value far exceeded that of an ordinary upper-grade Holy Grade defensive equipment. Treasures that could resist soul attacks were even rarer. Therefore, the value of this Heart Concentrating Necklace was comparable to a Demigod Grade defensive equipment.
However, this necklace's style was more feminine. A purple Heart Concentrating Gem was embedded in the center of the necklace. It was this Heart Concentrating Gem that played a crucial role! Because this necklace was made for female warriors, Liu Xiu always wore it inside and didn't let outsiders see it!
Relying on this Heart Concentrating Necklace, Liu Xiu was bitterly holding on in front of Luo Zheng.
"Huh?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of surprise. However, immediately after, an even more intense smile appeared on his face. "Your soul is far from the Battle Soul Realm, but you can withstand my soul pressure! Interesting. Let's see how far you can go! "
After saying that, Luo Zheng lifted his foot and took a step towards Liu Xiu.
With this step, Liu Xiu's face flushed with an unhealthy redness. However, in the blink of an eye, the redness disappeared and was replaced by a deathly pale complexion. However, Liu Xiu continued to grit her teeth and endure.
In fact, Luo Zheng's strength was already at the level of the Divine Sea Realm! However, he hadn't experienced the Life and Death Calamity. He hadn't opened the Divine Sea, and he didn't have his own domain!
However, Liu Xiu didn't want to admit this fact. He still regarded Luo Zheng as someone of the same generation. It was just that Luo Zheng was more outstanding among the younger generation!
It was also because of this that Liu Xiu refused to lower his head! If Luo Zheng was replaced by any other almighty Divine Sea Realm warrior, Liu Xiu would probably concede and lower his head.
One step …
Two steps …
Three steps …
With every step Luo Zheng took, the pressure on Liu Xiu increased. Liu Xiu's face became paler!
After six or seven steps, Liu Xiu's face was as white as a sheet of paper …
"I, I will fight you to the death!"
Liu Xiu couldn't stand it anymore. At this moment, a small halberd appeared in his hand!
Looking at the halberd in Liu Xiu's hand, Luo Zheng's lips curled up. "I heard that although this Heavenly Thunder Halberd is a consumable item, it is still a Semi-Divine Artifact. Its value is immeasurable. Your father must have given it to you to save your life, right?"
"This short halberd can't be bought without three to four hundred thousand top grade True Origin Stones. I just want you to return a mere nine thousand top grade True Origin Stones to Huo Chen. As a dignified Crown Prince, you don't know how to settle this debt? You haven't learned arithmetic yet, right? "
"I know that you have a lot of face. However, even if you are the Crown Prince of the Divine Kingdom, you must learn how to judge the situation. In other words, the Divine Kingdom is just a 'Divine Kingdom'. Even in the Great Thousand World, it is nothing. In the Upper Realm, it is as insignificant as a speck of dust. If you can understand this point, you will know what to do."
Luo Zheng's eyes were cold, and his words were mixed with an irresistible majesty!
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Huo Yuner's eyes flickered slightly. On one hand, she was grateful to Luo Zheng for helping her and her brother, but on the other hand, Luo Zheng's words enlightened her on another aspect!
She had always been in her father's swaddling clothes, which was why she could be so serene and elegant, and even very willful! However, although her father was the Monarch of the Divine Kingdom, he was destined to be unable to protect her for a lifetime. Not to mention, her future could be even broader. Perhaps she had thought too simply of her life's goal …
Luo Zheng didn't care about 9,000 top-grade True Origin Stones. Yue Ying had already accumulated a huge amount of True Origin Stones by selling ores.
At that time, Liu Xiu also didn't care about these 9,000 top-grade True Origin Stones. If he really returned these True Origin Stones, then he would truly be bowing his head to Luo Zheng. This was something he was unwilling to do no matter what!
Therefore, even if Luo Zheng's words were reasonable, Liu Xiu still refused his proposal. The True Origin Stones in his hands suddenly flashed, and a purple thunder wrapped around the two feet long Heavenly Thunder Combat Halberd!
The runes on the Heavenly Thunder Combat Halberd began to flicker constantly! Obviously, it had been activated by Liu Xiu!
Seeing this scene, all the martial artists' faces changed. Many of them had seen the power of this Heavenly Thunder Combat Halberd. In almost an instant, it had obliterated so many resentful spirits. This Heavenly Thunder Combat Halberd was comparable to a Divine Sea Realm expert!
Many martial artists retreated. Even Yue Ying, Huo Yuner, Huo Chen, and the others distanced themselves from Luo Zheng. If they were affected by the power of this Heavenly Thunder Combat Halberd, even if they didn't die, they would be severely injured!

Chapter 842
Liu Xiu's nerves were stretched taut at this moment! How could he not understand Luo Zheng's math?
The value of this Heavenly Thunder Halberd was simply immeasurable in the four Divine Kingdoms!
In the Upper Realm, the Heavenly Thunder Halberd might only be equivalent to a few hundred thousand top-grade true yuan stones, but in the Lower Realm, even if you had the money, you couldn't buy it. Treasures at the level of semi-divine tools could be regarded as priceless treasures.
Last time, in order to save his life, he had to use the Heavenly Thunder Halberd, which made him extremely distressed. Now, he had to use the last Heavenly Thunder Halberd against Luo Zheng?
"Crackle … …"
The surface of the Heavenly Thunder Halberd was constantly emitting tiny lightning bolts. On the surface of the Heavenly Thunder Halberd, there was a circular red rune. As long as he gently pressed his finger on the red rune, the Heavenly Thunder Halberd would be activated and shoot out in an instant!
His finger was slightly trembling. Luo Zheng in front of him was giving him too much pressure. Even his consciousness was beginning to blur!
At this moment, Liu Xiu also wanted to give up. His instinct was to fear Luo Zheng. That instinct kept reminding him not to oppose this person. This person was absolutely not someone he could provoke.
But at this moment, another voice sounded, "Kill him, as long as you kill Luo Zheng! Even if you have to pay the price of the Heavenly Thunder Halberd, it's still worth it. Father Emperor will definitely reward you greatly! "
A stream of blood pumped out from his madly beating heart. It flowed along his aorta and rushed into his eyes. His eyes were suddenly blood-red. At the same time, his finger pressed down on the rune on the Heavenly Thunder Halberd!
"Buzz ~"
A sharp and ear-piercing sound came from the short Heavenly Thunder Halberd, which made people's ears very uncomfortable.
That was the sound of the lightning force rushing out. Then, from the front part of the Heavenly Thunder Halberd, a huge lightning bolt suddenly burst out.
"Go to hell! This commoner! " Liu Xiu shouted. From the beginning to the end, he didn't put down his dignity. Because of this, he refused to bow down to Luo Zheng.
Although Liu Xiu's voice was covered by the loud sound of thunder, Luo Zheng still clearly heard Liu Xiu's words.
"Hehe …"
Luo Zheng had a smile on his face. In the blink of an eye, his entire body was covered by an incomparably thick lightning bolt!
This enormous power of thunder and lightning was like a river, rushing towards Luo Zheng, extending all the way to an extremely far distance …
"Pa —"
Every single Deity genius was blinded by this dazzling light!
This thick bolt of lightning destroyed everything in its path before finally charging towards the vast Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea! The surface of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean still glowed with a faint golden light. Under the influence of the violent lightning energy, much of the golden light was dispelled …
The dazzling lightning lasted for a long time before it gradually dissipated. Everyone slowly opened their eyes and looked at Luo Zheng with their eyes wide open.
Luo Zheng was still standing in the same place, completely unharmed!
Even a mighty warrior of the Divine Sea Realm would be in a difficult situation when struck by lightning …
