Seeing this scene, a Deity genius secretly sighed. "This Luo Zheng, is he the incarnation of God …"
"Don't expect to fight against him. If a False Tribulation Realm can do this, he will probably be able to do whatever he wants in the entire Great Thousand World in the future …"
"It's power transfer, the ability of a little monster. However, a little monster can't use it to this extent."
After all, the Heavenly Thunder Combat Halberd was a Quasi Divine Tool. If Luo Zheng only used his physical body to resist the lightning energy, he wouldn't be able to completely resist it. Even with his Saint Tool body, he would probably be seriously injured.
However, Luo Zheng didn't plan to use his physical body to resist from the beginning. The moment Liu Xiu activated the Heavenly Thunder Combat Halberd, he transferred the damage he suffered to the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean!
Although the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean was only an inland sea, the area of the entire sea was still quite large. No matter how thick the lightning energy was, it could be dissolved by such a large sea. The lightning that attacked Luo Zheng couldn't cause any waves in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean at all!
Luo Zheng released his soul pressure and took another step forward. At the same time, he shook his head and said, "This business of yours is a big loss!"
Liu Xiu clenched his teeth tightly. Looking at the unharmed Luo Zheng, his eyes were filled with disbelief. He suddenly roared, "So what! My father will definitely kill you! You're no match for my father! "
"I don't deny it." Luo Zheng nodded and took another step forward.
"Then why don't you quickly kneel down? I can beg my father to spare your worthless life. Wahahaha!" Liu Xiu had already sunk into madness. His eyes were filled with bigotry.
"You're not qualified." Facing the crazy Liu Xiu, Luo Zheng's eyes were clear and calm. At the same time, he took another step forward!
"Pa!"
At this moment, a soft cracking sound suddenly came from Liu Xiu's body!
Countless fragments suddenly appeared from the Heart Solidifying Gem in the Heart Solidifying Necklace on Liu Xiu's chest. This gem released an invisible protective barrier that blocked Luo Zheng's soul pressure. However, as Luo Zheng pressed closer, the Heart Solidifying Gem could no longer bear the violent pressure and finally cracked!
Liu Xiu's soul was not much stronger than other warriors. Without the protection of the Heart Solidifying Gem, it was equivalent to using his fragile soul to directly face Luo Zheng's pressure!
In just a breath's time, Liu Xiu felt the world spinning. Then, his eyes rolled back, his body stiffened, and he directly fainted.
Looking at Liu Xiu on the ground, Luo Zheng shook his head slightly. However, he didn't plan to kill Liu Xiu.
After merging with the will of the world, Luo Zheng could indeed resist the attack of a Divine Sea Realm warrior. However, Luo Zheng was no match for those who had comprehended the realm. Once he was enveloped by the realm and cut off from the will of the world, he was no match for the Divine Sea Realm warrior. Therefore, he didn't want to provoke Liu Zan, the Battle Emperor of Yu the Great.
All the Immortal Tier geniuses stared at Luo Zheng in a daze. Their eyes were filled with fear.
They all knew that Luo Zheng was very powerful. Previously, Luo Zheng had only fought with top Immortal Tier geniuses like Yun Longtao, Huo Yuner, and the little monster in the arena. Therefore, everyone acknowledged that Luo Zheng was powerful, but they couldn't tell how powerful he was!
Now, these people deeply understood how terrifying Luo Zheng's strength was!
With Luo Zheng's strength, as long as he had enough time and space, he could kill all the Immortal Tier geniuses here! The gap between them wasn't just the gap of one realm, but the gap of two realms. Although Luo Zheng was only at the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm …
"He fainted." Luo Zheng smiled. "Let's talk about it when he wakes up!"
Huo Yuner exhaled softly and said, "No need. It's just 9,000 True Meta Stones."
Although it was a huge sum of money for Huo Chen, Huo Yuner was just angry that her brother was deceived by Liu Xiu. She couldn't bear it. However, in terms of real value, 9,000 top-grade True Meta Stones were nothing.
Moreover, Liu Xiu had paid a heavy price for these True Meta Stones. The value of the Heavenly Thunder Halberd he used was immeasurable.
At this moment, Huo Yuner was worried about Luo Zheng. He had already angered Battle Emperor of Yu the Great. If Battle Emperor of Yu the Great came to the Secret Realm of the Feather Emperor, would he directly attack Luo Zheng? If it really came to that, she would definitely intervene!
As long as she stood up, her father would definitely stand up as well … Huo Yuner had already made up her mind.
"Mine!"
Soon, Luo Zheng threw this matter to the back of his mind. He had important matters to attend to! After absorbing so much Heaven Amplification Essence and igniting more than 10,000 dragon scales, his current goal was to smash the Golden Leaf and take out the Black Star Pickaxe. Then, he would head to the top of this mining tree.

Chapter 843
Under the fearful gazes of the Immortal Realm geniuses, Luo Zheng started to climb up the pyramid! In the end, he climbed to the thirty-fifth level.
"What is he doing on the 35th floor?"
"I don't know … I think he went to that narrow door!"
"The Golden Leaf? His target is the Black Star Pickaxe? It's futile. Does he have the confidence to take it out? "
The special narrow door on the 35th floor could fall into the middle section of the mining tree. In other words, it was a light golden leaf. Inside this leaf, there was a supreme artifact.
This information was obtained from the Sky Feather Holy Sea a long time ago. Therefore, most warriors in the four Divine Kingdoms knew about it!
However, everyone knew that there was a supreme artifact here. It was a strange pickaxe, but no one could take it out.
For so many years, many Divine Sea Realm warriors had thought of many ways, but no one could break open that light golden leaf and take the Black Star Pickaxe.
However, Luo Zheng walked in so casually. Did he have a way to open that light golden leaf?
Previously, Luo Zheng gave people the feeling that he could do anything. Therefore, at first, they thought that he could do it. However, after thinking about it carefully, they all shook their heads and denied it.
"Impossible! If the Black Star Pickaxe could be taken out so easily, there would be Divine Sea Realm warriors who got it already! "
"That's right. I think Luo Tianxing is just trying. However, a supreme artifact is not something ordinary. He can't take it out! "
"This guy's brute force is indeed strong. However, he could only injure Battle King Yu the Great. Unless his strength is 10 times stronger, there is still a chance!"
While people were discussing, some warriors even started to climb up the pyramid. The 35th floor was dangerous for Immortal Realm geniuses like them. However, if they ran into green Ore Devouring Bugs and escaped in time, it wouldn't be a big problem as long as they didn't run into purple Ore Devouring Bugs. The purple Ore Devouring Bugs usually lived above the 40th floor. So far, only Luo Zheng had lured them down from the 40th floor.
Therefore, many warriors entered the narrow door on the 35th floor and the narrow door below.
Because each narrow door could only allow one warrior to enter, some brave warriors would even enter the narrow door above Luo Zheng …
Standing on the pale golden leaf, Luo Zheng was speechless as he looked at the people above his head, below his feet, to his left, to his right, and even to his left.
It was just smashing a leaf. What was there to see? He even wanted to come in and watch.
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, did not try to avoid it. Taking away the Black Star Pickaxe would definitely cause a huge commotion, so there was no way he could avoid being noticed. However, he did not like to be surrounded like this.
"Should I lure a purple Ore Devouring Bug to chase these guys away?" Luo Zheng thought so in his heart. But after thinking about it, he decided to let it go. If they wanted to watch a good show, he would let them watch as much as they wanted.
He took a few steps on the pale golden leaf and arrived at the Black Star Pickaxe. Then, he took a deep breath and closed his eyes.
After entering the saint tool body, Luo Zheng did not know how much power he could bear. When he had just absorbed the Sky Evolution Essence, Luo Zheng wanted to test it out. However, he was interrupted by Huo Yuner. Now, he wanted to test it out!
From the moment he closed his eyes, Luo Zheng began to absorb the power of the dragon scales.
One scale, ten scales, one hundred scales, one thousand scales …
When he was in the immortal tool body, Luo Zheng's body could only bear the power of one thousand scales. If he absorbed any more, his body might collapse.
However, this time, Luo Zheng directly opened one thousand scales. Next, he opened two thousand scales!
Luo Zheng could feel the terrifying power slowly flowing through his body. It was like a condensed liquid, slowly moving through his limbs and bones.
His body was still able to withstand the power of two thousand dragon scales. Again!
Three thousand!
Next, Luo Zheng instantly activated the power of 3,000 dragon scales!
"I can still bear it!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt that every cell in his body was expanding. Each of his cells contained an extremely strong power. If all of this power were to be released in an instant, what kind of terrifying state would it reach?
He had not reached his limit yet! He could still bear it!
Luo Zheng took a deep breath again and opened four thousand dragon scales!
"Crack! Crack! Crack!
Crack! "Luo Zheng's bones began to emit violent cracking sounds!
Ever since Luo Zheng's body had been cultivated to a Xuan Artifact, his bones rarely made such a sound because every part of his body was incomparably hard. The reason why such a sound erupted from his body was because the joints in his body were expanding and could not bear the force. That was why such a sound erupted from his body!
Because of the power, every cell in Luo Zheng's body expanded again! At this moment, his body had expanded to its limit!
"Look! Luo Zheng seems to have grown taller! "
"His body seems to have expanded by two times!"
"How on earth did he do it? I feel that his body is filled with power! "
Above Luo Zheng's head and on the leaves on both sides, many warriors were lying on the edge of the leaves and watching. The braver warriors were focused on the changes in Luo Zheng's body. The timid warriors glanced at him and looked around. If the Ores Devouring Bugs came, they only needed to turn over slightly and they would be able to escape from the leaves …
"Is this the limit? Is this the limit? "Luo Zheng frowned.
He felt a swelling pain coming from every part of his body. He was unable to control the power of four thousand dragon scales!
"Alas, it seems that I still have to undergo Body Forging! It's just that I haven't collected enough Primordial Astral Body! " After attaining the Immortal Artifact Body, Luo Zheng had undergone Body Forging. His body had become much stronger. However, after Luo Zheng entered the Divine Artifact Body, he did not have the opportunity to undergo Body Forging. After all, the red dragon was now in a deep sleep.
Would the power of four thousand dragon scales be enough to break open this golden leaf?
Luo Zheng frowned. His face revealed a hesitant expression … No! He quickly denied this idea.
These Divine Sea Realm warriors had thought of many ways to take away the pickaxes in the golden leaf. Although only one Divine Sea Realm warrior could enter the golden leaf, there were still several Divine Sea Realm warriors on the left, right, above, and below. If they all attacked together, they still could not break the golden leaf. It was estimated that the power of four thousand dragon scales alone would not be enough to break it open.
"Five thousand!"
Luo Zheng did not hesitate. He continued to borrow more dragon scales!
However, just as the power of five thousand dragon scales burst out, Luo Zheng felt severe pain! This pain came from every cell in Luo Zheng's body. The degree of pain was imaginable! It was unbearable!
"Selfless … Realm!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng used a technique that he had previously used. Under the state of Selflessness, Luo Zheng could temporarily forget about the pain in his body. He was like a bystander, treating his body as if he was a spectator.
In fact, the Selfless Realm had both advantages and disadvantages. Pain was beneficial to the human body. When the body felt pain, it was actually the body telling you that it had exceeded its capacity or that there was something wrong with it …
If one could not feel pain, then it would only bring more harm to one's body!
However, Luo Zheng had no other choice. Since he had made the decision, he would try his best.
The power of five thousand dragon scales poured into Luo Zheng's body. Because every part of his body had expanded to the limit, the popping sound disappeared. However, after only one breath, a wound suddenly appeared on Luo Zheng's chest!
"Pop!"
The sound of cloth tearing was heard, and a dark red arrow of blood suddenly appeared on Luo Zheng's chest. The arrow of blood had torn a long strip of his robe at his chest! Luo Zheng's chest was torn open by a huge force. Blood rushed out at an extremely high speed, instantly tearing his clothes apart!
"Pop! Pop! Pop! Pop … "

Chapter 844

Chapter 845

After obtaining the Will of the World from the little monster, Luo Zheng could connect with any material in this world!
A flower, a piece of wood, a mountain, a sea …
Everything he saw could establish this incredible connection with Luo Zheng. Even everything he had never seen could be connected with him. Because the memory of the whole world had been firmly stored in Luo Zheng's mind, he was now the spokesperson of this world.
He could feel the original Will of this world. In the future, Luo Zheng would definitely explore it himself, but now was not the time.
Using the method of transferring power, Luo Zheng could bear a huge amount of power without any harm, because any form of power would be replaced by something else. On the West Dragon River, Luo Zheng was replaced by the mountains by the river. Chapter 846

This Black Star Pickaxe was extremely important to Luo Zheng. How could Luo Zheng allow others to touch it?
Therefore, this time, Luo Zheng's soul pressure burst out completely!
Luo Zheng's speed of falling was extremely fast. In the blink of an eye, he had fallen from the 35th floor to the 20th floor together with the Black Star Pickaxe and the fragments of the Golden Leaf. At this moment, he was already quite far away from Huo Yuner and Prince Yan!

Huo Yuner sat on the edge of the leaf and swung her legs. Her gaze was indifferent …
If she had seen this scene in the past, no matter who it was, she would have tried to persuade the other party to stop. Under this kind of Soul Suppression, it would be difficult for those people to survive … However, at this moment, the scales in her heart were completely tilted. She thought in her heart, "Luo Tianxing did the right thing. Who told you to snatch the Black Star Pickaxe? You deserve it."
There were still some warriors on the leaf, and their faces were filled with joy. Just now, they also wanted to rush down and fight, but they were either too far away from Luo Zheng's leaf, or they restrained themselves. Especially those warriors who restrained themselves. Even though they were so far away, they were still suppressed by Luo Zheng's Soul Suppression. The ten or so Divine Ranked geniuses who were chasing after the Black Star Pickaxe were only one or two zhang away from Luo Zheng. The closest ones were not even one zhang away, and one of them was only a foot away from the Black Star Pickaxe. That Divine Ranked genius had already stretched his hand towards the Black Star Pickaxe, but at this moment, his hand was frozen at this distance, unable to move an inch further …
In an instant, these ten or so Divine Ranked geniuses were corroded by Luo Zheng's Soul Suppression. Streaks of blood flowed out from their mouths, noses, ears, and eyes. During the process of falling, the blood was still flowing out, and from their bodies, no signs of life could be felt.
Soon, Luo Zheng and the Black Star Pickaxe landed at the bottom of the ring of light, and those Divine Ranked geniuses followed closely behind.
On one side of the pyramid, under the flickering ring of light, Luo Zheng stood firmly in the large pit that was previously created, and the Black Star Pickaxe was firmly nailed to the ground.

Not all of the Divine Ranked geniuses had entered the mining tree. After all, each leaf could only accommodate one warrior. If they were too far away, their vision would be blocked, and they wouldn't be able to see anything. Therefore, most of the Divine Ranked geniuses stayed outside the pyramid.
Now that these Divine Ranked geniuses saw the scene in front of them, each and every one of them was scared out of their wits. At the same time, their faces were filled with vigilance. This question had been passed down by word of mouth among the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms. Now, the answer was as clear as day. Who could do it? Luo Zheng could do it!
Luo Zheng carried the Black Star Pickaxe and walked out of the huge pit. His mind wasn't on these people at all. To Luo Zheng, these Divine Ranked geniuses were nothing. He had already focused all his attention on the Black Star Pickaxe in his hand!

"This is the so-called supreme artifact? The question is, what's so special about it? "

However, apart from the fact that the Black Star Pickaxe was extremely hard, Luo Zheng couldn't tell which part of it looked like a Divine Artifact. Moreover, it was a supreme artifact …

Chapter 847
Everyone stared at Luo Zheng. Although many of the god-ranked geniuses had greedy looks on their faces, the corpses in the pit were still vivid in their eyes.
Snatching something from Luo Zheng's hands? That wasn't taking a risk. That was simply courting death!
Therefore, everyone could only keep a certain distance from Luo Zheng and look at him from afar.
This Black Star Pickaxe was very short and small, so it looked like a small hammer. After observing it for a long time, Luo Zheng still couldn't figure out the secret behind it.
"Does this pickaxe need to be activated with true energy?"
Thinking of this, a strand of true energy appeared in Luo Zheng's hand and poured into the pickaxe.
Generally speaking, true energy could easily invade weapons. However, he faintly felt that this pickaxe seemed to reject true energy. His true energy simply couldn't be poured into it …
"Perception …"
Next, Luo Zheng used his soul perception. However, just as he touched the Black Star Pickaxe, he was also blocked by an invisible force.
"Do I need to drip blood to recognize it as the owner?"
Luo Zheng blinked his eyes and gently flicked his finger. A strand of blood shot out. However, before the blood could splash onto the Black Star Pickaxe, it slid to the sides.
This …
Looking at the Black Star Pickaxe, which was impervious to everything, Luo Zheng suddenly felt as if he had entered a treasure mountain but returned empty-handed. He had no idea what to do with this pickaxe.
Luo Zheng helplessly blinked his eyes and looked at the god-ranked geniuses in the distance. At this moment, no one dared to approach him. He also couldn't expect to get the method from them.
Just as Luo Zheng was at his wit's end, a faint voice rang out in his mind …
"Black Star Pickaxe, hehe, a supreme artifact … I knew it was buried in the Tree of Earth. I didn't expect you to really take it out …" Qinglong's faint voice floated over.
"You're awake!" Luo Zheng's face revealed a surprised expression. He originally thought that Qinglong would be in a deep sleep for a long time.
Qinglong's faint voice drifted over. There was even a hint of exhaustion in his voice. "I can feel this supreme artifact."
Luo Zheng was also worried. Judging from Qinglong's tone, it seemed that it knew a lot about the Mining Tree and the Black Star Pickaxe. Luo Zheng's face lit up. "It seems that I can't use this pickaxe."
Qinglong chuckled and said, "It would be strange if a supreme divine artifact could be used as soon as one obtained it."
"Then what should I do?" Luo Zheng asked hurriedly.
The Dragon Bloodline Clan was a branch of the True Dragon Clan. They had half dragon and half human blood. Qinglong was a Super Divine Beast in the True Dragon Realm. He was a true True Dragon. If even Qinglong did not know how to use the Black Star Pickaxe, no one else would.
"I'll teach you how to recognize a master," Qinglong said casually.
"Is it easy?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Follow my instructions. It won't be too difficult," Qinglong said again.
Hearing Qinglong's words, Luo Zheng heaved a sigh of relief. He was now in the territory of the four Divine Kingdoms. According to King Yan and the others, the Divine Sea Realm experts would come to the Emperor of Feathers Mystic Realm to catch the Ore Devouring Bugs. They hoped to take the Soul Cores from the Ore Devouring Bugs. If he could not make the Black Star Pickaxe recognize him as its master, it would definitely be taken away by the War Emperors and Battle Emperors.
"Do you see the three divine patterns on the head of the pickaxe?" Qinglong continued, "You only need to activate the three 10-star divine patterns. Then you can make it recognize you as its master."
Luo Zheng nodded. The three 10-star divine patterns on the Black Star Pickaxe were inherited from the True Dragon Clan. They had nothing to do with the human race.
Luo Zheng had wondered if he needed to activate the three 10-star divine patterns before he could make it recognize him as its master. That was why he tried to infuse his True Yuan into the Black Star Pickaxe. However, the Black Star Pickaxe did not react at all. Luo Zheng could only feel helpless …
"You only need to draw the other three 10-star divine patterns according to the three 10-star divine patterns. Then you can activate it," Qinglong said with a smile.
"The other three 10-star divine patterns?" Luo Zheng was shocked. "Don't tell me they're also 10-star divine patterns?"
With Luo Zheng's current soul force, he could only inscribe a five-star or six-star Divine Rune. He couldn't even inscribe a seven-star Divine Rune. As for a ten-star Divine Rune, he didn't even dare to think about it.
Qinglong said calmly, "Hehe, if it's a 10-star divine pattern, I'm afraid there are only a few people in the entire universe who can make this Black Star Pickaxe recognize him as its master … Think about it. Then you'll understand if you reverse the patterns."
"Reverse the patterns?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows. The three 10-star divine patterns appeared in Luo Zheng's eyes.
In his mind, Luo Zheng began to construct the opposite lines of Divine Inscriptions. Following which, a look of astonishment appeared on his face. "These are actually three One Star Divine Inscriptions. Not only that, these are the Divine Inscriptions I used to meditate when I was cultivating Divine Inscriptions!"
This ten-star Divine Rune was actually a one-star Divine Rune …
Luo Zheng did not know if this was a coincidence or a deliberate move by the almighty who designed the divine patterns.
"Yes! However, the number of people who know this secret is too few … Even if someone took the Black Star Pickaxe, they would not be able to make it recognize him as its master if they didn't know its secrets, "Qinglong said.
Luo Zheng nodded. "I understand. Then I'll make it recognize him as its master now!"
At this point, Luo Zheng could not wait any longer. He grabbed the pickaxe and glanced at it. Seeing that everyone's eyes were on him, Luo Zheng smiled coldly. Suddenly, he waved the pickaxe and smashed it into the ground.
Although it did not recognize him as its master, the Black Star Pickaxe's hardness was not ordinary. Under Luo Zheng's swing, a deep hole was dug in the ground. Then, Luo Zheng jumped into the hole and released his True Yuan to envelop the surroundings.
After all, only three 1-star divine patterns were needed to undo the 10-star divine patterns on the Black Star Pickaxe. The four Divine Kingdoms also had Divine Pattern Arts inheritances and Divine Pattern Masters. He did not want this secret to be leaked.
At the bottom of the hole, Luo Zheng took out some materials and skillfully made rune ink with his hands.
The difficulty of creating a One Star Meditation Rune wasn't high. Luo Zheng had some leftover materials, so it didn't take long for him to prepare them. Then, he spread out a piece of rune paper, and a wisp of his soul flew out from between his brows. After connecting with the rune pen, he started waving the rune on the paper.
With Luo Zheng's current foundation, making a 1-star divine pattern was only a matter of seconds. After a few dozen breaths of time, the three meditation divine patterns were ready.
He held the three meditation divine patterns and compared them with the 10-star divine pattern on the Black Star Pickaxe.
Indeed, if the 1-star divine pattern was drawn in the opposite direction, it would be the same as the 10-star divine pattern.
Originally, Luo Zheng thought that the 10-star divine pattern would be very complicated. Now, it seemed that it was far from what he had imagined. It seemed that all paths led to the same destination.
"What now?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Use your Zhen Yuan to activate the 1-star divine pattern, then imprint it on the pickaxe," Qinglong said.
Following Qinglong's instructions, Luo Zheng imprinted a divine pattern on the pickaxe. The moment Luo Zheng imprinted it, the divine pattern on the pickaxe suddenly released a bright light.
Luo Zheng did not ask Qinglong again. He imprinted a second divine pattern, and then a third …
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
At this time, the Black Star Pickaxe was finally activated!
A magnificent color appeared on the surface of the pickaxe. The color was like a layer of oil that was poured into water, and it bloomed under the light.
"So beautiful!" Luo Zheng exclaimed.
"Don't exclaim. Blood, it recognizes a master. But let me remind you, this is a supreme artifact. It has its own consciousness and can judge its own master. Not everyone can be its master," Qinglong said.
"Yes! Understood! "Luo Zheng nodded.
After working for so long, even if he could not recognize a master, Luo Zheng would accept it. After all, he had done his best!
After nodding, Luo Zheng flicked his finger. A drop of blood flew towards the Black Star Pickaxe again …

Chapter 848
The drop of blood splashed onto the Black Star Pickaxe, but it didn't seep into the pickaxe. However, it didn't roll off the pickaxe, nor did it bounce off the pickaxe.
The drop of blood was like a small insect, slowly crawling on the pickaxe.
"Will it work?" Luo Zheng nervously stared at the drop of blood.
The drop of blood slowly slid down the pickaxe's head. Luo Zheng's gaze followed the movement of the drop of blood.
As the drop of blood slowly rolled down the pickaxe, Luo Zheng's heart became heavier and heavier. From the looks of it, it seemed like …
Just as Luo Zheng was feeling depressed, the drop of blood gently shook and suddenly burst open. It spread on the pickaxe's head and then completely soaked into the pickaxe!
"Did, did it work?" Luo Zheng's face revealed an expression of ecstasy!
Immediately after, Luo Zheng felt as if he had established some kind of connection with the Black Star Pickaxe. This connection was like the connection between flesh and blood.

However, the difference was that Xun, as Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit, had her own soul and thoughts. On the other hand, although the Black Star Pickaxe had its own consciousness, it didn't have its own thoughts. This was because its consciousness was only used to recognize its master. It was a relatively simple consciousness.

When Luo Zheng first saw the three ten-star divine runes on the Blackstar Pickaxe, he had high hopes for it. That was why he was so desperate to get it out of the Blackstar Pickaxe.
In any case, this Blackstar Pickaxe was a supreme artifact. Although it was a pickaxe for mining, it could also be used as a weapon! Could it be that this pickaxe could only dig through things?
Seeing Luo Zheng's disappointed face, Qinglong slowly explained to Luo Zheng, "Don't underestimate this Blackstar Pickaxe. A supreme artifact contains a unique law of causality! There are very few things it can't dig through. "

Luo Zheng heard Xun mention the law of causality, and his heart trembled.
This law of causality was extremely tyrannical! Back then, Cui Xie's Wind of Life and Death was still vivid in his mind. It was because of the law of causality. One cause, one effect. It was simply irresistible!
"Yes, and it's a unique law of causality. Only this can be called a supreme artifact," Qinglong said.
"Then what is the law of causality? What is the cause and the effect?" Luo Zheng asked.
Qinglong thought for a moment before saying, "The cause is the Blackstar Pickaxe itself, and the effect is the ability to dig through everything. Therefore, it can be said that because it digs, it digs through everything …"
Hearing Qinglong's words, Luo Zheng couldn't help but roll his eyes. What kind of law of causality was this?! In the end, it was still digging …
Qinglong probably wasn't satisfied with his explanation. However, this Blackstar Pickaxe was indeed a very special pickaxe. The blacksmith who created this Blackstar Pickaxe wanted to create the most powerful pickaxe in the universe. Originally, he didn't want to use it as a weapon.
However, just from this point alone, this law of causality was actually not bad. Since it could dig through everything, then all defenses would be ineffective against this pickaxe.
It was a pity that this was a pickaxe and not a sword. If it was a supreme artifact level sword, it would contain the law of karma that could break through everything. Who could resist the sword technique unleashed by this sword?
As for this pickaxe … since ancient times, there was no such thing as a pickaxe, right?
Luo Zheng didn't think too much about it. He worked so hard to get this Blackstar Pickaxe for the leaves on the top floor. Since this Blackstar Pickaxe was so powerful, it wouldn't be difficult for it to dig through the leaves on the 45th floor. With the status of a Divine Sea Realm martial artist, they didn't care about ordinary ores, and they couldn't dig through the leaves on the 45th floor and above. Therefore, they came to the mining tree to catch Ore Devouring Worms. They didn't kill all of the Ore Devouring Worms. These Ore Devouring Worms laid eggs on the roots of the mining tree, and new Ore Devouring Worms would hatch every year.

To put it bluntly, the Divine Kingdom Continent belonged to the four royal families. The entire continent was like the backyard of the four royal families. The Liu Family's Crown Prince was in such a state in their backyard. Who in the Divine Kingdom Continent dared to challenge the authority of the royal families?

At this moment, Zhou Huang said indifferently, "Liu Zan, since you can't control the Black Star Pickaxe, you should return it to Luo Zheng!"
The King of Sky Fortune Kingdom also took a step forward and stood beside Zhou Huang. This time, it seemed that no one was on the side of the Battle Emperor Yu the Great.
The Battle Emperor Yu the Great's eyes revealed a trace of coldness. However, he still didn't return the Black Star Pickaxe to Luo Zheng. Instead, his figure flashed and he rushed towards the pyramid.
He still didn't give up.
Even if he couldn't control the Black Star Pickaxe, he still wanted to try. After all, it was a supreme artifact. The pickaxe's strength should be much stronger than a saint artifact.
Seeing the Battle Emperor Yu the Great rush towards the 45th floor of the pyramid, the three Kings sighed. The Tianfeng War Emperor said, "Forget it. If he wants to try, then let him try."
After the Battle Emperor Yu the Great entered the narrow door of the 45th floor, he fell onto a leaf on the 45th floor.
There was a purple Ore Devouring Bug resting on the leaf. When it saw the Battle Emperor Yu the Great enter, it flapped its wings and flew towards him.
However, before the purple Ore Devouring Bug could get close to the Battle Emperor Yu the Great, he punched out. An invisible wave spread out and the purple Ore Devouring Bug was sent flying by the Battle Emperor Yu the Great's strength.
If it was in the past, the Battle Emperor Yu the Great would have been interested in catching the purple Ore Devouring Bug. However, now that he had the Black Star Pickaxe, he couldn't be bothered with it.
After chasing the Purple Ore Devouring Bugs away, he took two steps forward and raised the Black Star Pickaxe in his hand. His eyes flashed with a hint of ruthlessness as he smashed the pickaxe down heavily.
"Break!"
Within the Divine Sense Sea within his body, waves of turbulent waves surged, and the power produced by his true essence instantly erupted!
....

On the forty-fifth level of the leaf, there was a law of karma.

Because of this law of karma, it was impossible to break it with brute force. It wasn't because the Battle Emperor Yu the Great wasn't strong enough. It was because of the law of karma.
The Black Star Pickaxe had the law of karma. Most importantly, the law of karma of the Black Star Pickaxe was stronger than the law of karma of the leaf. If the two laws of karma were to fight, the Black Star Pickaxe would definitely win.
Unfortunately, the Battle Emperor Yu the Great couldn't control the Black Star Pickaxe. Similarly, he couldn't activate the law of karma of the Black Star Pickaxe.
Battle Emperor Yu the Great's heart was in turmoil.

"Luo Tianxing, be careful!" Huo Yuner hurriedly reminded him when she saw the Black Star Pickaxe flying toward him.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed and the power of the dragon scales surged out of his body. In an instant, the power of three thousand dragon scales gathered on his body. He had a faint smile on his face. He stretched out two fingers and easily caught the Black Star Pickaxe.
Seeing this, the expressions of the Divine Ocean Realm experts changed several times.
Everyone could clearly see that the power of Battle Emperor Yu the Great was not small. Even they had to use all of their strength to catch the Black Star Pickaxe.
However, Luo Zheng lifted it as if it were nothing! He caught the Black Star Pickaxe with just two fingers. This kid … How strong was he?

As for Huo Chen, Huo Yuner, Yan Wang, and Yue Ying, the Divine Ocean Realm geniuses, of course, they followed behind. Although there was the threat of the Purple Ore Devouring Bugs on the top floor, with so many Divine Ocean Realm experts in the mining tree, the threat was not great.
Luo Zheng did not enter the top floor. Instead, he went straight to the 45th floor. The first ore he needed was the Life Crystal on the 45th floor. The Life Crystal contained the power of life. He only needed one to save Ning Yudie.
The moment Luo Zheng stood on the 45th floor, many Divine Ocean Realm experts also entered. They stood on the leaves on the 45th floor, 46th floor, and the top floor!
The leaves on the 45th floor, 46th floor, and 47th floor did not contain many ores. However, almost all of them were rare treasures. Each of the ores could be used as a material for a Quasi Divine Artifact, or even a Divine Artifact …
Luo Zheng took two steps on the wide leaf and found the Life Crystal!

The Divine Ocean Realm experts discussed animatedly on the leaf. Their eyes were full of expectation.

When the two laws of cause and effect clashed, the law of cause and effect on the Black Star Pickaxe had the absolute advantage!

There were many soul cores on the 45th, 46th, and 47th level. However, after so many years, they could only place their hopes on the purple Ore Devouring Bugs. Over the years, the four Divine Kingdoms had only obtained about a hundred soul cores!
It would be fine if these soul cores could be used all the time. However, there would be wear and tear in the process of using them. For example, only thirty-six soul cores could be used this year!
In order to enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm, one must use a Soul Core to possess the corpse of a member of the Dragon Bloodline Clan. However, the Divine Sea Realm cultivators of the four Divine Kingdoms had been there for so many years. How could that be enough? As a result, the four Divine Kingdoms would argue over the distribution of soul cores every year!
Perhaps there was no need to compete this year …
All the Spirit Sea Realm warriors had the same thought. Everything was thanks to this little guy in the False Tribulation Realm!
Following Zhou Huang's instructions, Luo Zheng moved his feet and found a round ore. The shape of this ore was very unique. It was perfectly round, and it was contracting time and time again, as if it was a small heart beating.

With that thought in mind, Luo Zheng waved his pickaxe and easily broke the leaf into pieces. He then retrieved the soul core from the leaf.

Many Divine Sea Realm warriors had strange expressions on their faces. Ever since they found the way to enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm, no one below the Divine Sea Realm had ever entered. Luo Zheng's request was indeed against the rules …
However, before Zhou Huang could speak, the Sky Fortune King, who had been silent all this time, said, "Tianxing is not an outsider. He is a member of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. In the past, the number of soul cores we had was limited. Now that we have Tianxing's help, we don't have to be afraid of more people!"
Hearing the Sky Fortune King's words, Zhou Huang also said, "That's right. The number of soul cores in these three levels is probably more than 200. With Tianxing, it won't be a lot!"
Battle Emperor Da Yu snorted. Under everyone's opposition, he had no other choice. Besides, he was counting on Luo Zheng to dig out the soul cores!

Starting from the Life or Death Realm, warriors couldn't avoid the Life or Death Tribulation. However, most Life or Death Realm warriors were destined to die in this Life or Death Tribulation!
The number of Life and Death Realm warriors in the four Divine Kingdoms was innumerable, and could be counted in the tens of thousands. If the Life and Death Realm warriors that had lived for tens of thousands of years were counted, the number would be countless times more. But how many of them had reached the Spirit Sea Realm?
Ninety-nine percent of the Life or Death Realm warriors eventually died in the Life or Death Tribulation!
Everyone was afraid that the Life or Death Tribulation was too difficult, but this Calamity Flower could really increase the difficulty of the Life or Death Tribulation? Luo Zheng couldn't figure it out.
Qinglong said indifferently, "In the lower world, the Life or Death Realm can be used to ascend after reaching the Life or Death Realm. However, in the big world, the Life or Death Realm is a new beginning. It's the first step of a warrior's journey! The Life or Death Minor Tribulation is not only a tribulation, but also a baptism for the warrior! Whether it's the True Dragon World or other big worlds, the Life or Death Minor Tribulation is the most important thing! The more Minor Tribulations one passes, the more potential one has in the future! "
Warriors who reached the Life or Death Realm after going through five or six Minor Tribulations were considered the bottom class in the upper world!
Warriors who reached the Life or Death Realm after going through seven Minor Tribulations could only be considered second-class.
Among the many forces, warriors who could go through eight Minor Tribulations were enough to enter the top class. They were worthy of being nurtured by the big forces.
As for warriors who went through nine Minor Tribulations, they were the top class! After all, this kind of character would shake the world in the future and become the favorite of this era. In a big clan, perhaps only five or six of them would be born in ten million years …
Hearing Qinglong's words, Luo Zheng felt a chill in his heart. It seemed that there were no heaven-grade geniuses in the upper world. With the division of God-grade geniuses and the means of the big clans in the upper world, it was very simple to let a warrior break through to the Life or Death Realm. However, the Heavenly Tribulation was very influential and could be changed!
"If that's the case, the upper world should also pay attention to this Calamity Flower?" Luo Zheng asked.
Qinglong smiled faintly, "The upper world doesn't have a Tree of Earth. This Calamity Flower can be considered a benefit for you lower world warriors!"

Chapter 853
Many big worlds were rich in resources, whether it was cultivation methods, inheritances, or the vastness of the world.
Therefore, on the path of cultivation, warriors from the lower worlds were far from comparable to those from the upper worlds.
In the eyes of the warriors from the upper worlds, those from the lower worlds were nothing more than country bumpkins from the remote mountains. Only a few of them could advance to the upper worlds!
However, this world was relatively fair. Although the upper worlds had their advantages, the warriors from the lower worlds also had their own unique advantages. For example, the competition was not as fierce.
Like the Calamity Flower, which increased the difficulty of crossing the tribulation, warriors from the lower worlds might avoid it. However, those powerful warriors from the Life and Death Realm were hoping to enter the Spirit Sea Realm after several tribulations! How could they be willing to increase the difficulty of crossing the tribulation?
However, for warriors from the upper worlds, the Calamity Flower was an absolute treasure. Even a Divine Artifact was not as valuable as the Calamity Flower.
Luo Zheng was destined to not be limited to a small lower world. Therefore, from the False Tribulation Realm to the Life and Death Realm, he had to build a foundation from now on.
However, the more powerful a warrior was, the more difficult the tribulation would be. Ning Yudie cultivated the Nine Transformation Technique, so her strength was far beyond warriors of the same realm. Therefore, when Ning Yudie broke through the False Tribulation Realm and reached the Life and Death Realm, the minor tribulation she encountered was unprecedentedly powerful. Even the rare Nightmare Tribulation came down.
Luo Zheng was only in the middle stage of the False Tribulation Realm. His physical strength was comparable to that of a Spirit Sea Realm warrior. If a Spirit Sea Realm warrior did not use his Realm to restrict Luo Zheng, then Luo Zheng would be invincible in this world!
If he wanted to reach the Life and Death Realm, the difficulty was already unimaginable. If he added the Calamity Flower …
Luo Zheng looked at the beautiful Calamity Flower without hesitation. He walked over and smashed the Calamity Flower with the Black Star Pickaxe in his hand!
An invisible force suddenly erupted from the Black Star Pickaxe. Luo Zheng did not know if there was anything in the Upper World that could block the Black Star Pickaxe, but in this Great Thousand World, there was probably nothing that could block it. The law of causality was too overbearing.
"Clang!"
Under Luo Zheng's attack, the flower suddenly shattered. The polygonal petals were like glass, shattering into countless tiny pieces, revealing a small part of the flower's stamen.
"Pick the stamen and drink the nectar." Qing Long knew the benefits the Calamity Flower could bring to Luo Zheng. Therefore, he hoped that Luo Zheng would make good use of this opportunity.
Many Spirit Sea Realm warriors didn't leave. They stood still on the petals and watched this scene. When they smashed the flower open, they were all envious!
The flower at the top of the tree had been blooming for many years. Many Divine Sea Realm experts had tried to guess the function of this flower. However, the flower at the top of the tree was the same as the leaves. They could not pick it. But now, Luo Zheng smashed the flower and took the stamen. How could they not be envious?
Luo Zheng ignored their gazes. The stamen was a column as thick as a wrist. It was similar to the natural crystallization of some ores. However, the center of the column was filled with a light green liquid. When Luo Zheng held it in his hand, he immediately smelled an intoxicating fragrance!
"It smells so good!" Luo Zheng praised. Then, he put the stamen near his mouth and poured the light green liquid into his mouth.
When the light green liquid entered his mouth, Luo Zheng felt a strong burning sensation. The burning sensation spread from his mouth to his throat, then to his chest, and finally to his stomach! It was as if a flame was poured into his body!
Luo Zheng could endure the burning sensation. However, what followed was extremely uncomfortable! He felt as if all of his internal organs were burning, and an intense pain came from his stomach! At the same time, the True Essence in his Dantian and the Vital Spirit in the Baihui Stage were almost out of control!
The intense pain was countless times worse than what Luo Zheng had experienced when he was forging his body. Even though Luo Zheng tried his best to endure the pain, he could not help but let out a low roar!
"Ah …"
The Realm of Oblivion …
Originally, Luo Zheng could easily immerse himself in the Realm of Oblivion. However, because of the pain, he could not even enter the Realm of Oblivion. He could only roll around on the broad leaf!
Battle Emperor Yu the Great, who was standing on another leaf, sneered. "How dare you eat it when you don't know what it is! You deserve it! "
However, apart from Battle Emperor Yu the Great, Zhou Huang and the others also looked worried. They didn't know whether the flower was poisonous or not. What if it was poisonous? What if it was?
Luo Zheng rolled down from the leaf and fell straight down.
When the other people in the mining tree saw Luo Zheng fall, they all left the leaf.
As the light circle flashed, Luo Zheng's figure appeared on the side of the pyramid again. The pain in his abdomen became more and more intense. Luo Zheng's body twisted more and more. Fortunately, his body was very strong. Under the continuous rolling, his body was not injured at all. However, the ground was full of holes …
"Luo Tianxing, what's wrong?" Huo Yuner and the others saw Luo Zheng's actions. Together with King Yan, Huo Chen, and the others, they gathered around.
King Yan frowned and said, "Could he have been poisoned?"
Huo Yuner shook her head and said, "That's impossible. Even the Dragon Arsenic could not do anything to Luo Zheng. What other poison can cause Luo Zheng such pain?"
At this moment, Zhou Huang, who had just left the mining tree, answered, "He swallowed the stamen of the flower at the top of the mining tree. However, I don't know why he has such a strong reaction!"
"I think the stamen is one of the most poisonous things in the world. Anyone who consumes it will die. This fellow deserves it." Battle Emperor Yu the Great continued Zhou Huang's words.
When the Tianfeng War Emperor heard this, he said coldly, "Even if Luo Zheng dies, you don't know how to control the Black Star Pickaxe. No one in the four Divine Kingdoms can mine. What are we going to do in the future?"
This year, they had mined enough soul cores with Luo Zheng's help. However, soul cores would be depleted. As long as the four Divine Kingdoms did not control the Heavenly Star Secret Realm, they would not be able to live without soul cores. If these soul cores were slowly depleted, wouldn't they have to go back to catching Ores Devouring Bugs? In this way, they would only be able to obtain one soul core after several years. The most ridiculous time was when they did not obtain a single soul core for nine years!
"Hmph, there are so many soul cores this year. The four Divine Kingdoms have already occupied the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. In the future, we won't need any more soul cores!" Battle Emperor Yu the Great was speechless. He could only defend himself like this.
Just as the few Divine Sea Realm experts were discussing, Luo Zheng suddenly stopped rolling. Then, the aura coming from Luo Zheng's body became significantly stronger!
"Eh? Luo Tianxing's aura has become stronger! "One of the Divine Sea Realm experts said.
"He should have broken through. He is now in the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm!" another person said.
"He actually broke through at this critical moment …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's pain had indeed disappeared. However, the True Yuan in his lower abdomen had condensed into an unprecedented form! After the True Yuan was extremely compressed, the gas-like True Yuan began to liquefy!
Only Life and Death Realm experts could gradually liquefy their True Yuan! In other words, after Luo Zheng entered the late stage of the False Tribulation Realm, he began to directly break through the bottleneck of the Life and Death Realm!
The True Yuan in Luo Zheng's Dantian was continuously compressed, and the speed at which it flowed also became slower and slower …
For most Life and Death Realm experts, the process of liquefying their True Yuan was extremely slow. Moreover, the liquefied True Yuan flowed like water. However, as Luo Zheng's True Yuan continued to liquefy, it became more and more viscous. It was as viscous as glue.
"Circulating my True Yuan is so strenuous! It seems to be solidifying … "Luo Zheng frowned as he lay on the ground.
Who knew that Qinglong would say with a face full of shock, "True Yuan solidifying, this … Life and Death Realm experts are actually solidifying!"
When Luo Zheng heard Qinglong's words, a strange expression appeared on his face. "True Yuan solidifying? What do you mean? "

Chapter 854
Normally, when a martial artist liquefied their true essence in the Life and Death Realm, they would be able to store even more true essence in their dantian! But after reaching the Divine Sea Realm, they would be able to open up their own world, and their dantian would be like a vast ocean.
And after reaching a higher level, that vast ocean could even give birth to life. Those life forms could even multiply, give birth to civilizations, and even cultivate!
It was at this time that the liquefied true essence would slowly solidify, forming continents within the Divine Sea.
Logically speaking, with Luo Zheng's current cultivation, he was still far from solidifying his true essence. But, his true essence had already begun to solidify!
How could Qinglong not be surprised by this?
Even Azure Dragon, who was known for his vast knowledge, had no idea what was going on!
As the true essence within Luo Zheng's body gradually solidified, it began to rotate slower and slower. It was at this time that a dark cloud suddenly appeared in the sky!
"The sky is clear for thousands of miles. How could such a dark cloud appear out of nowhere!?"
"It's really strange. How could the sky change color so quickly?"
"That dark cloud is expanding! It's expanding so quickly! "
Everyone looked up at the strange phenomenon that had appeared in the sky.
In the originally clear blue sky, a small dark cloud suddenly appeared. That dark cloud was like a drop of ink in a pool of clear water. It began to expand at an unbelievable speed. In the blink of an eye, the entire dark cloud had covered Feather Emperor Island!
A moment ago, it was still daytime. In the next moment, dark clouds covered the sky. Everyone felt as if they were in the middle of a dark night. And that dark cloud was still rapidly expanding!
After ten breaths of time, the dark cloud had covered the entire Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean …
After an incense stick of time, the dark cloud had covered the entire continent of the Divine Kingdoms …
The vast lands of the four Divine Kingdoms were plunged into darkness. All the major cities and towns in the Divine Kingdoms lit up their oil lamps. Some of the older generation were muttering to themselves as they looked at the sky, praying that the gods wouldn't descend upon them …
Luo Zheng stared at the pitch-black sky. Suddenly, he felt a strong sense of malice! This was the malice of the heavens!
"Is this my first minor heavenly tribulation?"
Luo Zheng had experienced a minor heavenly tribulation before. When Ning Yudie underwent her heavenly tribulation, there was also a huge heavenly phenomenon. However, it only covered the area above the Cloud Hall. The dark cloud before him was so vast that one couldn't see the end of it. It was easy to imagine the power of Luo Zheng's minor heavenly tribulation!
"What a powerful aura! This is Luo Tianxing's Life and Death tribulation!" Zhou Huang sighed, "This aura to surpass my tribulation!"
Seeing this scene, the Tianfeng War Emperor sighed. "Look at this momentum. How can Luo Tianxing survive this?"
After breaking through to the Life and Death Realm, the warriors would be warned and rejected by the Heavenly Dao, and the Heavenly Tribulations would fall one after another. No matter if it was the Life and Death Realm or the Spirit Sea Realm, or even higher realms, there would be different Heavenly Tribulations waiting on them!
............
Everyone knew that Luo Zheng's strength was far beyond his peers. If he reached the Life and Death Realm, the heavenly tribulation he attracted would be even more powerful!
However, no one could imagine that Luo Zheng's heavenly tribulation would be so terrifying. It was beyond the scope of the minor heavenly tribulation of Life and Death Realm. It was even more terrifying than the major heavenly tribulation of Divine Sea Realm experts!
"Rumble …"
A ray of light was suddenly released from the dark clouds. A dragon formed by lightning was constantly circling in the dark clouds. The moment the lightning dragon lit up, the originally dark Divine Kingdom Continent became as bright as day! Everything in the world, mountains, rivers, towns, old people's faces, puppies' fur … were all illuminated at this moment!
However, this ray of light only lasted for a breath's time. In the blink of an eye, the lightning dragon's figure was hidden in the dark clouds again. In the blink of an eye, the world was immersed in darkness again.
"Rumble …"
Another loud sound came. The lightning dragon pierced through the sky again. The ray of light flickered again. It was so bright that it almost blinded the eyes of all living creatures...
. Some mortals in the towns knelt down under the lead of their clansmen. They prayed to the sky, hoping that Heaven would forgive their sins and purify their souls.
Looking at this horrifying power, Luo Zheng swallowed his saliva. There was even a trace of fear on his face! He even regretted swallowing the nectar in the stamen of the Calamity Flower.
It would be a lie if he said he wasn't afraid. Even those Divine Sea Realm experts were frightened by this level of power!
This minor heavenly tribulation was not targeted at them, but it still made them fearful. Luo Zheng was the main character of this heavenly tribulation. This heavenly tribulation that was about to descend was targeted at him, so it was natural for him to be afraid!
However, Luo Zheng's fear only lasted for a short while. Then, Luo Zheng clenched his fists. His face was resolute.
This was a heavenly tribulation!
Luo Zheng had no room to retreat. This heavenly tribulation was a baptism for Luo Zheng. It was a step he had to take on the Martial Dao!
Only by taking this step would Luo Zheng be qualified to reach the Life and Death Realm. He would advance step by step toward the Divine Sea Realm and the Upper World.
"Heaven …" Through Xun's information, Luo Zheng already knew that this heavenly tribulation was the heaven. He looked up at the sky and said, "Come!"
"Rumble, rumble, rumble …"
The thunder that resounded throughout the entire Divine Nation Continent became more and more intense. It was like a giant dragon was moving slowly above the dark clouds. It was moving step by step toward Luo Zheng, who was on the Feather Emperor Island.
"Yun 'er, Chen' er, get behind me!" The Tianfeng War Emperor said to Huo Yun 'er and Huo Chen.
At the same time, Zhou Huang also asked King Yan and Zhou Ning to stay close to him.

The other Immortal Realm geniuses didn't have the protection of Divine Sea Realm experts, so they could only hide as far away as possible …
"Father, Luo Tianxing will be fine, right?" Huo Yuner asked worriedly when she saw that.
The Tianfeng War Emperor didn't know how to answer this question. If it was an ordinary Immortal Realm genius undergoing a heavenly tribulation, they could make some judgments based on their experience. However, Luo Zheng couldn't be judged by common sense! Even the Tianfeng War Emperor didn't have the confidence to pass this heavenly tribulation. Therefore, the Tianfeng War Emperor couldn't give Huo Yun 'er an optimistic answer.
Seeing that her father didn't say anything, Huo Yun 'er didn't ask any more questions. Everyone had an answer in their heart. Huo Yun' er also knew how difficult this heavenly tribulation was.

The other Divine Sea Realm experts were shocked. Luo Zheng was finished …
"Boom!"
Another bolt of lightning pierced through the dark clouds. The lightning dragon was already above Luo Zheng.
It's coming …
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He began to extract the vital essence from his dantian bit by bit. Due to the solidification of the vital essence, Luo Zheng found it more difficult to extract the vital essence. The solidified vital essence was difficult to circulate, so he had to get used to it bit by bit! Over time, Luo Zheng's meridians became much thicker than those of ordinary warriors!
"Body-protection vital essence …"
A faint purple vital essence shield enveloped Luo Zheng.
At the same time, Luo Zheng began to spread out his consciousness to communicate with the stars …
"Stellar Battle Body …"
Above the dark clouds, two beams of starlight pierced through the thick clouds and shone on Luo Zheng's body!
"This is starlight! The power of the stars! What kind of cultivation method is Luo Zheng cultivating? " A Divine Sea Realm expert was surprised. This Divine Sea Realm expert was a warrior who visualized the stars. However, no matter how hard he visualized, no matter how realistic the stars were, he couldn't trigger the starlight! He couldn't figure out how Luo Zheng did it. How could he trigger the stars that were billions of miles away? This was simply too miraculous!
Looking at the starlight on Luo Zheng's body, many Divine Sea Realm experts were speechless. When the West Dragon River changed, they thought Luo Zheng had shown all his strength. But now, it seemed that Luo Zheng still had a huge trump card hidden in his body …

Chapter 855
Even though Luo Zheng used his True Essence to protect his body and activated his Starry Combat Body, he didn't feel at ease at all. His eyes were still full of coldness.
Previously, when Ning Yudie was going through her Life and Death Tribulation, the first wave of Thunder Tribulation was the weakest. However, the following waves of Heavenly Tribulations were each stronger than the last!
Now, the first wave of Thunder Tribulation was already so terrifying. What about the following waves of Heavenly Tribulations?
Luo Zheng didn't dare to imagine and didn't want to think about it!
Even if he couldn't avoid it, he could only deal with it as it came!
When the Lightning Dragon was getting closer and closer to Luo Zheng, the dark clouds above his head suddenly rotated slowly. In the process of rotation, a hole appeared in the center! A ray of sunlight strangely shone through the hole. This scene was very bizarre!
However, that ray of sunlight only lasted for a blink of an eye. Then, it was covered by a blazing white light. Soon, a dragon claw formed by lightning stretched out from the hole!
Many Divine Sea Realm warriors looked at the huge dragon claw. Their eyes flashed and their hearts were filled with awe.
When the dragon claw reached out from the hole, it suddenly tore!
The thick layer of dark clouds was directly torn apart. However, in the eyes of the onlookers, it was as if the sky was directly torn apart!

"Roar!"

This lightning dragon was as big as a mountain range! However, the moment it rushed down, its body shrank rapidly. However, shrinking did not mean that its power had weakened. Instead, the power of this lightning dragon was constantly increasing!
"The Heavenly Tribulation is a tribulation from the heavens. It cannot be avoided. The only thing I can do is to resist this dragon …"

The power continued to flow into Luo Zheng's body.
One thousand pieces …
Two thousand …
Three thousand pieces …
Four thousand …

This Minor Heavenly Tribulation was not limited to one wave. Getting injured now would not be a good thing. Therefore, even if Luo Zheng was facing this Lightning Dragon, he had no choice but to advance!
The power of four thousand dragon scales poured into Luo Zheng's body. Suddenly, all the bones in his body made cracking sounds. His entire body suddenly doubled in size. Luo Zheng was like a giant. He stood in place, facing the Lightning Dragon in the sky alone!

Ten thousand feet, one hundred feet, one hundred feet!

When a martial artist cultivated, regardless of whether they followed the Heavenly Dao's rules or established their own rules to resist the Heavenly Dao, they had to cross this hurdle.
The Heavenly Dao was the nightmare that lay above every martial artist's head!
If he wanted to become stronger, if he wanted to step onto a higher peak of martial arts, he could not relax the momentum in his heart no matter what. Even if he had to face a calamity from the heavens, he had to grit his teeth and go against it!

Normally, a martial artist's Protective True Yuan wouldn't be particularly strong. Luo Zheng also didn't practice any cultivation methods related to Heavenly Dao. Facing this level of Heavenly Dao tribulation, his Protective True Yuan was instantly shattered!

The 'Stellar Combat Body' was a transcendent divine might. Even though Luo Zheng had only cultivated to the second form, the might it displayed could not be underestimated.

I fear that the 'Stellar Combat Body' won't be able to resist it … "Luo Zheng's eyes were indifferent. At this time, he had already entered a state of complete concentration. Once the starlight disappeared, he could only rely on his mortal body to resist this Lightning Dragon!
Crackle crackle crackle …
Violent arcs of lightning tightly wrapped around Luo Zheng's body. It was like the hands of a death god were caressing Luo Zheng, wanting to drag his life into hell!
Soon, the power of the stars finally disappeared. The two stars on the horizon also disappeared into the clouds.
Then, a bone-piercing pain spread through his body!
Luckily, Luo Zheng's body was extremely tyrannical. If it were an ordinary late False Tribulation realm martial artist, then once they lost the protection of their mortal body, they would have been instantly swallowed up by this powerful Heavenly Dao Tribulation lightning, leaving not even ashes behind!
Even for Luo Zheng, black marks began to appear all over his body. As the dense lightning struck him, his entire body instantly turned pitch black, as if he were a coal miner that had just crawled out of a mine!

This scene was extremely shocking in the eyes of many Divine Sea Realm martial artists! It was as if Luo Zheng had really torn a True Dragon into two halves with his bare hands!

"What should I do …" Luo Zheng's face was a bit distressed.

Among all the Origin Laws, the Law of Time was the most difficult, followed by the Law of Space. Thunder, Fire, Ice, Metal … these Origin Laws were relatively easier to comprehend!
Because Luo Zheng had been baptized by the Law, he could quickly comprehend even the most difficult Law of Time, let alone the Law of Thunder, which was much easier than the Law of Time.

Chapter 856
Yes, comprehend the Law of Thunder and control the Law of Thunder! Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat.

Luo Zheng had comprehended the first layer of the Law of Thunder on the True Dragon Clan's obelisk. This was the foundation of Luo Zheng's Law of Thunder!

The first layer of the Law of Thunder was destruction …
The second layer was purification, and the third layer was Heaven's Tribulation …
When ordinary warriors advanced to the Life and Death Realm, the Life and Death Tribulation they triggered would often have the third layer of the Law of Thunder. Probably because of this, the third layer of the Law of Thunder was called Heaven's Tribulation!
However, the Lightning Dragon Luo Zheng was facing contained the fourth layer of the Law of Thunder.
He quietly looked at the Lightning Dragon, but his mind was spinning rapidly. The profound Law contained in the dazzling lightning was slowly being unraveled by Luo Zheng!
The higher one's affinity with a Law, the faster one could comprehend the Law. After experiencing the baptism of the Law, it wasn't difficult for Luo Zheng to comprehend the high-level Origin Laws. However, the low-level Laws weren't too difficult for him to comprehend!

One breath …
Ten breaths …
Twenty breaths …

Although the fragments of the Law of Thunder from the first layer to the fourth layer were constantly pouring out in his mind, Luo Zheng had already completed one-third of the puzzle in a short period of time. However, his body couldn't bear it anymore. Luo Zheng felt that his body was getting weaker and weaker. If he continued to stand here and let the Lightning Dragon strike him, his body would be destroyed before he could fully comprehend the fourth layer of the Law of Thunder!

In the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, the huge amount of water continued to churn. Waves splashed, and finally, a huge tsunami was formed! Chapter 857
The Heavenly Tribulation was a tribulation sent down by the heavens. This lightning dragon belonged to the Heavenly Dao, so it was actually an ownerless object!

From this perspective, the Heavenly Tribulation was difficult for warriors, but it was also an opportunity to strengthen themselves.
Therefore, from the Life and Death Realm, every Life and Death Tribulation was an experience of life and death. At the same time, it could make one stronger!

White Brow Daoist's eyes flickered. He stood still and observed carefully.

In White Brow Daoist's opinion, a genius like Luo Zheng was nothing in a Big Realm, not to mention the three major human forces and tens of thousands of Big Realms.

Luo Zheng sat where he was, still digesting the power of thunder and lightning in his body. The pure power of thunder and lightning spread inside Luo Zheng's body, differentiated, and poured into each of his cells, stimulating his physical body to grow rapidly!
Any Power of Law was not only used for destruction. For example, the Law of Fire originally symbolized destruction, but it contained the power of rebirth and nirvana.
The Law of Thunder could bring vitality to destruction. As the saying went, only when the spring thunder shook the earth could it wake up all hibernating things and rejuvenate the natural world. This was the beginning of life!
It was said that after opening the Spirit Sea and solidifying the Zhen Yuan, thunder and lightning had to be constantly introduced into the Dantian. Under the stimulation of the power of thunder and lightning, life could be born in the Dantian. This was the fourth level of the Law of Thunder. "Shape!"
Soon, his physical body seemed to be reborn. The layer of carbonized skin on the surface began to crack constantly, revealing the bright red and tender flesh. When the carbonized skin completely fell off, Luo Zheng's physical body looked like it had just been born from a mother's womb.
"Will there be a second wave?"
After safely passing through the first wave of tribulation, Luo Zheng looked up at the sky.
If it was an ordinary warrior, when breaking through to the Life and Death Realm, most of them would only face the first wave of tribulation! The stronger the talent, the more waves of tribulation and the more powerful it would be. Even those god-tier geniuses often faced more than one wave of tribulation. Numerous golden swords appeared in the sky. Each sword's edge was shining with a dazzling cold light. One thousand swords, ten thousand swords, one hundred thousand swords, one million swords …

Chapter 858
Luo Zheng took a deep breath when he saw the golden swords gathering around him. His relaxed muscles tensed up again!
These golden swords were formed from the condensation of laws and didn't contain any Heaven's Evolution Essence. Therefore, Luo Zheng couldn't determine the grade of these swords. However, judging from the cold light emitted from the edges of these swords, they were definitely not weak.
After a short while, the golden cloud moved without any wind. The first golden sword hummed softly and suddenly shot towards Luo Zheng!
Then, the second, the third …
The cloud formed by the golden swords suddenly turned into a golden torrent that surged towards Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng took a step forward and stood on the spot. He was like a sea stabilizing needle that was firmly nailed to the ground. He looked at the golden torrent with resolute eyes.
"Back then, when I underwent the Metal Tribulation, I caught the 'Demon Heart Battle Armor' and used more than 200,000 supreme-grade True Origin Stones to escape. These swords are even sharper than the ones I faced back then. This kid doesn't have the protection of the Demon Heart Battle Armor …" The Divine Sea Realm almighty who had undergone the Metal Tribulation shook his head and said.
The Demon Heart Battle Armor was a Quasi Divine Artifact protective armor. As a defensive treasure, its value was comparable to an ordinary Divine Artifact. This Divine Sea Realm almighty had borrowed it from the Tianfeng War Emperor.
Hearing this, the Tianfeng War Emperor smiled helplessly. "If the Demon Heart Battle Armor was still here, I could lend it to Luo Tianxing. Unfortunately …"
The Demon Heart Battle Armor had been destroyed decades ago.
As he spoke, the golden torrent had already rushed towards Luo Zheng!
"It's coming!"
Looking at the first golden sword, Luo Zheng grabbed it with one hand. The golden sword was instantly crushed by Luo Zheng!
"In the end, these aren't real swords. They're only formed from the condensation of the Metal Laws. Their strength isn't even comparable to a Divine Artifact." After crushing the first golden sword, Luo Zheng understood.
However, the number of golden swords suddenly increased. Luo Zheng couldn't crush them one by one. He could only let the golden swords stab him from all directions!
Luo Zheng was like a person who couldn't swim in a large river. In the blink of an eye, he was submerged in the golden torrent.
"Ding ding ding ding ding ding ding —"
Luo Zheng's body was as tough as a Divine Artifact. Every golden sword that hit Luo Zheng's body turned into golden powder. The fine powder quickly turned into bits of the Metal Laws and dissipated into the air.
However, although these golden swords were not as tough as Luo Zheng's body, their edges were extremely sharp. Each of them left an extremely fine scratch on Luo Zheng's body. As more and more golden swords continued to stab at Luo Zheng, more and more scratches appeared on his body. When the number of scratches accumulated to a certain extent, the edges of the swords cut open Luo Zheng's skin, causing blood to spurt out!
After resisting to this extent, Luo Zheng sighed softly and closed his eyes …
Thousands of miles away, on the west side of the Divine Kingdom Continent, where people rarely went, stood a towering mountain. Suddenly, invisible sword energy appeared and began to erode the vast mountain range!
The mountain, which was thousands of feet high, was being eroded at a speed visible to the naked eye. It was as if an invisible mouth was gnawing at the mountain. After a few breaths, the mountain no longer existed. It turned into specks of dust and drifted in all directions.
One tower, two towers, three towers ….
After the time it took to burn a stick of incense, the mountain range disappeared into thin air.
The golden swords on the Feather Emperor Island disappeared as well.
To other people, crossing a tribulation was like walking between life and death. It was called a tribulation of life and death. But to Luo Zheng, it wasn't that difficult.
No matter how powerful this tribulation was, as long as it didn't cut Luo Zheng's connection with the Great Thousand World, it couldn't kill Luo Zheng!
A tribulation that could destroy the heavens and earth did exist. In the Upper Realm, when some super powerful figures crossed a tribulation, the power they unleashed was enough to destroy a Great Thousand World.
After the "Metal Tribulation", the "Wind Tribulation" appeared in the sky. The Five Elements Tribulation appeared one after another …
After an hour, Luo Zheng looked at the clear sky and blinked his eyes. "Is it finally over?"
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt relaxed. During the process of crossing the tribulation, the True Essence in Luo Zheng's dantian was still gradually solidifying. Just as Luo Zheng was about to stand up and leave, the shadow of a pure white lotus flower appeared in the sky! However, the lotus flower was still budding and hadn't bloomed yet!
"What's that?"
"Why did a huge lotus flower appear in the sky?"
"Is this also a tribulation? It doesn't look like one! "
Those Divine Sea Realm experts were astonished. They stared at the shadow of the lotus flower in the sky in a daze.
They didn't know, but Daoist White Eyebrows knew very well!
Therefore, when White Brow Daoist saw the shadow of the budding lotus, his expression changed abruptly, and his voice trembled. "It really appeared. A lotus flower bloomed in the minor heavenly tribulation. A lotus flower bloomed. It really bloomed! This … this … "
When Bai Yuerong saw this, her beautiful eyes blinked and she fell into deep thought.
Through some top-secret documents in the Esoteric Sect, she naturally knew about the existence of the lotus flower.
The so-called geniuses in the Upper Realm were classified differently from those in the Lower Realm. There was no such thing as an Earth-Grade genius, a Heaven-Grade genius, or a God-grade genius. The only way to measure a warrior's potential was through crossing a tribulation.
According to the documents, the lotus flower seemed to be a product that surpassed the Heavenly Way. If a lotus flower appeared after crossing a tribulation, it meant that the warrior had the potential to surpass the Heavenly Way.
Of course, it didn't mean that one could surpass the Heavenly Way with a lotus flower. In the 100,000 Great Realms, there were many warriors with lotus flowers. However, how many people could surpass the Heavenly Way in 3.6 billion years?
"But according to the documents, lotus flowers are usually only born in a major tribulation. Why … did Luo Zheng give birth to a lotus flower in a minor tribulation?" Bai Yuerong stared at Luo Zheng in a daze. Although she and Luo Zheng were enemies, with the appearance of the lotus flower, she became more and more curious about Luo Zheng.
Therefore, Bai Yuerong said, "I wonder how many petals Luo Zheng can bloom. Three petals or five petals?"
Daoist White Eyebrows shook his head and said, "A warrior who can bloom three petals is enough to become the core of a Nine Great Holy Land. Five petals, hehe, is enough to enter a tenth-grade Holy Land and become the core of a super-first-rate force! Saintess, I'll have to trouble you to report this matter through the Reverse Spirit Channel! "
Bai Yuerong nodded. As the Saintess of the Secret Sect, she was very clear about the importance of this matter. Since Luo Zheng had bloomed a lotus flower during his tribulation, the conflict between her and Luo Zheng was probably nothing …
Hearing Bai Yuerong's words, the almighty Divine Sea Realm warriors felt a chill in their hearts!
Although these almighty Divine Sea Realm warriors were awe-inspiring in the Divine Kingdom Continent, and they might be famous in the lower realms, compared to the powerhouses in the upper realms, they were nothing!
Although they were the kings of seventh-grade Divine Kingdoms, the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea was only an eighth-grade Holy Land of the Dragon Bloodline Clan. They had spent so many years and still hadn't completely occupied it! Then what was a ninth-grade Holy Land? What was a tenth-grade Holy Land? It was simply unimaginable!
At this moment, the pure white lotus flower above Luo Zheng's head slowly rotated. After a while, a pure white petal slowly bloomed from the budding lotus flower!
The moment the petal fell, a fragrant wind spread out and drilled into everyone's nose!
"This lotus flower is so fragrant!" Huo Yuner couldn't help but sniff. Although the fragrance was rich, it wasn't greasy. Taking a deep breath, the fragrance seemed to be able to seep into one's soul.
"The first petal," Bai Yuerong said while biting her lips. She knew about this lotus flower from literature, but after all, this was the first time she saw a warrior bloom a lotus flower. Seeing this scene, she was actually looking forward to seeing more petals of this lotus flower.
Luo Zheng stood in place and looked at the slowly rotating lotus flower in the sky. Through Qinglong, he already knew what this lotus flower represented. At this time, he also quietly looked at the rotating lotus flower with eyes as pure as water.
As the lotus flower continued to spin, another petal fell down.
"The second petal …" Bai Yuerong continued to count.

Chapter 859
If three petals bloomed, it would be enough to become the core of a Rank Nine Sacred Ground …
"I knew this kid wasn't simple. If he could ascend to the Upper Realm, he would be a big shot in the future!" Zhou Huang chuckled.
However, Battle Emperor Yu the Great's voice was heard again. "Hmph, so what? No matter how talented this kid is, he's still trapped in the Lower Realm just like us! "
These words made Zhou Huang frown. However, what White Brow Taoist said next made the hearts of the Divine Ocean Realm cultivators pound.
"If this kid can bloom enough petals, it'll be enough for the humans of the Upper Realm to pay attention to him. They'll probably pay a huge price to repair the Ascension Passage to the Upper Realm!" White Brow Taoist said calmly.
