At this moment, all the Divine Ocean Realm cultivators were unable to remain calm.
They had worked so hard to reach the Divine Ocean Realm, but they were trapped in the Lower Realm, unable to advance to a higher realm. One could imagine how much of a blow it would be to a warrior.
However, the Ascension Passage being destroyed was something they couldn't do anything about. This wasn't something the Divine Ocean Realm cultivators could interfere with!
If the Ascension Passage was repaired, it meant that they could ascend to the Upper Realm, pursue higher challenges, and achieve greater individual strength … Perhaps after entering the Upper Realm, they would face more difficult tests and more struggles between life and death.
However, a warrior could stand proudly in the world for a while, but he couldn't live an ignoble life forever! Moreover, a higher cultivation meant a longer lifespan.
The voices of several Divine Ocean Realm cultivators were filled with excitement. One could even hear their voices trembling …
The three Divine Ocean Realm cultivators of the Profound Jade Martial House were among them. They were extremely excited and stared at Luo Zheng with complicated expressions. From the moment Luo Zheng stepped into the Profound Jade Martial House, they were looking forward to Luo Zheng's performance. However, they never thought that Luo Zheng's talent would be tied to their fates.

"Sir, Sir … How many petals do we need to open in order for our family to pay attention to us and help us repair the ascension tunnel?" a Spirit Sea Realm warrior asked.
This was a very crucial question. Everyone was so excited that they forgot to ask.
White Brow Daoist thought for a moment and said, "Maybe seven, at least seven! However, I wasn't sure if a flower with seven petals could be repaired. But if it had nine petals, then it would definitely be repaired! When the petals of the first layer fully bloomed, there were nine petals! In other words, the legendary Nine-leaf Lotus Flower … Hehe, but it's difficult for even one of the countless Holy Lands in a Great Realm to be born. "As he spoke, Daoist White Brow shook his head." It's too difficult. Even if it's a cultivator who has attained the Seven-leaf stage, there are only a few hundred of them in a Great Realm. Some of the weaker Great Realms have even fewer. "
Upon hearing White Brow Daoist White Brow's words, many Spirit Sea Realm warriors looked disappointed.
A Great Realm was unimaginably huge. There were only a few hundred cultivators who had attained the Seven-leaf stage in a Great Realm. The probability was pitifully small.
Moreover, according to White Brow Daoist White Brow, even if the Seven-leaf Lotus Flower was opened, the Upper Realm might not pay attention to it. Only with a Nine-leaf Lotus Flower could the ascension tunnel be repaired with a 100% certainty!
The flames of hope were finally ignited in everyone's hearts, but White Brow Daoist White Brow poured a bucket of cold water on it. The Spirit Sea Realm warriors felt a chill in their hearts …
Just as they were feeling disappointed, Huo Yuner smiled and said, "Look! The third petal! The third petal … "
Although everyone was disappointed, they still had some hope. Although the possibility was so small that it was negligible, they did not believe that a cultivator from the Lower Realm could fight against countless geniuses in the Great Realm! They were not on the same level at all!
"The third petal," Bai Yuerong said calmly. She looked at Luo Zheng in a daze, and her gaze became more and more complicated. She thought of the time when this guy released bad luck on her through the Wheel of Fortune.
White Brow Daoist White Brow nodded calmly. "Mhm, not bad. A Three-leaf Lotus. This kid should be proud. When I was undergoing my tribulation in the Spirit Sea Realm, I only attained three leaves."
"Supervisor, you're a Three-leaf cultivator?" a Spirit Sea Realm warrior asked.
White Brow Daoist White Brow's face revealed a hint of pride. "That was during my first tribulation in the Spirit Sea Realm. I only attained three leaves. During my second tribulation, I attained five leaves!"
Attaining five petals was indeed something to be proud of. The Spirit Sea Realm warriors in front of them had never seen such a thing before …
The Three-leaf Lotus above Luo Zheng's head was still spinning slowly. Everyone was waiting quietly. To the four Divine Nations, the number of leaves Luo Zheng's Lotus Flower was even more important than exploring the Heavenly Star Secret Realm! After all, they could think of ways to explore the Heavenly Star Secret Realm every year. This was the first time they saw a cultivator with a Lotus Flower during a Heavenly Tribulation!
Huo Chen, who had been observing in a daze from the side, could not help but shout, "The fourth petal! Open! Open!"
Huo Yuner, who was beside him, smiled and said, "Brother Chen, this isn't something you can shout …"
However, who knew that as soon as Huo Yuner finished speaking, the fourth petal would tremble slightly and slowly open!
"Since the fourth petal has already been opened, the fifth petal will definitely open. This Luo Tianxing has already far surpassed me." White Brow Daoist White Brow sighed and smiled.
As expected, after the fourth petal opened, the fifth petal also fell!
At this moment, all the Spirit Sea Realm warriors' breathing began to quicken …
This Lotus Flower had an odd number of petals. Since the fifth petal had already been opened, Luo Zheng only needed one more petal to open and he would have attained seven leaves!
Attaining the ninth leaf was too difficult. However, attaining seven leaves was enough to attract the attention of the humans in the upper realm. There was a high possibility that they would help repair the ascension passage! As long as one more petal was opened …
"One petal, just one petal will do. Luo Tianxing, do your best!" Zhou Huang muttered.
"Who knew that this brat would mysteriously become our hope?" The Tianfeng War Emperor shook his head and said, "I hope he succeeds!"
Many Spirit Sea Realm warriors were also praying softly. At this moment, they were afraid of disturbing Luo Zheng and all of them kept their voices as low as possible. In truth, Luo Zheng was not the one who controlled the opening of the Lotus Flower. Even if Luo Zheng had nothing to do with fighting, he would open as many petals as he needed. However, these Spirit Sea Realm warriors were too cautious because they placed too much importance on this matter.
Under everyone's anticipation, the Lotus Flower continued to slowly spin. However, the petals did not fall.
"One petal, just one petal … I beg you," a Spirit Sea Realm warrior sobbed. His face was filled with agony. That Spirit Sea Realm warrior's temperament and elegance had completely disappeared. He seemed like a pitiful woman begging for mercy. However, he did not know who he was begging to. To Luo Zheng? To the Heavens? Or to the Lotus Flower?
The other Spirit Sea Realm warriors did not laugh at him. If begging was useful, they would put aside their pride and beg for mercy. However, this was not the time to be concerned about their pride.
Luo Zheng blinked his eyes and looked at the Lotus Flower in the sky. He did not know that because of him, he had caused an uproar at the Pyramid. Everyone was waiting for the Lotus Flower.
After waiting for a long time, Luo Zheng simply sat down with his legs crossed and closed his eyes. Qinglong had told him that the number of petals he could open was up to fate. It was not something he could control with his own will. There was no point in being anxious.
The problem was that although Luo Zheng thought this way, the other Spirit Sea Realm warriors did not. They did not know the origin and function of the Lotus Flower, but they knew that it would work if they were sincere. At such a critical moment, Luo Zheng did not pay attention to the Lotus Flower. Instead, he closed his eyes and meditated indifferently. How could this be?

Chapter 860
Battle Emperor Yu the Great had never been in such a predicament before. Previously, he was overbearing and wanted to kill Luo Zheng many times.
However, at this moment, he wanted to ask Luo Zheng to be more serious. Although being serious wouldn't be of much use …
Battle Emperor Yu the Great's ambition and desire were the strongest compared to the other three Divine Monarchs. Because of this, he wanted to ascend more than anyone else. The greater the expectation, the greater the disappointment. Hence, his emotions fluctuated even more violently.
Previously, Battle Emperor Yu the Great felt that Luo Zheng didn't have a high chance of reaching the seventh leaf. However, when he saw that Luo Zheng had already opened five leaves, he was filled with anticipation.
However, the enmity between him and Luo Zheng was too great. It wasn't convenient for him to show himself. He could only beg Zhou Huang.
Because of the previous incident, Zhou Huang had become enemies with Battle Emperor Yu the Great. The Tianfeng War Emperor had almost fought with Battle Emperor Yu the Great because of this incident. However, because the four Divine Nations had been living in harmony for so many years, everyone held back.
When Zhou Huang saw Battle Emperor Yu the Great's expression, he snorted coldly. He stepped down from the pyramid and walked toward Luo Zheng.
He wasn't doing this for Battle Emperor Yu the Great. He was doing this for the sake of all the Divine Sea Realm experts in the four Divine Nations.
Luo Zheng hadn't completed his tribulation yet. Zhou Huang didn't know if he could get too close to him. He maintained a distance of several hundred feet from Luo Zheng. Then, he whispered to Luo Zheng, "Tianxing, this … the lotus flower has opened. Isn't it inappropriate for you to meditate by yourself?"
When Luo Zheng heard Zhou Huang's words, he opened his eyes. He looked at Zhou Huang curiously and asked, "My Lord, what do you mean by inappropriate?"
Zhou Huang didn't know how to remind Luo Zheng. After thinking for a while, he said, "I mean, can you be more serious?" As he spoke, he pointed at the slowly spinning lotus flower in the sky.
"Serious?" At this moment, Luo Zheng finally understood Zhou Huang's words. They probably felt that he shouldn't meditate at this moment. He should be serious.
The problem was, how could he be serious?
He had no control over how many leaves the lotus flower could open. That was what Qinglong had told him: he could open as many leaves as he wanted!
However, when he saw Zhou Huang's expression, Luo Zheng felt a little embarrassed. He chuckled twice and stood up again. He stared blankly at the lotus flower in the sky. His heart was incomparably puzzled. What did it have to do with them how many lotus flowers I opened?
It was just that Zhou Huang had been taking care of Luo Zheng for a long time, so he could only pretend. How could he know that the number of lotus leaves that were opened was already tied to the fates of these Spirit Sea Realm warriors!
The lotus flower was still spinning slowly, neither too fast nor too slow. It did not disappear, nor did it open another lotus leaf.
As everyone waited, they became more and more anxious …
"Open it! Hurry up and open it! "
"Sigh, can't you be more straightforward? This kind of people's hearts are broken! "
"I've been waiting here for half an hour. It feels like I've been waiting for a year …"
The lifespan of a Spirit Sea Realm warrior was calculated in tens of thousands of years. In their lives, one hour was just like a white colt passing by. However, this time, the white colt that had passed by was unwilling to take the first step. Therefore, in their eyes, this two hour period was extremely long!
After an hour, the lotus flower suddenly trembled!
"Open, open, open … Is it going to open?! It's opening! "Seeing the movement of the lotus flower, a Spirit Sea Realm warrior roared.
If a Spirit Sea Realm warrior really roared in excitement, his voice would be extremely powerful. The Spirit Sea Realm warriors present were still fine, but the Immortal Realm geniuses were all dizzy. Even Luo Zheng could not help but cover his ears.

"It's opening! It is indeed open … "Zhou Huang murmured. He was also very excited, but he still lowered his voice in the midst of his excitement.
On the lotus flower, the sixth lotus leaf slowly fell down. The pure white petals stretched out, blooming an indescribable beauty in the eyes of everyone!
Huo Yuner sat on the steps of the pyramid. His slender body relaxed, but his eyes were blurred as he stared at the petals. He said in a low voice, "This lotus flower is really beautiful …"
"Great, there's hope for this Ascension Passage …"
"After so many years, I thought we would all die in this big wide world! I didn't expect that hope would come like this … "
Many Spirit Sea Realm warriors were overwhelmed with emotions. It was as if there were flames burning in their chests!
However, at this moment, Daoist White Eyebrows poured a bucket of cold water on them. "As I said, there's hope for opening the Ascension Passage by opening seven lotus leaves. However, the probability is only about 30%. Seven lotus leaves are already rare in the world, but repairing the Ascension Passage from the Upper Realm is also a troublesome matter! I can't guarantee it! "
The Ascension Passage was usually repaired from the Lower Realm. If one wanted to forcefully repair it from the Upper Realm, it would be the so-called "reverse repair". This reverse repair was hundreds of times more difficult than repairing it from the Lower Realm. Indeed, one would have to pay a higher price to succeed.
"30, 30% … Supervisor, please help us. We can …" Battle Emperor Yu the Great was originally immersed in joy, but at this moment, he suddenly became nervous.

We've been searching for the Ancestral Dragon egg for so many years, but you still can't get it. This big wide world is actually not worth the Upper Realm's attention! "
"This …" Battle Emperor Yu the Great was immediately choked by Daoist White Eyebrows' words.
The difference between the Lower Realm and the Upper Realm was like the difference between a capital and a small mountain village. What would they care about in the Lower Realm? Even if it was a divine weapon, they probably wouldn't care about it!
However, Daoist White Eyebrows said again, "But don't worry, I'll help you. Besides, we still have to rely on the Holy Maiden to convey this news to the Upper Realm!"
As soon as he finished speaking, many Spirit Sea Realm warriors stared at Bai Yuerong in unison. Bai Yuerong nodded seriously and said, "I'll try my best …" In fact, Bai Yuerong also knew that she was just a mouthpiece. The person who could really decide this matter was the supervisor.
"The seventh leaf is open!"
While everyone was talking, the seventh lotus leaf was put down.
The number of lotus flowers bloomed was an odd number. Since the sixth leaf was opened, the seventh leaf would definitely be opened. This was not unexpected.
At this moment, a Spirit Sea Realm warrior let out a long sigh. "Sigh, since the seventh leaf has already opened, why don't we just open the ninth leaf …"
Daoist White Eyebrows also nodded, but he snorted with a smile. "Yes, if this Luo Zheng can open the nine-leaf lotus flower, the Upper Realm will have no reason to not pay attention. No matter the price, they'll have to repair this Ascension Passage!"
As soon as he said this, many Spirit Sea Realm warriors began to look forward to it …
It was not that they were greedy, but the Ascension Passage was too important. There was only a 30% chance of opening the seventh leaf, but opening the ninth leaf was a sure thing. It was impossible for them not to look forward to it.
However, Daoist White Eyebrows said again, "Unfortunately, the probability of a nine-leaf lotus flower, hehe … It's already amazing for a large realm to have one. When Daoist White Eyebrows said this, many Spirit Sea Realm warriors finally calmed down.
Of course, they were looking forward to it, but the probability was so small that their expectations couldn't be too strong. They had a feeling of resignation!
A warrior with a nine-leaf lotus flower couldn't even be born in a large realm, so how could it be born in a Lower Realm?
Many Spirit Sea Realm warriors also gradually calmed down. They regained the bearing that a Spirit Sea Realm warrior should have and looked at the slowly rotating lotus flower indifferently.
Very quickly, another two hours passed. Suddenly, the lotus flower trembled slightly again!

Chapter 861
Previously, every time the lotus flower trembled, a leaf would appear. Now that the lotus flower was trembling again, could it be that another leaf was about to appear?
White Brow Daoist's words had extinguished the hopes of those Spirit Sea Realm experts. But this shock immediately drew everyone's attention back to the battle!
Didn't Daoist White Brow say that there wasn't even a single martial artist in a large world that could open a nine-leaf lotus flower? Such a character would definitely alarm the Upper Realm!
As for the ascension passage, it would be repaired no matter how great the price was!
Even Daoist White Brow's expression became incomparably solemn. His gaze was like lightning as he stared fixedly at the trembling lotus flower!
Compared to those Divine Sea Realm experts, he had a clearer understanding of this lotus flower and what it meant to have a nine-leaf lotus flower.
When a martial artist cultivated, they could follow the heavens and absorb the laws of the Heavenly Dao step by step. From the lower level laws to the origin laws, then to the higher level laws, which was the law of cause and effect …
However, if one followed the heavens, they would eventually encounter a considerable bottleneck. They wouldn't be able to transcend this cycle of reincarnation, nor would they be able to transcend the shackles of the Heavenly Dao!
Only a martial artist who cultivated in defiance of the heavens could surpass the Heavenly Dao and achieve greater achievements!
Not all martial artists could surpass the Heavenly Dao. Even if a nine-leaf lotus flower was opened, it only meant that a martial artist had the potential and talent to do so. Only such a person could be called a genius in the Upper Realm!
The meaning of the word genius was different in different places.
A genius in the Lower Realm referred to a person with talent. It was a person who had a unique talent in a certain aspect!
And a so-called genius in the Upper Realm referred to a creature who had the talent to surpass the Heavenly Dao!
A martial artist who opened a nine-leaf lotus flower was able to bring out the potential of surpassing the Heavenly Dao to the limit. Such a character, placed in any race or force, was an existence like a supreme treasure.
Compared to the Nine-Petal Lotus Flower, the baptism of the Laws was nothing. So, although he was surprised that Luo Zheng was able to comprehend Huo Yuner's Laws of Time in such a short period of time, it was only worthy of his attention, and it was far less shocking than the Nine-Petal Lotus Flower!
The group of Divine Sea Realm cultivators heard White Brow Daoist's words and understood that the Nine Petal Lotus Flower was very powerful. Even a big world couldn't produce such a person.
The problem was that this group of Divine Sea Realm experts had never been to the Upper Realm. They didn't have a very direct understanding of a large realm or a hundred thousand large realms. It was like a person from the capital telling a remote mountain village how big and how rich the capital was. The person from the remote mountain village would only have a rough idea. Even if this group of Divine Sea Realm experts used their imagination, it would still be very difficult for them to perceive how big a large realm was and how rare a Nine-petaled Lotus Flower was …
They did not care about this. All they knew was that once the Nine-Petal Lotus was activated, the ascension passageway would definitely be repaired. That was enough!
The nine-petaled lotus flower was still trembling slowly. Luo Zheng's eyes were fixed on the huge lotus flower as he quietly looked at it. In all fairness, he naturally hoped that all the leaves of the lotus flower would open. However, Qinglong had told him that it was not up to him to decide how many petals he could open.
The number of leaves almost determined the height a person could reach in a big world. This was a preview of a cultivator's fate.
Luo Zheng did not have a clear understanding of the strength of big worlds, Realm Lords, and Heavenly Venerables. However, he understood the strength of that person in the Void Spirit Sect. That person's strength was probably an existence that surpassed Heavenly Venerables. He had only met Luo Yan for a short time before he was taken away by that young man. If he wanted to see Luo Yan again, he could imagine the height he had to reach!
Even if he activated the Nine-Petaled Lotus Flower, it did not mean that he could reach the height he wanted …
Under Luo Zheng's gaze, the Lotus Flower trembled continuously. One of the last two leaves on the Lotus Flower was finally released slowly.
"It's open, it's all open! It looks like we've finally endured all these years! "
"The eighth leaf is open, hahahaha … We don't have to seek those unorthodox methods anymore. We can ascend openly!"
At this moment, a Divine Ocean Realm expert's eyes were brimming with tears of excitement!
In order to break through to the Divine Ocean Realm and ascend, this group of Divine Ocean Realm experts who had lived for tens of thousands of years had done everything they could.
They went overseas to seek opportunities, risked their lives, and went to the edge of the Great Thousand World. They dreamed of crossing the world barrier, entering another Great Thousand World, and then ascending …
In the deep sea, they hunted some extremely ferocious creatures, hoping to find some special methods from them to help them break through …
In these long years, they had spent too much time. Their goal was to become stronger and ascend!
Even Divine Sea Realm Almighties were not invincible in this large world. In the process of searching for these opportunities, many Divine Sea Realm Almighties had fallen and paid the price with their lives. In the end, they still died with grievances.
But who would have thought that today, they would be able to fulfill their lifelong dream – to repair the ascension passage?
However, these people never dreamed that the Northern Heaven Domain, which was extremely barren in their eyes, had a Rising Dragon Platform three years ago in the Central Domain. This Rising Dragon Platform originally belonged to the Dragon Bloodline Clan of the Sky Feather Sacred Ocean. Using this Rising Dragon Platform, they could ascend to the Great Thousand World.
Because the Rising Dragon Platform ascended to the Great Thousand World, they were imprisoned for a thousand years!
A thousand years was a long time, but compared to the long lifespan of Divine Ocean Realm experts, it was nothing. Exchanging a thousand years of lifespan for ascension was definitely a good deal …
Under the constant trembling of the lotus flower, the eighth leaf finally descended slowly.
After the eighth leaf descended, the ninth leaf also slowly descended under the gazes of the crowd …
The nine leaves had finally fully bloomed. This perfect lotus flower slowly rotated in the air.
Luo Zheng's mouth curved into a smile. Although the difficulty of ascending to the Life and Death Realm this time was not small, and the process was a little complicated, the result was pretty good.
Everyone also understood that everything was settled. They let out a sigh of relief in their hearts, and a feeling of joy filled their chests.
However, just when everyone thought that it was over, the lotus flower unexpectedly trembled once again!
"Eh?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly. He stared at the lotus flower, and a blank expression appeared on his face. The nine leaves had already bloomed, so he didn't know why the lotus flower was still trembling.
"Supervisor, what's going on?" A Divine Ocean Realm expert asked.
White Brow Daoist's face was also full of astonishment. Logically speaking, when the nine leaves bloomed, the lotus flower should have maintained its perfect state. However, it was still trembling, as if something was trying to break free from the lotus flower. What was going on?
For a moment, White Brow Daoist couldn't figure it out. The expression on his face could only be described as "baffling."
Bai Yuerong looked at the lotus flower and gently bit her lips. In her mind, she was memorizing the top-secret literature …
The history of Esoteric Religion was very long. It was said that the inheritance of Esoteric Religion could be traced back to the last Evolution Era. In other words, Esoteric Religion existed before many creatures were born in this Evolution Era. When an Evolution Era ended, the Heavenly Dao would be destroyed. However, in this universe, there were always powerful people who could continue the inheritance. Some inheritances could transcend time, time, and even the Heavenly Dao.
Therefore, although Bai Yuerong wasn't as knowledgeable as White Brow Daoist, as a Saintess of Esoteric Religion, she could come into contact with some information that White Brow Daoist himself couldn't access.
The lotus flower was divided into nine leaves. The nine leaves represented the nine treasures of the lotus flower …
However, this was only the first layer of heaven. It was the Lesser Realization Lotus Flower. If one could break through the Heavenly Barrier, they would enter the Mahayana Realm and open up 27 heart sources. That would be the second layer of heaven …
Thinking of this, Bai Yuerong's heart suddenly beat faster. She blurted out, "Supervisor, is Luo Zheng going to open the second layer of heaven? Is it the Mahayana Lotus Flower? "
She wasn't sure, so she could only say this to White Brow Daoist.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 862
Although Daoist White Brow didn't know what was going on, it didn't mean that he didn't know what the Mahayana Lotus Flower was!
However, he never thought that the kid in front of him could really open the second layer of heaven and enter the Mahayana Lotus Flower realm.
This was because in Daoist White Brow's knowledge, this was simply impossible.
This lotus flower was also divided into Hinayana and Mahayana. Hinayana could only reach nine leaves, while Mahayana could reach twenty-seven leaves …
There were many cultivators who opened the Hinayana Lotus Flower, but it was hard to find a single person who could open the Hinayana Lotus Flower to nine leaves in a great realm. This was already extremely rare. However, according to an era, which was 3.6 billion years, the number of cultivators who could open the Hinayana Lotus Flower was definitely not small.
However, cultivators who could open the Mahayana Lotus Flower …
In these tens of thousands of years, perhaps someone in a corner of a great realm had opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower. After all, the Upper Realm was really too big. One couldn't blame Daoist White Brow for being ignorant.
However, from what Daoist White Brow knew, there wasn't a single one!
He only heard that Celestial Venerable Ling Xiao of the Firmament Realm had opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower after crossing the Reincarnation Tribulation 2.6 million years ago. As for whether other Celestial Venerables had opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower, Daoist White Brow wasn't sure.
That was a treatment that only Celestial Venerables had. Could this kid in front of him really open it?
Daoist White Brow's eyes revealed an unprecedented seriousness. If the Mahayana Lotus Flower was still under his control, Daoist White Brow had completely given up now. This was because he didn't know what it meant. The only thing he knew was that if this matter was reported through the reverse spirit channel, it would probably cause an unprecedented sensation!
He felt that his throat was a little dry. It had been many years since the Upper Realm sent him down to supervise the four Divine Nations in retrieving the Ancestral Dragon egg!
Daoist White Brow was clear about his fate. He was afraid that only by retrieving the Ancestral Dragon egg would he be qualified to return to the Upper Realm.
If he could unearth a cultivator who had opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower, his fate would probably change, and he might even obtain unimaginable benefits.
However, could Luo Zheng really open the second layer of heaven?
White Brow Daoist was still very doubtful. This possibility was so small that it was almost negligible …
However, after thinking about it carefully, it wasn't impossible. The Nine-Petal Lotus Flower was the limit of the Lesser Realization Lotus Flower. If one counted all the cultivators who had opened the Nine-Petal Lotus Flower 3.6 billion years ago, and then counted the cultivators who had opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower, this probability wouldn't be too low!
The Nine-Petaled Lotus Flower trembled more and more violently in the air, and the speed at which its leaves spun also became faster and faster.
Luo Zheng had no idea what was going on. He could only ask Qinglong in his mind, "Qinglong, hasn't the Nine Petal Lotus finished blooming? Could it be that this thing can germinate and grow a second flower? "
Qinglong's laughter sounded in his mind. "Hahahaha, your idea is too weird. How can you grow a second flower? It seems that you're about to open the second layer of heaven. You have the fate of Battle of the Grand Worlds, so it's indeed possible. Let's wait and see!"
"What on earth is the second layer of heaven?" Luo Zheng was still puzzled.
Qinglong explained indifferently, "This is the difference between the Lesser Realization Lotus Flower and the Mahayana Lotus Flower. You will understand in a while."
As soon as Qinglong's voice fell, a stamen slowly rose from the center of the Nine-Petal Lotus Flower. Strangely, there was a small flower bud in the center of the stamen.
Under the watchful eyes of the crowd, the stamen rose higher and higher, and the flower bud continued to expand at a speed visible to the naked eye. In a short while, the flower bud had already surpassed the nine-petaled lotus flower, and layers of leaves tightly wrapped around it!
"There really is a second Nine-Petal Lotus Flower. I was right," Luo Zheng said with a chuckle.
"How can you be right? This is just a Nine-Petal Lotus Flower … "Qinglong retorted.
When that even bigger lotus flower slowly rose, the lotus leaves on top of it began to open one by one, one by one …
"One, two, three, four …" Huo Chen looked at the leaves and counted continuously.
Not long after, the bigger Nine-Petal Lotus Flower had opened a total of 26 petals. Only one petal had not bloomed, and it made people feel that it was slightly incomplete.
The two layers of Nine-Petal Lotus Flower bloomed and slowly floated in front of everyone, emitting a sweet fragrance. Dazzling streamers escaped from the petals.
"This person …" Bai Yuerong sighed.
White Brow Daoist immediately said, "He at least has the destiny of a Battle of the Worlds. In this Great Era that has descended, he may not be the absolute protagonist, but he can at least play an important role!"
"A Battle of the Worlds …" Bai Yuerong originally wanted to say this. How could she not know that a Great Era had descended? Otherwise, how could the four Divine Kingdoms produce so many god-grade geniuses? In the history of the Divine Kingdoms, this had never happened before!
Although she knew that a Great Era had descended and that many god-grade geniuses had appeared, Bai Yuerong had never imagined that a warrior with the destiny of a Battle of the Worlds would appear in this Great Thousand World.
At this moment, Bai Yuerong suddenly remembered what had happened in the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb. In the Wheel of Fortune in the Heavenly Mist Immortal Tomb, Luo Zheng's luck had been very bad at the beginning, or it could be said that it had been very bumpy. However, after he had overcome it, he had been the first to rise and pass the Wheel of Fortune!
When she thought of this and saw the Mahayana Lotus Flower that Luo Zheng had opened, Bai Yuerong's guess was verified …
No wonder his luck was so strong. No wonder … Bai Yuerong muttered to herself.
The Tianfeng War Emperor had a smile on his face. He had already shifted his gaze away from the Lotus Flower above his head. At this moment, he was sizing up Luo Zheng. In the eyes of the Tianfeng War Emperor, this Luo Zheng was becoming more and more pleasing to the eye. He was becoming more and more perfect!
The Tianfeng War Emperor had a short temper and a straightforward personality. Most warriors who cultivated the Law of Fire had such a personality. Since he had something in his heart, he couldn't hide it. At this moment, he looked at Huo Yuner, who was sitting beside him. She was at the age where she was about to blossom. Her entire body was brimming with a youthful aura. Although warriors had a long lifespan, she still needed to find a dual cultivation partner!
The Tianfeng War Emperor was extremely picky. In fact, many Divine Sea Realm Almighties had proposed marriage to him, but he had rejected all of them. In the eyes of the Tianfeng War Emperor, among the younger generation of warriors in the four Divine Kingdoms, there was no one who was worthy of his daughter!
Although this Luo Zheng's origin was unclear and he seemed to be from a small place like the Tianbei Region, even the War Emperor himself couldn't imagine how great this kid's future would be if the War Emperor placed so much importance on him. Such a character couldn't be missed.
Therefore, the Tianfeng War Emperor opened his mouth and asked, "Yuner, what do you think of this kid?"
The Tianfeng War Emperor didn't know how to avoid the topic. When he spoke, there were many people around. Although Huo Yuner was innocent and naive, she was already at this age. How could she not understand the meaning behind her father's words?
Her beautiful eyes were originally fixed on Luo Zheng. When the Tianfeng War Emperor spoke, her face turned red. She retracted her gaze and said coquettishly, "Father, what are you saying?!"
The Tianfeng War Emperor chuckled. He knew that his daughter was shy. However, this wasn't a problem that could be solved by being shy. If he missed this opportunity, there wouldn't be another one. He had to find a chance to ask Luo Zheng directly.
When the conversation reached the ears of the other Divine Ocean Realm experts, a trace of understanding appeared on their faces. This Old Huo's brain was indeed fast! If she could become Luo Zheng's Dual Cultivation Partner, with this Luo Zheng's talent, his future would be immeasurable!
Therefore, the other Divine Sea Realm warriors had their own plans as well, especially those who had daughters and were both talented and beautiful …
The lotus flower in the sky slowly rotated. Finally, its color dimmed and it slowly disappeared from everyone's sight.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng sighed and said, "Alas, I thought that the last leaf would open!"
In his mind, Qinglong sneered and said, "Dream on. You should be satisfied that the Mahayana Lotus Flower opened to this degree. Moreover, after this lotus flower appears, it will appear every time you undergo a tribulation …"
Luo Zheng nodded and walked towards the pyramid. He still had a large amount of precious ores to process!

Chapter 863
The thin line appeared abruptly in the sky. However, as soon as it appeared, everyone, including Luo Zheng himself, froze on the spot as if they had been immobilized.
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a great power! This power seemed to have completely surpassed the limits of living creatures!
Everyone felt that their bodies had been frozen. Their blood had stopped flowing and their hearts had stopped beating. Only their consciousness was still moving slowly. However, they felt that their souls were as insignificant as dust under this thin line …
"What's that …"
"How can there be such a powerful thing? It's just a thin line, but I can feel such a great power!"
"I, I'm too insignificant. I'm just living an ignoble life …"
The mighty warriors of the Divine Sea Realm had lived for tens of thousands of years. They had a long lifespan, extensive knowledge, and mature thinking. Needless to say, their hearts of martial arts were as firm as iron walls!
However, under the power of that thin line, some of the mighty warriors of the Divine Sea Realm showed signs of collapsing …
The power of that thin line was aimed at Luo Zheng. The others were still some distance away from Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng himself bore the brunt of it!
Luo Zheng suddenly raised his head and stared at that thin line with a cold expression. "Qinglong, what's that …" Luo Zheng didn't think too much. At this moment, he felt that he was facing a life or death situation. Therefore, he asked Qinglong at once. At least, he wanted to know if he had a way to break it!
However, Qinglong slowly said, "I don't know …"
Even Qinglong hadn't seen this thing before. It indeed couldn't answer Luo Zheng's question.
But soon, Luo Zheng knew what it was.
The thin line continued to extend, extending for several meters in the sky. Then, the thin line split into two, becoming two thin lines. After the two thin lines separated, the ends were still connected, forming an arc that outlined the shape of an eye. In the center of the eye, there was only a black eye!
That was an eye.
There were tens of thousands of species in the universe. Every species had a different appearance. However, there was one thing that couldn't be separated from — eyes!
"Eye, whose eye is this?" Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes. His face was full of caution. He didn't feel any kindness from this eye. On the contrary, he felt a trace of fierce hostility from this eye!
No, maybe it shouldn't be called hostility. Luo Zheng thought indifferently, "I'm not qualified to make it hostile. I'm too insignificant … This eye appeared out of curiosity. That fierce hostility should come from instinct. As long as it wants, it can kill me in an instant!"
Luo Zheng's eyes met the eye as he stood there quietly!
After a while, the eye suddenly blinked, and a black ray of light shot out from the black pupil!
Then, the eye blinked again and disappeared in the sky. However, that black light didn't disappear. Instead, it flew in a certain direction. Then, it kept refracting in the sky and kept approaching Luo Zheng!
Daoist White Eyebrows and the other Divine Sea Realm experts didn't say anything at this moment. Their faces were pale as they looked at the black light refracting in the sky …
Luo Zheng had just bloomed. In the hearts of the Divine Sea Realm experts, Luo Zheng was their hope to ascend to the upper world. Now, if anyone wanted to take away Luo Zheng's life, they would protect Luo Zheng and kill him! It could be said that in their eyes, Luo Zheng had become the key to ascending to the upper world. He was also the core and the most important person.
But at this moment, they didn't move!
That black light that kept refracting and changing directions was too terrifying. They couldn't even think of resisting!
"Buzz …"
The black light suddenly extended to a distance of several feet. Then, it changed directions again at an angle of 30 degrees and extended to another 70 or 80 feet. It refracted in different directions, but it was getting closer and closer to Luo Zheng. It was only a matter of time before it shot toward Luo Zheng!
How could he resist this black light …
Luo Zheng didn't have much time left. He knew that he couldn't resist this black light. His Protective True Essence couldn't, his Divine Weapon Body couldn't, and his Starry Combat Body couldn't. The only thing he could rely on was the will of the Great Thousand World!
"Can I only use my strength to resist this black light? Can I use the entire Great Thousand World to resist it?" At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly had a crazy idea!
Given the strength of that eye, it could destroy the entire Great Thousand World with just a wave of its hand …
Now, Luo Zheng had only one way left!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's mind began to spin rapidly. He was crazily absorbing the memories in his mind, the memories projected by the will of the Great Thousand World!
At this moment, he included the memories of all the mountains in the Great Thousand World in his mind!
Mountains, mountains …
Eastern Region, Purgatory Mountain, Jade Dragon Mountain, the 33 peaks, and the vast mountains in the north of the Eastern Region.
Central Domain, Western Mountain Range, Hundred Thousand Mountains, Nine Dragons Mountain.
The entire North Heaven Region had a total of 730,000 mountains of various sizes …
Divine Country Continent, 1.3 million mountains …
Sea God Continent, 860,000 mountains …
Apart from this, there were also numerous mountain ranges on barren islands …
There was also a Savage Land that was even larger than Divine Country Continent, and more than two million mountains …
In total, there were more than five million mountains!
With a thought, Luo Zheng calculated all the mountains!
After thinking for a bit, Luo Zheng excluded the Eastern Region. Just before the black light rushed toward Luo Zheng, he activated his strength transfer!
"Since I am already the representative of this Great Thousand World, I can be considered to have the right to control this Great Thousand World. Please help me pass through this disaster," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
The "you" Luo Zheng spoke of was actually for the Great Thousand World's will.
Although Luo Zheng had merged with the will of the entire Great Thousand World, he still couldn't be considered the master of this Great Thousand World. He couldn't resist this black light, and could only rely on the Great Thousand World to resist.
Of course, the will of the Great Thousand World was the collective body of all the creatures in the Great Thousand World. It wasn't a single person, nor did it have a specific thought process. For example, the little monster was the embodiment of the Great Thousand World's will, but even the little monster didn't know about this.
Luo Zheng said this only out of respect for the creatures of the Great Thousand World, and also out of respect for himself!
As soon as his voice fell, the black light already shot toward Luo Zheng.
Everyone present could only watch this scene in a daze. After seeing this black light, no one could think of resisting, because it was simply impossible to resist!
In an instant, the black light hit Luo Zheng's chest and quickly sank into it. In the next moment, Luo Zheng's entire body, including his clothes, lips, the whites of his eyes, and fingernails, turned black in an instant!
After this black light drilled into Luo Zheng's body, it instantly wrapped up every cell in Luo Zheng's body and then destroyed them! If someone could see inside Luo Zheng's body, they would find that his brain, internal organs, bones, blood vessels, and every drop of blood had turned black …
This was a method of obliteration. After obliterating Luo Zheng in this way, not even a strand of hair or a single cell in his body would be left in this world. It was a complete disappearance, a million times cleaner than burning bones and scattering ashes!
After a breath, an extremely terrifying power was released!
At the same time, every part and cell in Luo Zheng's body began to bear a huge impact of power!
Just as the energy of this black light began to bloom, the first mountain began to collapse!
"Boom …"

Chapter 864
The two Snow Flood Dragons' nests were built in this mountain, but in an instant, the entire mountain disappeared. Caught off guard, the two Snow Flood Dragons started falling down from the sky. Although flying was an easy task for the Snow Flood Dragons, as they had awakened their intelligence, they couldn't understand what was happening in front of them. Why would a perfectly fine mountain suddenly disappear?
But, what happened next shocked the two Snow Flood Dragons. The towering mountains in front of them were disappearing at an incredible speed. Every time a mountain started collapsing, it would disappear in an instant!
"Rumble, rumble, rumble …"
The western region of the Central Domain was uninhabited. The mountains here were endless, and there were more than 50,000 to 60,000 of them. With so many mountains, even a Void Tribulation warrior would need a lot of time to travel across them.
But, right at this moment, all the mountains were destroyed in just a few breaths of time …
This strange scene didn't only happen in the western region of the Central Domain, all the mountains in the Central Domain were being destroyed!
On top of a 300 meter tall mountain south of Tianqi City, the three Alliance Chiefs of the Merchant Alliance were hosting a meeting. The participants were all the investors of the Merchant Alliance. Currently, only the World Business Alliance was the biggest organization in the entire Central Domain. However, Shi Kefan didn't have the ambition to establish a Sacred Land. Although the Merchant Alliance was a powerful organization, they were still businessmen at heart.
But, just as the crowd was about to gather, the small mountain under their feet suddenly started collapsing from the middle.
Everyone was shocked, and before they could react, the ground beneath their feet disappeared, and the entire small mountain was destroyed in an instant …
"Is … is this an enemy attack?" A Divine Core warrior screamed out. Although the shadow of the Kingdom of Evil Deities had disappeared, it had still left a deep impression on many warriors.
"Attack my ass!" Yan Yueshan who was floating in the air cursed.
Many warriors could destroy a mountain, and Yan Yueshan and the others could do the same. It was difficult to destroy a mountain, but the problem was, they couldn't destroy a mountain in an instant! This kind of divine ability had gone beyond their imagination!
Even worse was that along with an even more intense sound of collapse, the 1,500m tall White Named Mountain outside Heaven Awaken City had suddenly vanished ….
In Tianqi City, as long as one raised their head towards the south, they would be able to see the black shadow of Bai Ming Mountain. This mountain was like a giant, and it was guarding the southern region of Tianqi City. Right at this moment, the black shadow suddenly disappeared!
"Bai, Bai Ming Mountain is destroyed as well …" Yan Yueshan swallowed his saliva......... Everyone's eyes were filled with fear, but they had no idea what was happening....
............
Thousands, tens of thousands, hundreds of thousands of mountains. Regardless of whether it was a 300-meter-tall hill or a 3000-meter-tall mountain, they were all annihilated by an irresistible force.
..........................
The mountains in the entire Great Wide World were constantly being annihilated.
Luo Zheng was still covered in a black shadow, as if he had just crawled out from a black swamp. He stood still, but his mind was still spinning rapidly. Through the new memories in his mind, Luo Zheng was trying to figure out how many mountains were left in the Great Thousand World!
5 million mountains …
4 million mountains …
3 million mountains …
2 million mountains …
1 million mountains …
"Even if all the mountains are destroyed, they still can't resist the power of this black light?" Luo Zheng's eyes flickered.
He could feel the black light's power was still being unleashed, but he had no idea how much power it contained. But, Luo Zheng still had a powerful backing, the ocean and the continent …
Just now, Luo Zheng didn't choose the ocean as the target of the transfer of power!
Previously, in order to resist the power of the Heaven's Tribulation, Luo Zheng transferred most of the power to the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea, which set off a huge tsunami. It was only because the mighty warriors of the Divine Sea Realm intervened that the tsunami was stopped. Otherwise, the loss of the four Divine Kingdoms would be immeasurable. Those warriors might not be too afraid of the tsunami, but the civilians wouldn't. The casualties would be extremely heavy.
If the power of this black light was transferred to the ocean of the Great Thousand World, it would probably cause an unimaginable tsunami, perhaps a tsunami of tens of thousands of feet. Under such a tsunami, whether it was the Sea God Continent, the Divine Kingdom Continent, or the Central Domain, they would all be destroyed. Ninety-nine percent of the creatures would be destroyed. This was the reason why Luo Zheng didn't choose to transfer the power to the ocean.
Luo Zheng also didn't want to transfer the power directly to the continent. If he transferred the power to the Divine Kingdom Continent, it would fall apart in an instant, no matter how vast the Divine Kingdom Continent was!
But …
If Luo Zheng had no choice, he seemed to have to do this.
But this wasn't something he wanted to see!
Although the path of a warrior was one where the strong preyed on the weak, Luo Zheng didn't pursue strength alone. He didn't want to blindly pursue power, and he didn't want to sacrifice hundreds of millions of creatures to achieve his goal.
This didn't mean that Luo Zheng was kind-hearted, benevolent, righteous, and compassionate. It was just that he didn't want to lose his last trace of humanity and break free from the so-called law of the jungle like a wild beast.
Five hundred thousand mountains …
Luo Zheng clenched his teeth, and his brows were deeply furrowed.
Three hundred thousand mountains …
The speed at which the mountains were being annihilated was far beyond Luo Zheng's imagination!
Two hundred thousand mountains …
At this moment, Luo Zheng's heart was beating faster and faster. He was about to face a difficult choice. Should he die? Or should he let hundreds of millions of creatures die?
This multiple-choice question was very simple for many martial artists. But to Luo Zheng, it was too difficult …
One hundred thousand mountains …
Fifty thousand mountains …
Ten thousand mountains …
If all these mountains were annihilated, and the power of the black light still didn't weaken, Luo Zheng might have to use the mountains in the Eastern Territory.
He thought about the 33 peaks where he used to live. There were countless disciples cultivating there, and most of them were Xiantian lifeforms who couldn't fly. If those mountains were annihilated, who knew how many people would die?
Just as Luo Zheng's face became more and more unsightly, he finally felt the power of the black energy begin to weaken …
This black light came with great momentum, but it also left quickly. Almost in the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng's originally black body began to change, and the black energy quickly disappeared!
Luo Zheng's body seemed to be washed clean, and soon returned to normal.
"I made it through …" At this time, Luo Zheng's face didn't have the slightest trace of joy. Instead, it revealed a trace of sadness.
He had indeed made it through without paying too much of a price, but the whole Vast Thousand World had paid the price in Luo Zheng's place.
Although there weren't many creatures in those mountains, there were five million mountains. The number of creatures sacrificed, including humans, was definitely not small …
"Luo Zheng, he withstood that black light. Even so, it couldn't kill him. This guy is really …" Bai Yuerong was so shocked that even breathing became difficult.
Many Divine Sea Realm experts were also silent. They knew Luo Zheng could transfer power. That little monster of the Nine Cauldrons Martial House had demonstrated this ability in front of them more than once, but no one knew what the limit of this ability was. Could it be that he could transfer power without limit?
These Divine Sea Realm experts didn't know the origin of that black light, but as far as they were concerned, it was easy to annihilate so many Divine Sea Realm experts here. Even if there were a hundred times more Deep Sea Realm experts here, they still wouldn't be able to resist that black light!
Even so, Luo Zheng could still resist such an attack. What did he sacrifice? Where did he transfer the power to?
There were no mountains on the Feather Emperor Island, so the Divine Sea Realm experts didn't know that all the mountains on the continent of the Divine Nation had disappeared …
Zhou Huang couldn't help but be curious. He asked, "Tianxing, I know you used your power to transfer power, but … where did you transfer the power to?"
Luo Zheng blinked and then answered, "Mountains, mountains …"
"Mountains? How many mountains? "Zhou Huang asked curiously.
"The whole world," Luo Zheng said sadly.

＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 865
The whole world …
Many Divine Sea Realm cultivators were speechless …
"In other words, all the mountains in the four Divine Kingdoms have been destroyed?" Zhou Huang asked again.
"Not only the four Divine Kingdoms, but also the Central Domain and other places. Almost all the mountains in the Great Thousand World have been destroyed," Luo Zheng answered truthfully.
In fact, when Luo Zheng said that he would transfer the power to the mountains, many Divine Sea Realm cultivators already had a rough idea.
When a Divine Sea Realm cultivator attacked, such as War Emperor Yu the Great, the transfer of power by Luo Zheng would be enough to destroy a mountain range …
The power emitted by the black eye was so terrifying, and the power contained in the black light was unimaginably powerful. If all the power was transferred to the mountains, how many mountains would it take?
There were many mountains and mountain ranges in the entire Divine Kingdom. No one had done a specific count, but with a rough estimate, there should be at least a few hundred thousand mountains, right?
So many mountains, were they not enough to offset the power contained in the black light?
How many mountains would it take to offset the power? One thousand? Ten thousand? Or one hundred thousand?
The correct answer was close to five million …
All the mountains in the world, except for the ones in the Eastern Domain, were destroyed.
Those Divine Sea Realm cultivators were silent for a while. Then, their expressions changed drastically. In the end, they were only shocked. They could not estimate the impact this would have on the whole Great Thousand World.
What was the limit of Luo Zheng's transfer of power?
He could transfer power to the whole Great Thousand World at will. Could it be that he represented the whole Great Thousand World?
Finally, someone came to this conclusion. Of course, they still could not guess that Luo Zheng had merged with the will of the world. From a certain point of view, Luo Zheng was the whole Great Thousand World!
"What is that black eye?" Tianfeng War Emperor let out a long sigh and asked in a low voice.
The power of one move could destroy the mountains of the whole world. What was that thing …?
The most important thing was that the owner of the eye must have thought that Luo Zheng was too insignificant to block the Black Ray's killing blow. Therefore, after releasing the Black Ray, he left this world.
Just a casually released ray of black light could destroy all the mountains in the world. If he released a few more rays of black light, wouldn't he be able to destroy the entire Upper-Class World?
What kind of existence was that? Everyone found it hard to imagine …
"I don't know." Luo Zheng shook his head. Even Qinglong couldn't answer this question, so naturally, Luo Zheng couldn't.
However, White Brow Daoist said, "I don't know what it is, but to have such power, such a powerful existence, it has to be at least at the level of a Celestial Venerable. Perhaps it is a Celestial Venerable!"
"At least at the level of a Celestial Master?" Luo Zheng suddenly understood.
Heavenly Venerate powerhouses were top-notch existences even in the universe. Destroying a Great Wide World wasn't too difficult for them.
However, the Azure Dragon expressed the opposite opinion in his mind. "It doesn't have to be a Celestial Venerable to be able to do this. A Realm Lord from the Upper Realm is enough to destroy a Greater Thousand World. However, whether it's a Realm Lord or a Celestial Venerable, they can't do this so easily. I can't determine the origin of this eye …"
"I see." Luo Zheng nodded.
He couldn't determine the origin of this eye. Who was it? Why did it target him?
No matter what, with the support of the whole world, Luo Zheng could be considered to have survived a disaster! As for the last strange black eye … it could be considered a part of the Heavenly Tribulation.
Of course, Luo Zheng was not a foolish person. He was also vigilant. Luo Zheng shouldn't have any powerful enemies in the Upper Realm. There must be a reason why the black eye targeted him. What was the reason that his blooming lotus alarmed it? Or was there some other reason? No matter what, he had to be very careful in the future.
Now that Luo Zheng had stepped into the Life and Death Realm, the slowly rotating True Essence in his body was as viscous as glue. This way, his Dantian could bear more True Essence, and the True Essence itself was more condensed. His strength had also increased by a lot.
After completing the breakthrough in his cultivation, Luo Zheng set his goal on the Heavenly Star Mystery Territory. He had promised to help Qinglong obtain the Ancestral Dragon egg, so he had to make this trip no matter what. However, before he set off, Luo Zheng had to deal with the things in his hands first …
However, when Luo Zheng approached the pyramid, he felt a very strange atmosphere. Everyone looked at him in a completely different way.
Most of the Divine Sea Realm experts believed the words of Battle Emperor Yu the Great and the Saintess. They were well aware that Luo Zheng did not come from the four Divine Kingdoms. Although they were not as hostile as Battle Emperor Yu the Great, they were definitely not close to Luo Zheng. Instead, they felt like they were trying to sort him out.
However, there was a trace of kindness in everyone's eyes now. It was as if they were completely treating Luo Zheng as a family member …
Being stared at by dozens of old monsters like this, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a chill in his heart. He suddenly suspected that these Divine Sea Realm experts had taken the wrong medicine.
Of course, Luo Zheng did not know that he had become a treasure in the eyes of these Divine Sea Realm experts. At this time, Luo Zheng had become the key to opening the Ascension Passage. Not only that, Luo Zheng, who had opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower, had a bright future in the Ascension World. It was definitely not a bad thing to have a good relationship with Luo Zheng.
While feeling this chill, Luo Zheng said, "Okay, now it's time to distribute the minerals that we have excavated!"
Luo Zheng did not intend to take all the precious minerals that were excavated from the top of the mining tree. First of all, he was not a blacksmith. Although most of the minerals were precious, they were not of much use to Luo Zheng. However, Luo Zheng had asked Qinglong to identify them. Some of the minerals could be used to create high-level divine runes. Luo Zheng would keep these things.
As for the other things, Luo Zheng intended to let Zhou Huang distribute them. After all, he was in the territory of the four Divine Kingdoms. Although these things were excavated by Luo Zheng himself, it was unrealistic for him to take all of them for himself.
However, as soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, a Divine Sea Realm expert said with a smile, "Distribute? Little friend Tianxing, those minerals were excavated by you alone. It doesn't seem appropriate to distribute them to everyone like this, does it? "
"That's right." Zhou Huang grinned and said, "There are many minerals among them. They are all priceless. How can we just give them to others? However, we do need some of them. Tianxing, sell them to us at a price!"
"This …" Luo Zheng was speechless. He deeply suspected that these Divine Sea Realm experts had taken the wrong medicine. Why did their attitudes take a 360-degree turn after he transcended a tribulation?
Could it be that they were plotting something …
Taoist Bai Mei and Bai Yuerong were both quietly sizing up Luo Zheng. The news about Luo Zheng must be reported through the Reverse Spirit Channel. However, now was not the time. It was better to wait until Luo Zheng had processed the minerals.
Luo Zheng couldn't find an answer after thinking for a while. He even communicated with Qinglong in his mind for a while. Qinglong couldn't figure out what these guys were thinking either. After all, Qinglong didn't know that Luo Zheng was bound to the Ascension Channel.
Unable to figure it out, Luo Zheng simply ignored this problem. Instead, he took out the minerals one by one from the Sumeru Ring.
Previously, when Luo Zheng was excavating, many Divine Sea Realm experts were eagerly watching the whole process. They knew very well what kind of minerals Luo Zheng had.
There weren't many minerals in the 45th to 47th layers, but each of them had a peculiar shape.
For example, the first crystal that Luo Zheng took out. When he took it out of the Sumeru Ring, it slipped through Luo Zheng's fingers like water and fell to the ground. However, the blue liquid soon merged with each other and formed a small ball that kept bouncing on the ground.

Chapter 866
Luo Zheng had once learned from Xun that even in the Upper Realm, True Origin Stones were one of the main circulating currencies.
Since these Divine Sea Realm experts were stupid and were willing to give him True Origin Stones, how could he refuse?
However, what Luo Zheng didn't expect was that as soon as he finished speaking, a Divine Sea Realm Almighty laughed and said, "I cultivate the Water Law, and I happen to need this Water Core. How about I buy it for 30,000 top-grade True Origin Stones?"
Thirty thousand top-grade True Essence Stones was a considerable amount. Luo Zheng didn't hesitate, and the first transaction was concluded just like that.
There were many precious ores in his ring. This batch of ores was worth around 30,000 to 50,000. There were even a few that went up to 100,000!
One had to know that a Divine Kingdom's treasury would only have around 200,000 to 300,000 top-grade True Origin Stones. However, the Divine Sea Realm experts were usually rich and could afford this price.
Therefore, after Luo Zheng valued the ores one by one, he estimated that he had around 3 million top-grade True Origin Stones.
This place could be considered to be as rich as a nation, and as rich as a God Nation …
After processing the ores, Luo Zheng took out the "Soul Cores" and gave them to Zhou Huang.
These "Soul Cores" were the key to entering the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. Luo Zheng didn't have much use for them. He only needed to ensure that he could enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm.
However, Luo Zheng was still very interested in how to use these Soul Cores.
These Divine Sea Realm experts came to Feather Emperor Island to kill Ore Devouring Bugs in hopes of obtaining one or two Soul Cores. However, they unexpectedly obtained more than 100 Soul Cores. This time, there were enough spots for the exploration of the Heavenly Star Secret Realm.
"Okay, since the Soul Cores have been distributed, it's time to fly to the Divine Emperor Secret Realm! Tianxing, I'll bring you along! "Tianfeng War Emperor suddenly said with a smile.
Many Divine Sea Realm warriors glared at the Tianfeng War Emperor, but in their minds, they were cursing this sly old fox. He was going to make the first move. He was probably going to sacrifice his own daughter …
However, the Tianfeng War Emperor had stood up for Luo Zheng before, so he could be considered to have taken the initiative.
Luo Zheng nodded and didn't express any objection. In fact, after breaking through to the Life and Death Realm, he could rely on his own strength to deal with the Blood Sacrifice Force of the Sky Feather Saint Ocean. He didn't need anyone's help. However, the Tianfeng War Emperor's hospitality made it difficult for him to refuse.
"Wait." At this moment, Bai Yuerong walked towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng frowned when he saw Bai Yuerong. This woman was his only enemy in the four Divine Kingdoms. It was also because of her that he had been targeted by Battle Emperor Yu the Great.
"What's the matter?" Luo Zheng asked gloomily.
Bai Yuerong smiled and suddenly took out a rhombus-shaped purple crystal from her hand and aimed it at Luo Zheng.
"What do you mean?" Seeing the crystal aimed at him, Luo Zheng showed a trace of vigilance.
Bai Yuerong said calmly, "Don't be afraid. It's just a recording of you."
"A recording?" Luo Zheng asked.
The crystal in Bai Yuerong's hand was equivalent to the crystal used to make a letter tablet. It could seal a recording. However, her crystal could pass through a reverse spirit channel. As the Saintess of the Secret Sect, she shouldered the responsibility of communicating with the upper and lower realms.
Now that a warrior who had opened a Mahayana Lotus Flower had appeared in the four Divine Kingdoms and had opened a total of thirty-five lotus leaves, she had to report this person truthfully.
Seeing Luo Zheng's resistance, the White Eyebrows Daoist explained to Luo Zheng with a smile.
At this moment, Luo Zheng finally understood why the Divine Sea Realm experts' attitudes had suddenly changed. So this was the reason.
However, which branch of the human race in the upper realm did this human belong to?
Was it Yun Luo's branch? Or was it Daoist Tian Miao's branch? Or was it another human force?
Luo Zheng didn't ask. He had never been to the upper realm's human race and didn't have a clear understanding of the human race's strength. Since this was the case, it was better for him to play dumb.
After Bai Yuerong was done, the Tianfeng War Emperor waved his sleeves and a stream of True Essence gushed out. It wrapped Luo Zheng, Huo Chen, Huo Yun 'er, and the others. Then, he felt a powerful force pulling him up into the sky and flying towards the depths of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea.
The other Divine Sea Realm experts also flew up. Yan Wang, Zhou Ning, and the others were also brought away from the Feather Emperor Island.
However, not everyone was qualified to enter the Divine Emperor Secret Realm. In fact, only a small portion of the Divine Sea Realm experts would enter the Divine Emperor Secret Realm every year. This was because there were only so many soul cores. It was useless no matter how many people went …
This time, the four Divine Kingdoms could be considered to have "struck it rich". The number of soul cores suddenly increased by several times. Therefore, other than the Divine Sea Realm experts, a few of the younger generation could also be brought along.
However, this was only limited to the crown princes and princes of the Divine Kingdoms. The rest of the god-tier geniuses could only return home.
Luo Zheng was wrapped in the Tianfeng War Emperor's True Essence as he flew through the sky. The Tianfeng War Emperor took this opportunity to talk to Luo Zheng about all sorts of things.
As the King of a Divine Kingdom, he was actually asking about Luo Zheng's background. He was like a matchmaker. Luo Zheng was still speechless. However, Luo Zheng still answered him one by one.
It was only when the Tianfeng War Emperor suddenly asked Luo Zheng if he was married that Luo Zheng realized what the War Emperor meant.
Glancing sideways, he glanced at Huo Yuner next to him. Huo Yuner buried her head very low, and her ears were red.
Luo Zheng smiled and replied, "I'm not married. However, my family has already made a promise."
In Luo Zheng's opinion, when he returned to the Central Domain, he would fulfill his promise and hold the Dual Cultivation Ceremony.
Hearing Luo Zheng's reply, the Tianfeng War Emperor revealed a disappointed expression. He wanted to arrange a marriage for his daughter. Although it was normal for a man to have three wives and four concubines, it was even more so in the world of cultivators. His own harem of beauties was far more than three thousand. However, as the Princess of a Divine Kingdom, she couldn't possibly be a concubine like ordinary people.
The Tianfeng War Emperor was still muttering to himself. Huo Yun 'er's face was also filled with anger.
She had always been obedient in front of her Emperor Father. However, when her Emperor Father talked about matchmaking in front of her, she couldn't bear it anymore. Furthermore, when she heard that Luo Zheng had already made a promise, she was slightly disappointed. However, she grumbled, "Emperor Father, don't ask about these things."
The Tianfeng War Emperor still had a benevolent smile on his face as he said, "What's wrong with asking? Aren't I thinking for your sake?"
Huo Yun 'er fiercely glared at her Emperor Father. Her gaze faintly revealed a complicated expression.
Since ancient times, beauties loved heroes. Ever since Luo Zheng defeated her by the West Dragon River, Huo Yun 'er's heart had indeed been moved. However, she was still a young girl who wasn't even twenty years old. How could she be willing to reveal her feelings? Now that her Emperor Father had brought it up, she couldn't hold her face back.
Fortunately, at this moment, a black island appeared in front of everyone.
Even though there was still a distance between them, everyone could feel the layers of Yin Qi emitted from the island. This was one of the Four Great Secret Realms of the Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean, the Divine Emperor Secret Realm.
This Divine Emperor Secret Realm was actually the graveyard of the Dragon Bloodline Clan.
In this graveyard, there were millions of corpses of the Dragon Bloodline Clan!
After the Dragon Bloodline Clan's Blood Sacrifice, all the living creatures of the clan had turned into a cloud of blood mist, which turned into a dense curse energy that spread throughout the entire Heavenly Feather Sacred Ocean.
However, the corpses in the graveyard weren't living creatures. Therefore, these corpses weren't affected by the Blood Sacrifice.
Frankly speaking, if it weren't for the Heavenly Star Secret Realm, the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms wouldn't be interested in the Divine Emperor Secret Realm. They might be able to obtain some funerary items in this graveyard, but many Divine Sea Realm Almighties didn't care about it at all!
However, the Blood Sacrifice in the Heavenly Star Secret Realm was too strong. Even Divine Sea Realm Almighties couldn't resist the Blood Sacrifice. In the end, they figured out a helpless method, which was to use the Soul Cores to control the corpses of the Dragon Bloodline Clan. Then, they would use the corpses to enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm.
This was the reason why everyone came to the Divine Emperor Secret Realm!

Chapter 867
The entire Divine Emperor Island was a huge graveyard. The color of the graveyard was black.
On the beach of the Divine Emperor Island, the other Divine Sea Realm cultivators had been waiting for a long time. When they saw Huo Yuner, Zhou Ning, and the rest of the younger generation beside the four monarchs, they all had strange expressions.
"Monarch, why did you bring them to the Divine Emperor Secret Realm?" A Divine Sea Realm cultivator of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom couldn't help but ask.
This group of Divine Sea Realm cultivators waited here instead of going to the Feather Emperor Island. Therefore, they didn't know that Luo Zheng had obtained the Black Star Pickaxe and a large number of "soul cores".
Because there weren't enough soul cores every year, these Divine Sea Realm cultivators fought to be the first to enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. Therefore, every year, many Divine Sea Realm cultivators would quarrel over the right to obtain "soul cores".
When there weren't enough soul cores to go around, some Divine Sea Realm cultivators even suggested that everyone should have a competition.
However, on second thought, this suggestion was too unrealistic. When Divine Sea Realm cultivators fought, their power was too great. If they fought like this, the Divine Emperor Island would be destroyed in just a few rounds …
This conflict hadn't been resolved even until now. But why did the monarchs bring the princes and crown princes here? Why did Luo Tianxing come as well? Were they planning to give some of the soul cores to the younger generation this year?
However, Zhou Huang smiled faintly and said, "Zhou Qing, take out all the spare Spirit Shrines!"
Hearing this, the Divine Sea Realm cultivator named Zhou Qing was shocked. "Monarch, how many soul cores did you get this time? Do we need to use the spare Spirit Cages? "
The Spirit Cages were used to activate the soul cores. They were also made by the craftsmen of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom. Before leaving this year, they counted the soul cores. There were 29 soul cores, which meant 29 Spirit Cages were needed to store the soul cores.
Normally, on the Feather Emperor Island, one would be lucky to get one soul core after killing all the Ores Devouring Bugs. Getting two soul cores was unprecedented. It hadn't been seen for decades.
Could it be that the monarchs were so lucky this time that they got three or even four soul cores?
Spirit Niches weren't anything precious. Zhou Qing had prepared two hundred spirit niches, but no matter how many spirit niches there were, they would be useless if they didn't have soul cores.
Zhou Huang smiled faintly and said, "Take out all the spirit cages. We've obtained more than a hundred soul cores this time."
"What?!"
"More than a hundred soul cores! Doesn't that mean everyone can enter the Heavenly Stellar Secret Realm? "
"How is this possible? There should only be this much in the mining trees, right? It's impossible for the Ore Devouring Bugs to dig them all out! "
Not only did the Ore Devouring Bugs have to dig out the soul cores, they had to dig them out in the next few days. Otherwise, the ores would be digested by the Ore Devouring Bugs if they took too long.
That was why they didn't believe that the soul core had been retrieved from the Ore Devouring Bug's stomach.
However, a Spirit Sea Realm warrior asked, "My Lord, did you figure out a way to open the top leaf?"
After thinking about it, this was the only possibility. However, these Spirit Sea Realm warriors knew very well how difficult it was to dig out the top leaf. That was why they all looked serious and excited at the same time.
Zhou Huang smiled and said, "Everyone, we have to thank Tian Xing. These soul cores were all dug out by him, one by one."
His words shocked the Spirit Sea Realm warriors again. As for the twenty or so Spirit Sea Realm warriors who rushed over from the Feather Emperor Island, they were all smiling. They were also shocked at the beginning. None of them expected Luo Zheng to be able to retrieve the Black Star Pickaxe.
However, compared to the Black Star Pickaxe and the mining trees, they weren't as shocked as Luo Zheng's Heavenly Tribulation …
After that, Zhou Huang recounted what happened on the Feather Emperor Island to the Spirit Sea Realm warriors. When the Spirit Sea Realm warriors looked at Luo Zheng again, their expressions changed.
Repairing the Ascension Passage … To them, this was unprecedented good news. Compared to this good news, exploring the Heavenly Stellar Secret Realm seemed like a small matter.
However, the Spirit Sea Realm warrior called Zhou Qing still quickly took out the spare Spirit Cages.
Luo Zheng saw that the so-called Spirit Cages were small boxes. These small boxes were exquisitely made. They were big at the top and small at the bottom. The bottom was supported by four small legs. The most special thing about these boxes was their material. Luo Zheng tried to use his senses to check them out, but he was instantly repelled.
These Spirit Cages seemed to be able to block spiritual senses …
Seeing the Spirit Sea Realm warriors busy setting up these cages, Luo Zheng just stood to one side quietly. No matter what, these Spirit Sea Realm warriors wouldn't stop him from entering the Heavenly Stellar Secret Realm. He just had to wait.
After setting up the Spirit Cages, Zhou Huang started to distribute the soul cores.
Because there were enough soul cores this time, not only did all the Spirit Sea Realm warriors get a soul core, but Huo Yuner, Huo Chen, Zhou Zhihua, Zhou Ning, and the other princes and princes also got one.
Of course, because Liu Xiu's soul was injured, it wasn't suitable for him to enter the Divine Emperor Secret Realm this time.
After all the preparations were done, the Tianfeng War Emperor said, "Okay, this exploration of the Heavenly Stellar Secret Realm will probably have the largest number of people in recent years. The corpses that everyone occupies should be more powerful! However, this will depend on everyone's abilities! "
After the Tianfeng War Emperor made his announcement, some Spirit Sea Realm warriors sat down cross-legged in front of the Spirit Cages. Then, they took out the soul cores in their hands. After the soul cores were taken out, they spun and emitted a strange power.
Then, they threw the soul cores into the Spirit Cages. The moment the soul cores sank into the Spirit Cages, a strong suction force shot out from the Spirit Cages.
On the other side of the Spirit Cages, a faint black shadow appeared. It was visible to the naked eye!
"Spiritual Body?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was so surprised, Yan Wang said, "This Spirit Cages can help our souls leave our bodies and enter the graveyard to occupy the corpses."
If a warrior's soul was strong enough, he could take over the corpses. However, if he wanted to control the corpses, he had to rely on the soul cores to temporarily occupy the corpses!
At this time, many Spirit Sea Realm warriors were sitting beside the Spirit Cages. They threw their soul cores into the Spirit Cages. Their souls were sucked into the Spirit Cages. After they fused with the soul cores, they turned into black shadows and rushed forward.
Yan Wang, Huo Yuner, Huo Chen, and the others followed suit and threw their soul cores into the Spirit Cages. After a while, three black shadows appeared from the side of the Spirit Cages. From the shape of the black shadows, one could vaguely recognize the three of them. For example, Huo Yuner's soul was exquisite and delicate, while Huo Chen's soul was burly …
Seeing this, Luo Zheng also threw his soul core into the Spirit Cages. After a while, he felt a strong suction force coming from the Spirit Cages!
This suction force was targeted at Luo Zheng's soul. Luo Zheng didn't choose to resist it. Instead, he threw his soul into the Spirit Cages!
Soon, Luo Zheng felt that his soul had fused with the soul core. Then, he appeared on the other side of the Spirit Cages. His soul could leave his body and move freely.
After his soul left his body, Huo Yuner, Huo Chen, Yan Wang, and the others all looked over at him …
For example, the souls of King Yan, Zhou Ning, and Huo Yuner were all grey and showed signs of instability. On the other hand, the souls of the Divine Sea Realm warriors were pitch black. The souls of the Combat Soul Realm warriors looked very stable.
However, Luo Zheng's soul was shining with a green luster! In addition, there was also a faint trace of bright light in his soul. That was the will of the Great Wide World!
"Luo Zheng, your soul …" Huo Yuner used her consciousness to communicate with Luo Zheng.
