Before this, Luo Zheng's soul was hidden in his mind. No one could see it unless they entered his mind. Now, it couldn't be hidden anymore.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said to them, "Let's go. Let's go!"
Many souls that had fused with their soul cores entered the depths of the Divine Emperor Secret Realm at this moment.
The Divine Emperor Secret Realm was a perfectly renovated cemetery. After these souls flew for a short distance, they stopped at the entrance of the cemetery. Next to the entrance of the cemetery, there was a lamp hanging.
"All of you, wait for a moment." A Spirit Sea Realm warrior released his consciousness and immediately enveloped Luo Zheng and the others.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 868
Now that everyone was in the form of a soul, their relative strength must have been greatly weakened.
Luo Zheng was still fine. His soul was originally stronger than others in the same realm. Although he hadn't reached the Blue Soul Realm, after merging with the Will of the Realm, even the souls of Divine Sea Realm experts were far inferior to his.
The lamp hanging in front of the graveyard was a tomb-guarding lamp!
This Gravekeeper Lamp hanging here could pierce all souls. The Divine Sea Realm expert stopped Huo Yuner and the other juniors because he was afraid that their souls would be destroyed by this Gravekeeper Lamp.
Luo Zheng stood still and didn't move. He was trying to communicate with Qinglong in his mind. The Battle Emperors and Battle Emperors had been searching for the Ancestral Dragon Egg in the Tian Chen Mystery Realm for so many years, but they still hadn't taken it out. If Luo Zheng wanted to get it, he had to get Qinglong's help.
The Dragon Bloodline Clan and Qinglong were both part of the True Dragon Clan. He might need Qinglong's help.
However, after communicating, he soon got a response from Qinglong. It didn't seem to be a big problem. Luo Zheng was relieved.
"Wait a moment. You can go in after the Gravekeeper Lamp goes out!" the Divine Sea Realm expert said to Luo Zheng and the others.
Then, Luo Zheng saw a black shadow rushing into the cemetery at an incredible speed!
The moment the black shadow rushed in, the Gravekeeper Lamp suddenly emitted a dazzling light that shot toward the soul.
"This light is also a soul attack!" Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched. There were many magical things in the Dragon Bloodline Clan.
However, that black shadow was also the soul of a Divine Sea Realm expert. When the light of the Gravekeeper Lamp shot toward him, part of his soul suddenly changed shape and turned into a shield in front of him.
"Soul Shield." Seeing this, Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
The soul of that Divine Sea Realm expert was only at the Battle Soul Realm. Even so, he had to do this to resist the Gravekeeper Lamp.
With the strength of Luo Zheng's soul, he should be able to walk over directly without suffering too much damage …
Not long after the Divine Sea Realm expert's soul rushed over, the Gravekeeper Lamp suddenly flickered and then gradually dimmed.
At this moment, the other Divine Sea Realm experts' souls entered in a line. As for the younger generation like Luo Zheng, they followed behind.
This huge cemetery almost occupied the entire Divine Imperial Island. Exquisite tombstones stood on both sides of the road. The ground was covered with a thick layer of dust.
It had been many years since anyone had set foot in this place. Even the people of the four Divine Kingdoms had only used their soul bodies to enter this place.
"There are over a million corpses of the Dragon Bloodline here? So many years have passed, those corpses should have long turned into skeletons, right? "Luo Zheng used his consciousness to communicate with Huo Yuner and the rest.
He didn't expect Huo Yuner to be even worse than Luo Zheng. She didn't know the situation at all. Instead, she was a bit worried. "Isn't it disrespectful to occupy other people's bodies?"
Among the younger generation of warriors, only Prince Yan could answer this question. He said to Luo Zheng, "As far as I know, the corpses we want to occupy are not here. They are in the depths of the cemetery. The corpses buried under these tombstones are ordinary people. Their corpses have not undergone any special treatment. Not to mention the corpses, even the skeletons have turned into dust after so many years and completely disappeared from this world."
Luo Zheng nodded. Ordinary bodies couldn't cross thousands or even tens of thousands of years without being treated with a secret method.
Those Divine Sea Realm experts' souls led the way in the front. Behind them, there were only a dozen or so juniors.
Although it was dark and gloomy all around, they didn't encounter any danger.
After walking for a distance, a flight of stairs appeared in front of everyone. On both sides of the stairs, there were platforms built in circles. These platforms were arranged like terraced fields.
On this platform, there were many houses. These houses were only about the height of a person, and there were many coffins placed in them!
Luo Zheng raised his head and counted. These terraced platforms had a total of 13 levels. The first level had the most platforms. There were about a thousand of them. On each platform, there were three houses. On the second level, there were two houses. The higher they went, the fewer platforms there were. The number of houses and coffins also decreased.
Many souls came to the bottom of the platforms, but they didn't step on them.
At this moment, a Divine Sea Realm expert said to Luo Zheng and the others, "The bodies on these platforms are arranged according to their strength and status. The higher the level of the body, the more powerful it was when it was alive. This time, when I brought you into the Godly Emperor Secret Realm, I didn't want you to occupy a powerful body! You just have to be careful of the Soul Purifying Light on the steps. This Soul Purifying Light can purify your soul in an instant! With the strength of your souls, you have no room to resist! You have to be careful! In a while, we will climb up. When the Soul Purifying Light flashes, you will enter the platform on the side. Understand? "
Luo Zheng, Huo Yuner, and the others didn't quite understand, but they still nodded.
These Divine Sea Realm experts naturally didn't expect these juniors to do anything. However, now that they had obtained so many soul cores at once, they suddenly felt like they had become nouveau riche. That was why they brought the princes and princes of the four Divine Kingdoms in here to train …
However, accidents could happen during the training. That was why they specially arranged people to warn Luo Zheng and the others about some things they should pay attention to in this Godly Emperor Secret Realm.
Just as the Divine Sea Realm expert finished speaking, a ray of light rushed down from the center of the steps!
That ray of light lit up layer by layer, spreading all the way down from the top of the steps. When it reached the end of the last step, the tens of thousands of steps suddenly flashed with intense light. It was as if the steps in front of them were made of light.
The Divine Sea Realm experts were still fine. It wasn't their first time entering the cemetery. For people like Huo Chen, Huo Yuner, Yan Wang, and the others, when they saw the light on the steps, their faces immediately revealed a fearful expression.
The light lasted for about ten breaths of time before it quickly dimmed. The moment the light dimmed, the Heaven Wind War Emperor, who was at the front, ordered, "Everyone, go!"
At this moment, all the souls silently shot up the steps!
Luo Zheng also shot up at this moment. Yan Wang, Huo Yuner, and the others followed closely behind him!
Everyone's souls were rapidly climbing up the steps. However, not a single sound was emitted. Souls themselves couldn't make any sound. Furthermore, souls could only communicate with each other through their consciousness. Even if there was a sound, no one could hear it!
After running up the steps for about ten breaths of time, a ray of light suddenly appeared at the top of the steps. Then, that ray of light started to spread downwards at an extremely fast speed!
"Retreat! Onto the platforms! " The Heaven Wind War Emperor's consciousness spread out. In an instant, everyone rushed towards the platforms on both sides!
The Heaven Wind War Emperor stood on a platform on the left. His eyes were locked onto Huo Yuner and Huo Chen. Among all his children, he cared about these two the most. Of course, he was worried that Huo Yuner wouldn't be able to reach the platform in time and would be purified by the Soul Purifying Light …
As the Soul Purifying Light spread downwards, everyone had already jumped onto the platforms on both sides. No one was harmed.
Now, they had only reached the second level. The platforms were connected to each other. Luo Zheng gave the coffin a glance.
"Are the corpses in the coffin perfectly fine?" With that thought in mind, Luo Zheng decided to use his consciousness to probe the coffin. But too bad, as soon as his consciousness spread out, it was blocked by the coffin.
Needless to say, this coffin seemed to be made of the same material as the coffin. It was able to completely isolate the consciousness from the outside world.
Seeing this, Yan Wang smiled and said, "The corpses on the second level are too weak. They are only at the Divine Core realm. There's no value in occupying them. We should at least choose the corpses on the upper level!"
Luo Zheng nodded his head with a smile. He knew that using corpses to enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm was very important. Corpses that were too weak would have a hard time moving around in the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. Therefore, choosing the corpses this time was very important.
This time, Luo Zheng looked at the top level. He wondered how strong the corpses on the top level were. Were they the corpses of Divine Sea warriors?

Chapter 869
Luo Zheng had no idea how strong the corpse he would be able to find would be. But, when it came to choosing, the higher the better.
The Soul Purifying Light on the stairs lasted for about ten breaths of time, then it started fading away.
Right when the Soul Purifying Light was fading away, the Tianfeng War Emperor's order was transmitted to all the warriors in his consciousness.
"Let's go!"
All the warriors on both sides of the stairs immediately left the platforms and started rushing up the stairs.
Every time the Soul Purifying Light dimmed, there would be a gap of ten breaths of time. Taking advantage of this gap, the warriors were able to climb up another floor.
Flying at high speed, the warriors passed a few dozen stairs. When the light at the top lit up, the Tianfeng War Emperor gave out another order, "Retreat! Split up and go to the platforms!"
Without making any noise, all the souls flew up to the platforms on both sides of the third level. There were many houses lined up on both sides of the platforms. However, the warriors in the coffins underneath the houses were only at the Late Divine Core realm.
The Dragon Bloodline was stronger than all four Divine Kingdoms combined. There were countless Divine Core and Void Tribulation warriors in the four Divine Kingdoms, so the number of Divine Core warriors in the Dragon Bloodline was definitely not small. However, there were only about 600 to 700 houses on the third floor.
Those Divine Core warriors who were buried here were definitely the core members of the Dragon Bloodline. But, they had died at a young age, and that was why their bodies were buried here.
This time, Luo Zheng didn't bother looking at the coffins. Weak corpses were useless to Luo Zheng.
Everyone was waiting for the Tianfeng War Emperor's order with a serious expression. The Soul Purifying Light was a fatal threat to the soul. Furthermore, the movement of over a hundred people couldn't be coordinated. If they were to hesitate and fall behind, the Soul Purifying Light would flicker on the stairs. If that happened, they would be in big trouble. Their soul would be purified in an instant, and their physical bodies would become corporeal …
"Don't let your guard down, the Soul Purifying Light will spread from top to bottom. The higher we go, the less time we have!" The Tianfeng War Emperor stared at the Soul Cleansing Light that resembled an upright cone of light. As the thin and long cones of light dimmed and dissipated, the Tianfeng War Emperor released his consciousness again. "Go!"
Everyone's movements were uniform. There was almost no pause or delay as they stepped onto the stairs again.
"Fourth floor, disperse!"
The Tianfeng War Emperor gave the order and jumped onto the platform beside him!
However, when the Tianfeng War Emperor jumped onto the platform and looked back, he was shocked. Huo Yuner was actually at the back!
Just now, when everyone jumped onto the platform, Huo Yuner took a few steps towards the side of the platform. She seemed to be observing the Dragon Bloodline Clan's inscriptions on the coffin and was slightly distracted.
When the Tianfeng War Emperor's consciousness spread out, Huo Yuner's movements were slightly slower.
Huo Yuner was slow by a beat, and fell to the very back. At this time, the majority of people had already jumped onto the platform, and the Soul Purifying Light on the platform covered them layer by layer!
The Soul Purifying Light was much stronger than the Gravekeeper Lamp at the entrance of the graveyard. Even if a Divine Sea Realm expert touched it slightly, his soul would be purified!
Seeing this, how could the Tianfeng War Emperor not be anxious?
"Sister!" Huo Chen was also anxious. Although he had a dull personality, he was very serious in his work. Therefore, when the Tianfeng War Emperor gave the order, Huo Chen followed the group and stepped onto the fourth floor.
Now that he saw Huo Yuner falling behind, how could he not be anxious? He didn't think too much and wanted to rush out to save Huo Yuner!
However, as soon as Huo Chen took the first step, a Divine Sea Realm expert with sharp eyes and agile hands blocked his path.
The Soul Cleansing Light had already lit up and extended from the top of the stairs. In just one or two breaths of time, Huo Chen rushing out was no different from committing suicide.
All the warriors looked at Huo Yuner who was on the stairs with a trace of regret in their eyes.
This Huo Yuner was truly a proud son of heaven. As a descendant of the phoenix and having a real phoenix as a contract beast, even if she ascended to the Upper World, she would not lose to the top geniuses of the Upper World. Her future achievements would probably be greater than those old monsters of the Spirit Sea Realm.
Was she going to die here today?
The Tianfeng War Emperor was even more anxious! But what was the use of being anxious? His soul was the most powerful among all the Divine Sea Realm experts. Even he didn't dare to touch the Soul Cleansing Light. There wasn't enough time!
However, at this moment, a figure dashed out!
"Luo Tianxing?"
"You can't go!"
"Luo Zheng! Don't go! "It was Zhou Huang who stopped Luo Zheng.
Even if Luo Zheng's soul was much stronger than the other warriors here, so what? There wasn't enough time. No matter how powerful his soul was, it was like throwing an ice cube into magma when it encountered the Soul Cleansing Light. It would be purified in an instant!
What was more, the others could die except for Luo Zheng!
The reason was simple. Luo Zheng was the only hope for these Divine Sea Realm experts to repair the ascension tunnel!
Dozens of Divine Sea Realm experts' consciousnesses floated over. All of them said the same thing: Luo Zheng, don't go down.
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng didn't pay attention to these consciousnesses. His soul turned into a black shadow and shot down. At this moment, the Soul Cleansing Light had already spread down the stairs.
The dazzling Soul Cleansing Light formed slender light cones on the stairs and shot up into the sky. In one breath's time, the Soul Cleansing Light could cover all the stairs on the thirteenth floor. Now, it had already reached the fourth floor. In other words, in one third of a breath's time, the rest of the stairs would be covered.
Luo Zheng didn't have time to check the distance between him and the Soul Cleansing Light. The moment he dashed towards Huo Yuner, he held her in his arms and continued to dash forward.
He didn't want to save Huo Yuner and bring her back to the fourth floor. There was no time to do so in such a short time. Therefore, Luo Zheng held Huo Yuner in his arms and dashed towards the platform on the right side of the third floor!
Just as the Soul Cleansing Light spread under Luo Zheng's feet, Luo Zheng's and Huo Yuner's souls had already flown out. Because the impact was too great, Luo Zheng and Huo Yuner's souls flew out too fast. They couldn't defend themselves and flew over the platform on the third floor, falling heavily onto the platform on the second floor!
However, the moment they fell, Luo Zheng adjusted the angle and placed Huo Yuner on top of them. It was like his own soul acted as a "soul cushion".
Luo Zheng's soul was extremely strong. However, Huo Yuner was different. Before her soul entered the Battle Soul Realm, her soul was as weak as a newborn baby. Although Huo Yuner's soul had been tempered, it wasn't much stronger.
Huo Yuner, who was sitting on top of Luo Zheng, was still in a daze. Just now, she did hesitate for a moment. Actually, since she was late, she could have waited for the next wave of Soul Cleansing Light to disappear. However, because of her hesitation, her speed became even slower. She could only watch as the Soul Cleansing Light spread down layer by layer. She didn't have any space to dodge.
Seeing this scene, the dozens of Divine Sea Realm experts let out a long sigh of relief.
The Tianfeng War Emperor was worried about his daughter. After entering the Divine Sea Realm, the Tianfeng War Emperor didn't have many things to do. Therefore, he placed most of his attention on his daughter. He didn't want to lose his life while exploring the Heavenly Star Secret Territory. Seeing that Luo Zheng saved Huo Yuner's life, the Tianfeng War Emperor's face was filled with gratitude.
However, the other Divine Sea Realm experts were worried about Luo Zheng. They were waiting for the Saintess to inform the Upper Realm. If Luo Zheng died here, their hopes would be dashed. Compared to Huo Yuner's life, Luo Zheng's life was much more important. If they could have stopped Luo Zheng just now, they would never have allowed Luo Zheng to take such a risk!
The Soul Cleansing Light was still shining on the stairs. Huo Yuner climbed up from Luo Zheng's body and communicated with Luo Zheng using her consciousness, "Thank you."
Luo Zheng smiled. His consciousness flashed out, "No problem. Let's continue!"

Chapter 870
In fact, it wasn't difficult to climb the steps in the cemetery. As long as one seized the opportunity, it wouldn't be too dangerous.
In the four Divine Kingdoms, except for the first time when a Spirit Sea Realm cultivator died without knowing about it, no one else had died in the process.
After the Soul Purifying Light had been extinguished twice, Luo Zheng and Huo Yuner returned to the fourth floor.
The Tianfeng War Emperor also expressed his gratitude to Luo Zheng. Then, the group started to climb the steps in the cemetery again.
The fifth floor, the sixth floor, the seventh floor … Many Divine Sea Realm experts managed to climb up safely. However, the higher they climbed, the shorter the interval between the Soul Purifying Light!
For Divine Sea Realm experts, the pressure wasn't too great. However, for Huo Yuner, Zhou Ning, and the other False Tribulation Realm experts, the pressure was increasing.
"Ning 'er, Zhi Hua, it will be too difficult for you to climb any further. Even if you manage to climb up, I'm afraid you won't be able to occupy the bodies of those experts. This floor should be your limit. You can occupy the corpses on this floor." Zhou Huang's divine sense slowly floated over.
Although these powerhouses had been dead for countless years, it wasn't easy to take over their corpses. The Soul Core could reduce the repulsive force of the corpse to the greatest extent, but the stronger the corpse, the stronger the repulsive force!
Zhou Ning, King Yan, Huo Yuner and the rest of the younger generation nodded their heads gently. Although they had followed their fathers into the Divine Emperor Mystery Land, to them, the experience was more important. They did not necessarily want to gain anything from it.
"Let me help you open the coffins." The Tianfeng War Emperor walked over from the side of the platform. He entered the room and stood beside a coffin.
After the Tianfeng War Emperor stood properly, he suddenly closed his eyes and murmured a few words. Then, he communicated with his divine sense, "These corpses have been here for countless years. You must maintain a basic level of respect for them."
Then, the Tianfeng War Emperor stretched out his hands. His hands suddenly changed shape and flowed along the grooves on the sides of the coffin. The soul could change shape at will. When a part of the Tianfeng War Emperor's soul entered the coffin, his soul fluctuated slightly. The soul power of the War Soul Realm suddenly erupted. Then, everyone saw the coffin being opened.
Inside the coffin lay a Life and Death Realm woman!
He didn't know what method the Dragon Bloodline used to preserve the corpse. After so many years, there wasn't the slightest bit of decay. The woman still maintained her peerless appearance. She was wearing a light blue palace dress. Other than her slightly pale face, she looked like she was asleep!
The Tianfeng War Emperor asked, "Which one of you will …"
However, as soon as he asked this question, everyone looked at him in surprise …
Of the younger generation of martial artists, only the most outstanding princes and the Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince were able to follow. The only woman was Huo Yuner.
Seeing everyone's astonished gazes, the Tianfeng War Emperor smiled awkwardly and said, "Yun 'er, you can control this corpse."
Huo Yuner blinked and stared at the body in the coffin, then nodded. "Yes!"
Controlling a corpse was completely different from seizing one's body. However, injecting one's soul into a corpse was definitely not a very pleasant experience. However, this was also the only way to enter the Heavenly Star Secret Territory.
After that, Huo Yuner leaped onto the coffin. Her face revealed a hesitant expression as she looked at her father.
The Tianfeng War Emperor smiled and walked to the side of the coffin. He stretched out his hand and suddenly pressed it on the corpse's head! The corpse suddenly opened its mouth, and its eyes suddenly opened.
Huo Yuner was, after all, a girl. Even though she knew that there wasn't anything scary about this corpse, she was still shocked.
"Don't be afraid. Just fuse with her," the Tianfeng War Emperor encouraged.
Huo Yuner stared blankly at the corpse. A determined expression appeared on her face. Then, she entered the corpse.
Following that, rays of grey light emerged from the corpse's head. These rays of grey light were the energy of the Soul Core. Previously, it had completely fused with the soul. However, after Huo Yuner fused with the corpse, it began to disperse throughout the corpse's body.
Seizing a corpse was completely different from seizing another's body. Seizing another's body did not require the help of a Soul Core. This was because the target of seizing another's body had to be alive. Seizing another's body would only damage the target's soul.
However, these corpses had been dead for countless years. All of their blood had dried up. The corpses were already dead. If one wanted to activate these corpses, they could only rely on tyrannical methods. The Soul Core was exactly that.
After the grey light merged with the corpse, the palace-robed lady's fingers suddenly moved. After a short while, her slowly closed eyes suddenly opened!
Following that, everyone saw the corpse's movements were extremely stiff. It flipped out of the coffin like an unskilled puppet and walked a few steps in a strange manner.
It was obvious that Huo Yuner was not very familiar with controlling this corpse. However, her movements quickly became more and more skillful. After a few breaths of time, Huo Yuner's legs were pressed together and she assumed a very ladylike posture. It was just like Huo Yuner's usual posture, tactful and elegant.
Luo Zheng revealed a faint smile. Even though she was controlling a corpse, she still maintained her original habits.
"I … I can control it …" Huo Yuner said. However, because she was controlling the corpse of the palace-robed lady, her voice was completely different.
"Very good." The Tianfeng War Emperor nodded. "We will help you open the other coffins. You can occupy these corpses."
Hence, a few Divine Sea Realm Almighties came over and opened the other coffins, revealing the corpses inside. Other than the first corpse of the palace-robed lady, the other corpses were all male.
These male corpses were all wearing golden armor. There were even high-grade Saint Artifacts placed beside them. These were probably the funerary objects.
King Yan, Crown Prince Zhou Ning, Huo Chen, and the other princes of the Divine Kingdoms also occupied a corpse.
This process did not take up too much time. It only took about the time it took for an incense stick to burn. After they occupied the corpse, Zhou Huang looked at Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng had reached the Life and Death Realm and his strength was hard to estimate, he was still one of the younger generation in the eyes of the Divine Sea Realm Almighties. The meaning of his gaze was simple. Should Luo Zheng also choose a corpse?
The Heavenly Star Secret Realm was not a place easy to enter. Challenging a powerful corpse was also a very important factor. Of course, Luo Zheng would not randomly choose a corpse.
Before they could say anything, Luo Zheng's consciousness had already floated out. "I will go up with you!"
The Tianfeng War Emperor chuckled. "Tianxing's soul is already close to the Blue Soul Realm. He is much stronger than us old guys. Of course, he is qualified to go up!"
Therefore, Luo Zheng followed the group of Divine Sea Realm Almighties and continued to climb up while avoiding the Soul-purifying Light.
As for Huo Yuner, King Yan, and the others, they had already occupied the corpses and their souls were deep in the minds of the corpses. The Soul-purifying Light targeted the soul. For them who already had a physical body, it could not cause any damage. Therefore, they did not avoid the Soul-purifying Light at all. They walked up the stairs unhurriedly.
The eighth floor, the ninth floor, the tenth floor …
The number of coffins on the platform of the third floor was decreasing. The cultivation of the corpses in the coffins was also getting stronger.
Starting from the tenth floor, the corpses in the coffins were already at the Divine Sea Realm!
"I'll take this floor." A Divine Sea Realm warrior chose to stay and take one of the corpses on the tenth floor. It seemed that this Divine Sea Realm warrior had just reached the Combat Soul Realm. If it weren't for the fact that Luo Zheng had dug out so many Soul Cores, he wouldn't have been able to enter this cemetery this year.
If he was not confident in the strength of his soul, it would be useless even if he went up. He might as well occupy the corpse on this floor.
Therefore, starting from the tenth floor, more and more Divine Sea Realm Almighties chose their corpses.
The eleventh floor …
The twelfth floor …
On these two floors, there were already 60 to 70 Divine Sea Realm Almighties who had escaped.
By the time they reached the 13th step, the vast majority of martial artists had already taken control of a corpse. Thus, they had all used their 'mortal' bodies to climb up the stairs. Only Luo Zheng, Zhou Huang, the Tianfeng War Emperor, Battle Emperor Da Yu, and the Sky Fortune Kingdom King had not chosen to use their own corpses.
The five of them all set their sights on the thirteenth floor!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 871
The thirteenth floor was the top of the stairs. After Luo Zheng and the other four monarchs reached the top, they no longer needed to avoid the Soul Purifying Light.
At the top of the thirteenth floor was an open circular platform. Standing here, one could overlook the entire Divine Emperor Secret Realm. However, the Divine Emperor Secret Realm was only a huge cemetery. There wasn't much scenery to look at.
Around the platform, coffins were placed one after another!
From the first floor to the thirteenth floor, those who were able to place their corpses here were definitely the core members of the Dragon Bloodline Clan. The design of the coffins on each floor was very exquisite.
The coffins on the top floor were even more exquisite.
The coffins were engraved with profound divine runes. What was even more frightening was that most of the divine runes were flickering with a luster!
In other words, these divine runes were still in an activated state!
In order to activate the divine runes, one must continuously supply true essence. Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of curiosity. Could it be that below the cemetery, there was a huge true essence stone that continuously supplied true essence and kept the divine runes in an activated state?
"This, how is this done? How many true essence stones are needed to keep the divine runes in an activated state? " Luo Zheng pointed at the shining divine runes and asked. Even if there was a huge true essence stone, it couldn't keep the divine runes in operation for thousands of years. The Dragon Bloodline Clan's blood sacrifice had been destroyed for at least ten thousand years …
Seeing Luo Zheng's puzzled look, Zhou Huang smiled faintly and said, "These divine runes aren't supplied with true essence stones! Look at your feet and you'll know. "
Luo Zheng looked down and saw a huge divine rune outlined on the ground below. Seeing this, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. His face revealed an expression of sudden realization.
He recognized this divine rune.
Among the divine rune techniques that Qinglong passed on to him, this was a very important divine rune. It was a seven-star divine rune called "Spirit Gathering".
Through this seven-star divine rune, it could continuously gather the heaven and earth origin energy in the sky and transform it into true essence. The divine runes on the coffins were operating through this "Spirit Gathering" divine rune.
"However, these coffins are engraved with divine runes. I'm afraid it will be difficult to occupy the corpses in the coffins, right?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes, the owners of the coffins on the 13th floor were all at the peak of the Spirit Sea Realm. I'm afraid they were even more powerful than us when they were alive," Zhou Huang replied.
In all these years, the four Divine Kingdoms had only managed to successfully occupy the thirteenth layer of the corpse once!
Thirty-six years ago, the Tianfeng War Emperor had succeeded once. With the help of the powerful corpses in the thirteenth level, the exploration of the Heavenly Star Secret Territory had progressed further.
But after so many years, he had only succeeded once! Since then, the four monarchs had repeatedly climbed to the top floor and tried to occupy the corpses there, but they all failed. In the end, they could only settle for the next best thing, which was the corpses on the twelfth floor. One could imagine how difficult it was to occupy the corpses on the top floor.
"Why is it so difficult?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Even if these Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders were stronger than Zhou Huang when they were alive, they shouldn't be much stronger. After all, Zhou Huang, the Tianfeng War Emperor, and the others were all at the peak of the Divine Sea Realm. It was only because they were limited by the resources of the lower realms that they were unable to break through to the Divine Sea Realm.
Zhou Huang's face revealed a bitter smile. "It's very simple. We can't remember their names …"
"What kind of reason is that?" This explanation caused Luo Zheng to be even more surprised.
Zhou Huang's consciousness faintly floated over. He said, "You will understand in a moment. The corpses on the thirteenth floor are the most important figures of the Dragon Bloodline Clan. How could they tolerate others desecrating their corpses? If you want to take away their corpses, it will be extremely difficult! "
At this time, Battle Emperor Yu the Great, the Sky Fortune Kingdom King, and the Tianfeng War Emperor were all walking along the platform. The other Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders followed closely behind them.
Soon, the Sky Fortune Kingdom King stopped beside a snow white coffin. Beside this coffin, there were several unknown small trees. These small trees had been growing for tens of thousands of years, but they weren't even as tall as a person.
"Name." The Sky Fortune Kingdom King's consciousness spread out.
A Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elder replied, "The owner of this corpse is called Snow Sealing Dragon Star! He was once the leader of the Dragon Bloodline Clan's Holy Land. He has already been dead for 40,000 years … "
This was the Dragon Bloodline Clan's graveyard. The person lying here must have been dead for a time longer than the Dragon Bloodline Clan had been destroyed!
"Snow Sealing Dragon Star? Snow Sealing Dragon Star … "The Sky Fortune Kingdom King's consciousness constantly repeated this name.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng was even more curious. Was this name really that important? Why did he have to repeatedly mention this name in order to occupy this corpse? Luo Zheng couldn't understand it at all.
After the Sky Fortune Kingdom King repeated this name over and over again, he faintly said, "Open the coffin. I'm ready!"
The Sky Fortune Kingdom King was still in soul form. It would be difficult for him to open this coffin with his battle soul. However, there were already many Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders that had obtained this' corpse '. Although it wasn't as useful as his own body, it should still be able to display around 70% of his strength.
Several Divine Sea Realm warriors went up and stood around the coffin where the corpse of the Sealing Snow Dragon Star was. They worked together to open the coffin.
For such a character to lie here, there must be perfect protective measures. As the several Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders tried to open the coffin, the coffin suddenly began to violently shake. The divine runes on it began to wildly flash.
Weng weng weng …
After a series of violent vibrations, everyone heard the fierce roar of a dragon!
Roar!
From the divine runes, seven or eight small dragons formed from true essence shot out, rushing towards the several Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders.
"Six-star divine rune, Azure Dragon Seal." Looking at these small dragons, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
The power of a five-star divine rune was enough to resist a Life and Death powerhouse, and a six-star divine rune was enough to resist a Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elder!
However, there were many Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders on this platform. Wanting to rely on a six-star divine rune to resist so many Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders was impossible.
The several Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders seemed to be extremely skilled. After moving the Azure Dragon Seal, they immediately gave up on opening the coffin. Instead, they shot towards the sides, causing the seven or eight small dragons to madly chase after them.
At this moment, several other Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders quickly approached the coffin and began to attack the six-star divine rune on the coffin!
Under the joint attack of these Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders, the six-star divine rune quickly disintegrated. Then, the seven or eight small dragons also slowly dissipated.
From beginning to end, the Sky Fortune Kingdom King stood in the same place. He was still silently chanting the four words' Snow Sealing Dragon Star '. He didn't pay attention to the process of the others fighting against the small dragons!
'It seems that this name is really important,' Luo Zheng silently thought.
After breaking the defensive rune on the surface of the coffin, the several Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elders once again controlled their corpses and slowly opened the coffin.
Inside this coffin, there was a middle-aged man with a head full of silver hair lying inside. This silver-haired middle-aged man was the 'Snow Sealing Dragon Star'. Even with his eyes closed, one could still feel the mighty aura that came from the corpse of the Snow Sealing Dragon Star. This sort of mighty aura was the standard temperament of a ruler!
This temperament was present on the bodies of the four Heavenly Monarchs, but it was not as fierce as this man's.
It could be imagined that when this Snow Sealing Dragon Star was alive, he must have been a peak Divine Sea Realm Supreme Elder. Perhaps he could be considered the most powerful martial artist in this boundless universe!
The Sky Fortune Kingdom King looked at the silver-haired middle-aged man in the coffin, and his face revealed a trace of caution. He turned his head and nodded to the other three Heavenly Monarchs, and then suddenly jumped into the coffin!
The moment he rushed towards the 'Snow Sealing Dragon Star', the Sky Fortune Kingdom King suddenly stretched out his hand and pressed it between the 'Snow Sealing Dragon Star's' eyebrows! With this press, the corpse opened its mouth as if it were a conditioned reflex. Then, the Sky Fortune Kingdom King's soul drilled into the corpse.

Chapter 872
The Sky Fortune Kingdom King was obviously much more powerful than Huo Yuner. When Huo Yuner had controlled the corpse, her movements were as stiff as a wooden doll; she couldn't even walk properly.
But after the Sky Fortune Kingdom King entered, he seemed very natural, as if this was his own body. After he crawled out, he gently leapt up and stood firmly on the ground.
But this was also related to experience. The Sky Fortune Kingdom King had entered the Divine Emperor Mystic Realm countless times to control corpses, so he was naturally much more skilled than Huo Yuner.
As the Sky Fortune Kingdom King crawled out, he pressed his hand to his forehead, a pained expression on his face.
At this time, the one in pain was definitely not the Sealing Snow Dragon Star. This Sealing Snow Dragon Star had died countless years ago. The one in pain was the Sky Fortune Kingdom King who had already escaped into his mind!
At the same time, in the center of the platform, a stone pillar slowly rose up!
"Ga ga ga ga …"
This stone pillar was 20 feet wide. This stone pillar had been hidden in the center of the platform and was as high as the ground. Now that this stone pillar had suddenly appeared, Luo Zheng was startled.
It was just that the other Divine Sea realm elders were already used to this. They probably already knew that there was a stone pillar here!
As the stone pillar slowly rose up, Luo Zheng's eyebrows constantly jumped!
In the center of this stone pillar, there were four table-sized rivets! And at the end of these rivets, there were four pitch black chains hanging down. These four chains extended down the middle, firmly locking onto a coffin!
This coffin was completely different from the other coffins. It emitted a deep blue luster; it was a transparent crystal coffin!
Because this crystal coffin was inlaid into the stone pillar and was firmly locked by the chains, the person in the coffin could not lie down. At this time, they had to maintain a standing posture in the crystal coffin!
The corpse in the crystal coffin was a white-clothed young girl. Her body was only covered with a thin layer of clothing, and her bare legs were firmly locked by the black chains that extended from the crystal coffin …
"What a beautiful woman. Just who is this woman?" The Sky Wind Divine Kingdom's Crown Prince muttered as he looked at the youth in the crystal coffin.
Indeed, even the Divine Kingdom Crown Prince, who was used to seeing beautiful women, couldn't help but sigh when he saw this young girl's appearance!
Luo Zheng stared at it for a while, but he couldn't figure out what was going on.
Who knew that under everyone's gazes, the girl's eyes would suddenly open, and a pair of golden pupils would look down on everyone!
"Ah …"
Huo Yuner was originally scrutinizing the girl. Women were very sensitive to appearances, even if it was just a corpse …
For example, although the corpse that Huo Yuner had occupied was very beautiful, there was still some disparity when compared to Huo Yuner. However, the corpse of the young woman in the crystal coffin was even more beautiful than Huo Yuner.
But under Huo Yuner's scrutiny, the girl suddenly opened her eyes, giving her a fright.
This scene not only scared Huo Yuner, but even Luo Zheng, Huo Chen, and the other martial artists that didn't know what was going on were also frightened. As for the other Divine Sea realm Supreme Elders, they probably saw this scene every time they entered, so they didn't reveal any surprise. Instead, they revealed looks of worry as they looked towards the 'Sealing Snow Dragon Star', the corpse that the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King had occupied.
They saw the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King clutching his head as he stumbled across the platform.
He was already in pain before, but when the young girl opened her eyes, he clutched his head!
As he stumbled, the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King slowly approached. Then, with a 'plop' sound, he fell to his knees, and his head involuntarily slammed against the ground, emitting a loud 'dong' sound!
This corpse was very strong. Not only was the corpse not injured when its head hit the ground, it even broke a piece of the ground.
At the same time, a massive pressure spread out from the young girl's body. However, this pressure wasn't aimed at anyone else, but at the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King.
"Brave warrior of my Dragon Bloodline Clan, since you have awakened, do not forget your mission. Do not become a walking corpse … answer my question, and tell me your name!" The young girl lightly said.
"Name?" Hearing this name, Luo Zheng's eyebrows shot up. He remembered Zhou Huang's words. They couldn't remember names?
Wasn't this Divine Sea realm Supreme Elder called 'Sealing Snow Dragon Star'? It shouldn't be too difficult to say this name …
Who knew that as the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King's head slammed against the ground, his throat would constantly whimper, "My name is, my name is …"
"Sealing Snow Dragon Star! King, it is Sealing Snow Dragon Star! "
"Sealing Snow Dragon Star …"
Several Divine Sea realm Supreme Elders reminded him.
But no matter how loudly they shouted, the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King didn't seem to hear them. His mouth continued to vaguely whimper, "My name is Feng, Feng Xue … my name is …"
It seemed that wanting to say the words' Sealing Snow Dragon Star 'was extremely difficult!
"Tell me your name …" The young girl faintly repeated.
"Ahhhhhh …" The Sky Fortune Kingdom's King became more and more pained, "My name is, my name is …"
The young girl faintly said again, "Tell me your name, otherwise I will treat you as a lost soul and refuse your awakening."
"My name is Lu Chengyan!" In the end, not only did the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King not say the words' Sealing Snow Dragon Star ', but he instead revealed his own name.
After the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King spoke, the Divine Sea realm Supreme Elders all revealed looks of disappointment.
Zhou Huang regretfully said, "Aiya, I still failed."
After the name was announced, the young girl in the crystal coffin lightly said, "You have already bewitched yourself to death."
After she finished speaking, the corpse of the 'Sealing Snow Dragon Star' began to violently shake. Then, the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King's soul was directly ejected from the corpse of the 'Sealing Snow Dragon Star'!
After the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King's soul was ejected, the corpse of the Sealing Snow Dragon God actually began to slowly move towards its own coffin, and then slowly lay back down!
At this time, the young girl slowly closed her eyes. From this young girl's body, one couldn't feel any breath, nor could they feel any life force. But just now, she had opened her eyes as if she were alive. This scene was truly strange.
The Sky Fortune Kingdom's King's soul gently floated in the air, a trace of regret appearing on his face. "Aiya, it's still difficult for me to resist this girl's will. As long as I am enveloped by her will, I am unable to call out that name … Old Fire, how did you answer that name?"
The Sky Wind War Emperor bitterly smiled and said, "Haven't I said it many times already? I really don't have any recollection of it. I don't even know how I answered it. Maybe I just guessed it right."
At this time, Luo Zheng completely understood.
The truth was that the Dragon Bloodline Clan didn't expect that the four Divine Kingdoms would use the soul cores to control their corpses. It was just that the Dragon Bloodline Clan had preserved the corpses of their top powerhouses in order to deal with other matters. However, if the corpses were preserved like this, there was a high chance that they would become a 'zombie' existence!
In order to avoid this situation, the Dragon Bloodline Clan had set up such a method in this cemetery. If a corpse really did become a 'zombie', then the young girl would test its mind. If the test failed, then the soul controlling the corpse would be ejected.
What the Dragon Bloodline Clan didn't expect was that they would eject many souls, but the souls that were ejected were human souls …
"Don't be discouraged. Old Lu, why don't you try again?" The Sky Wind War Emperor comforted.
The Sky Fortune Kingdom's King shook his head. "Forget it. I'll go to the twelfth floor and choose a corpse. Old Fire, Zhou Huang, Liu Zan, you guys try yourselves!"
As he finished speaking, the Soul Purifying Light on the stairs disappeared, and the Sky Fortune Kingdom's King shot towards the twelfth floor.
The Sky Wind War Emperor and Zhou Huang looked at each other. Zhou Huang then said, "Alright, let me try this time!"
As he finished speaking, Zhou Huang also circled around the platform. He didn't choose the corpse of the Sealing Snow Dragon Star. Instead, he stopped on top of a black coffin.
When Zhou Huang's soul stopped, a Divine Sea Realm powerhouse beside him said, "This is the leader of the Dragon Bloodline Clan 70,000 years ago. His name is' Feng Xiao '. Two words, a name that's easy to remember …"

Chapter 873
The four Divine Kingdoms knew the people in the coffins like the back of their hands. In order to obtain their corpses, they had done a thorough investigation many years ago.
The problem was that the names of the corpses were not easy to remember. After fusing with the corpses, the corpses would instinctively reject the corpses.
Under the interrogation of the young girl in the stone pillar, the memories in the soul would fall into a state of chaos. It would be very difficult to recite the corpse's name.
The Tianfeng War Emperor had managed to guess correctly once, but he didn't know how he had done it. Thus, in all these years, he had only succeeded once.
The Divine Sea powerhouses opened the coffin. Inside the coffin lay a thin old man. This old man was the "Feng Xiao" that the Divine Sea powerhouse had spoken of.
The Tianfeng War Emperor didn't hesitate. His soul gently floated and suddenly entered the old man's mouth!
After a while, the Tianfeng War Emperor fused with Feng Xiao's corpse and slowly crawled out of the coffin.
This time, the rejection between the Tianfeng War Emperor and the corpse seemed to be even more intense. The Tianfeng War Emperor controlled the corpse like a drunkard. He staggered and involuntarily walked towards the stone pillar.
The next step was the most crucial. Only by answering the name of the corpse would he be able to escape the control of the young girl in the pillar. However, the Sky Fortune King had already failed once, so in their opinion, the Tianfeng War Emperor's chances of success were not high …
Sure enough, under the young girl's interrogation, the Tianfeng War Emperor revealed a pained expression. He held his head and shook it, unable to say the two words "Feng Xiao".
The final result was the same as the Sky Fortune King's. The Tianfeng War Emperor's soul was forcefully ejected by the young girl in the pillar …
The Tianfeng War Emperor's face was full of depression. However, he didn't choose a corpse from the 12th floor like the Sky Fortune King. Instead, he continued to ponder. He wanted to try again.
As for Battle Emperor Yu the Great and Zhou Huang, they didn't choose to try either. They also walked around the coffins.
Even the Feather Emperor, God Emperor, and Dream God Emperor had found it difficult to enter the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. In the end, those difficulties had been solved one by one. If everyone could easily occupy the corpses of the super experts on the top floor, they would be able to explore the Heavenly Star Secret Realm.
But even now, the four Divine Nations still hadn't come up with a way to break it.
The three monarchs were still hesitating. Luo Zheng was also frowning …
Just as Zhou Huang had said, this problem was actually a problem of naming. If one could accurately and clearly say the corpse's name in front of the girl in the pillar, one could successfully inherit the corpse.
But was it really that difficult to spit out a few words?
Just now, the Tianfeng War Monarch and the Sky Fortune Kingdom Monarch had repeatedly memorized this name; it was simply impossible for them to forget it. Not only that, but there were also people beside them constantly reminding them, but they simply couldn't remember the correct name …
"Name …"
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed and he walked towards a coffin.
"Tianxing, do you also want to give it a try?" Zhou Huang asked.
Many Divine Sea realm masters also focused their eyes on Luo Zheng. Although Luo Zheng's cultivation wasn't high, everything that had happened to him couldn't be measured with common sense. Perhaps he could come up with a solution?
Thinking of this, some Divine Sea realm masters became excited. One of them said to Luo Zheng, "The owner of this coffin is called Feng Zhan! He is the most powerful warrior of the Dragon Bloodline. According to the records, this person was born with divine strength and a domineering aura! "
"Feng Zhan." Luo Zheng repeated this name twice.
"Shall we help you open the coffin?" A Divine Sea realm master asked.
"Wait a moment!" Luo Zheng lightly said. He didn't choose to open the coffin and directly occupy Feng Zhan's corpse. Instead, he returned to the side of the stone pillar.
"What is Luo Tianxing trying to do?"
"I'm not sure, I don't understand …"
"He seems to want to carve something on the stone pillar. Could it be that he wants to carve his name on it? It won't work! "
In order to occupy the corpse on this floor, many Divine Sea realm masters had tried their best. They had tried to carve his name, but even if someone whispered in their ears, they still couldn't get the name out. What was the use of carving a name?
Standing next to the stone pillar, Luo Zheng reached out a finger. That finger gradually became sharp. Luo Zheng gently waved it and began to carve on the stone pillar.
He was indeed carving a word, but it wasn't the word "Feng Zhan". Instead, he was breaking down the name "Feng Zhan"!
The name of the Dragon Bloodline was still in the language of the human race. The language of the human race could be broken down into dots, horizontal, vertical, curly, curved, and hooks. Among these eight strokes, the most representative one was the word "Yong", which contained all the ways to write all the words.
Luo Zheng broke down the name stroke by stroke and carved it on the stone pillar. After finishing this preparation, Luo Zheng returned to the side of the coffin and said with a smile, "Okay, I'm ready now!"
Seeing this, the Divine Sea realm masters felt it was strange, but they didn't ask much. They just wanted to see if Luo Zheng's method was successful. If it was, they could all try to imitate him.
Therefore, these Divine Sea realm masters broke the runes on the coffin and opened it.
This coffin was longer than the others. In this coffin, there was a mighty man sleeping. Beside this man, there were two golden axes.
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate. He took out his Sumeru Ring and placed it next to the coffin. This Sumeru Ring was very important to Luo Zheng, so Luo Zheng didn't leave it on his body. After entering the soul form, he took off the Sumeru Ring.
After making the necessary preparations, Luo Zheng rushed toward the burly man. The moment he rushed toward the burly man, Luo Zheng followed suit. He opened the burly man's mouth and poured his soul into it!
Soon, Luo Zheng's soul entered the man's mind. The moment his soul entered the corpse, Luo Zheng felt a strong repulsive force!
Normally, if a warrior wanted to possess a body, he would have to destroy the opponent's soul in order to take over the opponent's body.
However, this warrior named "Feng Zhan" had been dead for many years. His soul had disappeared completely. Perhaps he had entered the cycle of reincarnation. Therefore, this wasn't called possession. It was just taking over his body.
However, when a warrior cultivated to this level, even if he had been dead for many years, there would still be an indelible power left in his body!
Therefore, when some warriors died in the wilderness, mosquitoes and wolves wouldn't dare to come near them.
That invisible repulsive force wanted to push Luo Zheng's soul out. If it were an ordinary Battle Soul, perhaps he wouldn't be able to withstand it. He would be forcefully pushed out of the corpse's mind.
For example, the Divine Sea realm masters of the four Divine Kingdoms knew that their souls couldn't enter the mind. Therefore, they chose the bodies on the eleventh and twelfth floors. This was the reason.
Luo Zheng's soul was already stronger than the souls of ordinary Divine Sea realm masters. Even the four kings couldn't compare with him. Not to mention that he had merged with the will of the world! His soul faced the repulsive force and forcibly occupied the mind!
However, although his soul had entered the mind, he couldn't control the body. He still had to wait for the power of the Soul Core to spread out and merge with the body.
Streaks of grey light surged within the body of this muscular man. After a few breaths, Luo Zheng gradually gained some awareness. He felt that he had two hands and two legs. Gradually, he felt that he could control the body …
"Rise!"
Luo Zheng wanted to turn over and stand up. However, he still underestimated this matter. When he stood up, he couldn't stand steadily and fell back into the coffin.
Although Luo Zheng's actions were very funny, no one laughed. With Luo Zheng's age and cultivation, he should be proud of himself just by being able to get into the body!
Luo Zheng had just fallen back into the coffin when he suddenly jumped up. His tall and sturdy body stood firmly on the ground like an iron tower. At the same time, he opened his bell-like eyes.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 874
Everyone was used to Luo Zheng's weakened appearance. Seeing Luo Zheng occupy such a tall and sturdy corpse, they were not used to it.
Yan Wang, Huo Yun 'er, Huo Chen, and the others looked at Luo Zheng and even grinned.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a faint smile. He casually walked a few steps. In a short time, he was already able to control the corpse freely.
However, at this moment, he suddenly felt a wave of pressure on his body!
In his empty mind, blood-red ripples suddenly spread out, and the surroundings of his mind became blood-red. This pressure did not come from the outside world, but from the instincts of the corpse!
Through the soul core, Luo Zheng had already gained control of the corpse. However, at this moment, he discovered that he could not control the corpse. In an uncontrollable state, the corpse staggered towards the stone pillar.
"It's coming!" Luo Zheng suddenly became cautious.
Previously, the Tianfeng War Emperor and the Sky Fortune King had already demonstrated to Luo Zheng that they were unable to resist the command of the girl in the pillar. They involuntarily walked towards the stone pillar.
This was not the key point, but the fact that he could not answer the corpse's name.
"Sealing Slash …" Luo Zheng softly muttered in his heart.
Soon, he felt the corpse stagger all the way and suddenly kneel in front of the stone pillar. At the same time, Luo Zheng felt the blood-red ripples in his mind become more and more intense. It was these blood-red ripples that completely isolated Luo Zheng's soul!
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt that everything was so far away from him!
Someone seemed to be talking to Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng could not hear a single word clearly. The voice seemed to be transmitted from the horizon, soft and ear-piercing.
At the same time, Luo Zheng's thoughts became chaotic!
"Answer me … your name." Soon, the voice of the girl in the pillar rang out.
"My name, my name …" In the incomparable chaos, Luo Zheng could not remember the corpse's name at all …
"It's very strange. I can't remember the name of this corpse at all. I remember it clearly just now!" Luo Zheng was also depressed. No matter how hard he tried to recall, he still couldn't remember anything about this corpse.
"Tell me your name, or I will …" The girl's voice was very clear, and Luo Zheng heard it clearly.
If he still couldn't think of that name, he would be kicked out of his mind. Luo Zheng had already seen this happen twice, but the more anxious he was, the more he couldn't think of it. It was as if that name had been completely erased from his memory.
Name …
Just as Luo Zheng was hesitating, his gaze passed through the red ripples and finally landed on the stone pillar!
The name was Eight Strokes Calligraphy of Yong!
Luo Zheng dismantled the names of the corpses one by one and placed them on the stone pillar. As long as he restored them bit by bit, he would be able to get the names.
Horizontal, horizontal, vertical... horizontal...
Luo Zheng's mind was in chaos, but the girl in the pillar only affected a part of Luo Zheng's mind, not all of it.
According to the symbols that Luo Zheng had left on the stone pillar in advance, it wasn't particularly difficult to spell out such a name!
"The first word, Seal!"
"Restrain, Horizontal... Vertical... Hook..."
"The second word, Chop!"
The name was indeed spelled out, but at this moment, Luo Zheng had a very strange feeling about these two words!
The two words "Sealed Slash" were also common words, but he suddenly realized that he couldn't recognize these two words at all! These two words should be very familiar, but in Luo Zheng's eyes, they became very strange.
This very strange feeling troubled Luo Zheng.
Obviously, through the Eight Strokes Calligraphy of Yong, he got the two words "Chop", but he couldn't recognize them. Luo Zheng suddenly felt very helpless.
At this moment, the red ripples suddenly spread crazily in his mind, and soon, his soul was involuntarily forced out...
Then, the corpse of "Feng Zhan" staggered back to the coffin and slowly lay down.
Seeing this scene, everyone revealed a helpless expression. It was obvious that Luo Zheng didn't have the will to resist the girl.
"How do you feel?" Zhou Huang smiled faintly. In fact, he had already expected this result.
Luo Zheng laughed and said, "I have already spelled out these two words, and I know that the owner of this corpse was once called Chop."
"Oh?" Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Zhou Huang, Battle Emperor Da Yu, and the others' eyes lit up. It seemed that this method was really effective?
However, Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders and said, "It's useless even if you spelled it out. The girl in the pillar's will shouldn't be able to resist. I guess that there is only one way to successfully pronounce one's name."
"What way?" Tianfeng Battle Emperor asked.
"Instinct. Only by relying on instinct can one pronounce the corpse's name. Occupiers like us can't pronounce the name!" Luo Zheng said.
From birth to death, only one's name remained unchanged. Unless one became an idiot, no one would forget their own name. Pronouncing one's own name had almost become an instinct. Therefore, in front of the girl in the pillar, not only was someone unable to pronounce the corpse's name, but he was also able to pronounce his own name.
Only by relying on one's own instinct could one break free from the chaotic thoughts. The problem was, how difficult was it to do this without any obstacles?
Perhaps, it was simply impossible for a normal person to do this...
Unless one's personality was split, and a part of it would fantasize day and night that they were another person's identity. If this continued, a person would be no different from a crazy person.
Hearing Luo Zheng's analysis, Zhou Huang and the others felt that it was very reasonable. As for Tianfeng Battle Emperor's success last time, it could only be attributed to luck.
"Sigh, it seems like we still won't succeed this time." Zhou Huang's face revealed a gloomy expression.
The difficulty of the Heavenly Star Secret Realm was far greater than the other three secret realms. Without the strongest corpse, it was impossible to retrieve the Ancestral Dragon's egg. This time, they still couldn't occupy the corpse on the thirteenth floor. It was simply impossible for them to obtain anything in the Heavenly Star Secret Realm.
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Since I can't occupy the corpse in the coffin, I can change my target."
"Change your target? You mean the corpse on the twelfth floor? "Tianfeng Battle Emperor asked.
Luo Zheng smiled and his gaze fell on the girl in the pillar. He didn't know what cultivation level this girl had, but from the "residual aura" emitted from her body, she was definitely much stronger than the other supreme experts on the thirteenth floor!
When the supreme experts of the Divine Ocean Realm saw Luo Zheng's gaze, they were slightly shocked. One of them said, "This girl … is an expert of the Ultimate Divine Realm! She's even stronger than the Military Governor. How could she occupy her corpse? Also, also … we don't even know if she's dead or alive! "
Ultimate Divine Realm …
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
In the Upper Realm, the Life and Death Realm was only the starting point. The Divine Ocean Realm was the entry level. The Ultimate Divine Realm was the backbone of the Upper Realm warriors!
The Military Governor was referring to White Brow Daoist. He should also be at the Ultimate Divine Realm. As for Daoist Tian Miao, he should also be at the Ultimate Divine Realm. However, what about Yun Luo? Although the cultivation level of Yun Luo's doppelganger wasn't high, she had a million doppelgangers. Her own strength was probably far beyond the Ultimate Divine Realm …
Luo Zheng couldn't come into contact with more powerful existences. Even if they were at the Ultimate Divine Realm, Luo Zheng could only look up to them.
Therefore, after hearing the words of the supreme expert of the Divine Ocean Realm, Luo Zheng became even more interested in the girl in the pillar.
"Let's not talk about whether the girl in the pillar is dead or alive. First of all, we can't undo the chains. We can't even destroy the crystal coffin. It's impossible to control her body," Zhou Huang said.
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng smiled strangely and slowly returned to the side of Feng Zhan's coffin. He reached out his hand and took out a small Sumeru Ring.
When the supreme experts of the Divine Ocean Realm saw this, they all revealed expressions of realization. Their eyes were filled with joy!

Chapter 875
The Heavenly Star Secret Realm was above the other three Secret Realms. It must be the core of the Dragon Bloodline.
After so many years, these Divine Sea Realm cultivators still couldn't take the Ancestral Dragon egg. If Luo Zheng wanted to win, he would have to find another way.
In that case, obtaining the corpse of the Ultimate Divine Stage girl was something that Luo Zheng had to do.
In fact, when Luo Zheng took out the Black Star Pickaxe, the other Divine Sea Realm cultivators were also tempted, especially Battle Emperor Yu the Great and the others.
It wasn't that they didn't want the girl in the pillar!
However, whether it was the crystal coffin or the chains, they couldn't be taken apart …
Now that Luo Zheng took out the Black Star Pickaxe, they were also tempted. After entering the Heavenly Star Secret Realm, everyone was willing to choose a stronger corpse. If Luo Zheng opened the crystal coffin, they could also try to take over the girl's corpse!
However, at this moment, no one fought with Luo Zheng.
Obtaining the Ancestral Dragon egg might be a great achievement, but offending Luo Zheng because of this achievement would be stupid.
For example, Battle Emperor Yu the Great already had a grudge against Luo Zheng. Now, it was too late for him to repair his relationship with Luo Zheng. How would he dare to fight with Luo Zheng and offend him?
Luo Zheng took out the Black Star Pickaxe from his Sumeru Ring and slowly floated towards the girl in the pillar.
The four Divine Kingdoms had ransacked the entire Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea, so they had a clear understanding of the identities of the corpses in the cemetery. But, this young girl's identity was the only one they didn't know. Her origins were mysterious.
Although Luo Zheng's expression was serious, he had some concerns in his heart. For example, if this girl in the pillar really didn't die and was released by Luo Zheng like this … would it cause trouble?
His soul held the Black Star Pickaxe and slowly floated beside the stone pillar. Through the flawless crystal coffin, Luo Zheng could clearly see the girl in the coffin.
She didn't have many clothes on her body. Her bare hands and feet were tightly bound by iron chains. On her white skin, there were shocking marks.
Why was this young girl bound here? What was her identity?
After observing for a while, Luo Zheng suddenly swung the Black Star Pickaxe in his hand!
After all, there were sword techniques, saber techniques, and spear techniques in this world, but there was no such thing as a pickaxe technique. However, as a supreme artifact, the law of causality that was contained within the pickaxe was extremely powerful! Luo Zheng trusted the Black Star Pickaxe very much. There was probably nothing in this world that the Black Star Pickaxe could not dig.
"Clang!"
Although Luo Zheng's Blue Soul Mirror was extremely sturdy, it was still a far cry from his physical strength.
However, using the Blackstar Pickaxe didn't require a lot of strength. With a light swing, the head of the pickaxe hit the crystal coffin, causing cracks to appear on the coffin.
"Ding ding dong dong …"
After the cracks appeared on the crystal coffin, it seemed to become extremely weak. Without needing Luo Zheng to hit it a second time, the cracks continued to spread on the coffin, and the shattered crystal turned into powder and fell to the ground!
Soon, the entire crystal coffin was peeled off from the stone pillar. However, the young girl's body was still bound by the iron chains and was firmly bound to the stone pillar.
"There's actually a faint fragrance. Could it be that she's still alive …" Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a trace of astonishment. After opening the crystal coffin, a young girl's body fragrance wafted out.
Out of curiosity, Luo Zheng reached out his hand and touched the young girl's chest.
"Badump, badump …" When Luo Zheng touched her chest, he immediately felt a strong and powerful heartbeat!
This was the body of a living person! It was not the body of a dead person! Luo Zheng's heart was immediately shocked!
Although the young girl could speak, her voice was very mechanical. It didn't sound like the voice of an intelligent creature. The people of the four Divine Kingdoms couldn't determine whether she was dead or alive …
Now, Luo Zheng was sure that this was a living person!
Didn't the Dragon Bloodline Clan sacrifice all of their living beings? Why was there still a living person here? What was the purpose of her existence here?
Perhaps her soul had already been destroyed, and her body was preserved by some secret method. Luo Zheng guessed in his heart.
If that was the case, Luo Zheng's plan to possess her body would be a lot simpler. This physical body was a living body, and he wouldn't even need a soul core to control her physical body if he possessed it directly. Furthermore, a living physical body would be able to unleash one hundred percent of its power, while using a soul core to control a corpse would be a lot less effective.
"Then let's begin." Without thinking much, Luo Zheng waved the Blackstar Pickaxe and broke the iron chains that bound the young girl's hands. After putting the pickaxe back into his spatial ring, he reached out and gently pinched the young girl's cheeks. Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said, "Sorry, I can only occupy your body first!"
After saying that, Luo Zheng's soul entered the young girl's mouth!
Soon, Luo Zheng's soul appeared in the young girl's mind. The moment his soul entered, Luo Zheng's brows furrowed, and a trace of vigilance appeared on his face.
It was too smooth …
Luo Zheng's experience told him that anything that went too smoothly was bound to have its strangeness.
This young girl's strength was stronger than the corpses in the coffins, but Luo Zheng easily occupied her mind. He didn't even feel a trace of rejection. Just this point alone was enough to make Luo Zheng vigilant.
"What about fusion? I don't seem to be able to fuse? "
At this time, the soul core should have taken effect and helped Luo Zheng's soul control over this body. However, in this pitch-black mind, Luo Zheng didn't feel the soul core at all!
Luo Zheng was a little flustered. Almost instinctively, he wanted to quickly leave this mind!
"Retreat!"
Luo Zheng's speed wasn't slow. From the moment he realized that something was wrong to the moment he took action, only one or two breaths of time had passed. However, he quickly discovered that he was already in a state of helplessness!
Just then, a dignified female voice sounded in his mind, "Since you've already entered, you still want to leave so easily?"
"Sure enough, it's a trap." Luo Zheng's expression slowly calmed down. Just now, he was flustered because he thought that he still had a chance to retreat. However, now that he was trapped, he was no longer flustered.
As the saying went, since he was already here, he should take things as they came. Right now, he could only remain calm.
"Who are you?" Luo Zheng's consciousness spread out.
"Me? Haven't you stared at me many times? Don't tell me you still don't know who I am? "The female voice sounded again.
Luo Zheng blinked his eyes and replied indifferently, "So you're not a dead person, but why are you sealed in this cemetery …"
"Humph, who said that I'm not a dead person? I'm indeed dead, but I didn't expect that after so many years, someone would still foolishly enter my mind to accompany me! "The soul replied indifferently.
"I'm indeed dead …" Luo Zheng couldn't understand this sentence, and there was a puzzled expression on his face.
A corpse with its own soul and an intact body. The beating power of her body's heart was even stronger than Luo Zheng's. How could she be considered a dead person?
"Humph, it's hard to explain it to you. However, since you've intruded into my mind, you can stay with me. There's no need to go out!" The voice sounded again indifferently.
Luo Zheng cupped his hands in salute in the dark mind and said to her, "Senior, I didn't know that I had intruded into your mind by accident. If I've offended you in any way, please forgive me. Therefore, please be magnanimous …"
Just as he was explaining politely, the female voice responded sharply, "Ha ha ha … Didn't know … Didn't know? Do you think I'm a three-year-old child? You obviously want to seize my body, but you're still trying to find excuses. You want me to let you go? Dream on! "
It seemed that this woman wouldn't let him go. Luo Zheng frowned, and his soul suddenly accelerated, rushing out of the mind.

Chapter 876
It was these red ripples that locked Luo Zheng firmly in his mind.
"You want to escape? Hmph, with your soul that hasn't even reached the Blue Soul Realm? "The voice sounded again.
Luo Zheng continued to attack the edge of his mind, but when he heard this, his heart sank. It seemed that the Blue Soul Realm was nothing to this woman.
This time, Luo Zheng could be considered to have fallen into big trouble.
Luo Zheng attacked the edge of his mind several times, but he still couldn't succeed. In the end, he could only give up temporarily. After all, the power of the soul was limited. Even Luo Zheng couldn't endlessly consume the power of the soul.
He quietly floated in the darkness. After waiting for a while, he spread his consciousness into his mind again. "There's no value in keeping me here. Why don't you let me out …" Luo Zheng still tried to persuade the woman.
The woman didn't respond to Luo Zheng. She just threw Luo Zheng into the darkness. Because Luo Zheng didn't control the woman's body, his soul couldn't sense anything outside. Therefore, he felt as if he had entered a pitch-black prison.
Seeing that the woman didn't respond, Luo Zheng continued to spread his consciousness.
"Hey!"
"Say something!"
"Your body has been bound in this crystal coffin for so many years. I've helped you to open the chains!"
"The other chains bind your legs. If you want to be free, I can help you open them!"
No matter what Luo Zheng said, he couldn't get a response from the woman.
Luo Zheng curled his lips. He was bored to death and floated in his mind. There was no need to mention how depressed he was. He shouldn't have entered the woman's mind. He should have just knocked her down from the stone pillar and thrown her to a random place … This way, he wouldn't be affected by the woman. He could have occupied any of the corpses on the thirteenth floor!
It was all because of his greed. Although those Super Mighty Beings were powerful, they were only slightly stronger than the Monarchs. They couldn't compare to this Ultimate Divine Realm girl in front of him!
Luo Zheng couldn't be blamed. How could Zhou Huang, Battle Emperor Yu the Great, and the Tianfeng Battle Emperor not have such worries? It was just that this crystal coffin was opened by Luo Zheng. They couldn't compete with Luo Zheng because of their reputation.
Just as Luo Zheng was reflecting on his actions, blood-red ripples suddenly appeared in his mind. After those ripples appeared, they quickly flew towards Luo Zheng!
"Dodge!"
Seeing those blood-red ripples, Luo Zheng's soul began to dodge in his mind!
Although Luo Zheng's dodging speed was not slow and the blood-red ripples could not catch up with the speed of Luo Zheng's soul, there was still a limit to how much space Luo Zheng's soul could move in. On the other hand, the blood-red ripples were endless.
Not long after, the blood-red ripples covered his entire mind. Luo Zheng was surrounded by these red ripples. He was in his soul form and couldn't use the Law of Space. Now, Luo Zheng had no space to dodge.
Soon, a large red hand stretched out from the red ripples and grabbed Luo Zheng's soul. Then, it pulled Luo Zheng's soul down into his mind!
"Rumble …"
Luo Zheng could feel his soul falling downwards!
While falling, Luo Zheng seemed to have penetrated through the clouds. He saw flashes of lightning twirling in the sky, constantly releasing blazing white lightning.
"An illusion? Or an illusion? "
While falling, Luo Zheng actually saw a vast sea. The sea water was constantly surging, and in the middle of the sea, there seemed to be a sizable island!
The island was covered with a layer of emerald green plants. Bright lightning flashed in the sky, directly striking the forest on the island. Then, a raging fire appeared …
Being dragged by the big red hand, Luo Zheng arrived at the center of the island. While Luo Zheng was still in shock, he saw a group of people standing not far from him!
These people's clothes were very primitive. The weapons in their hands were long spears made from tree trunks. Their clothes were made from leaves. It seemed like these people didn't have much intelligence.
However, Luo Zheng's attention wasn't on these primitive people. This was because Luo Zheng saw that in the center of this group of people, there were four primitive people carrying a simple palanquin made of wooden sticks. Above the palanquin, there was a pair of slender legs hanging down. A pair of golden eyes indifferently looked at Luo Zheng. That delicate face had a faint mocking expression!
It was the girl in the stone pillar! To be exact, this was the girl's soul!
The group of primitive people kept making noises. All kinds of strange sounds came from their mouths. They carried the girl towards Luo Zheng.
As the young girl gently raised her hand, they put her down.
"Where is this?" Luo Zheng asked.
The girl smiled faintly and said, "Warriors of the lower realm are really ignorant. You don't even know this place!"
Luo Zheng frowned. In fact, he had already guessed it, but he didn't dare to be sure.
Before, Luo Zheng already knew that from the Life and Death Realm, the True Yuan in the body would constantly liquefy. When the liquefy reached a certain level, the True Yuan in the body would be able to open up a Spirit Sea in the body!
It was said that after reaching the Spirit Sea Realm, the Spirit Sea in the body would be able to give birth to life. However, this possibility was relatively small. Most of the time, it was only possible after cultivating to a higher level!
After breaking through the Spirit Sea Realm and reaching the Extreme Divine Realm, the "True Yuan Ocean" in the body would slowly solidify. Then, it would slowly give birth to all kinds of creatures …
Luo Zheng had only heard about this before. However, now that he saw it with his own eyes, everything seemed so real. He actually didn't dare to make any conclusions.
However, hearing the girl's tone, Luo Zheng coldly said, "Extreme Divine Realm. It's just a world in the Dantian. This kind of knowledge is nothing. How can you say that I'm ignorant?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the girl's attitude took a 180-degree turn. She clapped her hands and said with a smile, "That's right!"
"Why did you bring me here?" Luo Zheng asked.
The girl blinked her eyes and said, "It's you who wanted to take over my body and invade … Well, invade my body! Since you failed, you naturally became my captive! Do you understand your situation now? "
Looking at the girl's almost crazy appearance, Luo Zheng knew something was wrong …
He didn't know how many years this girl had been sealed in the crystal coffin. After being sealed in there for so many years, she would most likely have some personality disorder. This kind of situation was indeed very troublesome.
"Let me out. My companions are still waiting for me outside. If I don't go out, I'm afraid they will destroy your body." Luo Zheng didn't have any good ideas. He could only threaten her like this.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, a look of disdain appeared on the girl's face. "Your companions? You mean those little guys at the Spirit Sea Realm? "
Little guys at the Spirit Sea Realm …
