In Luo Zheng's eyes, those War Emperors and War Emperors were old monsters who had lived for tens of thousands of years. However, in the eyes of this girl, they were not even worth mentioning.
Seeing Luo Zheng's silence, the girl continued indifferently, "You think they can hurt me just because you used the Black Star Pickaxe to open the crystal coffin? If you don't believe me, you can try. If those guys dare to come … "At this point, the girl paused for a moment and then laughed," There really is an idiot who came! "
Luo Zheng saw the girl close her eyes slightly. When she opened them again, a golden light suddenly flashed in her eyes. Then her tone suddenly became incomparably languid, "Okay, a little guy at the Spirit Sea Realm has been killed by me."
"What …" Luo Zheng was shocked. His soul was trapped in the girl's inner world. Now Luo Zheng couldn't see the situation outside. But in the blink of an eye, she killed a Spirit Sea Realm powerhouse outside? This …
"You're lying to me, right?" Luo Zheng thought for a while and said with a dignified expression.
The girl's voice suddenly became cold, "Lying to you? Is it necessary? Originally, I didn't want to kill him. But since you dare to threaten me with these little guys at the Spirit Sea Realm, of course, I have to prove it to you. I have already melted that guy's body and soul. Heh heh, he dared to steal the body of the Dragon Bloodline. He deserved it! "

Chapter 877
When Luo Zheng's soul entered the girl's mind, the Soul Core didn't work and Luo Zheng's soul wasn't ejected.
The Divine Sea Realm cultivators outside were worried about Luo Zheng.

Luo Zheng smashed the crystal coffin as soon as he came. It was definitely a risk to occupy this girl's body.
However, this girl had been trapped here for countless years. Even if she was a Divine Realm cultivator, her lifespan should have been exhausted. After experiencing the Heaven's Decay, she couldn't escape death!
Countless powerful cultivators in the universe couldn't escape the limitation of their lifespan. The only difference was the length of their lifespan.
Therefore, this risk was definitely worth it. The Tianfeng War Emperor, Zhou Huang, and War Emperor Yu the Great all wanted to try, but Luo Zheng beat them to it.

After she opened her eyes, her golden pupils were like fiery eyes. Under the golden light, the Divine Sea Realm cultivator's head exploded and he howled in pain.
Soon, cracks started to appear on the surface of the corpse occupied by the Spirit Sea Realm warrior, as if countless cracks had appeared on the dry land!
These corpses were at least 10,000 years old. The blood in them had long dried up. Therefore, when the corpses disintegrated, no blood flowed out. However, under this disintegration, the soul of the Spirit Sea Realm warrior was forced out from the disintegrated corpses.

These Divine Sea Realm warriors thought that Luo Zheng's soul was more powerful than any of them. It shouldn't be a problem for him to enter the Godly Emperor Mysterious Realm. With Luo Zheng's strength, he could easily occupy the corpse of a Divine Sea Realm warrior.
But who would have thought that Luo Zheng actually wanted to occupy the body of the girl in the pillar?
This time, they were in big trouble …
Many Divine Sea Realm warriors, Huo Chen, Yan Wang, and the others all retreated to the side. They nervously stared at the girl in the pillar, afraid that she would suddenly attack.
If this girl was really alive, with her cultivation at the Ultimate Divine Realm, she could probably kill everyone here!

Zhou Huang and the Tianfeng War Emperor's expressions were also uncertain. According to the original plan, everyone would enter the cemetery and occupy a corpse, then continue towards the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. As for the four monarchs, they would think of a way to occupy the corpses on the thirteenth floor. If they couldn't, they would settle for the next best thing. For example, the Sky Fortune Kingdom Monarch had done this.
But who would have thought that Luo Zheng would come and mess up their original plan? He actually wanted to occupy the body of the woman, but the result was that he had fallen into such a troublesome situation.
Zhou Huang hesitated for a moment, then clenched his teeth and said, "Wait!"
Right now, they couldn't be sure that Luo Zheng had died. However, after seeing the tragic end of the Divine Sea Realm warrior whose soul had been melted, the chances of Luo Zheng's death were very high.
However, the fate of the world was determined by the heavens, but it still had to follow some logic.
Luo Zheng's destiny was extremely strong, not to mention that he had opened the Mahayana Lotus Flower. Such a person with such great destiny shouldn't have fallen in a lower realm. He should have a stronger mission and a broader stage.

Although the power of the blood sacrifice in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea would gradually recover with the passage of time, there were still a few days, so it wasn't too urgent.

Within his inner world, bursts of lightning flashed and thunder roared, sprinkling in the sea of true essence …
The woman didn't reveal too much malice towards Luo Zheng. She just asked Luo Zheng to follow her obediently, then she was carried by the group of aboriginals to the side of the island.

"These humans, did you create them yourself?" Seeing the humans in the tribe, Luo Zheng asked.

"Boom!"
As she spoke, another bolt of lightning struck the sea. This bolt of lightning exploded within the inner world, as if it wanted to destroy the world. The entire sea of true essence began to crazily roar.
Seeing Luo Zheng's frightened expression, the woman smiled and said, "Don't be afraid. The power of this thunder isn't strong. It's just that in the inner world, it feels very frightening. In order to obtain this thunder, I spent a great deal of effort! The crystal coffin can't draw in thunder. It can only be drawn in during a thunderstorm through the chains. It's too troublesome … "
The Extreme Divine Realm could indeed solidify true essence. However, even if a continent was solidified within the Divine Sea, it would only be a lifeless world. In order to fill one's body with vitality, one could only draw in thunder and continuously strike it. Only then would some low level life forms slowly form within the sea of true essence!
However, life forms would slowly evolve. This process would often take hundreds, thousands, or even tens of thousands of years to produce fruitful results!
"It took me a hundred and twenty thousand years to nurture this island and all the creatures on it." The woman sighed softly.
"120,000 years, only so little …" Luo Zheng was stunned. This speed was much slower than he had imagined.
The woman faintly smiled and shook her head. "You don't understand. The cultivation of the inner world is accelerated. From the birth of a world to the birth of the first insignificant life form, it might take 100,000 years. However, for a civilization to grow, it actually only takes a few thousand years!"
"So that's how it is." Luo Zheng blinked. He didn't feel too much malice from this woman, so he cautiously asked again, "But why did you lock me up here?"
Unexpectedly, the woman faintly smiled and said, "It's too boring to create a world by myself. You stay here and create with me. Isn't it very fun?"
"…"

This out-of-body state can last for a few days, but if it lasts too long, the connection between the soul and the body will be cut off. I'm afraid it will be troublesome."

Chapter 878

Luo Zheng understood that Feng Nianyun was chained here and sealed in the crystal coffin. She couldn't move. Moreover, it seemed that she had been sealed for countless years. Even if the lifespan of a Divine Realm cultivator was much longer than that of a Divine Ocean cultivator, her lifespan should be less than a hundred thousand years.

However, the weakness of this method was obvious. Before the Soul Sealing Technique was broken, one couldn't fully control their own body. Their soul was imprisoned in their own body. Other than creating a world when they were bored, there was nothing else to do …

However, Feng Nianyun was sealed in her dantian because she had no choice.
"I can't control my own body, but your soul has fused with the Soul Core. I can help you control my body!" Feng Nianyun suddenly said with a smile.
Luo Zheng was also speechless in his heart, but his face revealed a joyful expression.

Chapter 880

Because of the power of blood sacrifice, he couldn't fly after entering the Divine Emperor Island as a soul. The occupied body didn't have any True Essence. The only thing he could rely on was the strength of his body. Normally, the Divine Ocean Realm warriors in front of him could fly freely in the sky. But now, they could only run wildly on the ground.

Not only did he catch up, but Luo Zheng couldn't control his speed. He flew over and landed right in front of the Divine Ocean Realm warriors. After all, this body wasn't Luo Zheng's. When he landed, he couldn't control his body well. He smashed into the graveyard, creating a huge hole in the ground.
"Hey, this is my body. Don't you dare mess around!" Due to the Soul Sealing Technique, Feng Nianyun was no longer able to control her body. However, this was her body after all. Although it wouldn't hurt her body, it wasn't a good thing.
Luo Zheng laughed mischievously. Then, he got up from the ground and finally stopped these guys...
He walked over with a smile on his face. However, the faces of these Divine Sea Realm warriors were filled with coldness!
Most of the Divine Sea Realm warriors were nervous. Zhou Huang, the Tianfeng War Emperor, and the other Country Lords were slightly more powerful. Their expressions were slightly calmer. But at this moment, they were prepared to fight with their lives on the line.
However, would they be able to fight against a Ultimate Divine Realm warrior?
Moreover, they were now occupying other people's corpses, so they couldn't use their full strength.
This time, the top powerhouses of the four Divine Kingdoms were in trouble …
Zhou Huang's expression was calm. He looked at the smiling girl and said, "Senior, we accidentally barged into this cemetery. If we have disturbed you, please forgive us …"
Seeing their expressions, Luo Zheng suddenly had the urge to play a prank on them. He put away the smile on his face and said coldly, "Forgive us? Accidentally barged in? Hmph! What a nice way to put it! "
Zhou Huang and the others' hearts sank when they heard this. This girl didn't come with good intentions!
All the Spirit Sea Realm warriors looked terrible. If they were to fight with this girl at the Extreme God Realm, then all of them would die here!
However, at this moment, Huo Yuner rushed out from the crowd and asked, "Senior, what did you do to Luo Tianxing? Is he dead? "
Even now, Huo Yuner still seemed to care about him. Luo Zheng was touched. He suddenly smiled at Huo Yuner and said, "Of course I'm fine!"
Hearing this inexplicable sentence, everyone was confused.
However, Huo Yuner's face was also full of shock. She looked at the girl in front of her and suddenly felt that the girl's smile was somewhat familiar.
Even if a person changed their appearance, or even changed their body, their previous habits, some small actions, and even their smile would not change. A possibility suddenly jumped out from her chest!
Huo Yuner was the first to react. "Tianxing, you're Luo Tianxing. You did it!"
Luo Zheng nodded gently with a smile on his face.
At this moment, the Divine Sea Realm warriors finally realized that this guy was Luo Zheng himself! He had successfully occupied the body of the girl in the pillar!
Zhou Huang, the Tianfeng War Emperor, and the others relaxed. They didn't expect Luo Zheng to succeed.
"Tianxing, since you've succeeded, then shouldn't we …" The Tianfeng War Emperor quickly said.
Just now, they couldn't merge with the bodies of the super powerhouses on the 13th floor because of the girl in the pillar. However, now that Luo Zheng had directly taken the girl's body, they could go back and take the other bodies!
Luo Zheng had taken the girl's body, which was the same as removing a key obstacle!
Luo Zheng nodded at them and said, "Yes, you can go and occupy the other bodies!"
The Heavenly Wind War Emperor, Sky Fortune Kingdom King, Battle Emperor Yu the Great, and Zhou Huang all had happy expressions on their faces. Thus, the four of them immediately chose to turn back. There were even some Divine Sea realm Supreme Elders who believed that their soul force was comparable to the four Emperor's that were also eager to give it a try! After all, everyone wanted to occupy a stronger body!
Luo Zheng, Huo Yuner, Huo Chen, King Yan, and the others stood in place, waiting for them to switch bodies.
At this time, Huo Yuner, Huo Chen, King Yan, and the others all looked at Luo Zheng with strange expressions. Huo Yuner herself slowly walked over with a smile.
Generally speaking, occupying a body was done by someone of the same sex. Occupying the body of the opposite sex was indeed a very strange experience. This was equivalent to Luo Zheng temporarily possessing a girl's body …
Before this, they didn't know that the girl was Luo Zheng and were naturally afraid of her Ultimate Divine Stage cultivation. Now that they found out that it was actually Luo Zheng, they all surrounded her and sized her up!
King Yan chuckled and said, "Tianxing, how do you feel?"
Luo Zheng really couldn't answer this question. As a man, he naturally didn't like this feeling. However, this was the body of a Ultimate Divine Stage powerhouse. With the help of Feng Nianyun's body, his chances of entering the Heavenly Star Secret Realm and obtaining the Ancestral Dragon egg would increase by many times.
"Not good." Huo Yuner looked left and right, then shook her head and said, "Not good."
Luo Zheng looked at Huo Yuner in confusion and asked, "What's not good?"
"You're wearing too little!" As she spoke, Huo Yuner took out a set of clothes from her Sumeru Ring and threw it to Luo Zheng. Although most warriors entered the cemetery as souls, they still carried their Sumeru Ring with them. After all, there were some important items that they needed.
Feng Nianyun's clothes were very thin, and her slender and round legs were exposed. Huo Yuner paid attention to these details.
However, when Luo Zheng picked up the clothes that Huo Yuner threw over, he was speechless. It was actually an emerald green dress.
Luo Zheng could only decline Huo Yuner's kindness. He took out a set of men's clothes from his Sumeru Ring and casually wrapped it around his body.
The four Monarchs didn't need to interrogate for names. Seizing the bodies on the thirteenth floor was a simple matter. After they returned and changed bodies, they quickly returned.
Whether it was Zhou Huang or the Tianfeng War Emperor, the four Monarchs couldn't suppress the excitement in their hearts. Although they were occupying other people's bodies, their eyes were filled with excitement.
Previously, they had only obtained bodies on the thirteenth floor once. This time, all four of them had obtained bodies! This time, they could possibly explore the entire Heavenly Star Secret Realm in one go. They could even obtain the Ancestral Dragon's egg!
"This time, we still have to thank Tianxing!" Zhou Huang said to Luo Zheng.
The Tianfeng War Emperor agreed. "You're right. If it weren't for Tianxing, we wouldn't have been able to obtain so many soul cores. We wouldn't have been able to occupy these bodies!" The Tianfeng War Emperor occupied the body of "Feng Zhan". At this moment, he was like an iron tower. He stood in the middle of everyone and exuded a domineering aura.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said a few words of modesty. He then said, "The next Heavenly Star Secret Realm will require the help of the Monarchs!"
He looked at the end of the cemetery. The next Heavenly Star Secret Realm was above the other three secret realms. It was the core of the Dragon Bloodline Clan.

Chapter 881
The entire Divine Emperor Island was a huge graveyard. Along the way, both sides of the road were densely packed with tombstones.
The Holy Land of the Dragon Bloodline was an eighth-rank force. Warriors who were buried in the graveyard would not be nameless. Those who were buried on the high platform were all core members of the Dragon Bloodline Clan. Apart from the surname "Feng", there were many experts with other surnames in the Dragon Bloodline Clan. Even the mighty warriors of the Divine Sea Realm were not qualified to keep their bodies. They could only be buried in the graveyard, slowly decaying into a pile of yellow soil under an inconspicuous tombstone. The only difference was that the tombstones of the mighty warriors of the Divine Sea Realm were all made of golden dragon heads, which could be recognized at a glance.
Looking at these tombstones, the mighty warriors of the Divine Sea Realm sighed. If the Ascension Passage could not be opened, their final fate would still be to be stuck in the Divine Sea Realm. When their lifespans were exhausted, they still could not escape death.
In the end, they would just hold a grand funeral and sleep in an exquisite coffin, slowly being forgotten by others …
The warriors in the universe used their entire lives to surpass this limit and escape from the pursuit of the God of Death.
After leaving the graveyard, there was a dark forest in front of them. At the end of the forest was the tail of the Divine Emperor Island. The Divine Emperor Island was an area where a "funnel" extended from the tail. At the end of the funnel was a long and narrow sea road.
This road, which was about twenty feet wide, extended all the way into the sea. However, it cut the sea into two halves. It was like a brown ribbon lying on the surface of the sea. It was a marvelous sight.
Under the leadership of the White Eyebrows Taoist by the West Dragon River, a sacrificial ceremony was held. The Blood Sacrifice Force in the Heavenly Feather Holy Sea was dispelled a lot. However, the Blood Sacrifice Force surrounding the Heavenly Star Secret Territory did not weaken at all. Streams of golden seawater, like pure gold being melted, were constantly slapping on this narrow road.
The Blood Sacrifice Power in this area was very dense. Even the Divine Sea Realm experts couldn't break through. However, the Blood Sacrifice Power had no effect on the Dragon Bloodline. This was why the Divine Sea Realm experts wanted to seize the corpses. Only in this way could they enter the Tian Chen Secret Realm safely.
The Tianfeng War Emperor's tower-like figure stopped in front of the long and narrow road. He then said to the crowd, "This is the Dragon Bloodline Clan's Path of the Saint. At the end of this road is the entrance to the Heavenly Star Secret Territory. However, this Path of the Saint is not easy to pass. Everyone, be careful."
Hearing his father's reminder, Huo Chen asked stiffly, "Father, what dangers are there in the Saint's Path?"
The Tianfeng War Emperor smiled faintly and replied, "You guys encountered the Arrow Delaying Zone in the Feather Emperor's Secret Realm, right?"
Many of the juniors nodded their heads. The slow-flying swords still gave them a lingering fear. If it wasn't for Luo Zheng pushing the Divine Origin Magnet Stone forward, all of the young warriors would have been trapped in the Arrow Delaying Zone.
The Tianfeng War Emperor continued, "The Feather Emperor's Secret Realm was the Arrow Delaying Zone. In a sense, this Saint's Path is the Arrow Delaying Zone!"
"Arrow Delaying Zone?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched. "This road is so narrow. If there is a rain of arrows, how can we dodge them?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Tianfeng War Emperor smiled faintly and said, "Although the four Divine Kingdoms are unable to explore the entire Tian Chen Secret Realm, we have thought of countless methods to explore it step by step. Back then, in order to pass through this Saint's Path, we sacrificed more than ten Divine Sea Realm experts. This is the experience we gained through blood!"
Then, the Tianfeng War Emperor's gaze fell on Zhou Huang. Zhou Huang opened his mouth and said, "Take it out!"
As soon as Zhou Huang finished speaking, the four Divine Sea Realm experts from the Black Iron Divine Kingdom took out huge shields that were six meters long from their Sumeru Rings!
They inserted the huge shields into the ground one by one. Luo Zheng's gaze swept across them. There were more than ten huge shields.
The surface of these shields emitted a faint seven-colored luster. Luo Zheng could feel the Power of the Five Elements within them.
"Divine Origin Magnet Stone!" Luo Zheng's eyes flickered as he asked, "I thought the Divine Origin Magnet Stone couldn't be processed?"
Luo Zheng had seen the hardness of the Divine Origin Magnet Stone. The Power of the Five Elements within it was extremely chaotic. Because of this, there was no absolute power that could restrain it. Coupled with the fact that it was incomparably tough, even if a Divine Artifact were to strike it, the Divine Artifact would collapse!
Therefore, even a Divine Sea Realm expert couldn't shake the Divine Origin Magnet Mountain.
Seeing Luo Zheng's gaze, Zhou Huang's eyes revealed a trace of pride. He smiled and said, "It's true that the Divine Origin Magnet Stone can't be processed. However, this Divine Origin Magnet Stone fell from the sky. When it fell, it caused the entire Great Thousand World to tremble. There were some small fragments that fell from the Divine Origin Magnet Mountain. Most of those fragments were collected by the Black Iron Divine Kingdom."
Hearing Zhou Huang's explanation, Luo Zheng finally understood. He looked at the huge shields again. There were indeed small Divine Origin Magnet Stones embedded in the shields. As for how the Black Iron Divine Kingdom pieced the Divine Origin Magnet Stones together, Luo Zheng didn't know.
It seemed that the four Divine Kingdoms had exhausted all means to enter the Heavenly Star Secret Territory …
"Alright, it's getting late. Set up the shields and let's go! Yun 'er, Huo Chen, you juniors should follow behind us. The difficulty of the Rapid Arrow Area is much more terrifying than the Slow Arrow Area! "The Tianfeng War Emperor warned.
When the juniors heard this, their hearts skipped a beat. They nervously followed behind the Tianfeng War Emperor.
An Almighty of the Divine Sea Realm carried a huge shield and walked directly in front. He held the shield horizontally and faced the Path of Saints head-on.
Four Divine Sea Realm experts carried huge shields and stood on both sides. Another two Divine Sea Realm experts carried huge shields and aimed them at the sky. The last Divine Sea Realm expert stood at the back.
The weight of these huge shields wasn't light. However, since they were occupying the corpse of a Divine Sea Realm expert, it was easy for them to lift these huge shields.
The blacksmiths of the Black Iron Divine Kingdom even made delicate buckles on the sides of the huge shields. The Divine Sea Realm experts only needed to touch the sides of the huge shields and the buckles would come together. Thus, the huge shields in the hands of the eight people formed an airtight box that contained over a hundred people.
The Tianfeng War Emperor shouted, "Let's go!"
Everyone stepped forward at the same time. The eight Divine Sea Realm experts also carried their huge shields and kept the same pace as everyone else. That long and narrow "box" slowly moved on this Saint's Road across the sea.
Everyone's speed wasn't fast. It was only slightly faster than an old man walking. After everyone walked for about ten feet, the sound of countless waves crashing could be heard from the surrounding golden sea.
Dragon-shaped statues began to emerge from the seawater! These Dragon-shaped statues had azure arrows in their mouths!
The Tianfeng War Emperor's expression didn't change. He only said indifferently, "It's coming. Hold on tight to the shields!"
The eight Divine Sea Realm experts' expressions turned serious and they held on tightly to the huge shields in their hands.
Just as the box moved another ten feet on the Path of the Saint, the sound of a mechanism being pulled could be heard!
"Whoosh!"
An azure arrow flew along the surface of the sea, leaving a trail of blue ripples in the air as it shot toward the small box.
Seeing the speed of the arrow, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. The rapid arrow zone was much more troublesome than the slow arrow zone. Even if a Divine Sea Realm expert's body was hit by this arrow, a huge hole would be pierced through his body. Moreover, this arrow was blue and should be coated with Dragon Arsenic!
Luo Zheng's body was Feng Nianyun's body, not his own Saint artifact body. If he was hit by this arrow, there was no way he could survive.
The arrow, which was originally a hundred feet away, arrived in front of everyone in the blink of an eye. It heavily hit the huge shield on the left!
"Boom!"
Under the high-speed impact, a single arrow contained an irresistible force. The "box" formed by the huge shield tilted to the right and almost slipped into the sea!

Chapter 882
The corpses of these Divine Ocean Realm experts had around 70% of their strength. Just one arrow was enough to release such terrifying power …
The expressions of the few juniors immediately became tense!
The Tianfeng War Emperor noticed Huo Yuner and Huo Chen's expressions and smiled. "Don't be afraid. We've walked this Saint's Road many times. There won't be too much danger!"
He originally wanted to say that the real danger was in the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. However, there was no need to scare them now.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
After the first arrow flew over, three more arrows flew over from the right side of the "box"!
"Right side! Pay attention to your strength! "The Tianfeng War Emperor commanded again.
The two Divine Ocean Realm experts on the right side became serious and increased the strength in their hands!
"Puff puff puff!"
The arrows shattered when they collided with the giant shield. This time, the box only shook slightly and continued to move forward steadily on the Saint's Road.
"The arrows in the Rapid Arrow Zone are hard to dodge. However, with the giant shield, it won't be too much of a problem," the Crown Prince of the Tianfeng Divine Nation said with a smile.
After all, there were so many seniors protecting them this time. The Crown Princes and Princes could finally relax.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
As the "box" composed of giant shields walked further and further on the Path of Saints, more and more dragon-shaped sculptures rose from the sea surface in a straight line, and more and more blue arrows were shot out.
These arrows didn't just come from the left and right. Some of them came from the front and the sides. Some of the dragon statues even raised their heads and the arrows flew in a parabola, shooting down from above!
Fortunately, the eight giant shields blocked the top, front, back, and sides of the box. No matter which side the arrows came from, they couldn't penetrate the giant shields. The rain of arrows that came from all directions couldn't do anything to them.
Although their speed wasn't fast, they had already walked one-third of the way in the time it took for an incense stick to burn.
"Puff puff puff puff …"
One arrow after another landed on the shields like raindrops. The Tianfeng War Emperor was focused on reminding the Divine Sea Realm warriors who were carrying the shields to maintain their balance and not fall into the sea.
"The Path of the Saint is only 20 Li long. At this speed, we can pass it in half an hour," Zhou Huang said to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. He walked calmly in the middle of the crowd. Anyway, he didn't need to worry about passing the Saint's Road, so he wasn't in a hurry.
Right at this moment, a statue slowly rose up from the surface of the sea around 6km to the right of the Path of the Saints. It wasn't a dragon statue, it was actually a human statue!
This humanoid statue looked like it was carved out of white jade. From its face to its body, every part of it was carved in a vivid and lifelike manner. Its eyes seemed to be made of some kind of gemstone, shining with a scarlet light. What was even stranger was that although it was a statue, its four limbs were able to move nimbly, as if it was a living person!
The humanoid statue held a dark golden longbow in its hand, and there were dozens of black arrows on its back. When it slowly rose up from the sea, it reached out and took out a black arrow from the quiver on its back, nocked it on the dark golden longbow, and aimed at Luo Zheng's group.
Originally, Luo Zheng looked indifferent as he advanced with the Divine Ocean Realm experts. On this trip to the Heavenly Star Secret Territory, he was very relaxed. It seemed that even Feng Nianyun's body, which was at the Divine Ocean Realm, was useless.
However, right at this moment, a chill suddenly rose from his back. It seemed that his body instinctively felt a great danger!
"Danger?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered as he looked around through the giant shield.
Right at this moment, Feng Nianyun's soul in his Inner World transmitted a message, "Idiot, it's not the right side, it's the left side. You've alerted that guy!"
"That guy? Who is it? "Luo Zheng immediately looked to the right. His eyes narrowed slightly as he found something unusual.
The statues rising and falling around them were almost all in the shape of dragons. However, about two hundred feet away, there was a "person" standing there!
Feng Nianyun said indifferently, "The Dragon Bloodline Archer, one of Master Feng Bo's proudest masterpieces."
"Can it penetrate these giant shields?" Luo Zheng continued to ask.
Feng Nianyun smiled faintly, "Probably. Although the Divine Magnetism Stone is strong, these eight giant shields are not completely made of Divine Magnetism Stone. They are made of small pieces of Divine Magnetism Stone. They shouldn't be able to block his arrows! That guy's arrows … are extraordinarily powerful! "
As soon as Feng Nianyun finished speaking, the Dragon Bloodline Archer had already drawn the longbow. Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng suddenly shouted, "Everyone, be careful! Watch out for the right side … "
As soon as he finished speaking, the Dragon Bloodline Archer released the bowstring …
"Buzz —"
An ear-piercing sound of the bowstring vibrating was immediately transmitted to everyone's ears.
The sound of the bowstring vibrating was like the ripples of fish scales on the surface of the sea!
Then, everyone saw a black ray of light shooting towards them!
In the blink of an eye, there were not many people who could react in such a short time. After the black light shot out, another explosion sounded in the air, and the speed of the black arrow doubled!
When the black arrow was about fifty feet away from the "box" formed by the giant shields, there was another explosion, and its speed doubled again!
"Three-stage acceleration!"
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. His hands were as fast as lightning as he directly grabbed Huo Chen and King Yan while shouting at the same time, "Get down! Get down! "
He shouted so anxiously, but his voice was Feng Nianyun's crisp voice. However, only a few Divine Ocean Realm experts reacted. The other Divine Ocean Realm experts only had time to turn their heads. When they saw the black light, it was only two or three feet away from the giant shields …
"Dong! Dong! "
Everyone only heard two extremely soft sounds.
At this moment, everyone had stopped moving forward. Everyone's faces were filled with astonishment. However, their expressions were slowly changing from astonishment to fear …
On the "box" formed by the giant shields, there were two small round holes. They were the first giant shield on the left and the first giant shield on the right. This arrow had directly penetrated the giant shields!
Each giant shield was twenty feet long and ten feet wide. These eight giant shields formed a large space. However, with eighty to ninety Divine Ocean Realm experts squeezed into the giant shields, the space was definitely not enough. Everyone was almost squeezing and standing close to each other. There was not much room for movement.
After being pierced by the arrows, everyone looked at each other, only to see that the four of them were still standing there, motionless …
There was a small hole on the foreheads of these four people. Obviously, the black light that shot out just now had directly penetrated their foreheads!
The four of them were all Divine Ocean Realm experts and had the souls of the Battle Soul Realm. However, the arrow was too powerful. After penetrating their heads and entering their minds, it directly shattered their souls!
"Plop, plop, plop …"
After their souls were shattered, their bodies became ownerless and fell to the ground directly.
Huo Chen and King Yan's eyes were filled with great fear. The two of them were standing on the spot where the arrow had penetrated. If it weren't for Luo Zheng who pulled them away, the black light would have penetrated their minds. At this moment, their souls would have been turned into ashes!
"What, what the hell is this?"
"That statue, that human-shaped statue! His arrow can penetrate the shield! "
"What should we do? We can't move fast. We are moving targets … "
Four powerful warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm were killed by this one arrow. How could the others not be flustered?
The key was that everyone was trapped in the "box" formed by the giant shields. The space was limited and there was no room for them to dodge.
Unless they abandoned the "box". The problem was that if they abandoned the "box", they would have to face the endless "arrows" outside. They wouldn't be able to survive either!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 883
Now, everyone was stuck in a dilemma. They couldn't retreat, but they couldn't advance!
The faces of the four rulers were so gloomy that it seemed as if water could drip from them.
"What is that?" Luo Zheng squinted his eyes and asked.
Zhou Huang shook his head, "I've never seen it before. It has never appeared before!"
The Tianfeng War Emperor frowned and said, "Moreover, that human statue has intelligence. It knows that we are occupying the corpse, so the arrow is aimed at the head!"
Everyone was occupying the corpse. Now, they were equivalent to zombies, but zombies with souls.
If the arrow pierced through the corpse, there would only be a hole. It wouldn't cause any fatal damage, and there wouldn't be any blood flowing out.
But if the arrow pierced through the head, it wouldn't be good. Under the high speed arrow, the soul would be shattered!
Looking at the power of the arrow, it could even pierce through shields made of Divine Origin Magnetic Stones. Even the supreme being corpses occupied by the four rulers couldn't resist such power.
"Ruler, what should we do? Let's retreat! "
"Ruler, quickly think of a way …"
Some of the Divine Sea Realm masters were already panicking. On the contrary, although Huo Chen and the others were afraid, they didn't speak. Huo Yun 'er and the other younger generation martial artists knew that they couldn't change anything. They could only listen to their father and hope that he could come up with a solution.
But what could the four rulers do?
Battle Emperor Yu the Great, Zhou Huang, and the Sky Fortune Kingdom's Monarch all had impatient expressions on their faces. They had finally managed to seize the corpse on the thirteenth level with great difficulty. They were originally prepared to show off their strength in the Heavenly Star Mystic Realm, but before they even entered the Heavenly Star Mystic Realm, they had already encountered such trouble. Was there anything more depressing than this in the world?
At this time, the Tianfeng War Emperor shouted, "Let's go! Let's rush over! We can only rush over. The statue shouldn't be able to move! Everyone, keep your eyes on the giant shield and charge! "
As the ruler of a country, even if he couldn't think of a solution, he still had the courage to make a prompt decision. The Tianfeng War Emperor didn't know if his guess was correct. It was very likely that his order would lead to more deaths. However, no matter what decision he made, it was better than having a group of people standing here as targets!
When the Divine Ocean Realm experts heard the Tianfeng War Emperor's words, they no longer hesitated. Carrying the shields around them, they began to jog towards the Saint Realm!
Everyone was trapped in this "box". Even if they increased their speed, it wouldn't be particularly fast. However, it was still a lot faster than before.
However, at this moment, a Divine Sea Realm warrior pointed at the sea and said, "Look, that guy!"
As the Divine Sea Realm warrior pointed, the human-shaped statue leaped up and stepped on the surface of the sea to move.
"He actually followed me! And it can move in the sea … "A Spirit Sea Realm warrior shouted helplessly.
"He nocked the bow, he nocked the bow! Everyone be careful! "
Under everyone's gaze, the humanoid statue once again pulled out a black arrow from its back and lightly nocked it on the dark golden longbow. Once again, it aimed at this side.
Just a moment ago, everyone was still able to jog together. Now that they saw the humanoid statue aiming at them again, everyone began to panic. Originally, they were already very courageous inside the "box". Now that everyone was scrambling to avoid the statue, they became even more crowded!
"Everyone, calm down! You useless things, don't be too presumptuous! "The Tianfeng War Emperor suddenly roared.
The War Emperor's anger had some effect. Everyone calmed down a little. Then, the Tianfeng War Emperor said, "Everyone, spread out. Try not to squeeze together. If you squeeze together, more people will die!"
If everyone spread out, the arrow shot by the humanoid statue wouldn't be able to pierce through so many people so easily. However, if they squeezed together, the situation would be different. One arrow could probably kill five or six people, or even more.
Just as everyone spread out according to the Tianfeng War Emperor's order, another black light shot out from the humanoid statue's dark golden longbow!
"Sou — Pa, pa, pa!"
When the black light was first shot out, its strength didn't seem to be particularly terrifying. Its speed was only slightly faster than the arrow smeared with Dragon Arsenic. However, as it flew through the air, it would explode three times in a row. Each time it exploded, its speed and strength would more than double. After the third explosion, the speed and strength of the arrow had reached an extremely terrifying level!
The black light disappeared in front of everyone's eyes. Then, two small holes appeared on the giant shield.
Everyone looked at themselves and finally focused their attention on a Divine Ocean Realm expert. A small hole appeared on his forehead. Then, with a plop, he fell to the ground.
"Only one died this time," Zhou Huang murmured.
"There's still a long way to go before the Saint's Path. Even if we rush over, most of us will die!"
"The situation will be the same if we retreat!"
"Then what should we do?"
Although the second arrow only killed one person, many Divine Ocean Realm experts had fallen into a state of despair.
It couldn't be said that these Divine Ocean Realm experts were afraid of death. They had endured for so many years and finally, one day, they could open the path to ascension. But now, they had to face such a desperate situation. One could imagine how they felt at this moment!
"Send someone out to deal with that statue!" The Tianfeng War Emperor said in a deep voice.
"But the Saint's Path is next to the sea. We can't use True Yuan and can't fly!" A Divine Ocean Realm expert said.
As soon as he finished speaking, everyone's eyes fell on Luo Zheng!
Everyone knew that Luo Zheng was the only one who possessed a physical body. Previously, he had used True Yuan to fly. Moreover, his physical body was at the Divine Realm. Right now, their only hope was on him!
However, outside of the giant shield, they still had to face the rain of arrows. It was also very dangerous for Luo Zheng to go out.
Seeing that everyone was looking at him, he blinked his golden eyes and smiled faintly. "It seems that I have to go and destroy that statue!"
The Tianfeng War Emperor stared at Luo Zheng with burning eyes and said, "Luo Tianxing! If you can save us this time, you will be a great benefactor of the four Divine Kingdoms! In the future, if … "
Luo Zheng waved his hand and stopped the Tianfeng War Emperor. He said calmly, "We are all in the same boat in the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. Saving you is also saving myself. No need to say more. Open the giant shield!"
All the Divine Ocean Realm experts looked at Luo Zheng with grateful eyes.
Who could have imagined that this little guy was merely a representative of the Black Jade Martial House at the West Dragon River. After that, he used his formidable strength to crush the younger generation warriors of the same level. After entering the Sky Feather Sacred Ocean, he played a huge role. Now, he had become the only life-saving straw in everyone's hands!
At this moment, other than gratitude, they could only sigh.
The two Divine Sea Realm Almighties opened the buckle on the edge of the giant shield, and only a small crack appeared. Feng Nianyun's petite body was able to squeeze through the crack easily.
However, Luo Zheng didn't pay attention to the size of his chest. His chest rubbed against the shield, which caused Feng Nianyun to complain in his Inner World!
Of course, Luo Zheng chose to ignore her complaints …
"Crack!"
After letting Luo Zheng out, the two Divine Ocean Realm experts locked the giant shield together. That black light was fatal, and so were the other "rapid arrows"!
As soon as Luo Zheng left the protection of the "box", seven or eight blue arrows shot toward him. In a flash, they were only a few feet away from Luo Zheng.
"Go!"
Luo Zheng had already circulated his True Yuan. How terrifying was the speed of a Divine Ocean Realm expert? In almost the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng left his original spot and shot high up in the sky!

Chapter 884

Chapter 886

In the past, the seven Divine Kingdoms and the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land were born at almost the same time. At the very beginning, the humans of the Dragon Bloodline didn't interfere with each other's business.
In the beginning, the two races did not know of each other's existence.

It was at this time that the two races began to come into contact.

During this war, the strength of the seven Divine Kingdoms and the Dragon Bloodline Clan rose rapidly.
Even though the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land fought against the seven Divine Kingdoms alone, they did not fall into a disadvantageous position. On the contrary, the seven Divine Kingdoms had always been suppressed by the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land.
The most important reason for this was that the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land had the Rising Dragon Platform! Almost every Dragon Bloodline Clan in the lower realm had their own Rising Dragon Platform!
One had to know that only Divine Sea Realm powerhouses could enter the Ascension Passage and ascend to the upper realm. However, with the Rising Dragon Platform, living beings could break free from this restriction and send their younger generation martial artists to the upper realm to train!
When Luo Zheng had gone through the Rising Dragon Platform and returned to the lower realm, he had crushed all the geniuses in the Central Domain. The Rising Dragon Platform had been of great help to Luo Zheng.
Because of this, the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land developed much faster than the seven Divine Kingdoms. By the time the seven Divine Kingdoms had become quasi-seventh-grade forces, the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land had already become an eighth-grade Holy Land.

However, the human race of the upper realm had opened a reverse spirit channel through the Secret Sect. Afterwards, the human race of the upper realm had continuously sent aid to the seven Divine Kingdoms.

In the history of the seven Divine Kingdoms, this war was known as the "Dragon Slaying War". The entire war lasted for over a hundred years. In the end, three of the seven Divine Kingdoms were destroyed and the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land sacrificed the lives of all the living beings in the Holy Land to seal the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea.
There were countless treasures buried in the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land. In order to obtain these treasures, the four Divine Kingdoms had investigated for countless years and countless people had died in the Heavenly Feather Sacred Sea.
After countless attempts, they had opened the three great secret realms and obtained the method to enter the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land.
After absorbing the inheritance of the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land, the four Divine Kingdoms had rapidly developed. However, because of the "Dragon Slaying War", the ascension channel was destroyed. No one in the Great Thousand World could ascend and many Divine Sea Realm experts were trapped in the lower realm.

Feng Nianyun said indifferently, "Maybe the Ancestral Dragon egg hatched. However, this Ancestral Dragon … shouldn't be." Feng Nianyun was also puzzled. After all, she was only speculating. Moreover, some of her speculations seemed contradictory.
While everyone was puzzled, the Tianfeng War Emperor asked, "Yuner, your nemesis? What nemesis do you have? "
Huo Yuner shook her head. Her body continued to tremble as if she was in a world of ice and snow. "I, I'm not sure. I only have a vague feeling that it's very scary!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng walked in front of Huo Yuner. He stared at Huo Yuner with his golden eyes and asked, "Is it a dragon?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Huo Yuner's eyes flashed with fear. Originally, she had a vague feeling of this fear. However, Luo Zheng's words were like a candle in the darkness. Instantly, her perception became clear. "Yes, it's a dragon. That dragon is extraordinary!"
"Ancestral Dragon!"
"Could it be that the Ancestral Dragon egg has hatched?"
"This is troublesome. Didn't they say that the Ancestral Dragon egg can't be hatched? Let's go back. The appearance of the Ancestral Dragon … I'm afraid this Great Thousand World will collapse! "
As soon as Huo Yuner finished speaking, those Divine Sea Realm powerhouses followed her train of thought and naturally guessed that it was the Ancestral Dragon egg!
Their goal was indeed the Ancestral Dragon egg. The problem was that they just wanted to take away the egg, not a living dragon. It was said that the Ancestral Dragon was related to the entire True Dragon Clan. Even if it had just hatched, its strength wouldn't be weak. How could these Divine Sea Realm powerhouses touch it?
Just as these Divine Sea Realm powerhouses were about to retreat, that sound suddenly rang out again.
"Kacha, kacha, kacha, kacha …"
The voice was extremely clear, and it came from the side of the street. Not long after, everyone saw a black thing appear at the corner of the street. Everyone looked over and saw a small black dragon-shaped monster biting a piece of snow-white bone in its mouth, constantly gnawing on it.
This black dragon-shaped monster wasn't big. It had a dragon's head and a short body. Just by looking at its head, it looked like a small dragon. However, there was a pair of snow-white slender fangs on both sides of its mouth!
"Dragon?"
"Is this a dragon? How can a dragon look like this?"
"Even if it's a dragon, there's nothing to be afraid of …"
Everyone was also frightened by the sound, but when they saw the small dragon-shaped monster, their faces immediately showed signs of relief.
Although they were in possession of the corpse, their perception came from their souls. After a casual glance, they knew that this little thing wasn't very powerful.
"Hehe, Yuner, are you afraid of this little thing? I'll go and catch it for you! " A Divine Sea Realm powerhouse of the Tianfeng Kingdom suddenly rushed out and controlled the corpse to dash toward the small dragon-shaped monster at an extremely fast speed.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng shouted, "Don't go there!"
However, that Divine Sea Realm powerhouse was also very fast. He approached the dragon-shaped monster in an instant and reached out his hands, ready to catch it!
Right at this moment, the dragon-shaped monster suddenly raised its head and opened its huge mouth that was completely disproportionate to its body. Then, it roared at that Divine Sea Realm powerhouse. "Roar!"
A sound wave that was visible to the naked eye spread out. The sound of this dragon roar was completely out of proportion to that small dragon-shaped monster. Under the vibration of that sound wave, everyone covered their ears. At the same time, they saw that the upper half of that Divine Sea Realm warrior's body collapsed and turned into powder.

Chapter 888
Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …
The blue crystal-like building disintegrated into dust and drifted away.
Everyone was dumbfounded. No one expected this dragon-shaped monster to be so harmless. It could barely be counted as a Level 2 or Level 3 beast.
However, this roar was able to turn a Divine Sea Realm cultivator's corpse into dust?
What on earth was this thing?
The Divine Sea Realm cultivator's soul was hidden in the corpse's mind. His entire upper body was shattered by this dragon's roar. His soul was probably destroyed as well.
"Catch that thing! I want to avenge Wu Xian! "A Divine Sea Realm cultivator was enraged. He dashed towards the dragon-shaped monster directly!
"Watch out! This little thing is strange! "
"Don't get hit by its roar!"
Although the effect of this dragon-shaped monster's roar was amazing and could kill a Divine Sea Realm cultivator as soon as it appeared, most of the warriors present were Divine Sea Realm cultivators. In their eyes, the dragon-shaped monster's roar was indeed powerful, but it wasn't particularly scary. Therefore, no one ran away.
However, as soon as the Divine Sea Realm warrior reached the front of the dragon-shaped monster, he took a slight step and turned around to rush back!
"Run! Run! "The Divine Sea Realm cultivator shouted at the crowd.
Everyone was confused at first, but then, they saw hundreds of dragon-shaped monsters suddenly appear in the alley on the side of the street.
One small thing wasn't hard to deal with. Although it was deadly, it wasn't hard to subdue. The problem was that hundreds of dragon-shaped monsters appeared at the same time. That was a problem …
The astonishment on everyone's faces instantly turned into fear. Following which, the Tianfeng War Emperor ordered, "Everyone, run!"
However, before he could finish his sentence, he realized that everyone around him had already fled. Even Luo Zheng had suddenly rushed forward!
Just as everyone rushed out along the main road, they heard the roars of dragons!
Dozens of sound waves roared out from the side of the alley, directly shattering ten crystalline buildings on the side into pieces. The power of this dragon-shaped monster's roar was simply too great.
Soon, these dragon-shaped monsters caught up to the main road. Each of the dragon-shaped monsters took a deep breath, as if they wanted to release that deadly sound wave again.
"Split up! Not here! Everyone, run to both sides and gather at the Linglong Lake! " At the Tianfeng War Emperor's command, all the Divine Sea Realm warriors split up and went into the streets on both sides of the street.
"Roar …"
Just as they entered the street, dozens of sound waves blasted at them. These sound waves came from different directions. The sound waves that were released into the sky did not have any effect. Some sound waves that were released at a lower angle directly plowed a gully in the main road!
As for the surrounding exquisite buildings, they were all destroyed by the sound waves!
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng frowned. He jumped up and stood on the roof of a building. He stared at the little beast, which was jumping around excitedly.
"Feng Nianyun, what are these things?" Luo Zheng asked.
The trip to the Tian Chen Secret Land this time seemed to be very unusual. Since Luo Zheng had started in the Feather Emperor Secret Land, the difficulty of the Slow Arrow Zone had been much higher than usual. Now, these strange things had appeared out of nowhere. He was also very confused.
Luo Zheng was not very clear about Feng Nianyun's identity. However, he was sure that she was from the Dragon Bloodline Clan. She should know about these things.
Feng Nianyun, who was floating in his body, shook her head. "These little monsters seem to have the bloodline of the True Dragon Clan. However, their bloodline is not pure. I can't tell what they are!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng, who was standing on the roof, attracted the attention of a dragon-shaped monster. That dragon-shaped monster flapped its wings, which were not very wide. It flapped along the roof and jumped toward Luo Zheng with amazing jumping ability. At the same time, it opened its mouth!
"Roar!"
An invisible sound wave attacked Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's golden eyes flashed slightly. His figure swayed slightly and avoided the sound wave. He appeared behind the dragon-shaped monster. Then, with a fierce elbow strike, he directly hit the dragon-shaped monster's abdomen.
"Whoosh …"
The dragon-shaped monster's body was directly sent flying upward by Luo Zheng!
The physical strength of the Ultimate Divine Realm was very terrifying. It was probably comparable to the strength of Luo Zheng's 4000 dragon scales. With this blow, it flew thousands of feet into the sky before slowly falling down.
"Pa!"
The dragon-shaped monster slammed heavily on the ground.
Luo Zheng frowned and stared at the little thing. Logically speaking, his elbow strike should have directly shattered this thing. However, not only did it not shatter, after falling to the ground, it actually flipped over and climbed up from the ground as if it was not injured at all!
"How is this possible?"
Even if Luo Zheng's body was a Saint Artifact, after being hit by Feng Nianyun's physical body, although it would not kill him, he would not be able to get up for a while!
As for those Divine Ocean Realm experts, after being hit by this blow, they would probably have already died. The higher the level of cultivation, the greater the gap between the Ultimate Divine Realm and the Divine Ocean Realm was.
"Let's go!"
Luo Zheng saw another seven or eight dragon-shaped monsters coming directly at him. His figure suddenly floated and flew forward.
Compared to the others, Luo Zheng was much more relaxed. After all, he was occupying a physical body, and it was a Ultimate Divine Realm body. The others were only occupying corpses that had been dead for many years. Since they could not fly, they could only rely on their corpses to run wildly on the ground.
"Roar …"
"Roar …"
"Roar …"
In the midst of these roars, the surrounding houses were constantly collapsing and collapsing!
Moreover, from time to time, they could hear miserable screams. Probably, many people had died in the roars of these dragon-shaped monsters.
"Help!"
Just as Luo Zheng was flying, he heard Huo Chen's voice.
He was being cornered by two dragon-shaped monsters. There was no way for him to escape!
"Scram, you lowlife!"
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
At this moment, the Tianfeng War Emperor let out a roar as two golden axes flew out from his hands.
He was occupying the body of Feng Zhan. The corpse's strength was already extremely terrifying. Under the Tianfeng War Emperor's rage, the golden axes that he threw out contained the power to split mountains!
"Bang! Bang!"
The axes were extremely fast. The two dragon-shaped monsters did not have any space to dodge and were directly hit by the axes. The enormous force sent the two dragon-shaped monsters crashing into a nearby building.
"Boom!"
Under the impact of the two axes, the building collapsed with a loud bang!
"Let's go!" The Tianfeng War Emperor carried Huo Chen and madly ran away. However, shortly after, the two small monsters that were hit by the axes crawled out from the collapsed building.
Frankly speaking, the dragon-shaped monsters did not have any advantage in terms of speed or strength. However, their physical bodies were terrifyingly tough!
Even a young True Dragon would not have such a strong physical body. The group was completely helpless against the dragon-shaped monsters. They could only run away.
Dozens of Divine Sea Realm experts flew through the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land, avoiding the dragon-shaped monsters' pursuit. Fortunately, the speed of these small monsters was not fast. Under the group's mad dash, they gradually escaped from the pursuit of these small monsters.
Soon, the group had crossed half of the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land. Finally, they arrived at the center of the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land, where they slowly gathered at the Exquisite Lake.
Everyone's faces were filled with anxiety. After entering the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land so many times, especially the Tianfeng War Emperor, they knew very well where the danger was and where the safety was.
In the main city of the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land, not only were there no living creatures, but even the most troublesome puppets did not exist. However, these dragon-shaped monsters had mysteriously appeared this time. This was definitely not a good sign.
Huo Yuner stood by the Exquisite Lake, her face still pale. Seeing his daughter's expression, the Tianfeng War Emperor could not bear to see her like this. He could only comfort her, "Yuner, although these small monsters are powerful, they are not that scary!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng also appeared. In his hand was a dragon-shaped monster. He had just circled around the area and caught a dragon-shaped monster in his hand!
Everyone looked at the dragon-shaped monster with vigilance. However, Luo Zheng had already sealed the dragon-shaped monster's mouth, so it was not much of a threat.
"Look, even Tianxing caught one with his bare hands. There is nothing to be afraid of!" The Tianfeng War Emperor said with a smile.
Huo Yuner looked at Luo Zheng in a daze and said, "It's not that. The thing I am afraid of is not that!"

Chapter 889
After entering the Heavenly Chen Holy Land, Huo Yuner had been acting strangely.
These strange dragon-shaped monsters were troublesome to deal with, but for many Divine Sea Realm cultivators, they weren't particularly deadly. After all, they were just young beasts. As long as they didn't get surrounded, they weren't that scary.

A Divine Sea Realm cultivator nodded with a solemn expression, "Yes, I think so too!"
This thing was like a dragon but not a dragon. It was just a young beast and it was already so scary. What would happen when it grew up?
The most important thing was that even if this dragon-shaped monster was a young beast, there must be a mother beast that gave birth to it. Was this mother beast in the Heavenly Chen Secret Realm?
This wasn't difficult to deduce. Most Divine Sea Realm cultivators could deduce this.
The most important thing was, what was the mother beast that gave birth to these dragon-shaped monsters? What kind of strength did it have? Could they fight against it?

The four kings and the Divine Sea Realm cultivators of their Divine Kingdoms were working so hard. Wasn't their goal to get benefits from the reverse spirit channel?

"But if the governor asks, how will we answer?" A Divine Sea Realm cultivator asked with a serious expression.

Although the four Divine Kingdoms listened to the secret sect, they didn't have an attitude of submission. Everyone had to plan for their own future.
Everyone had reached the Spirit Sea Realm. Once Luo Zheng's news was reported and the ascension tunnel was repaired, all they had to do was wait for the day to come!
As for the Ancestral Dragon egg? That was indeed a good thing, but what did it have to do with them?
Seeing that everyone had the same attitude, the Tianfeng War Emperor was moved. After pondering for a moment, he said, "Alright, if that's the case, when the Governor asks, we'll all say that we failed. Something has happened in the Heavenly Holy Land. For safety reasons, we should return!"
As soon as the Tianfeng War Emperor finished speaking, the other Divine Sea Realm experts agreed with him. No one's life was easy to get. Maybe they could get some opportunities and benefits in the Heavenly Star Secret Territory, but it was not as important as their own lives.
However, just as everyone was about to retreat, they suddenly felt the ground tremble.
"Boom!"
That tremor seemed to come from underground, as if a giant beast was knocking on the ground.

"Rumble …"
The ground began to tremble as if a great earthquake had occurred. All the buildings in the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land began to shake and collapse!
"Let's go! No matter what it is, it can't be anything good. It has nothing to do with us! "Zhou Huang shouted and took the lead to run back.
Everyone felt a tinge of regret. When they entered the Heavenly Star Secret Territory, they should have retreated when they encountered the puppet archer.
Most importantly, they had been in the Heavenly Star Secret Territory for so many years. Although they did not get the Ancestral Dragon egg, they knew the dangers of the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land like the back of their hands. For more than a decade, no one had died in the Heavenly Star Secret Territory. It was because of this that they were numb. If they retreated now, they would be in big trouble.
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
Many Divine Sea Realm experts turned into streaks of light and ran back the way they came. As for the small dragon-shaped monsters on the way here, they did not care about them anymore.
Luo Zheng followed closely behind them. He had the same goal as these Divine Sea Realm experts. They were all here for the Ancestral Dragon egg. However, there was a fundamental difference. Luo Zheng obtained the Ancestral Dragon egg because of Qinglong. However, these Divine Sea Realm experts suddenly retreated. This was going to be troublesome!
However, at this moment, a black blade light suddenly appeared from a building in front of them!
"Whoosh!"
That black blade light spread out at an extremely fast speed.
"Pfft!"
The corpse of a Divine Sea Realm expert at the front was instantly cut in half. Then, the Divine Sea Realm expert's soul left his mind and floated to the side.
Seeing this, some of the Spirit Sea Realm warriors behind him jumped up and some fell to the ground to avoid being killed by the saber light. But none of them looked good.
"Who is it!"
Tianfeng War Emperor felt the presence of Genuine Origin Energy in this blade light. Clearly, it was not a Ferocious Beast or a puppet.
On the right side of the crowd was an exquisite tower. The foundation of this tower seemed to be very solid. Even in the earthquake, it did not collapse.
"Crack!"

"Ha, strange. Why are there a bunch of corpses running around here?" The young man in black sniffed. "They don't seem to be zombies. They seem to have abandoned their bodies and occupied the corpses of the Dragon Bloodline. Strange, strange, very strange … Senior Sister, what is going on?"
As soon as the young man in black finished speaking, another person stood out from the ruins. It was a woman with silver hair. Her silver hair aside, she was a peerless beauty just in terms of appearance! However, in the woman's right eye, a pale pink flower grew out. The roots of the flower pierced deep into her eye socket. It looked extremely terrifying!
"Hmph, isn't this simple? These guys can't even resist the power of the blood sacrifice. They actually came up with such a method. They used the Soul Core to control the corpses of the Dragon Bloodline Clan and sneaked into the Heavenly Star Secret Realm. They should be natives of this great world!" The silver-haired woman replied indifferently.
As soon as she finished speaking, a tall and sturdy man walked out from the front of the street, carrying a fiery red giant scythe on his shoulder.
The tall and sturdy man walked towards Tianfeng War Emperor and the others. He said indifferently, "It seems that our luck is not bad. There is no bait to lure that thing out. Although these guys are corpses, their souls are still alive. They are the best bait!"
The words of these three people were as if they did not put the Divine Sea Realm in their eyes. Whether it was the Sky Fortune King, Tianfeng War Emperor, or Zhou Huang, their expressions were extremely ugly!
"Friend, what do you mean?" Tianfeng War Emperor asked coldly.
The silver-haired woman smiled faintly and said, "Who is your friend? Isn't it obvious? We are capturing the Evil Dragon. We want to borrow your corpses and … your souls! "
"What arrogance! Do you think you can keep so many of us here? "War Emperor Da Yu said coldly.
Although these Divine Sea Realm cultivators could not display their full strength because of the corpses, they were still a group of Divine Sea Realm cultivators. They were not easy to bully.
"Hehehe … Arrogant? No one has ever said that before! "The silver-haired woman's laughter rang out.
The tall and sturdy man carried the giant scythe and walked towards the other side. He did not seem to care about Luo Zheng and the others. He said indifferently, "Pei 'er, capture them. Boss can't wait any longer!"

Chapter 890
The Divine Sea Realm cultivators were the top existences in the Great Thousand World. When had they ever been looked down upon like this?
Even though the strength of these three people was unfathomable, the Divine Sea Realm cultivators were filled with anger!
However, the other party had no interest in tangling with them!

God has arranged for these baits to appear in front of us. Haha, the boss's luck is really invincible!"
As soon as her bell-like laughter faded away, wisps of pink mist drifted out from the flower.
"Xiang Hui, Spiritual Confinement …"

"The Soul Core, the Soul Core has lost its effect! How is this possible! "

"I can't get out of this corpse's mind!"
The Divine Sea Realm cultivators used the power of the Soul Core to drive the corpses, but the silver-haired woman seemed to be able to easily disturb the power of the Soul Core!
It meant that she had easily cut off the connection between the Divine Sea Realm cultivators' souls and their corpses!
This wasn't the most terrifying thing. They could just give up on the corpses and leave. However, when some Divine Sea Realm cultivators tried, they found that their souls were firmly imprisoned in the minds of the corpses. They couldn't escape at all.
The silver-haired woman seemed to have sensed the actions of the Divine Sea Realm cultivators and said indifferently, "Don't try. A bunch of Battle Soul Realm cultivators can't break out of the confinement."
At this moment, even Luo Zheng couldn't move!
Even though he was occupying Feng Nianyun's physical body and not her corpse, he was still using his soul core to control this physical body. Due to the Great Soul-Sealing Technique, Feng Nianyun's physical body couldn't be controlled by force. After all, even Feng Nianyun's spirit couldn't control her physical body. Using her soul core was only a temporary replacement.
"Now, turn around." The silver-haired woman smiled faintly and walked past them. All the corpses turned around at the same time. Everyone was completely under the control of the silver-haired woman.
"Eh? Why is there still a living body? " At this moment, the silver-haired woman noticed Luo Zheng in the crowd. Then, she walked towards Luo Zheng. "And this body is actually at the Ultimate God Stage … It's actually controlled by a soul core. How strange."
The silver-haired woman sized up Luo Zheng. Indeed, she couldn't figure it out. She naturally couldn't have guessed that Feng Nianyun's free soul had been sealed by the Soul Sealing Art before being possessed by Luo Zheng.
"However, this body is really beautiful!" The silver-haired woman's eyes flashed with a strange luster. She leaned her face closer and kissed Luo Zheng's cheek. She smiled and said, "If I don't need this body later, I will keep you."
Luo Zheng didn't feel anything after being kissed by a woman. However, Feng Nianyun, who was in his internal world, had goosebumps. "Disgusting. Luo Zheng, I'm going to kill her!" Feng Nianyun roared in his internal world. At this moment, lightning flashed and thunder rumbled in his internal world. Feng Nianyun's soul was fluctuating violently. She seemed to be disgusted by the silver-haired woman's actions.
Luo Zheng said helplessly, "I can't even move now. I can't even protect myself. How can I kill her?"
No one would have thought that so many living beings would suddenly appear in the originally empty Heavenly Star Secret Territory. At first, it was those dragon-shaped monsters. Then, it was these three strange powerhouses …
What happened in the Heavenly Star Secret Realm? Were these people also here for the Ancestral Dragon Egg?
"Who are these people? Do you know? "Luo Zheng asked.
Feng Nianyun snorted coldly. Then, she said unhappily, "These people are from the Demi-Immortal Tribe! And they are hunters from the Demi-Immortal Tribe! "
"The Demi-Immortal Tribe!" Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly lit up. This wasn't the first time Luo Zheng came into contact with the Demi-Immortal Tribe. The Tower of Sin on the Sea God Continent belonged to the Demi-Immortal Tribe!
Back then, he passed the test of the Talent Stele and was recognized by the Demi-Immortal Tribe. However, Luo Zheng still didn't know what the Demi-Immortal Tribe was and what kind of abilities they had.
"What exactly is the Demi-Immortal Tribe?" Luo Zheng asked again.

"Didn't that woman say she would let you go?" Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
"I'd rather die than fall into her hands!" Feng Nianyun said angrily. Then, she continued, "The Demi-Immortal Tribe is a group of mavericks. At the same time, they are also a group of idiots!"
"Idiot?" Luo Zheng's face showed a puzzled expression. "Where did this come from?"
Feng Nianyun said indifferently, "The Demi-Immortal Tribe is also known as the Resetters. Strictly speaking, the Demi-Immortal Tribe isn't a tribe. Instead, they are a group formed by the super geniuses of various tribes. The outside world calls the Demi-Immortal Tribe the Resetters because they have always been planning to return the universe to zero!"
Luo Zheng's face was full of curiosity. "What do you mean by 'return to zero'?"
"To destroy the Heavenly Dao and stop the cycle of reincarnation!" Feng Nianyun quickly answered.
The Heavenly Dao was in a constant cycle of reincarnation. One cycle of reincarnation was 3.6 billion years. In other words, it was an epoch.
In the universe, there were many powerful beings who wanted to destroy the Heavenly Dao. At the end of each epoch, a great era would open and the Heavenly Dao would compete with it!
If the Heavenly Dao was destroyed, the epoch would come to an end. Then, a new cycle of the Heavenly Dao would be formed. The lower, middle, and upper laws of the universe would be reconstructed!
All of the civilizations in the universe were in a constant cycle of reincarnation. They were constantly starting anew and giving birth to new civilizations.
However, the Demi-Immortal Tribe was very unique. Their goal wasn't just to destroy the Heavenly Dao. They also wanted to stop the cycle of reincarnation and end it.
"If they end the cycle of reincarnation and don't give birth to a new Heavenly Dao, then …" Luo Zheng was even more curious.
Feng Nianyun smiled faintly and said, "Then the entire universe will become a land of death. No living beings will be born, no living beings will die, and no conflict will occur. Everything will return to zero."
Luo Zheng was speechless. "What's the point of doing that?"
"They think it's meaningful, so they're a bunch of idiots. They're very powerful idiots," Feng Nianyun said in a resigned manner. "That woman affected the power of the soul core, but I can undo it. I'll find an opportunity later. You just have to escape."
"But they …" The most powerful beings in the four divine kingdoms were all being controlled, including the four monarchs.
"Who cares if they die?" Feng Nianyun said in an annoyed tone.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly. Indeed, whether it was from his point of view or from Feng Nianyun's point of view, Luo Zheng didn't need to help the four divine kingdoms. However, if there was a chance to save their lives, Luo Zheng wouldn't give it up!
Under the silver-haired woman's control, the corpses turned into zombies that followed behind her slowly!
Many of the Divine Sea Realm cultivators were trapped in their minds and couldn't move. However, they could still observe their surroundings. As they continued to move forward, many dragon-shaped monsters rushed out.
The silver-haired woman didn't seem to care about them. She just kept moving forward. Just as the dragon-shaped monsters were about to pounce on her, the flowers in her eyes spun gently and a faint fragrance wafted out. Then, the dragon-shaped monsters became intoxicated and lay on the ground, motionless.
Just like that, she slowly moved forward. After passing by the Linglong Lake, she continued to move towards the center of the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land!
"Boom!"
"Boom!"

Chapter 891
Luo Zheng couldn't move, but he could feel the huge commotion in front of him. He was secretly shocked.

Feng Nianyun snorted. "Who knows? These idiots have always done strange things. Once, the Demi-Immortal Clan carried out a plan to destroy planets. They wanted to destroy all the planets in the universe. At that time, more than 20,000 planets were destroyed. In the end, they were slapped in the face by the Heavenly Dao, and many people died. Only then did they become obedient."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was completely speechless.
Thinking about what he experienced in the Tower of Sin, Luo Zheng had a bad premonition. He was recognized by the Demi-Immortal Clan. Did that mean he was also a member of the Demi-Immortal Clan?
However, it was better for him not to join such a crazy clan …
His body moved forward involuntarily. After passing through a pile of ruins, Luo Zheng saw a circular pit in front of him. Six people were standing at the edge of the pit! The burly man and the black-clothed young man were also standing at the edge of the pit!
Dragon-shaped monsters surged out from the crater!

As soon as those small dragon-shaped monsters jumped out, they attacked the people at the edge of the pit!
Waves of invisible sound waves shot toward the six people!
