"This guy is really patient. He can't even force it out!" The burly man said indifferently.
The silver-haired woman controlled the corpses of many Divine Ocean Realm experts and slowly walked over. She said, "If that's the case, let's try another method. Let's see if we can lure that thing out. Boss, you're really good at predicting things. The Heavenly Star Secret Territory should have been empty. We went out and wandered around, and we found some bait. I brought all the bait!"
With the addition of the silver-haired woman, the Demi-Immortal Clan had sent out seven people to try and capture whatever was inside the crater.
The person whom the silver-haired woman called "Boss" was wearing a blood-red cloak and a silver mask. Hearing this, the person wearing the silver mask nodded and said, "Then let's try it! Ha Peng, it's your turn! "
"Okay, Boss!" Among the seven, one of them was wearing a tattered straw hat. With a simple and honest smile, he took out a golden fishing rod!
He waved the fishing rod in his hand, and a green line swung towards the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms. Then, one of the Divine Sea Realm warriors was "hooked"!
The soul cores that the four Divine Kingdoms used to control the corpses had been sealed by the silver-haired woman, so all of the corpses and even the souls in their minds were like pieces of wood. They couldn't move at all, and they couldn't resist being hung up like this!
However, they were all in a state of consciousness …
A mighty warrior at the Divine Sea Realm had been fished up just like that. He had been used as bait to feed something in this pit!
This kind of fear made the souls of many Divine Sea Realm warriors tremble.
The silver-haired woman felt the fear in the souls of these Divine Sea Realm warriors. She said indifferently, "However, the stronger the soul waves, the more tempting it will be to that thing down there. Ha Peng, let's try it!"
Ha Peng nodded and waved the fishing rod in his hand. He slowly lowered the body that he had caught!
However, when he lowered the body halfway, dozens of dragon-shaped monsters suddenly rushed out of the crater. These dragon-shaped monsters rushed up and directly opened their mouths, releasing sound waves towards the body.
"Roar! Roar! Roar … "
Under this kind of angry roar, the body of the Divine Sea Realm warrior, along with his soul, was directly turned into powder!
The "Boss" with the silver mask said indifferently, "Hmph, this guy has a backbone. He didn't eat it. Ha Peng, put a few more in!"
Ha Peng's expression didn't change. He gently waved the fishing rod in his hand. The thin green line constantly fluttered and flew towards the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms.
All the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms had fear in their eyes. Although they couldn't move, they could still see. If they were touched by this thin fishing line, it would be the same as being sentenced to death!
It wasn't easy for them to reach the Divine Sea Realm. It wasn't easy for them to hear that the ascension passage could be repaired. Who would be willing to die here?
However, they didn't have the ability to resist. They could only watch as this fishing line dictated their life and death. If they had to blame something, they could only blame themselves for being too weak.
This time, the fishing line was gently strung and directly "hooked" six Divine Sea Realm warriors! Among them were two Divine Sea Realm masters of the Profound Jade Martial House. In the four Divine Kingdoms, they were considered to be above the masses and were known as "almighty" existences. However, in this place, they were reduced to insignificant "bait" …
"This time, I'll let the line be longer. Let it have a taste first. As the saying goes, once it has tasted the marrow, it will be hungry for more. Only when it has tasted the marrow will it take the bait. Hehe!" This time, Ha Peng didn't drag the six people up into the air. Instead, he directly released the fishing line and threw the six people into the big pit.
At the same time, the flower in the silver-haired woman's eyes turned slightly. Instantly, screams of despair came from the big pit.
"Eh? Little senior sister, why are these guys screaming? "The black-clothed young man asked curiously.
The silver-haired woman smiled faintly. "I've released a part of the confinement force. Naturally, they can scream and add some flavor to the guy down there! Hehe! "
"Little senior sister, you're too naughty …" The black-clothed young man shook his head and smiled.
Originally, being hung on the fishing line had already made these Divine Sea Realm warriors extremely fearful. Their souls were desperately struggling in their minds. When the silver-haired woman suddenly released a part of the confinement force, they naturally screamed out of instinct.
However, the screams only lasted for one or two breaths of time before they came to an abrupt end!
Then, everyone heard the sound of bones cracking coming from the big pit.
"Crack, crack …"
It was as if there was a big mouth that kept rejecting these corpses.
After waiting for a while, the silver-haired woman smiled and said, "It should be finished, right?"
"Again! This time, let's see if it takes the bait! " Ha Peng waved the fishing rod again and skewered a few more corpses. This time, the green fishing line was actually swinging towards Luo Zheng!
Seeing this thin green line, Luo Zheng and Feng Nianyun both frowned. Luo Zheng asked, "Now, do you want to resist?"
Feng Nianyun remained calm. "No hurry! Let's wait and see. The owner of this fishing rod is only at the Divine Realm. Among the seven people, I'm afraid I can't beat the guy with the silver mask! The key is, how much of my physical strength can you unleash? "
This was not Luo Zheng's physical body. The attribute of the True Origin cultivated was also very different from Luo Zheng's. If Luo Zheng were to control it, the effect would be greatly reduced. Perhaps Luo Zheng could only choose to run away, and he might not be able to escape.
There was only one chance. Luo Zheng had to be careful.
Just as the thin green line was about to wrap around Luo Zheng, the silver-haired woman stopped it and said, "Ha Peng, leave this for me!"
"Alive?" Ha Peng just realized that Feng Nianyun's physical body was not a corpse, but a living person.
"Yes." The silver-haired woman nodded.
Ha Peng smiled with a strange expression. "No wonder. I was wondering when your taste became so strange. You're interested in corpses!"
Hearing Ha Peng's words, the silver-haired woman's face darkened. "Talk more nonsense and I'll kill you!"
Ha Peng was used to it. He chuckled. The thin green line swung gently and swung towards Battle God Yu the Great!
Some of the Divine Ocean Realm experts present were killed, but there were still more than 70 people left. The probability of the kings being chosen was very high. However, Battle God Yu the Great was very unlucky. He was entangled by the thin green line and was immediately caught in the air!
At the same time, the flower in the silver-haired woman's right eye moved slightly, and another part of the seal was lifted.
As soon as the seal was lifted, Battle God Yu the Great roared, "I am the king of the Great Yu Divine Kingdom. I can give you all the power of the kingdom. Please spare my life!"

Chapter 892
When a warrior cultivated to the Divine Sea Realm, he would have one in a hundred million luck.
Even a Divine Sea Realm Almighty could only play a supporting role in the four Divine Kingdoms. The main characters of the four Divine Kingdoms were the four Monarchs!
Battle Emperor Yu the Great had such a strong luck. When he ascended to the Upper World in the future, his future would be limitless. How could he be willing to die here?
Ever since his body had been imprisoned, his thoughts had been constantly spinning. This was probably the biggest predicament he had ever faced in his life. Back then, when he had entered the sea to hunt down the cub that had swallowed the Aqua Beast, he had not felt so powerless!
In his soul form, he had almost no power to fight back in front of these guys. Battle Emperor Yu the Great racked his brains but could not think of a way out!
Therefore, Battle Emperor Yu the Great did not cry out when he was released. He wanted to seize this opportunity to tell the other party that he was the Monarch and that he was willing to pay any price in exchange for his life.
Ha Peng shook the fishing rod in his hand. This time, he did not intend to throw these corpses into the pit. Instead, he hung them above to lure the thing below.
The front end of the fishing rod was only as thick as a pinky finger. The green line was as thin as a strand of hair. However, there was no problem with it hanging six or seven corpses.
"Monarch?" Ha Peng suddenly laughed when he heard Battle Emperor Yu the Great's words. "How big is your Da Yu Divine Kingdom?"
"30 million acres! Four billion living beings! I can give up my position! I can let you be the Monarch of the Divine Kingdom and rule the world! " Battle Emperor Yu the Great, who was hanging in mid-air, could see the situation below the pit. He noticed that there was a pair of huge red eyes below the pit that were emitting a demonic glow. Battle Emperor Yu the Great realized that he did not have much time left. He seized every opportunity to speak! Or rather, to beg for mercy!
"30 million acres …" Ha Peng did not know whether to laugh or cry when he heard this. "How big is 30 million acres? It sounds quite big! "
The Great Yu Divine Nation wasn't small. Among the four Divine Nations, the Great Yu Divine Nation occupied the most land. However, Ha Peng didn't show the slightest bit of interest.
The silver-haired woman said coolly. "Thirty million acres is about one ten-millionth of the size of a Saint in the Demi-Immortal World. It's not even as big as my garden. These savages are really narrow-minded."

In other words, he already understood that everything he said was nonsense. These guys would never take a fancy to anything he had.
At this moment, the ground began to shake again!
"Boss, it's moving. That guy seems to be unable to hold himself back. It's working!" The silver-haired woman smiled with excitement on her face.
"Yes, everyone, be careful. It seems to be coming out." The man with the silver mask nodded.
The seven people standing at the edge of the pit seemed to have entered a battle-ready state. Even though they were very powerful, they didn't dare to relax in the face of the terrifying existence in the pit!
Battle Emperor Yu the Great also sensed that something was amiss. He saw that the pair of glowing red eyes below him were getting closer and closer. That thing was constantly rising!
"Roar …"
A pure black dragon's head suddenly drilled out of the huge pit!
This dragon's appearance was very unique. Ordinary true dragons didn't look fierce. As the saying went, dragons and phoenixes were auspicious. In fact, although a true dragon's aura was mighty, it gave people a peaceful feeling.
However, this giant dragon had six pairs of sharp horns on its head. A pair of blood-red dragon eyes exuded a strong evil aura. If an ordinary warrior was enveloped by this evil aura, he would have lost his mind in an instant!
"Roar …"
The pure black dragon leaped out of the pit and opened its mouth to bite at the corpses of the Divine Sea Realm experts.
At this moment, Battle Emperor Da Yu's face finally revealed a look of despair. He knew that no matter how much he resisted, he wouldn't be able to escape this calamity …
Seeing that he was already within the range of the dragon's mouth, there was no way he could survive this bite. However, at this moment, Ha Peng gently flicked the fishing rod and pulled the corpses of Battle Emperor Yu Da Yu and the others higher up into the sky.
The huge dragon bit down, but it bit nothing!
Of course, Ha Peng wasn't trying to save the lives of Battle Emperor Da Yu and the others. He was purely trying to lure more of the giant dragon out of the pit. As for whether Battle Emperor Da Yu and the others lived or died, it didn't matter at all.
"Roar!"
Although the giant dragon's aura was mighty and it didn't seem to be any weaker than an ordinary true dragon, it was probably still young and hadn't gained much intelligence.
Since it didn't bite down, it actually stretched out its body and raised its huge dragon head to continue chasing after the corpses above!
At this moment, Ha Peng grabbed the fishing rod in his hand and swung Battle Emperor Da Yu and the others out of the pit. Following which, Battle Emperor Da Yu and the others slammed heavily onto the ground. Only then did Ha Peng say, "It's up to you guys!"
As soon as Ha Peng finished speaking, the burly man who had been standing at the edge of the pit, ready to attack, leaped up. The fiery-red giant scythe in his hand instantly burst into flames!
In an instant, the burly man charged towards the giant dragon. The giant scythe drew a circular arc in the air and slashed heavily at the giant dragon's neck.
"Ha! Break for me! "
The tall and sturdy man let out a loud roar. This giant dragon was indeed an existence comparable to a real dragon. Even Luo Zheng was secretly shocked by the power released by the tall and sturdy man's attack. The power contained within the attack was difficult to estimate. However, the giant red scythe in the hands of this Divine Realm expert was at least a divine weapon. Its power was almost unimaginable to Luo Zheng!
However, when this unparalleled slash landed on the dragon's scales, it only produced a burst of intense sparks and the sound of metal clashing against metal. It did not manage to slice open the dragon's neck!
"What a strong defense! What kind of dragon is this? "Luo Zheng sighed and asked.
From the beginning, Feng Nianyun seemed to have stopped talking. She just stared blankly at the giant dragon in the pit.
Seeing that Feng Nianyun didn't respond, Luo Zheng could only ask again, "Hey, why aren't you talking?"
After a while, Feng Nianyun said, "No wonder. Those dragon-shaped monsters must have been summoned by this thing. I really didn't expect that after being trapped here for so many years, I would be able to wait until the day an evil dragon appeared …"
"Evil dragon?" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned and asked.
Feng Nianyun didn't hear Luo Zheng's words. She muttered to herself, "That's still not right. Why would an evil dragon appear? There's an ancestral dragon egg hidden in the sacred land of the dragon bloodline. Could it be that an evil dragon hatched from the ancestral dragon egg? That's impossible … "
"Hey, what's an evil dragon?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Feng Nianyun finally reacted and said, "The evil dragon is the opposite of the ancestral dragon. It's a belief inheritance derived from our race!"
"A belief inheritance derived from the ancestral dragon?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Feng Nianyun said, "Actually, whether it's the ancestral dragon or the evil dragon, they shouldn't exist in this world. They're just something imagined by the true dragon race!"
"Something imagined …" Luo Zheng felt that the more he listened, the more incredulous he felt.
Feng Nianyun understood Luo Zheng. As a warrior from the lower realm, Luo Zheng's understanding of certain things in the upper realm was still quite limited. She said curiously, "Didn't Seventh Grandpa tell you? It's not strange to imagine things. Just like your so-called visualization, once you visualize enough, you can use your True Yuan to visualize what you want! "

Chapter 893
If a warrior wanted to materialize their Quintessential Essence, they had to do it through visualization!
For example, to materialize Quintessential Essence into a saber, one had to find a real saber and visualize it continuously. The more serious the visualization, the longer the time, the sharper the Quintessential Essence turned into a saber!
However, such visualization also relied on Quintessential Essence because Quintessential Essence itself could materialize. It was just that the more realistic one's visualization was, the more realistic the object would be.
Without Quintessential Essence, one would not be able to think of a saber even if they racked their brains …
As for what Feng Nianyun said, gathering billions of people to visualize an object and then visualizing it alive, it sounded inconceivable. It was beyond Luo Zheng's comprehension.
"You can visualize a divine weapon out of thin air just like that?" Luo Zheng asked in surprise.
Feng Nianyun said indifferently, "There's nothing strange about that. The Ancestral Dragon is the direct religion of the True Dragon race. Therefore, the birth of the Ancestral Dragon can bring prosperity to the True Dragon race."

Feng Nianyun continued to answer, "How is that possible? Who's full or not full? It's just that no matter if it's a race or a living being, there's always a dark and bright side in their hearts. The Evil Dragon is the embodiment of the True Dragon race's dark side. It's a little like hell. It seems like you don't know either. In fact, the entire hell was created by all the living beings in the universe, right? "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was shocked once again.
The Yellow Springs River, the Gates of Hell …
Luo Zheng had heard people mention this place before, but he never imagined that hell was also a product of imagination. It was fine if it was just imagination, but the problem was that when one thought about it too much, it actually existed!
It seemed that even if one had a brain, one shouldn't think blindly. Luo Zheng sighed inwardly. There seemed to be too many secrets in this universe waiting for him to unearth!
"Roar!"
The Evil Dragon seemed to be in a state of rage. Flames began to emerge from its huge dragon head, as if a large dragon's breath was brewing in its mouth! Although the burly man's attack did not injure it, it had completely enraged the evil dragon.

"Huff huff huff …"
Countless green threads spiraled toward the Evil Dragon, wrapping around the dragon's mouth in the blink of an eye! Then, the tall and sturdy man actually stuck close to him and continued to use the giant scythe in his hand to launch a new round of attacks.
Looking at the evil dragon, Luo Zheng said, "This evil dragon doesn't seem to be very strong. The seven people from the Demi-Immortal Tribe are just a bunch of Ultimate God Stage experts. This evil dragon doesn't seem to be able to resist them."
Feng Nianyun calmly replied, "This evil dragon needs to undergo seven metamorphoses before it can truly grow. It seems that this evil dragon has only undergone three metamorphoses. Speaking of which, the Demi-Immortal Tribe has helped you by killing this evil dragon. If you leave this evil dragon alone, it will definitely consume the entire Great Thousand World as its food after completing its fourth metamorphosis. It will then plot to undergo its fifth metamorphosis. "
"Consume the Great Thousand World as its food?" Luo Zheng frowned. Luo Zheng had merged with the will of the Great Thousand World, but he didn't have the time to explore the mysteries of this will. "Mm." Feng Nianyun calmly said, "Let's not worry about this evil dragon for now. The seven of them are busy dealing with this evil dragon. You need to find a chance to escape."
In truth, the Demi-Immortal Tribe didn't have much desire to kill Luo Zheng and the others. In their eyes, the Divine Sea Realm experts of the four Divine Nations were insignificant ants. The key was that they could use them as bait after their deaths. If they couldn't be used, there was no reason to kill them.
However, since the Demi-Immortal Tribe was known as the Resetters and even tried to stop the cycle of reincarnation, there was a high possibility that they would kill the martial artists of the four Divine Nations after dealing with this evil dragon.
Luo Zheng couldn't even protect himself now. Escaping was probably his only choice. As for Huo Yuner, Huo Chen, and the other Monarchs, if he wanted to save them, he would have to find another way. However, there was no doubt that it would be difficult to save them.
"But the seven of them are all in the Ultimate Divine Realm. Can I really escape by controlling your body?" Luo Zheng asked hesitantly.
Feng Nianyun was relatively calm. She said, "Apart from their boss, the others are only in the Ultimate Divine Realm. It's not a big problem. However, when you control my body, you can only use less than 30% of my strength. I'll teach you an escape technique now. Later, you can use my True Yuan and get out of here as soon as possible!"
Luo Zheng didn't have a better idea at the moment. He could only use what he learned. At least it was better than using her True Yuan!
"This is a set of Wind Escape Technique. My True Yuan can match this Wind Escape Technique to the greatest extent, but how much you can comprehend will depend on yourself!" After Feng Nianyun finished speaking, she used her consciousness to transmit a set of Wind Escape Technique chants to Luo Zheng.
"This Wind Escape Technique is called Chasing Cloud Movement Technique! It was comprehended from the Divine Beast Chasing Cloud Lion. The Chasing Cloud Lion is one of the fastest Divine Beasts, second only to the Heavenly Rat! Comprehend it well. If you can comprehend about 30%, you can escape from them when they are unprepared. Remember … the Cloud Body Dharma Body allows you to travel a thousand miles in a day. You can travel above the Nine Heavens and below the Nine Underworlds … "
Chasing Cloud Lion?
When he heard Feng Nianyun teach him the Wind Escape Technique, Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
When he obtained a jade bone from the Chasing Cloud Lion and moved it into his Baihui Stage, he gained the Chasing Cloud Lion's innate speed. However, the jade bone was used to refine his body. It was only revealed when he used his Vital Essence.
However, Vital Essence and True Yuan were two different types of energy. Although their natures were poles apart, they still had something in common. In addition, Luo Zheng's current innate talent was extremely powerful. It wasn't difficult for him to comprehend the Chasing Cloud Lion's Wind Escape Technique!
Luo Zheng's soul quietly floated in Feng Nianyun's mind. He closed his eyes and began to comprehend.
Feng Nianyun's soul was in her inner world, quietly watching Luo Zheng. The Chasing Cloud Movement Technique was an extremely difficult movement technique. Moreover, one needed to comprehend the Wind Elemental Law. She didn't expect Luo Zheng to be able to learn it in a short time. It was just a last-minute preparation. Even if it wasn't fast, it was light. Coupled with her own body's compatibility, it would definitely be useful!
"Rumble, rumble …"
Above the crater, the Evil Dragon began to become irritable. The battle between the seven Demi-Immortals and the Evil Dragon had reached its climax.
After an incense stick's worth of time, Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes!
"How much did you comprehend?" In such a short time, Feng Nianyun didn't expect Luo Zheng to comprehend too much. As long as it was enough to stimulate the Wind Elemental Law in her own True Yuan, it would be enough!
"More or less." Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He was very sensitive to the Chasing Cloud Lion's innate talent. This movement technique was based on the Chasing Cloud Lion. Therefore, after receiving her teachings, Luo Zheng had a completely new understanding of this movement technique. From a certain perspective, this movement technique was originally tailor-made for Luo Zheng!
"Roar!"
The Evil Dragon broke free from Ha Peng's restraints and let out a violent dragon roar. Its entire body drilled out of the crater and spiraled into the sky.
"Luo Lin! Hold it back! "

The moment the imprisonment power was dispelled, Luo Zheng immediately felt that his soul and her body were connected again. At this moment, the True Yuan in his body was quickly mobilized by Luo Zheng!

Chapter 894
Although the Demi-Immortals had focused all their attention on the dragon, Luo Zheng's sudden disappearance still attracted their attention.

"This guy …"
Inside her inner world, Feng Nianyun was completely shocked!

But Feng Nianyun never expected that Luo Zheng would be able to completely comprehend the Chasing Cloud movement technique in such a short time!
Just how talented was this guy?

Even in the True Dragon Realm where geniuses were as common as clouds, she had never seen someone with such abnormal talent.
In thirty minutes, he comprehended seventy percent of the Chasing Cloud movement technique? No, eighty percent. It was probably more than that … The black line behind this guy was a phenomenon that would only appear when the Chasing Cloud Lion compressed the wind to the extreme when it was running at high speed!
Although Feng Nianyun could unleash the full potential of the Chasing Cloud movement technique, this cultivation technique was ultimately created by imitating the divine beast Chasing Cloud Lion's talent. When the Chasing Cloud Lion was running at high speed, it would leave behind a thin black line behind it. It was like a brush brushing black lines in the air!

Feng Nianyun was somewhat unable to accept this!
Luo Zheng didn't know that Feng Nianyun's heart was fluctuating in such a manner. He was sighing at how powerful a Paramount Divine realm body was. The Quintessential Essence within one's body was incomparably pure. Typically speaking, Quintessential Essence was long-lasting and long-lasting. However, he could sense that the explosive Quintessential Essence within Feng Nianyun's body was comparable to his Astral Essence.
.....
... In just a few breaths of time, Luo Zheng had already rushed to the exit of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land. He was heading straight for the arched door!
Due to his speed, Luo Zheng didn't even have time to check the situation of the arched door. He charged straight for it. In the end, he easily passed through the arched door. He discovered that he hadn't entered the teleportation channel. Instead, he had directly passed through the arched door.
"This … the teleportation channel has been closed!" Luo Zheng was immediately alarmed.
When he entered the Heavenly Feather Holy Land, he had relied on the two dragon-shaped keys to activate the arched door of the Heavenly Feather Holy Land. He had activated a teleportation channel within the arched door. But now, the teleportation channel had disappeared!
Without the teleportation channel, the arched door was nothing more than a decoration to Luo Zheng.
Following that, a bell-like laughter was heard. "You got a fright, didn't you? Be a good boy and come back with me. I won't kill you! "

" The silver-haired woman chuckled and said, "When we're done here, I can take you to a place! Even the ascension channel in this world has been destroyed. Whoosh …

Seeing this, Luo Zheng didn't stop and once again flew past.

"What is that?"

All kinds of strange flowers and grasses were everywhere, and vines were coiled around the outer walls of the spire. The most peculiar thing was that these plants were growing at a speed visible to the naked eye. They were growing at a crazy speed, and then they quickly withered, bloomed, bore fruit, withered, and sprouted seeds …
"Time! The speed of time passing in this barrier is so fast! "Luo Zheng said curiously," What is inside this tower? "
Feng Nianyun shook her head, "I'm not sure. The outer barrier has isolated almost all the aura inside …"
"Go in!" No matter what was inside, Luo Zheng didn't think too much about it and directly rushed towards the barrier. The moment he got close to the barrier, Luo Zheng poured out his True Essence and punched the barrier.
"Boom!"
Even with the control of his Divine Realm body, Luo Zheng's punch didn't break the barrier.

Chapter 895
After this punch, another snow-white flower bloomed. The silver-haired woman stepped out of the flower quietly. Seeing Luo Zheng's action, she smiled and said, "Qing Feng's barrier is not that easy to break!"

The silver-haired woman giggled. "If I tell you, will you follow me obediently? I promise you won't suffer any losses! "

"A supreme artifact! The Black Star Pickaxe! "The silver-haired woman screamed.

Hearing Qing's words, Boss's Divine Realm experts, and Stellar Holy land.

What they were afraid of had really happened. Qing Feng's barrier covered the spire. With Boss Mo personally changing the flow of time, it wouldn't take long for the Ancestral Dragon to hatch. They only needed to focus on dealing with the Evil Dragon.

The flow of time in the barrier was completely different from the outside world. The flow of time here seemed to have increased by countless times. The plants inside the barrier were constantly multiplying, dying, multiplying, and dying.

But because of the delay of a dozen breaths, the time between her and Luo Zheng was pulled apart by a large amount!
It must be known that the flow of time here was ten thousand times faster. In other words, the time the silver-haired woman hesitated for a dozen breaths was ten thousand breaths of time when Luo Zheng entered the barrier.
Of course, from Luo Zheng's point of view, he couldn't feel it at all.

Behind the door was a pitch-black space.
He turned his head and looked at the sky. Perhaps because of the Law of Time, under the interference of the Law of Time, the entire Heavenly Stellar Holy Land had disappeared from Luo Zheng's eyes except for the area around the spire.
In any case, the silver-haired woman didn't chase him in. Only then did Luo Zheng step into the door!
In the void, there were steps that emitted light. These steps circled the void and spiraled upwards. Luo Zheng stepped on the steps and began to climb up rapidly.
After climbing countless steps, Luo Zheng suddenly saw a huge chain at the top of the steps. Looking along the chain, Luo Zheng saw a small glowing object in the middle of the chain!
As for the glowing object, it was bound by numerous chains!

"The Ancestral Dragon egg." A calm voice came from his Inner World. Feng Nianyun obviously also noticed the Ancestral Dragon egg.

Chapter 896

" Feng Nianyun shook her head and said, "Unfortunately, I don't specialize in the Law of Space. I only comprehended one level of the Law of Space back then."

The Tower of Sin originally belonged to the Heavenly Feather Clan. Luo Zheng was not lying.

Chapter 897
"A pagoda?"

The three Towers of Trials of the Heaven Immortal Clan, although the names were different, regardless of whether it was the shape or the details, they were almost the same. In fact, the three Towers of Trials were not only used for trials, but also for suppression.
Gu Pei 'er's talent was extremely high. In the other "Illusory Pagoda", her talent was also ranked in the first row!
According to what Gu Pei 'er knew, in the Talent Stele's test for martial artists, only those who were ranked in the first two rows were qualified to be recognized by the Heaven Immortal Clan.
As for the martial artists below the second row, although their names were recorded, there were countless geniuses of this level in the Upper Realm. They simply didn't have the qualifications to be recognized by the Heaven Immortal Clan.

"Oh …" Gu Pei 'er's expression dimmed, "A guy whose name isn't even on the first row still says he belongs to the Heaven Immortal Clan. Aren't you thinking too much?"
Previously, Gu Pei 'er really thought that Luo Zheng's name was on the second row. If that was the case, then she could indeed treat Luo Zheng as a member of the same clan. The Heaven Immortal Clan valued quality over quantity. Compared to the martial artists of other clans, the Heaven Immortal Clan's overall numbers were still a lot lower. However, every one of them was the elite of the elite!

If the Ascension Passage of this Vast Thousand World was intact, even in the Demon Night Clan of the Upper World, Liu Yu would certainly be able to cultivate to a higher realm and strive for greater achievements.
Now, Luo Zheng couldn't even make it onto the list. This kind of trash, let alone the Heaven Immortal Clan, would not even be considered a genius in other clans. At this moment, a circle of cracks slowly appeared in the middle of the Ancestral Dragon egg. Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly and continued, "Thinking too much? That's not true. Although my name didn't make it into the six rows, my name is listed on it … "

In fact, warriors who could obtain a title were absolutely favored by the heavens in the universe! Therefore, the gap between titles was actually very small. Of course, even if it was a small gap, it was still a gap …
Moreover, titles were divided into gold, silver, and bronze. Gold was the highest, and bronze was the lowest.

After Luo Zheng said this, the flower in Gu Pei 'er's right eye suddenly began to spin rapidly. The roots of her flower were buried deep in her eyeball. Seeing the flower spinning like this, Luo Zheng was very doubtful whether she was in pain or not …

Because of this, Gu Pei 'er found it hard to believe.

Chapter 898
In the entire universe, all the races were trying their best to expand and develop.
If they wanted to develop quickly, they had to find the best geniuses in their race!
Therefore, every race would design a reasonable set of rules and conduct a series of tests and tests.
For each race, the test method was different.
In addition to observing the lotus flower, there were three races in the universe that had the most accurate test methods. They were the human race's "Heavenly Path", the demon race's "Divine-Devil Abyss", and the Demi-Immortal race's three pagodas.
If one could obtain a title from any of the three pagodas, they would receive the protection of the Demi-Immortal race and become a core member of the Demi-Immortal race! Even the lowest bronze titles, "Fledgling" and "Ocean Arrival".
So far, among the Demi-Immortals living in the world, there were no more than a hundred thousand creatures with titles! And among these hundred thousand creatures, there were eighty thousand creatures with bronze titles!
As for those with silver titles, there were more than 18,000!
And there were only a thousand creatures with gold titles …
The problem was that among these thousand, there were only fifty to sixty creatures with Rakshasa and Asura titles …
As for the Demi-Immortal titles.
Since ancient times, there were less than six. These six people were the absolute leaders of the Demi-Immortal race and were legendary figures in the universe.
This was why Gu Peier found it hard to believe!
A guy with such a heaven-defying talent actually appeared in front of her. Gu Peier suddenly felt that everything was a little surreal. It was not that she did not believe it, but she did not dare to believe it!
Previously, when she heard about the Linglong Pagoda, she was secretly delighted. This time, not only did the seven of them capture the Ancestral Dragon and the Evil Dragon, making a great contribution, but they also unexpectedly found out about the Linglong Pagoda's whereabouts. If they could bring the Linglong Pagoda back to the Demi-Immortal race, it would definitely be a great contribution. In comparison, the Ancestral Dragon and the Evil Dragon were not that important!
But now, they found a guy with a gold heavenly title, then the Linglong Pagoda seemed insignificant …
At this moment, Gu Peier even wanted to stop capturing the Evil Dragon and the Ancestral Dragon and go to the Linglong Pagoda to find out the truth. If this Luo Zheng really passed the test of the Talent Stele and obtained a gold heavenly title, then they could immediately bring this guy back to the Demi-Immortal race!
Gu Pei 'er could already imagine how shocked the higher-ups of the Demi-Immortal Clan would be when she brought him back ….
"You … You're really not lying to me?" Gu Pei 'er could not suppress the excitement in her heart, and her voice started to tremble.
As a matter of fact, Gu Pei 'er was very proud to be able to rank first on the Talent Stele. However, whenever she met any of the Titled Demi-Immortals, she couldn't help but feel a little unconvinced, and even a little inferior!
However, Gu Pei 'er also understood that talent was only one aspect of a martial artist. With her own hard work and good luck, it wasn't impossible for her to surpass those titled life forms. Such examples could be found everywhere.
But right now, Gu Peier did not have such a feeling when facing this fellow in front of her. That was because the gap between them was simply too great, so great that she could not even think of a comparison in her heart.
Luo Zheng nods and looks at Gu Pei 'er with a faint smile: "Yes, I didn't lie to you … but …"
"But what?" Gu Pei 'er blinked her eyes and asked. She was afraid that Luo Zheng would directly deny it and make her excited for nothing.
Luo Zheng chuckles, "This Ancestral Dragon seems to have hatched …"
Just as they were talking, the eggshell of the Ancestral Dragon egg, which was wrapped in layers of chains, finally cracked open completely. A soft nose directly pried open the eggshell, revealing a furry body inside the eggshell.
That small body did not look like a newborn dragon that had just hatched, but more like a small seahorse, with a slender head and a pair of large eyes that emitted a green light!
After the Ancestral Dragon came out of the eggshell, it swallowed the Ancestral Dragon's eggshell one bite at a time. After swallowing the eggshell, the little dragon looked around.
During this process, Feng Nianyun began to remind Luo Zheng, "Continue to make up a story to deceive that woman. Let the Ancestral Dragon notice you. As long as you succeed at the first glance, the mark will be planted …"
In Feng Nianyun's inner world, she heard Luo Zheng say a lot of things that seemed to have fooled the silver-haired woman. Although she had heard of the Demi-Immortal Clan's pagoda, she had never been there, so she did not understand the specific meaning of Luo Zheng's words.
Feng Nianyun was very impressed by Luo Zheng's words just now. Of course, she was not impressed by Luo Zheng's ability to fool people. She was impressed by Luo Zheng's ability to fool people. She felt that Luo Zheng was completely making things up.
Luo Zheng's ability to brag was really not for show.
After the furry little guy opened its big eyes and stared at Luo Zheng for a long time, Luo Zheng smiled and told Gu Pei 'er that the Ancestral Dragon had hatched!
"Caw, caw!"
The furry little dragon fluttered, but it easily floated out of the refracted space and pounced toward Luo Zheng.
If Gu Pei 'er had seen this scene before, she would have been furious. They had painstakingly set up all kinds of traps in the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land to capture the Ancestral Dragon and the evil dragon in one fell swoop. But now, Luo Zheng had ruined everything. This was definitely the worst thing that could happen.
But after Gu Pei 'er confirmed that Luo Zheng's words were most likely true, the Ancestral Dragon egg did not seem so important, so at this moment, her mood was very calm.
If this guy really had the title of Golden Demi-Immortal, it would be his fortune to be recognized by the Ancestral Dragon. It could even be described as destiny …
"Caw, caw, caw!"
The newly hatched little dragon did not alienate Luo Zheng at all. It pounced into Luo Zheng's arms, and its furry little head kept rubbing against Luo Zheng's chest.
Generally speaking, creatures that were born from eggs would have relatively complete mobility after breaking out of their shells. They would also be able to think independently to a certain extent. That was why this kind of creature could have imprinted behavior on its body. In other words, the first thing it saw was its mother.
But creatures like humans were completely ignorant after birth. They needed a long time of training and cultivation to gradually establish a certain degree of cognition. Such creatures would not have imprinting behavior.
The Ancestral Dragon might be the belief of the True Dragon Clan, but it was also affected by imprinting behavior.
Therefore, in the little dragon's mind, a strong imprint was produced! It was also under the influence of this imprint that Luo Zheng became its mother!
In order to prevent imprinting behavior, the seven people of the Demi-Immortal Clan had actually made several preparations. They originally planned to use the Law of Time to hatch the Ancestral Dragon, then seal it and bring it back to the Demi-Immortal Clan. As long as this Ancestral Dragon's imprint behavior did not occur, it would be fine. As for the rest, they would leave it to the higher-ups of the Demi-Immortal Clan to deal with.
However, the speed at which the Ancestral Dragon hatched was faster than they had expected. And when Luo Zheng was being chased by Gu Peier, he accidentally ran into the steeple. With Feng Nianyun's help, all their efforts were for naught.
After holding the little Ancestral Dragon, Luo Zheng originally thought that Gu Pei 'er would attack him in a rage. He was thinking about how to escape.
But now, Gu Pei 'er did not move at all. She just stood there and stared at him blankly. She even ignored the little dragon in his arms!
"What's wrong with this woman?" Luo Zheng was puzzled, so he asked Feng Nianyun in his inner world. Feng Nianyun shook her head, "I'm not sure. Did you just make things up and scare her?"
"I wasn't just making things up …" Luo Zheng retorted.
"Haha, who would believe you?" Feng Nianyun had seen Luo Zheng's bragging skills. She did not believe Luo Zheng's words at all.
At this moment, Gu Pei 'er said softly, "Come with me!"
"Come with you?" Luo Zheng was stunned.

Chapter 899
Gu Peier was very clear about the importance of the matter.
Although the Ancestral Dragon was important, compared to a warrior with a golden Heaven-rank title, it was as light as smoke and dust. It was a trivial matter.
Moreover, even if they found out that it was a mistake, they still had the ability to take the Ancestral Dragon away …
A mark was troublesome, especially the mark in the Ancestral Dragon. If they wanted to remove it, even the Heaven-rank Clan would have to pay a considerable price.
However, it was just a problem. A problem that could be solved was not a real problem.
As for a Heaven-rank title warrior, it was likely that no matter how much trouble they spent, it would not be enough. Therefore, after thinking for a while, Gu Peier decided to verify this matter first.
Seeing that Luo Zheng's golden pupils still showed a hint of vigilance, Gu Peier knew that the other party still had some hostility towards her. She smiled slightly, "I won't catch you. Can you follow me?"
Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
Therefore, Gu Peier walked down the stairs step by step, and Luo Zheng followed behind with the Ancestral Dragon in his arms …
Soon, after the two left the spire, they left the area affected by the Law of Time.
The two of them spent a full hour in the spire. However, under the influence of the thousandfold flow of time, an hour passed in the spire was only a moment to the outside world.
The moment they came out, the flow of time around Luo Zheng returned to normal, and all the plants around the spire covered by the Law of Time began to rapidly wither, bloom, and die. The cycle repeated.
"Whoosh!"
A black shadow, like a swallow shuttling through the clouds, sped towards this side. The figure suddenly stopped in the air. It was the black-robed young man called "Yu Ze".
When he saw Gu Peier, he asked loudly, "Little Senior Sister, what happened? Why did Qing Feng's boundary break …"
However, before Yu Ze could finish his sentence, his gaze landed on Luo Zheng, who was behind Gu Pei 'er. Then, he stared intently at the little dragon in Luo Zheng's arms. Immediately after, Yu Ze's eyes released two sharp rays of light that were as sharp as swords!
"What happened? The Ancestral Dragon … hatched? "
Previously, Yuze had entered the spire many times. Because the Ancestral Dragon was protected by a refracted space, they could not take it away. Therefore, they could only make such a compromise and use the flow of time to urge the Ancestral Dragon to hatch.
Therefore, even if Qing Feng's barrier was broken, they were not too worried. Even if the barrier was broken, it was impossible to take away the Ancestral Dragon egg.
The problem was that the golden-eyed woman had already appeared behind Gu Peier with a small dragon in her arms. This made Yu Ze a little confused. What exactly happened in the steeple?
Without thinking too much, Yuze's first thought was to snatch the little dragon from Luo Zheng's hands!
How could Luo Zheng be willing to stand in place and let Yuze snatch it? The True Origin Energy in his body began to circulate. He executed the Chasing Cloud movement technique and prepared to escape.
"You still want to escape?!"
Yu Ze sneered. He unsheathed the huge black blade in his hand and slashed towards Luo Zheng in an extremely fierce manner.
Gu Pei 'er furrowed her brows, and the flower in her right eye suddenly spun, and a pink light shot out from the stamen of the flower!
"Bang … …"
That pink light instantly shot onto Yu Ze's black blade, and the black blade was firmly sealed in place!
Yu Ze had originally maintained an extremely high speed, waving his black blade as he prepared to slash out. However, the sealing power of Gu Pei 'er's pink light was extremely tyrannical. Under such a seal, the entire black blade was wrapped within it, and the black blade also stopped in midair, unable to move!
Yu Ze completely did not expect that Gu Pei 'er would use such a powerful sealing power to seal his black blade. Under this slash, the blade in his hand suddenly could not be controlled. His hand slipped from the blade's handle, and he could only grab a handful of air and slash towards Luo Zheng. He looked very embarrassed … …
"Little Senior Sister, what are you doing? Could it be that you want to betray the Demi-Immortal Clan? "Yu Ze said coldly.
Seeing Luo Zheng holding the Ancestral Dragon and following behind Little Senior Sister, Yu Ze felt that something was very wrong. Now, Little Senior Sister actually turned around and stopped him. Yu Ze really could not understand this action, so he came to such a conclusion.
Who knew that Gu Pei 'er would shake her head lightly and say, "No, there's something more important."
"What's more important?!" Yu Ze angrily said. He had always respected his Little Senior Sister, but he really could not think of anything more important than the Ancestral Dragon. The purpose of their trip was to collect the newly hatched Ancestral Dragon and the infant Evil Dragon. Although the difficulty of this mission was not high, it was very important!
Gu Pei 'er thought for a moment. There were some things that she did not need to explain to Yu Ze, but seeing Yu Ze's furious expression, she still said, "I found the whereabouts of the Exquisite Pagoda."
"Exquisite Pagoda?"
Yu Ze was slightly stunned, and then he understood … …
As a member of the Demi-Immortal Clan, he had also entered the "Illusionary Pagoda" to train, and his name was on the first row with Gu Pei 'er.
At the same time, Yu Ze also knew that among the three pagodas used for training, one of them was lost!
These three pagodas were very important to the Demi-Immortal Clan. If they could find the Exquisite Pagoda, it was indeed not a small matter.
"But, but … … even if we found the whereabouts of the Exquisite Pagoda, so what? We can't let this guy take away the Ancestral Dragon! " Yu Ze said unconvinced. The Exquisite Pagoda and the Ancestral Dragon were completely unrelated to each other, and there was no conflict between them.
Gu Pei 'er said, "No need to say more. Let's go find Boss Mo first. You will understand in a while."
Yu Ze gritted his teeth, but in the end, he did not say anything. He was very clear about his little senior sister's personality. Under normal circumstances, she was always laughing and joking, and it was very difficult for anything to make her completely serious.
But now, Gu Pei 'er was so serious. Could it be that something big had really happened?
So Gu Pei 'er walked at the front, and Luo Zheng walked behind with the little dragon in his arms. As for Yu Ze, he used a fierce gaze to stare at Luo Zheng, afraid that Luo Zheng would accidentally slip away again … …
The three of them maintained a constant speed as they flew above the Heavenly Stellar Holy Land.
The little dragon in Luo Zheng's arms used its furry head to rub against Luo Zheng's face, and from time to time, it would make "ji ji gu gu" sounds. It was like a human baby, acting spoiled in Luo Zheng's arms.
At this moment, a sorrowful roar suddenly rang out.
"Ao — —"
When the roar was heard, the little dragon in Luo Zheng's arms suddenly trembled and buried its little head into Luo Zheng's chest. It saw Luo Zheng as its mother, and believed that Luo Zheng could protect it!
That roar came from the Evil Dragon in the distance!
Boss Mo was leading the other four people, and they were constantly suppressing the Evil Dragon that was still in its infancy.
"Be careful! This Evil Dragon is going to seal itself and undergo its fourth transformation! "
"Stop it, or we'll be in big trouble!"
"Attack its neck, I've already cut a hole in its neck … …"
"Boom, boom … …"
One powerful attack after another landed on the Evil Dragon's body, and the surface of its body emitted rays of intense light.
At the edge of the crater, the warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms were still staring blankly at the scene playing out in the sky, and their hearts were filled with shock!
The strength of these Reaching Divine Realm warriors was far beyond their imagination. However, the evil dragon's strength was just as horrifying. The evil dragon hadn't even matured yet. It was only equivalent to a Level-11 Ferocious Beast, which was equivalent to a human Divine Sea Realm warrior.
But this Evil Dragon was able to fight against multiple Ultimate Divine Realm warriors by itself. If this Evil Dragon were to mature, just how terrifying would its strength be in the future?
Of course, this was the worry of the Divine Sea Realm warriors. They couldn't even protect themselves right now, and most of them were thinking about their own fates … …

Chapter 900
Without the ability to fight back, they would be at the mercy of others. These warriors understood this better than anyone else as they advanced towards the Divine Sea Realm.
They weren't even worth the time of these Ultimate Divine Realm warriors to kill them. However, once they subdued the evil dragon, their fate would be death.
Even though these Ultimate Divine Realm warriors had no reason to kill them, they also had no reason to let them go. Why would the strong need so many reasons to kill the weak?
At this moment, every Divine Sea Realm warrior had a different mentality.
Some people were unwilling to accept this, such as Battle Emperor Yu the Great. He was unlucky enough to be used by Ha Peng to lure the evil dragon out of its cave. In the end, he managed to survive. However, it wasn't because these guys let him go. It was because he was lucky that he wasn't swallowed by the evil dragon!
Battle Emperor Yu the Great's mind didn't stop at all. He was still thinking about how to get out of this predicament!
Some of the martialists, such as the Tianfeng War Emperor, gradually felt relieved as they waited. However, he regretted bringing Huo Yun 'er and Huo Chen into the Tian Chen Secret Territory. The Tianfeng War Emperor naturally felt bad when he thought about how his daughter and son were going to die in the Tian Chen Secret Territory.
As for Huo Yuner, she was extremely calm. She was wondering if Luo Zheng had escaped.
"Roar …"
At this moment, the evil dragon roared again. It twisted its long and narrow body and tried to crawl back into the pit.
"Don't let it crawl back in! Stop it! "someone shouted.
Boss Mo, who was wearing a silver mask and holding a crooked walking stick, blocked the entrance of the pit before anyone else could!
"Roar!"
Facing Boss Mo, the evil dragon seemed to be afraid of him. It spat out a blazing dragon's breath!
Boss Mo was wearing a mask, so no one could see his expression. He pointed his crutch lightly, and an irregular crystal shield appeared in front of his crutch. This crystal shield directly blocked all of the dragon's breath outside!
Seeing that the dragon's breath couldn't do anything to this person, the evil dragon opened its mouth and tried to swallow Boss Mo whole!
Although this evil dragon was not like those mature True Dragons whose gigantic bodies could cover an entire continent, it was still many times larger than Boss Mo.
Therefore, when the evil dragon opened its mouth, it looked like an eagle trying to swallow a mosquito. Compared to the evil dragon, Boss Mo's body was about the size of a mosquito!
Boss Mo waved the crutch in his hand and made a circle in the air. The tip of the crutch revealed a faint line in the air. Boss Mo muttered softly, "The sky is spinning and the earth is spinning. Go down!"
When the Evil Dragon came into contact with those lines, its huge body suddenly flipped over!
It was as if there was an invisible giant in the air, grabbing the evil dragon with its huge hands and throwing it to the other side!
"Boom …"
The Evil Dragon's gigantic body smashed into the ground, causing a small earthquake. The force spread to the surroundings, lifting up the ground and causing it to ripple like waves.
"Rumble …"
The warriors of the four Divine Kingdoms all fell to the ground due to the earthquake. Many of them were even hit by the flying rocks.
Fortunately, the cultivation of the corpses they occupied wasn't low. When the rocks hit them, they were smashed into pieces … These corpses were enough to protect the souls in their minds.
The evil dragon was thrown down by this huge force, but it didn't suffer much damage. After realizing that it couldn't return to its lair, the evil dragon curled up on the ground and dust started to fall around it …
"Oh no! Stop it! It's going to seal itself! " Boss Mo's expression changed when he saw this scene. He shouted as he rushed toward the evil dragon.
The other four didn't slow down either, especially the burly man. He spun the scythe in his hand and rushed toward the evil dragon in a flash.
However, when the burly man got close to the dust, a miraculous scene appeared.
The dust in front of them should be insignificant. Even mortals who didn't cultivate could at most cover their mouths and rush over.
However, the burly man couldn't rush in. The dust was like a natural barrier, blocking the burly man outside.
"Break!"
The burly man waved the scythe in his hand.
"Clang …"
The scythe slashed through the air, but when the scythe's blade cut through the dust, it only created a small spark. It couldn't cut through the dust at all.
Seeing this, the burly man's expression turned ugly.
Not only the tall and sturdy man, but the others also had ugly expressions. As for Boss Mo, he slowly floated over and used the walking stick in his hand to lightly point at the dust. He then said indifferently, "This is troublesome. This evil dragon really sealed itself up. This sealing power is a kind of instinct of a super divine beast …"
"How long will this seal last?" One of the Demi-Immortals asked.
Boss Mo said, "It won't be too short, but it won't be too long either. The problem isn't that it's sealing itself, but … while it's sealing itself, it'll undergo its fourth transformation! At that time … it won't be something that we can fight against. "
The evil dragon had only undergone three transformations, and it already needed several Ultimate Divine Realm warriors to fight against it. If it underwent its fourth transformation, its strength would increase by leaps and bounds. They would probably need the higher-ups of the Demi-Immortals to come out and deal with it. This was something that none of the Demi-Immortals wanted to see.
"What do we do now?" The burly man asked with a frown. Just now, he had activated his Blood Berserk and managed to injure the Evil Dragon with great difficulty. Just as he was about to take down the Evil Dragon, he did not expect the Evil Dragon's innate talent to be so powerful. What kind of bullshit Dusty Force was this?
Boss Mo shook his head and didn't reply. He was wearing a mask, so no one could see his expression. However, as the leader of the seven, everyone still had to listen to him.
At this time, three figures flew over from the horizon. Boss Mo glanced through the mask and said, "Let's not worry about this first. It seems that Gu Pei 'er's situation will be more troublesome."
"More troublesome?" The burly man looked up. When his gaze moved away from Gu Pei 'er, his bell-like eyes suddenly widened. At the same time, he said angrily, "Damn, that woman took out the Ancestral Dragon's egg? And the Ancestral Dragon hatched! How could it be in her hands?! "
The "her" the burly man was referring to was naturally Luo Zheng.
A middle-aged man who looked like a scholar also frowned, "What's wrong with Pei 'er? She was supposed to chase after that woman, but why did she let her take the Ancestral Dragon?"
Boss Mo sighed softly and said, "Sigh, although I increased my luck before this trip, it seems that someone's luck is even stronger than mine …"
He sighed, as if he had experienced many vicissitudes of life.
As a core member of the Demi-Immortal Clan, Boss Mo had obtained a decent title in the Illusory Tower, and a silver one at that …
After experiencing so many things, Boss Mo believed in luck more and more, and believed in fate more and more!
Therefore, when he saw Luo Zheng holding the Ancestral Dragon and following behind Gu Pei 'er, he didn't get angry. Instead, he sighed.
"Pei 'er, what's going on? Why is the Ancestral Dragon being held by her?! " The burly man stared at Luo Zheng, his voice was extremely unfriendly. Just now, the Evil Dragon sealed him, causing him to fail at the last step. The burly man's mood was already extremely bad. In the end, the matter of the Evil Dragon had not been resolved, and the Ancestral Dragon had hatched, and it was being held by a fleeing aboriginal …
Gu Pei 'er's eyes flashed slightly. She couldn't be bothered with the burly man and descended. At the same time, Luo Zheng and Yu Ze also descended.
At this time, Boss Mo asked faintly, "Pei 'er, what's going on? Did the Ancestral Gragon hatch when she rushed into the steeple? "
Gu Pei 'er nodded, "You can say that."
"The question is, how did she break my barrier?" The middle-aged man who looked like a scholar asked curiously. He couldn't imagine how Luo Zheng could break the barrier that he had painstakingly set up.
Gu Pei 'er smiled faintly and said, "He has the Black Star Pickaxe, a supreme artifact. Qing Feng, no matter how strong your barrier is, it can't block it. The Black Star Pickaxe contains the law of karma, which is much stronger than your own!"
